.
Chapter 201 Three People Break Through to the Yang God Realm!
.

This divine blessing had cost him a considerable amount of power.

It wasn't a simple act of bestowing divine blessings upon so many people; Li Yue had combined the Mountain God's blessing with a significant drain of the divine essence long stored within the sacred mountain, distributing it among the crowd.

Fortunately, his gains were also substantial. This sacrificial ceremony had over 100,000 participants.

Compared to the previous event with only a few dozen participants, this was many times more.

Even now, his merit points were skyrocketing, increasing at an astonishing rate, causing his brow to twitch.

[Realm: Fourth-Order Sacred Mountain]

[Merit Points: 8.32 million/10 million]

[Divine Abilities: Mountain God's Blessing, Dharma Body of Heaven and Earth, Rebirth of Incense, Gratitude for the Source]

[Spells: Manifestation of Avatar, Control of the Five Elements]

[Magic Weapons: Mountain-Suppressing Seal, Heart Mirror, Divine Weapon Furnace, Wind-Stabilizing Pearl]

His merit points had directly surged by over three million, a terrifying amount, and were still increasing at an alarming rate.

Li Yue estimated that he could reach at least nine million today, and perhaps even ten million, directly breaking through to the fifth-tier Holy Mountain.

Then came the spell, Mastering the Five Elements.

[Harvested fifth-tier demon incense offerings, gained the spell *Mastering the Five Elements]

[Mastering the Five Elements: Heaven and Earth, the Five Elements and the Eight Trigrams. The Five Elements are the root of Heaven and Earth: Metal, Wood, Water, Fire, and Earth. They laid the foundation for Heaven and Earth, and are the origin of all things. Casting Mastering the Five Elements allows for a certain degree of control over things of the Five Elements.]

Since gaining Manifestation, Li Yue finally unlocked his second spell.

He had already unlocked many other supernatural abilities and magical artifacts, but this particular spell had previously only included the Manifestation ability.

This was because sacrifices were infrequent, occurring only once a year.

Moreover, Li Yue estimated that even with frequent sacrifices, the spell wouldn't unlock.

This was evident from the last sacrifice.

The Manifestation was unlocked upon receiving the first offering, which consisted of only a few pieces of cured meat and a bowl of rice and noodles.

Later, during the formal sacrifice, the offering was a low-ranking demonic tiger, and the spell didn't unlock then.

Therefore, unlocking the spell likely required a certain interval, or specific requirements for the offerings.

Thinking this through, Li Yue then focused his attention on the spell "Controlling the Five Elements."

The spell's description sounded incredibly powerful, comparable to supernatural abilities.

However, since it only allowed control of the five elements to a certain extent, the actual effect probably wouldn't be as exaggerated. After all, it was just a spell, ultimately a level weaker than supernatural abilities.

“I can give it a try.”

Li Yue thought to himself, and then found a patch of wasteland at the foot of the mountain.

This place used to be a lush forest, but all the trees had been cut down due to the development of Qianshan County.

Now, many saplings had been planted here, ordered by Bai Liang, but it would take more than ten years for them to grow into mature trees.

Li Yue's mind raced, and he directly unleashed his Five Elements Palm Technique—Wood!

Instantly, a green light enveloped the wasteland. Nourished by the green light, the planted saplings instantly rejuvenated, their leaves unfurling, their branches growing larger, and finally, they slowly rose from the ground, growing at a visible speed.

Li Yue watched this scene with surprise, not expecting the power of his Five Elements Palm Technique to be so considerable.

The saplings quickly grew into large trees, and the once barren land was transformed back into a lush, verdant forest in less than fifteen minutes.

The people who had stayed nearby were utterly astonished by this sight.

They all saw the green light descending from the sacred mountain, followed by this miraculous scene.

Wasn't this the legendary manifestation of the mountain god?

Those who had initially come simply out of conformity were now flushed with excitement, kneeling and kowtowing before the sacred mountain.

"Gods, protect us! Mountain God, please bless us!"

Li Yue hadn't expected that his attempt to manipulate the Five Elements would instantly convert the people of Qingzhou who flocked to Thousand Mountain Manor into his followers.

After testing the power of the Wood element, he continued to experiment with several others.

The power of Water caused the river to flow backward from the foot of the mountain towards the sacred mountain, and he could even conjure water mist to bring down a torrential rain.

The power of metal allows him to turn stone into gold; by expending his energy, he can transform various stones into incredibly sharp ores.

The power of earth allows him to casually create a small mountain from the ground with a flick of his wrist—this is the most suitable ability for him.

Li Yue sensed that he could raise the divine mountain even higher, reaching the heavens.

However, he didn't do so. The current height was already quite good; going any higher would be pointless.

Besides, there was a hole in the sky, which the laws of heaven and earth hadn't yet fully repaired.

Who knew what lay outside the hole? Getting too close was never a good idea.

The power of fire is simpler; he can control various flames.

Most interestingly, the five elements can be used in combination.

For example, fire combined with earth can create magma.

Various combinations are quite fascinating.

The power is a bit weak, but that's normal, considering it's magic.

After Li Yue tested the power of the Five Elements, his merit points rose to over 8.6 million.

At this rate, reaching over 9 million today was a certainty.

Advancing to the fifth-tier Sacred Mountain wouldn't be impossible.

His divine sense turned towards the sacrificial plaza.

The crowd had been absorbing the divine essence for some time and was almost finished.

Suddenly, a brilliant Buddhist light erupted, and the old monk in the crowd abruptly unleashed a terrifying aura, spiritual energy flowing back, countless celestial opportunities locking him in.

Some were the first to awaken, their faces filled with shock upon seeing the old monk's condition.

"Hiss, is he about to break through to the Yang God Realm!?"

Recognizing the old monk's situation, several Yin God Realm cultivators who had finished absorbing the divine essence retreated, making way for him.

But this wasn't the end. The next moment, Chu Wuliu and Hu Zhengqiang also unleashed fierce auras, countless spiritual energies flowing back around them.

They were also about to break through to the Yang God Realm!

The consecutive breakthroughs of three people to the Yang God Realm astonished everyone.

They thought to themselves, "As expected of the Divine Essence! Absorbing just a little has brought such a huge improvement; I can't suppress it anymore and am about to break through to the Yang God Realm."

After absorbing the Divine Essence, Bai Liang directly broke through from the Profound Realm to the Enlightened God Realm.

Wu Ze directly reached the peak of the Enlightened God Realm, just one step away from breaking through to the Yin God Realm.

He Liyuan, Du Tiandao, Chen Jianchi, Qian Ke, and others also received significant improvements.

Everyone's faces lit up with joy, then they looked at the three people breaking through in the center of the arena.

"After breaking through to the Yang God Realm, will they have to return to the capital?" Wu Ze asked.

"Not necessarily. The situation is complicated now. Qingzhou no longer belongs to the imperial court. Besides, our hearts are already with the Divine Mountain, so why would we go back to the capital?"

Bian Nong said.

They are now people of the Divine Mountain.

Since the Mountain God bestowed this divine gift, helping Chu Wuliu and the other two break through to the Yang God Realm, there must be a deeper meaning behind it.


.
Chapter 202 Taking the Initiative
.

Breaking through to the Yang God Realm is extremely difficult, let alone such a desperate, unprepared breakthrough.

This caught Chu Wuliu and his companions completely off guard.

If it weren't for the overwhelming power and high rank of the divine essence, they would likely have failed to break through today.

After breaking through to the Enlightenment Realm, Bai Liang focused on consolidating his realm.

Therefore, Wu Ze continued to preside over the sacrificial ceremony.

First, he cleared out irrelevant personnel to avoid disturbing Chu Wuliu and his companions' breakthrough.

Then, he had patrol members and several Yin God Realm experts protect the three of them. Since the breakthrough to the Yang God Realm would take an unknown amount of time, this arrangement would allow them to handle any incidents promptly.

The sacrificial ceremony was drawing to a close.

The twelve demon corpses had transformed into ordinary wild animal carcasses, but their flesh was firmer and more potent than that of ordinary beasts, and consuming them would greatly replenish qi and blood.

Wu Ze glanced at them and then instructed Xiao Nian to distribute the offerings, letting everyone taste what a fifth-tier demon beast was like.

As for demonic creatures, forget about them. Who knows if they're even edible? They certainly wouldn't try.

So they simply dragged them to the spirit field, chopped them up, and composted them as fertilizer.

Half a day had passed by the time they finished. Returning to the temple, they found Chu Wuliu and the other two still in the process of breaking through.

"Breaking through to the Yang God Realm, is it really that difficult?"

Seeing the sweat beading on their foreheads and their struggling expressions, Wu Ze looked surprised.

"It's incredibly difficult. Above the fifth rank, each breakthrough is extremely challenging. The Yin God transforms into the Yang God, and from then on, the primordial spirit can roam freely during the day, truly achieving the ability to travel to the North Sea in the morning and the Cangwu Mountain in the evening." Bian Nong spoke in a deep voice, looking at the three in the process of breaking through with envy.

"Rather than saying this is breaking through a realm, it's more like the primordial spirit is undergoing a sublimation, a process of transforming from Yin God to Yang God. In this process, the primordial spirit will undergo a qualitative change, becoming much stronger!" he continued.

Wu Ze listened, seemingly understanding but not quite, as he hadn't yet reached the Yin God Realm and his understanding of the Primordial Spirit wasn't very profound.

"What about the seventh rank, the Unity Realm, above the Yang God Realm?" Wu Ze suddenly asked.

"Uh..." Bian Nong paused, hesitatingly replying, "I'm not entirely sure about that either. For me, the Yang God Realm is already the limit; I dare not even dream of reaching the Unity Realm."

"The Unity Realm is a watershed, just like the difference between the Profound Realm and the Enlightened God Realm. The Unity Realm is the same as the Yang God Realm; compared to the Yang God Realm, the Unity Realm represents a qualitative leap."

"According to everyone, the Unity Realm allows the Primordial Spirit to completely merge with the physical body, achieving unity of spirit and body, condensing the True Spirit, and reaching the ultimate sublimation! The True Spirit is immortal, allowing one to enter reincarnation and relive life and death!"

"Unity of spirit and body, condensing the True Spirit?" Wu Ze murmured these eight words, a sense of understanding dawning on him.

From Bian Nong's words, he understood that above the seventh rank, there was no longer a Primordial Spirit, but rather a True Spirit.

A true spirit is far more powerful than a primordial spirit; as long as it isn't destroyed, it has a chance to be resurrected.

Seeing that Wu Ze seemed about to ask something more, Bian Nong quickly stopped him, saying, "Don't ask anymore. I know even less about the Venerable Realm beyond that. Not to mention the legendary Saint Realm; you couldn't find a single one in the entire Great Chu Dynasty."

Wu Ze smiled awkwardly and didn't ask any more questions.

Knowing about the Unity Realm is already quite good.

As for the Eighth-Order Venerable Realm, it's still a long way off for him; he doesn't need to worry about it now.

Actually, if it weren't for the Full Moon Yin God, he could try to break through to the Yin God Realm right now.

To pursue perfection, he has to temporarily suppress his realm.

Thinking of the Full Moon Yin God, Wu Ze wondered if the Yang God Realm had a Full Sun Yang God.

He immediately asked Bian Nong about this.

Bian Nong looked puzzled and replied, "Yes, it does, but it's not called the Full Sun Yang God; it's called the Heavenly Eclipse Yang God. Only when the sun is completely eclipsed, when the celestial dog eats the sun, can one achieve the Heavenly Eclipse Yang God."

"And most importantly, you can only have one: the Full Moon Yin God or the Heavenly Eclipse Yang God. If you condense the Full Moon Yin God, you can't condense the Heavenly Eclipse Yang God. Similarly, if you want to condense the Heavenly Eclipse Yang God, you can't condense the Full Moon Yin God."

Hearing Bian Nong's answer, Wu Ze was surprised; he hadn't expected such a statement.

It seems there's no way to have both.

The opportunity to achieve the Full Moon Yin God status is in a month, so he can only choose the Full Moon Yin God status; the Heavenly Eclipse Yang God status is out of the question.

Wu Ze thought to himself.

After stabilizing his cultivation level, Bai Liang immediately went to the temple.

However, Chu Wuliu and the other two were still in the process of breaking through.

"They won't be able to break through for a while; it might take some more time. We can keep an eye on things here," Bian Nong said to Bai Liang.

Bai Liang nodded after hearing this, asked a few more questions, and then turned to leave.

He still had many matters to handle. The threat from the demons hadn't been eliminated, and now the capital had dealt him another major blow.

He received a letter from his brother-in-law, Hua Wuzei, informing him that Emperor Chu had ordered all kinds of geniuses from the capital to leave and go to Qingzhou for training.

Among these geniuses, each one was renowned, with the lowest cultivation level being the Yin God Realm, and many even reaching the Yang God Realm.

Moreover, there were also renowned top geniuses, once famous throughout the land.

For example, the two brothers of Xuanqing Temple, Wuyuan Wuhui and Jueqing, wielding swords and sabers respectively, their intents perfectly aligned, could rival all others and fight beyond their level when they joined forces.

The Four Vajras of Jinlong Temple—do not look at them, do not speak of them, do not move them, do not listen to them. Each of the four cultivated a Buddhist supernatural power, possessing unparalleled strength, making fighting beyond their level a piece of cake.

And the imperial court had even more powerful figures!

The son and successor of the great Confucian scholar Shangguan Liumo, whose Confucianism and Taoism had reached their pinnacle. It was rumored that Shangguan Liumo himself could not even defeat his own son in a debate on Confucian classics.

The son of Cha Yunpo, the foremost military general, possessed superhuman strength from birth. Having been trained in military strategy from a young age, he excelled in battle formations and was unmatched in direct combat.

And then there was the mysterious disciple of the Imperial Preceptor Yunji, said to be a person envied by heaven, able to foresee the future from childhood, possessing uncanny foresight.

Though blind, he could see into people's hearts, and would occasionally tell them that a hole had appeared in the sky, and two hideous monsters had crawled through it.

Besides him, several princes and princesses had also arrived.

After reading the letter his brother-in-law had sent, Bai Liang took a deep breath, feeling an unbearable headache.

"With so many prodigies, Qingzhou is truly going to be in complete chaos!" Bai Liang said in a deep voice.

The threat of demons was imminent, and before it could even be resolved, Emperor Chu pulled this stunt.

What was he trying to do? Was he deliberately creating more trouble?

Bai Liang couldn't understand. Perhaps Emperor Chu genuinely wanted to use this opportunity to train the capital's prodigies.

After all, prodigies who don't shed blood aren't true prodigies.

"The situation is becoming increasingly complex. We can't sit idly by and wait to be killed. We must take the initiative!"

Bai Liang said coldly.


.
Chapter 203 Keeping Demons Out of the State Gate?
.

Bai Liang then found Wang Lan and asked if he had completed the tasks he had assigned.

"Lord Bai, I have told Master Kongwang everything, without hiding anything," Wang Lan replied loyally.

"Kongwang has set off?" Bai Liang asked again.

Wang Lan nodded quickly and said, "Yes, Master Kongwang set off immediately after hearing what I told him, even skipping the sacrificial ceremony, saying that this matter was of utmost importance and could not be delayed."

Bai Liang breathed a sigh of relief upon hearing this.

When Wang Lan led Shishu County to join Qianshan County earlier, he had brought him a piece of news.

This news was now proving useful and extremely important, potentially influencing the current situation in Qingzhou.

Therefore, Bai Liang assigned Kongwang to this mission, as Kongwang possessed the Divine Concealment Talisman, making certain actions convenient and discreet.

"If this succeeds, you will have rendered a great service!" Bai Liang said to Wang Lan with a smile.

Upon hearing this, Wang Lan's expression immediately became excited.

"This is what I should do. Serving the sacred mountain is my duty!" Wang Lan declared, expressing his loyalty.

Bai Liang then approached Chen Jianchi and Qian Ke. They had originally planned to return to Shishu County after attending the sacrificial ceremony.

However, given the current urgency, Shishu County was outside the sacred mountain's boundaries. Bai Liang feared that if they went there, something might happen, and he wouldn't be able to help them then. Therefore, he advised them to stay on the sacred mountain.

But Chen Jianchi and Qian Ke refused outright.

"Brother Bai Liang, we are not the kind of people who would cowardly survive. In this time of crisis, if we don't go to Shishu County to maintain order, chaos will ensue, and everything we've done will be in vain. Therefore, we will not stay on the sacred mountain," Chen Jianchi said firmly.

Qian Ke's expression mirrored Chen Jianchi's.

They went to Shishu County, built a mountain god temple, and spread the mountain god's faith. They had already made a start, and the people of the county were gradually beginning to believe in the mountain god.

If they were to retreat and hide in the sacred mountain now, ignoring the people of Shishu County because of the threat of demons, it would all be in vain, and the people would utterly lose faith in them.

Bai Liang remained silent; he naturally understood this.

But if the demons truly arrived, Chen Jianchi and Qian Ke's strength would be wasted; they would simply be throwing their lives away.

"Then go," Bai Liang finally said. "Report any developments immediately, and I will send people to rescue you."

Chen Jianchi and Qian Ke, along with Zhang Shiye and Wang Lan, headed to Shishu County.

Wang Lan was somewhat reluctant; he wanted to stay in the sacred mountain, but Qian Ke disagreed.

Without him as the county magistrate, how could Shishu County be properly governed?

As for Zhang Shiye, since joining the sacred mountain, he had matured considerably.

From initially being cowardly and afraid of death, he was now willing to die for the sacred mountain without batting an eye.

This was undoubtedly partly due to the excessive consumption of divine water.

After dealing with these matters, Bai Liang made another trip to Tianyue County.

Tianyue County was now indirectly controlled by Qianshan County. Bai Liang's visit to Tianyue County was naturally intended to completely bring it under the control of Shenshan.

Tianyue County's population had dwindled to just over 600,000, and was still declining.

The departing people naturally all went to Shenshan.

Bai Liang then directly announced that Tianyue County would henceforth be under the jurisdiction of Qianshan County!

Er Qing and Du Tiandao would lead their men to take over.

This news caused an uproar among those who had originally planned to flee Tianyue County for Shenshan, and they all abandoned their plans to leave.

Bai Liang then spread the word that those who came from all over Qingzhou to seek refuge with Shenshan could go to Tianyue County and Taohua County, both of which belonged to Shenshan.

This would alleviate the population pressure on Qianshan County.

Meanwhile, in front of the temple.

Chu Wuliu and his two companions had reached the crucial moment of their breakthrough.

Their primordial spirits had been 99% transformed, only the final trace remained.

With a muffled explosion of spiritual energy, the old monk Sanfu was the first to break through to the Yang God Realm.

A unique aura belonging to the Yang God Realm spread out in ripples, directly forcing the protectors back several steps.

"Amitabha! This old monk has finally succeeded in breaking through!"

Sanfu laughed heartily, slapping the ground with one hand, his body, which had been sitting cross-legged, soared into the air.

Then, his primordial spirit left his body, roaming freely under the blazing sun!

Immediately afterward, Chu Wuliu and Hu Zhengqiang also successfully broke through, their primordial spirits leaving their bodies to experience the power of the Yang God Realm.

"Congratulations to the three fellow Daoists on breaking through to the sixth rank and becoming Yang God Realm cultivators!"

Bian Nong and the others offered their congratulations with great envy.

"Haha, likewise! You too have received the divine blessing of the Mountain God, and you're not far from the Yang God Realm."

Hu Zhengqiang laughed loudly, his heart filled with joy.

"That's still a long way off," Bian Nong and the others shook their heads.

Breaking through to the Yang God Realm is no easy feat. For those with average talent like them, it takes over a decade to break through a single realm.

Furthermore, there's a chance of failure; success isn't guaranteed every time.

A failed breakthrough damages the primordial spirit, requiring several years of recuperation before attempting another breakthrough.

The fact that Chu Wuliu and the other two were able to break through to the next level so easily was thanks to the mysterious effects of the divine essence.

"We must thank the Mountain God. If it weren't for the Mountain God's divine blessing, we wouldn't have been able to break through to the Yang God Realm so easily."

Chu Wuliu spoke.

Sanfu and Hu Zhengqiang wholeheartedly agreed.

So, led by Wuze, the three arrived at the temple and knelt down in utmost respect to express their gratitude.

"Disciples kowtow in gratitude to the Mountain God for his divine grace. We have no way to repay him, and we will certainly do our utmost to contribute to the sacred mountain!" the three said in unison.

Li Yue sensed this and looked over with his divine sense.

He had only bestowed the divine essence on a whim; he hadn't expected it to directly enable these three to reach the Yang God Realm.

However, this was for the best. With the Yang God Realm, Qianshan County could better cope with the complex situation in Qingzhou.

He had also seen the letter sent by Hua Wuzei.

He hadn't expected the Emperor of Great Chu to issue such an order, summoning all the geniuses of the capital to Qingzhou for training, including some at the Yang God Realm. Was this a direct disregard for the Tianshui Agreement, a complete break with tradition?

Li Yue didn't know the Emperor's intentions, but he was certain that the arrival of these geniuses in Qingzhou would definitely throw the entire situation into chaos.

There would certainly be powerful figures among the demons, and there was also a rebellious Dragon-Slaying Sect in the shadows.

Qianshan County was caught in the middle, which was indeed quite difficult.

Self-preservation is certainly not a problem, but the problem lies with the tens of millions of people in Qingzhou.

Once the demons enter Qingzhou, even if they are eventually eradicated, countless civilians will fall victim to their clutches and perish.

The only way to protect all the people of Qingzhou seems to be to keep the demons out of the state, blocking their army at the border and preventing them from entering!


.
Chapter 204 Leaving the Country
.

How to keep demons out of Qingzhou?

Li Yue pondered this question. Using his manifested avatar carrying the Mountain-Suppressing Seal might be possible.

However, if he intervened, it would inevitably alert the two Saints among the demons.

He wasn't sure if his current strength was sufficient to contend with a ninth-tier Saint, so to be on the safe side, he couldn't send his manifested avatar with the Mountain-Suppressing Seal to suppress the demons.

Since he couldn't act himself, he could only rely on his subordinates.

Chu Wuliu and the other two had just broken through to the sixth-tier Yang God Realm. Although their realms were still unstable, their strength was considerable.

As long as the demons weren't at the peak of the sixth tier, they could handle them.

However, given the precedent of the sixth-tier Blade Demon's death, the Holy Court certainly wouldn't send only a sixth-tier demon to Qingzhou; there would definitely be a more powerful demon leading the way.

Li Yue checked his panel again. His merit points had reached over nine million, and he was only one or two days away from breaking through to the fifth-tier Saint Mountain.

Breaking through to the fifth rank would grant him greater power to confront the two Saintly figures of the demons.

However, Li Yue always faced a dilemma: he was like a mountain, unable to move.

Therefore, he could only hope that after breaking through to the fifth rank of Saintly Mountain, his divine sense range would expand, allowing him to do more.

Right now, facing the overwhelming demons, he could only let Chu Wuliu and the others hold them off for the time being.

Anyway, if they couldn't win, they could retreat; within the range of the divine mountain, the demons were helpless.

The safety of the people of Qingzhou was a crucial issue.

If the geniuses from the capital could be utilized, they would be a great asset.

Qianshan County.

After Chu Wuliu and the other two broke through to the Yang God Realm, they were invited to the Mountain Tower.

Bai Liang directly handed everyone Hua Wuzei's letter.

After reading it, everyone frowned deeply.

"Qingzhou is in complete chaos. The imperial court, Xuanqing Temple, Jinlong Temple, plus the demons and the clandestine Dragon-Slaying Sect—the waters are getting increasingly murky!"

Hu Zhengqiang spoke in a deep voice, feeling utterly troubled.

"If the imperial court had come to assist in resisting the demons, that would be fine," Wu Ze said.

Chu Wuliu, however, said calmly, "Heh, based on my understanding of the imperial court, these 'prodigies' couldn't possibly be here to resist the demons. They're just here for training, to witness the strength of the demons, as stated in the letter. If they actually encounter demons, they'll run faster than anyone else!"

Bian Nong and the others nodded in agreement.

Those so-called 'prodigies' in the capital—did others not know?

Arrogant and conceited, only interested in pleasure, how many times have they ever left the capital, how many times have they actually fought?

They're all a bunch of pampered young masters and ladies.

Of course, there are some truly talented ones among them.

“We can contact them. Even if they can't fight the demons with all their might, sharing some of the pressure will lighten our burden,” Bai Liang said.

“Then let Sanfu contact them. He's a disciple of Jinlong Temple; he can contact the four prodigies there,” Chu Wuliu said, looking at the old monk.

But the old monk shook his head directly, his face solemn: “Chu Prefecture, you can't speak carelessly. This old monk has already found his spiritual mountain. As for Jinlong Temple, our connection has long since ended.”

These words left the others speechless, but they didn't say anything.

The final task fell to Chu Wuliu. He had some connections in the capital and knew a few people.

“I might not be able to persuade those people. The people of the capital are arrogant; they might not even respect me, the lord of a prefecture,” Chu Wuliu said.

“It’s alright, just deliver the message. Also, see what they’re doing in Qingzhou. They can’t just wander around aimlessly, can they?” Bai Liang said.

Chu Wuliu nodded and set off.

According to Hua Wuzei’s letter, many of the capital’s prodigies had already arrived in Qingzhou City.

These prodigies, befitting their privileged backgrounds, didn’t immediately think of investigating the demons’ whereabouts upon arriving in Qingzhou.

Instead, they were flaunting their cultivation in Qingzhou City, thoroughly enjoying their superiority.

They were currently preparing a grand gathering of prodigies, hoping to bring all the capital’s talents together in Qingzhou City to discuss their upcoming trials.

It could be said that they genuinely treated this trip to Qingzhou as a normal training exercise, lacking any sense of crisis.

Chu Wuliu shook his head after seeing this. Could this bunch of good-for-nothings really share the burden?

They might even become a hindrance later.

Qingzhou Border.

This was a border several hundred miles away from the border between Baijiangzhou and Qingzhou.

Beyond the border lay the territory of the demons.

However, it was once called Luohezhou, a very prosperous prefecture.

Connecting with Tianshuizhou, Luohezhou was named after the Luo River, a tributary of the Tianhe River. The river flowed into Tianshuizhou, making its waterways well-developed and boosting its economy.

However, it was conquered by the demons before the Battle of Tianshui.

The demons used Luohezhou as a springboard to launch the Battle of Tianshui.

The three members of the Qilin Clan were currently hidden around the border, monitoring the movements beyond.

Three golden rays of light, having just flown from the sacred mountain, entered the three men's bodies, nearly scaring them to death.

They later learned it was a divine gift from the Mountain God, which deeply moved them.

The Mountain God hadn't forgotten them; even without participating in the sacrificial ceremony, they had still received a divine gift.

The divine essence contained within the golden light was absorbed by the three men.

Originally, absorbing this divine essence should have allowed them to break through to the Yin God Realm in one fell swoop, but the golden light flickered and directly suppressed their cultivation level.

Now, their accumulated power was already astounding.

Once they achieved the Full Moon Yin God status, they could probably break through to the late Yin God Realm in one fell swoop!

Suddenly, the space around the Qilin shifted, his expression changed drastically, and he instantly retreated several meters, his weapon appearing in his hand.

"Who goes there!?" he said coldly.

With his shout, the Black Dragon and Fire Phoenix, lurking in other locations, were immediately alerted and surrounded him.

"Amitabha, it's me." A figure suddenly appeared—a young monk.

"Kong Wang!?"

Seeing the person before them, the three of them breathed a sigh of relief.

After confirming it was indeed Kong Wang, they stepped forward.

"Master Kong Wang, what brings you here? Didn't you attend the Mountain God's sacrificial ceremony?" Qilin asked, somewhat puzzled.

"No, this humble monk has a mission to fulfill," Kong Wang replied, shaking his head.

Qilin asked, "What mission? Is there anything we three brothers can do to help?"

"No, that's not necessary," Kong Wang shook his head, then said, "This humble monk needs to leave the country."

"Leave the country!?"

The three brothers were startled. The foreign lands were the territory of demons and monsters. Even Yang God level experts dared not venture beyond those borders; demons roamed everywhere!


.
Chapter 205 Gathering of Prodigies
.

Qilin was somewhat worried about Kongwang's safety, but remembering Kongwang's mysterious and unpredictable concealment techniques, he gradually relaxed.

However, he still reminded him, "The demon army has already stationed itself in the eastern corner of Luohe Prefecture, with countless Yang Shen realm cultivators among them. Master Kongwang, please be careful to avoid them!"

Kongwang nodded, memorized the location Qilin mentioned, and then his figure vanished on the spot, setting off to leave the border.

Crossing the border line, he entered the territory of Luohe Prefecture.

This was the border region, the territory of the demons.

Kongwang used the Divine Concealment Talisman to travel through Luohe Prefecture.

Everything here was completely different from within the border; there was no life. The only moving creatures he could see were snakes, rats, ants, and insects.

And these weren't ordinary snakes, rats, ants, or insects; they were mutated creatures corrupted by the aura of demons.

If they absorbed more blood energy, they would advance to higher realms and become true demonic creatures.

Kong Wang didn't kill the demons he encountered along the way. His journey wasn't about slaying demons; he came with a mission.

The land beyond was also far from peaceful. Although there were no humans, demons fought amongst themselves.

Kong Wang witnessed several major battles between demons, and these were large-scale battles.

The victors would devour the losers, wiping them out completely.

The rule here was survival of the fittest, incredibly cruel.

The demons also had their own cities, but compared to human cities, they were much more dilapidated and rudimentary.

There were no shops or restaurants; only various human beings chained together.

Kong Wang even saw many people of other races among them.

These were various small kingdoms and tribes scattered around the Great Chu Dynasty; they didn't belong to the Great Chu Dynasty.

With the demons wreaking havoc, and without the Great Chu Dynasty's immense fortune to protect its people, they became the demons' primary food source.

Within a year, countless small kingdoms and tribes were wiped out. Many fled to the Great Chu, while others escaped in the opposite direction, their fates unknown.

Kong Wang silently watched these enslaved humans; their eyes had long since lost their light, utterly empty.

For them, the moment they were captured by the demons, they were already dead.

The demons treated them like livestock, breeding them and then feeding on them.

It was terrifying; even suicide was a luxury for them.

Kong Wang felt unbearable pain, but he was powerless to do anything.

This demon city was guarded by two sixth-tier demon generals and three sixth-tier demon commanders.

Such formidable power made him hesitant even with the Divine Concealment Talisman.

"Amitabha..." he murmured a Buddhist chant, suppressing his complex emotions, then turned and left.

A token appeared in Kong Wang's hand.

The token was crafted entirely from black iron, gleaming with a dark light, and engraved with two large seal script characters in the center:

"Dragon Slayer!"

This was the token of the Dragon Slayer Sect, given to him by Wang Lan.

Wang Lan, this fellow, not only betrayed the General's Office and secretly contacted the prefectural government to plot a rebellion,

he even secretly maintained contact with the Dragon Slayer Sect; this token was given to him by them.

However, when he defected to Qianshan County, he revealed everything, and also handed the token to Bai Liang.

The situation in Qingzhou was complex, so Bai Liang considered using the token, hoping it might have unexpected effects.

Therefore, he sent Kong Wang with the token to search for the members of the Dragon-Slaying Sect.

The token had a sensor, allowing Kong Wang to sense the Dragon-Slaying Sect members on the other side.

However, the more he looked, the more something seemed amiss. The token pointed towards the eastern corner of Luohe Prefecture.

Wasn't that where the demon army was stationed?

.........

Qingzhou City.

Since the imperial court withdrew, the city had experienced chaos.

Later, it was only through the combined efforts of Kong Wang and Yue Qiu that order was restored somewhat.

After Kong Wang left, Yue Qiu temporarily took over maintaining order in Qingzhou City.

However, his strength was too weak to control the overall situation, especially after the arrival of a self-proclaimed prodigy from the capital, he completely lost control of Qingzhou City.

But he wasn't discouraged; on the contrary, he was excited.

The arrival of prodigies from the capital in Qingzhou—does this prove the imperial court hasn't abandoned Qingzhou?

As more and more prodigies arrived in Qingzhou City, Yue Qiu became even more convinced of this.

The imperial court certainly hadn't abandoned Qingzhou; otherwise, they wouldn't have sent so many prodigies from the capital to Qingzhou.

Moreover, each of these prodigies was renowned!

There was the eldest young master of the Zhao family, a top genius of the Tianmo Academy, already a Yin Shen realm cultivator at only thirty years old.

And then there was the daughter of the Minister of Revenue, known as one of the four beauties of the capital, wielding a soft whip with unparalleled skill and formidable strength.

Prodigies like these were simply countless.

Yue Qiu originally thought this was already quite astonishing, but he hadn't expected that the people arriving today were the real highlight.

“Wu Yuan and Wu Hui of Xuanqing Temple greet all fellow Daoists!”

With flashing blades and overflowing sword energy, two figures traversed the sky, appearing directly above Qingzhou City.

Everyone looked up, their faces trembling at the powerful aura emanating from the two figures!

"No Regrets?! They actually came!" A prodigy from the capital gasped.

Even as fellow prodigies, they bowed their heads in awe before Wu Yuan Wu Hui, forced to look up in disbelief.

"The Golden Dragon Temple, by special invitation, refrains from seeing, speaking, hearing, or moving, and greets all fellow Daoists!"

Golden light descended from the west, enveloping the entire city of Qingzhou.

Following the sound, everyone looked and saw a massive bell appear, upon which stood four figures.

They stood there, as immovable as Mount Tai!

"The Four Vajras of the Golden Dragon Temple have all come! This is defying the heavens! And the bell they stand on... could it be a seventh-tier magic treasure, the Maitreya Bell?!"

The appearance of the four from the Golden Dragon Temple caused an even greater sensation.

These four are all at the Yang Shen Realm. With their combined strength, plus the Maitreya Bell, even a seventh-tier Unity Realm expert wouldn't be invincible!

Wu Yuan looked at the four from Jinlong Temple, his expression slightly grave.

"Something's not right. Why did Jinlong Temple bring the Maitreya Bell along?" Wu Yuan asked coldly.

The Four Vajras are already incredibly powerful; even the two brothers working together couldn't guarantee a victory.

With such strength, why would they need to bring a seventh-tier magic weapon?

"They probably have other purposes," Wu Hui said calmly.

Before the clamor below could subside, a surge of literary energy appeared on the horizon, with streaks of dark clouds sweeping across the sky, using the heavens as paper and the clouds as ink.

In an instant, a figure was drawn, and the next moment, the figure stepped out of the painting, holding a paper fan, dressed in a white robe, and resembling a scholar.

"Shangguan Shenghua of Tianmo Academy greets fellow Daoists!" Shangguan Shenghua smiled, cupping his hands in greeting with his paper fan in hand.

"Shangguan Shenghua, what are you pretending for?" a disdainful voice rang out.

The drums thundered, a chilling aura enveloping the sky, and the roar of a thousand horses seemed to fill the ears of the crowd.

A muscular giant trampled Shangguan Shenghua's ink painting, stepping forward.


.
Chapter 206 A Madman
.

"Cha Xixing! Do we have a grudge?" Shangguan Shenghua's expression darkened upon seeing the newcomer, and he spoke coldly.

"No grudge," Cha Xixing replied crisply, brandishing his halberd.

"No grudge? If we have no grudge, why do you always target me? Have you lost your mind?"

Shangguan Shenghua abandoned all pretense of composure, becoming increasingly exasperated.

He was utterly fed up with this bully, who, without any understanding, was constantly making things difficult for him.

"We may not have a grudge, but our fathers are mortal enemies. A father's enmity is passed down to his son, so isn't it perfectly normal for me to target you?"

Cha Xixing stated matter-of-factly.

"You..." Shangguan Shenghua's face was ashen, and he said stiffly, "What do our fathers' feud have to do with us? We have no connection whatsoever. I... I'm truly speechless."

In the end, he had nothing more to say.

Indeed, what was there to say to this stubborn mule, Cha Xixing? It was pointless, like casting pearls before swine.

The crowd watching the conflict between the two found it all amusing.

In the capital, everyone knew the irreconcilable conflict between Prime Minister Shangguan and General Cha.

One represented the head of the civil officials, the other the head of the military officials.

Cha Xixing had accepted this fact, so naturally, he wouldn't give Shangguan Shenghua a friendly look.

Poor Shangguan Shenghua, already targeted by Cha Xixing in the capital, didn't expect to be targeted even outside the capital.

This infuriated Shangguan Shenghua, who resolved to stay far away from this stubborn bull to avoid any unpleasantness.

Just as the two were facing off, a figure slowly walked in from the city gate.

It was a woman, her eyes blindfolded with black cloth.

However, she walked with incredible speed and agility. Despite appearing slow, she could cover dozens of meters in the blink of an eye, making onlookers think they were seeing things.

In an instant, the woman appeared in the center of Qingzhou City, right where the prodigies were located.

The sudden appearance of this person startled everyone.

Upon seeing her face clearly, someone exclaimed, "Miss Yun, you've come too?"

This statement immediately caused a stir among the crowd.

Compared to the prodigies before them, this Miss Yun clearly held a much higher place in everyone's hearts.

"The Imperial Advisor actually sent Miss Yun to Qingzhou without any worries. Aren't they afraid of causing another uproar?" someone whispered, looking utterly puzzled.

Someone else, unaware of the inside story, asked curiously, "What do you mean? Is this Miss Yun very powerful? Why is everyone afraid she'll cause trouble?"

"You don't know? Which family are you from?" the person being asked looked at the questioner with suspicion.

"I'm from the Bai family of the capital. I really don't know these things," Bai Lihuo said softly.

"The Bai family?" the person muttered, a sneer playing on their lips, but they still replied, "So it's a declining family. It's normal that they don't know Miss Yun's reputation."

"Miss Yun is incredibly strong, that's undeniable. But what truly makes her famous and feared is that she's a madwoman!"

After saying this, the person's face showed lingering fear as they looked up at Miss Yun, afraid of being overheard.

"A madwoman? What do you mean?" Bai Lihuo was astonished, unable to understand why the Imperial Preceptor's personal disciple would be a madwoman; it was too unexpected.

"Why ask so many questions..." The man, annoyed by the questions, was about to refuse when Bai Lihuo handed him a bottle of pills.

"Ahem." Taking the pills, the man coughed, his tone becoming much more friendly.

"A madwoman, of course, means exactly what she says! This Miss Yun, although exceptionally powerful, is someone the heavens envy. From a young age, she could see the secrets of heaven, thus being intolerable to the Heavenly Dao. Have you heard of the Five Deficiencies and Three Lacks? So Miss Yun is blind, unable to see anything."

"And every so often, for unknown reasons, she would 'go into qi deviation' and then go mad. Rumor has it that she caused quite a stir back then, and in the end, the Imperial Preceptor had to intervene to resolve it for her."

After hearing Miss Yun's story, Bai Lihuo was secretly astonished.

Originally, they had thought that this Miss Yun was stunningly beautiful and had a captivating figure, and they could find an opportunity to get close to her. Now it seemed best to forget about it.

"I didn't expect all of you fellow cultivators to have arrived already; it's us who are late."

A domineering voice came from the sky, and a dragon-drawn carriage slowly approached, pulled by two fifth-tier demon horses, both already tamed.

Hearing this voice, everyone's expression changed.

Seeing the distinctive dragon-drawn carriage, they all reacted and respectfully bowed, saying, "Greetings, Your Highness the Third Prince."

Wu Yuan Wu Hui and the others also bowed to the carriage.

"This isn't the capital; such formalities are unnecessary."

The Third Prince's voice was domineering yet warm, making everyone feel very comfortable.

"I didn't expect Your Highness to come as well. It seems His Majesty values ​​this Qingzhou training very much," Cha Xixing said.

“My father’s attitude is resolute this time. I didn’t want to leave the capital, but I had no choice,” the Third Prince sighed.

“Coming here for training is much better than staying in the capital,” Shangguan Shenghua said with a smile.

He was practically rotting away in the capital; finally being able to leave was incredibly liberating.

“Let’s not dwell on that. Now that we’re in Qingzhou and everyone is gathered here, why not hold a banquet here? It would be a good start before our training,” the Third Prince said casually.

“Yes, Your Highness!” everyone responded.

However, being unfamiliar with Qingzhou, they ultimately had to entrust the task of arranging the banquet to Yue Qiu.

Not only that, but he also had to arrange accommodations for everyone.

This left Yue Qiu completely bewildered.

He thought to himself, “Weren’t you all here to slay demons and monsters? Why is your first priority upon arriving in Qingzhou to eat, drink, and have fun? It’s like you’re on vacation! This doesn’t make sense!”

Yue Qiu's temperament led him to speak directly, "Seniors, didn't you come to Qingzhou to protect its people and drive away demons?"

"Where did this blind fool come from? Are you questioning us?" one of the prodigies retorted sharply, unleashing his aura. The pressure unique to the Yin God Realm surged towards Yue Qiu.

Yue Qiu, with his Tongfa Realm cultivation, was utterly unable to withstand it and immediately coughed up blood.

In the end, it was only through the intervention of those inside the dragon carriage that Yue Qiu's life was saved.

"Zhao Fuhu, don't be so hot-tempered. Attacking the weak is not the behavior of a prodigy," the Third Prince's voice came from the imperial carriage.

Zhao Fuhu quickly lowered his head, repeatedly agreeing.

Yue Qiu wiped the blood from the corner of his mouth, his face pale, his steps unsteady.

He felt no fear, but rather a sense of loss.

He had initially thought these prodigies had come to protect the people of Qingzhou, but now it seemed that wasn't the case.

Chapter 207 Reunion
.

No one answered Yue Qiu's question, for in the eyes of these prodigies, Yue Qiu was no different from an ant.

Yue Qiu remained silent, only able to comply with their demands, arranging lodging and a banquet for them.

Watching these so-called prodigies of the capital, chatting and laughing, indulging in romance and boasting,

he felt utterly ridiculous.

Demons were practically at their doorstep, yet they still had the leisure to enjoy themselves; he truly overestimated them.

"You seem quite disappointed?"

Suddenly, a gentle voice rang out.

Yue Qiu was startled, turning around to see a blindfolded woman abruptly appear beside him, silently observing the people at the banquet.

"Shouldn't they be disappointed?"

Yue Qiu wasn't afraid; instead, he calmly replied, "The imperial court abandoned Qingzhou, leaving the people homeless. The demon army is about to attack, yet these people are still indulging in pleasure, showing no sense of crisis whatsoever. Do they not value the lives of the people?"

"You're right, the lives of the people are indeed not valued in their eyes," Miss Yun said calmly.

Yue Qiu was speechless, not expecting Miss Yun's answer to be so direct.

"If they don't value lives, then why did they come to Qingzhou? Just for this banquet?" Yue Qiu sneered.

"Of course not. They say it's for training, but also to let them see blood," Miss Yun said lightly.

Then she turned her head, her eyes, covered by a black cloth, seemingly able to see into Yue Qiu's heart.

"Do you want them to stand before the people and fight against the demons?" she asked, her red lips slightly parted, her voice seductive.

"Of course I do!" Yue Qiu nodded without hesitation.

"I have a plan, but it's very dangerous. It depends on whether you're willing to take the risk for the people of Qingzhou," Miss Yun said, pursing her lips.

Upon hearing this, Yue Qiu frowned, doubting the truth of her words.

"If it can truly protect the people of Qingzhou, what does it matter if there are mountains of corpses and seas of blood ahead?" he said in a deep voice.

"Alright!" Miss Yun smiled, then said, "You should have heard who I am."

Yue Qiu nodded; he had, of course, heard her.

The Imperial Preceptor's personal disciple, a person envied by heaven, capable of seeing through the secrets of the heavens and predicting the future.

"Since you know who I am, just do as I say." Miss Yun gave a strange smile, then her lips parted slightly, transmitting her voice to Yue Qiu.

After hearing what Miss Yun said, Yue Qiu was first astonished, then looked at her with a complex expression, finally taking a deep breath and nodding heavily.

"I'll do it!"

.............

Outside the capital, Luohe Prefecture.

Kong Wang had traveled several hundred miles and was now approaching the eastern corner of Luohe Prefecture.

He stopped at the back of a small hill, looking ahead.

Before him, billowing black smoke and surging demonic energy filled the air, extremely menacing. With just one glance, he knew it was the location of a demon army.

But why hadn't the demon army attacked yet? Were they preparing something?

Puzzled, Kong Wang dared not approach any further.

Although the Divine Concealment Talisman was powerful, there was no guarantee that there wouldn't be any powerful figures among the demon army.

It was better not to take that risk.

The token in his hand flashed continuously, pointing the way forward.

This indicated that members of the Dragon-Slaying Sect were among the demon army.

This put Kong Wang in a difficult position. He hadn't expected the Dragon-Slaying Sect to be mixed up with demons; no wonder they were able to operate so well abroad—they had already colluded.

He didn't proceed. His token was flashing, and similarly, the other party's token was also flashing faintly.

The other party would immediately understand the reason, so Kong Wang only needed to wait quietly for them to come to him.

About an incense stick's time passed. Sensing a living being approaching, Kong Wang quickly activated his Divine Concealment Talisman to hide himself.

A moment later, a figure draped entirely in black robes appeared on the hillside.

Judging from her figure, she was likely a woman, and with a good figure at that.

The woman looked back and forth on the hillside, then glanced at the continuously flashing token in her hand, her brow furrowed.

Finally, she lowered her voice and said, "Did you kill Wang Lan and take the token? Show yourself, I'm alone."

Kong Wang recognized the voice; it sounded somewhat familiar. His eyes flashed with golden light, and he saw the woman in black clearly.

"What a coincidence, it's her." Recognizing the person before him, Kong Wang was quite surprised.

Last time in Qingzhou City, he had casually saved Nangong Hu, and he hadn't expected to see her again after only a few dozen days.

Kong Wang revealed himself. Seeing someone suddenly appear, Nangong Hu was startled and took a few steps back. After recognizing him, she exclaimed, "It's you!? That monk from Qingzhou City?"

"Long time no see, Benefactor Nangong," Kong Wang said calmly.

Nangong Hu was filled with suspicion, but after confirming that Kong Wang was alright, her brows relaxed considerably.

"You monk, how did you end up abroad? And why is the token I gave to Wang Lan in your hands!?" Nangong Hu asked, puzzled.

“This token was given to me by Wang Lan. He's now a member of the Divine Mountain,” Kong Wang said simply.

Nangong Hu understood immediately.

Her eyes flashed with malice, and she coldly snorted, “That good-for-nothing Wang Lan actually betrayed me, turning to the Divine Mountain and selling me out!”

She had secretly bribed Wang Lan to plant a spy.

But she never expected that spy would betray her in the end.

She then looked at Kong Wang and asked, “So, little monk, you took the token and came across the border to find me. Is there something you need?”

“Not to find you, just a coincidence,” Kong Wang shook his head, then said, “I came here intending to use this token to plan something, but it seems that won’t work now.”

“Heh, is it because I recognized you?” Nangong Hu chuckled.

Kong Wang nodded.

"Speak," Nangong Hu said calmly, "what is your sacred mountain's purpose? If it's not too outrageous, perhaps I'll agree, considering you saved my life, little monk."

Nangong Hu removed the black veil covering his face, revealing his stunning beauty.

Kong Wang hesitated for a moment, then finally told Nangong Hu the purpose of his visit.

Nangong Hu frowned upon hearing Kong Wang's words, then his expression turned cold, and he remained silent.

"You've certainly made a good plan, even scheming against the Holy Court and our Dragon-Slaying Sect," Nangong Hu sneered.

"Amitabha, protecting tens of millions of lives in Qingzhou, such actions are perfectly justifiable," Kong Wang stated frankly.

Nangong Hu stared at Kong Wang for a long time before finally shifting his gaze.

“I want to ask you a question. If you can answer it, I will grant your request,” she said.

“Please ask.”

“Is the mountain god you worship a real deity?” Nangong Hu stared intently into Kong Wang’s eyes.

“Of course it is!” Kong Wang replied firmly.

“Alright! Then I agree!”

Chapter 208 Beat the drums, advance!
.

After bidding farewell to Kong Wang, Nangong Hu returned to the demon army's territory.

The demon army was already fully prepared, and more powerful figures had arrived.

Besides the demon king Quan Xiaotian, the Holy Court had also sent a seventh-tier demon king.

With two seventh-tier demons and over a dozen sixth-tier demons, annihilating Qingzhou would be a piece of cake.

"Greetings, Lord Quan. Something has happened in Qingzhou. All the fifth-tier demon generals lurking there have been killed," a fifth-tier demon hurriedly reported.

"What? All the fifth-tier demon generals are dead!?"

Quan Xiaotian turned around, his narrow eyes flashing with a cold, terrifying light.

"Who did this? Could it be that the Great Chu secretly intervened?" he asked coldly.

The messenger shook his head, replying, "No, the message explicitly mentioned the Divine Mountain. It's said that the people of the Divine Mountain launched a joint operation to wipe out all the demons within Qingzhou. They even took the corpses of fifth-tier demons back to the Divine Mountain to offer as sacrifices to the so-called Mountain God."

"The Divine Mountain!? It's that Divine Mountain again!?" Quan Xiaotian suppressed his anger.

"Again and again, do they really think we're easy to bully?" His tone turned grim.

The Heavenly Water Pact had lasted for three years, and during those three years, the demons hadn't made much of a move.

Had the Great Chu human race enjoyed a few years of peace and tranquility, forgotten the terror of the Holy Court?

"It's time to let the human race experience the fear of being dominated by us again!" Quan Xiaotian said in a sinister voice.

Then, he found Nangong Hu.

"Gentlemen, are you all ready? The army will set off immediately and march on Qingzhou!" Quan Xiaotian said decisively.

"Now?" Nangong Hu was surprised.

"Good, now!" Quan Xiaotian said in a deep voice.

Nangong Hu was silent for a moment, then nodded: "Everything is prepared. Although the time is a bit tight, it's not a problem."

"Then let's set off now. You go to the western corner of Luohe Prefecture first. Once we advance into Qingzhou, you infiltrate secretly!" Quan Xiaotian said.

"Okay, but I'd like to ask, how many powerful figures has the Holy Court sent?" Nangong Hu nodded, then asked again.

Quan Xiaotian frowned slightly, somewhat reluctant to answer the question.

But considering that the other party possessed the Heavenly Azure Fox bloodline, he slowly replied: "Besides myself, there is also a Demon King. There are more than a dozen sixth-tier demons!"

"And seventh-tier experts?" Nangong Hu was somewhat surprised, not expecting the Holy Court to attach such importance to Qingzhou.

"Naturally, even the Holy Venerable is paying attention to the variable of Divine Mountain. If it weren't for the situation in the Wilderness that required an eighth-tier Venerable to suppress it, I wouldn't be leading the team this time," Quan Xiaotian said calmly.

Nangong Hu understood.

It seemed the appearance of the Divine Mountain had already attracted the attention of the Holy Court.

Whether it was the Half-Saints managing the Holy Court or the two Saint Venerables standing at the pinnacle, they were all watching the situation in Qingzhou.

As for the Wilderness that Quan Xiaotian mentioned, that was a very distant borderland.

Those regions, even at the height of the Great Chu Dynasty's power, had not been incorporated into its territory.

The Wilderness was inhabited by non-human races, whose customs and cultures were completely different from those of the Great Chu Dynasty. There were also powerful individuals in the Wilderness.

However, the Wilderness is unfavored by heaven and earth, resulting in a harsh environment, scarce spiritual energy, and a sparse population, making it virtually invisible.

Now, according to Quan Xiaotian, the Wilderness has yet to be conquered by demons and remains resilient.

This is somewhat unexpected.

After agreeing to Quan Xiaotian's orders, Nangong Hu led the Dragon-Slaying Sect members away from the demon territory, heading towards Luohe Prefecture.

Quan Xiaotian watched Nangong Hu leave when suddenly a voice rang out, carrying a sinister laugh.

"This woman is suspicious. Aren't you afraid of being secretly harmed?"

As the voice fell, an elderly figure appeared.

"Heh, you think I don't know?" Quan Xiaotian's eyes turned icy.

"Since you knew there was something wrong, why didn't you capture her and interrogate her?" the Demon Serpent questioned Quan Xiaotian in a deep voice.

"Is it that I don't want to? She possesses royal blood; even if I had ten times the courage, I wouldn't dare to easily harm her," Quan Xiaotian said coldly.

"Royal blood? Azure Fox!?"

The Demon Serpent frowned, utterly surprised.

As a demon, he naturally couldn't sense the Azure Fox bloodline within Nangong Hu; he only learned of it from Quan Xiaotian's words.

"How is that possible? Azure Fox blood is extremely rare; how could there be a bloodline that has been lost? Besides, she clearly has human aura; she can't possibly be an Azure Fox!"

The Demon Serpent said sharply; human aura cannot be concealed no matter what.

“Because she is both human and demon.”

“A half-demon!?” The demonic serpent understood, its eyes narrowing slightly. Suddenly, it laughed, “Haha, I never expected a demon royal to have an affair with a human and give birth to such a half-demon bloodline. Interesting, very interesting!”

“Demonic serpent, you'd better watch your mouth. No matter what, she possesses the bloodline of the Azure Fox; it's not something you or I can laugh at.” Quan Xiaotian turned and glanced at the demonic serpent.

The demonic serpent's laughter abruptly stopped.

The hierarchy among demons is strict, with the two royal families being the most prestigious. All other demons must treat these two royal families with utmost respect.

Even a seventh-tier demon must obediently bow to a first-tier royal.

“Hmph!” The demonic serpent snorted coldly, expressing its displeasure, but did not continue its mockery.

The Azure Fox bloodline is extremely rare, with no more than an even number of offspring.

Who knows if this half-demon woman will be valued?

"Since they have problems, how will you handle it?" the Demon Serpent asked, turning to him.

"Ignore them for now. Send them to the right corner of Luohe Prefecture. If they know what's good for them, they'll stay far away. If they don't, kill them all, except for the Holy Maiden with royal blood!" Quan Xiaotian said coldly.

The Demon Serpent nodded; this was a prudent approach.

As for the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect, given their status, it was best for them to stay out of it.

They could report back to the Holy Court truthfully upon their return.

Next, Quan Xiaotian went to the center of the battleground.

At this moment, all the demons here were fully prepared, rubbing their hands together, their eyes bloodshot.

They couldn't wait to rush into Qingzhou, wreak havoc, and devour the blood of humans.

"Beat the drums, advance!" Quan Xiaotian took a deep breath, waved his hand, and gave the order.

As soon as he finished speaking, more than a dozen human-skinned monsters flew into the air, took deep breaths, and in the blink of an eye, their bodies swelled up.

Then, he pounded his bulging chest with both hands, producing a thunderous booming sound!

"Kill! Kill! Kill!"

"Invade Qingzhou, feast on blood, and strengthen our power!"

A deafening roar echoed through the heavens, demonic energy soaring and filling the air with malevolent power.

Under such terrifying pressure, even the sky seemed to lower.

And with a wave of Quan Xiaotian's hand, nearly one hundred thousand demons marched mightily towards Qingzhou!
