Chapter 209 is about to break through.
.

The demons have begun to move, their army launching a direct invasion of Qingzhou!

At the border, dozens of miles away, a vast expanse of demonic energy could be seen moving. The three members of the Qilin tribe all paled.

"Damn it, the demons have begun to enter Qingzhou!" Qilin said in a low voice, his expression grim.

Without time to say more, Qilin turned and, with Black Dragon and Fire Phoenix, quickly returned to the Divine Mountain.

He needed to get the news back to Qianshan County as soon as possible so that they could prepare!

A quarter of an hour later, the three of them finally returned.

Without resting, they rushed straight to the Mountain Tower. Xiao Nian, who was handling affairs on the first floor, saw the three of them return and was about to greet them.

However, to his surprise, the three of them hurriedly went upstairs, leaving Xiao Nian looking bewildered.

Bai Liang was assigning tasks to the cultivators of Baijiang Prefecture when Qilin and his two companions suddenly entered.

"Lord Bai, something terrible has happened! Demons have entered Qingzhou!" Qilin said in a deep voice.

Upon hearing this, everyone present changed their expressions.

"What was bound to happen has finally happened." Bai Liang frowned, looking at the map of Qingzhou in his hand.

The demon army entered Qingzhou from the eastern corner of Luohe Prefecture, meaning Longyun County would be the first to fall.

Longyun County has a population of over ten million; if it falls, it will be a living hell.

"Luohe Prefecture is over four hundred li from the nearest county seat of Longyun County. At the speed of the demon army's march, it will take them less than half a day."

Bai Liang spoke slowly.

"Half a day? That's simply not enough time. What do we do now?" Wu Ze's brows furrowed deeply.

Their current strength is utterly insufficient to withstand the demon army.

But if they don't intervene, Longyun County will completely fall.

"Has Chuzhou Prefecture arrived in Qingzhou City yet?" Bai Liang suddenly asked.

Sanfu thought for a moment and replied, "At the speed of a Yangshen Realm cultivator, they should have arrived long ago."

"Now we can only hope that Chuzhou Prefecture can reach an agreement with those geniuses," Bai Liang said.

"How about Sanfu and I go to Longyun County to check on the situation?"

At this moment, Hu Zhengqiang suggested.

"No, there are only two Yangshen Realm cultivators left in Qianshan County. If you leave, it will be very troublesome if you encounter any problems," Bai Liang immediately rejected.

Qianshan County currently only has three Yangshen Realm cultivators. One has already gone to Qingzhou City, leaving only two.

"Qilin, you three go to Shishu County first. Chen Jianchi and Qian Ke are there. If the situation is urgent, bring them back immediately. Make sure nothing happens to them!" Bai Liang looked at the three of them.

Qilin nodded, then turned and left with the Black Dragon and the Fire Phoenix.

Before leaving, the three went to the temple to offer sacrifices, thanking the gods for their previous blessings and praying for the Mountain God's protection before departing for Shishu County.

Next, Bai Liang ordered Du Tiandao to lead a patrol team to maintain order in Qianshan County.

At this crucial juncture, it was best to avoid any chaos.

Then he looked at Bian Nong and the others, saying, "Next, I will trouble you all to disperse around Liaoyun County and observe the movements of the demons."

"Don't worry, leave that to us. For the sake of the Mountain God, we will certainly do our best!"

Bian Nong and the others replied in unison.

Bai Liang then called Li Xue over. Seeing this, Wu Ze wanted to say something, but ultimately remained silent.

Li Xue noticed Wu Ze's concern and smiled, saying, "Uncle Wu, it's alright. The Mountain God bestowed the Divine Scroll, isn't it meant to be used?"

"You... handle it yourself, just don't act rashly," Wu Ze said in a deep voice.

Li Xue is currently at the Enlightenment Realm, with a lifespan of 180 years.

After deducting what she previously used, she has about 150 years left.

She can fight opponents three ranks higher, and can kill those below the seventh rank, including the seventh rank, by consuming her lifespan.

Bai Liang had already prepared for facing a seventh-rank demon by bringing Li Xue out.

Now they are just waiting for news from Chu Wuliu. If they can reach an agreement with the geniuses of the capital, their pressure will be greatly reduced.

If not, then Qianshan County will have to face the demon army alone.

Whether the people of Qingzhou could ultimately be saved was now up to fate.

A tense and anxious atmosphere spread throughout Qianshan County.

While the residents of the county town remained somewhat optimistic, those who had come from all over to seek refuge were shrouded in fear and unease.

These emotions permeated the population, spreading rapidly.

Many were on the verge of collapse, shouting cries of "Demons are coming! Everyone is going to die!"—these were the ravings of a madman.

However, their madness was short-lived; they were quickly apprehended by the patrol team.

Inside the Hundred Refinement Hall.

The students huddled together, discussing the crisis facing Qingzhou.

"The demon army has already invaded Qingzhou! Qingzhou is in grave danger!"

"We're safe in Qianshan County, protected by the sacred mountain. But the people of Qingzhou have no protection; they're like fish on a chopping board, at the mercy of the demons."

"That's true, but what can we do? Qianshan County's strength isn't enough to face the demon army."

"We're already cultivators, even if we're only at the Refining Technique Realm, we can still contribute!"

"Come on, just don't cause trouble for everyone, let alone contribute!"

By the end, everyone's faces were somewhat dejected.

"If only the Mountain God could manifest again!" one student said hopefully.

The others also had a sudden thought.

Yes, if the Mountain God could manifest, then that damned demon army would be nothing but ants, easily crushed!

At this moment, Li Yue didn't pay much attention to Qianshan County; his attention was entirely focused on the control panel.

After the sacrificial ceremony, his merit points skyrocketed, now approaching 10 million.

"Just 100,000 more, and I should be able to break through to the fifth-tier Sacred Mountain in about an hour."

Li Yue felt a surge of excitement.

Accumulating these 10 million merit points was truly difficult; if it weren't for the sacrificial ceremony, it would probably have taken several more months to achieve that breakthrough.

"I wonder what kind of new divine ability I'll unlock?"

Thinking these thoughts, and seeing his merit points steadily increasing, Li Yue shifted his focus to Qianshan County.

Although he hadn't paid particular attention to the situation in Qianshan County, he was generally aware of what was happening there.

He had naturally heard the Qilin's report.

The demon army had officially entered Qingzhou and was now heading towards Longyun County.

However, it's still unknown what level of power the demons have sent.

Li Yue estimates that their strength shouldn't be lower than the seventh rank.

After all, the sixth-rank Blade Demon died in Qingzhou; sending another sixth-rank would just be suicide.

"A bit unexpected. They're not heading straight for the Divine Mountain?"

Li Yue thought to himself.

He had initially thought that the demon army's first act upon entering Qingzhou would be to attack the Divine Mountain to avenge the sixth-rank Blade Demon.

But instead, they've invaded Longyun County in the opposite direction.

"What a pity," Li Yue said with some regret.


.
Chapter 210 Why betray the imperial court?
.

Qianshan County is over a thousand miles from Qingzhou City.

It took Chu Wuliu several hours to hurriedly arrive.

He had been to Qingzhou City before. Back then, the city was incredibly prosperous, and the soldiers guarding it were full of vigor.

But now, the guards were all ordinary people, even less prosperous than an average county town.

"The people have left, and the tea has grown cold; it's no longer as prosperous as before," Chu Wuliu sighed.

He then entered the city. He didn't conceal his aura, and as soon as he entered, a cultivator noticed him.

"Fellow Daoist, stop! Are you from the capital?" A Yin Shen Realm prodigy stopped Chu Wuliu, scrutinizing him from head to toe with suspicion in his eyes.

Chu Wuliu looked up, cupped his hands in greeting, and said, "Chu Wuliu, Prefect of Baijiang Prefecture!"

"Prefecture!?" The prodigy who asked the question was somewhat stunned, not expecting a prefecture official to come to Qingzhou City.

"Wait a minute, you say you're from Baijiang Prefecture, then why are you in Qingzhou? Are you lying?" the man questioned.

At this moment, the elites from the capital city who were nearby also noticed the commotion and gathered around.

"As the prefect of Baijiang Prefecture, I don't need to explain myself to you commoners," Chu Wuliu said with considerable authority, his hands behind his back, his aura of superiority naturally radiating out.

Furthermore, his Yang Shen Realm aura also spread out, immediately stunning everyone present.

The crowd exchanged bewildered glances, unsure of what to do.

"Your surname is Chu?" a voice came from afar, followed by Shangguan Shenghua arriving on foot, looking at Chu Wuliu with surprise and uncertainty.

Chu Wuliu recognized the newcomer and nodded, "Indeed, my surname is Chu."

"So it really is Prefect Chu, it's been a long time," Shangguan Shenghua said with a smile.

"And you are?" Chu Wuliu didn't recognize him.

“My father, Shangguan Liumo, has the Chu Prefecture forgotten?” Shangguan Shenghua said slowly.

“Prime Minister Shangguan? I see…” Chu Wuliu remembered the person before him and smiled as well.

Before taking up his post in the prefecture, he was practically Shangguan Liumo’s student, and had even visited the Prime Minister’s residence, seeing a young Shangguan Shenghua.

However, Shangguan Shenghua’s appearance back then was rather youthful, quite different from now, which is why he hadn’t recognized him.

“Haha, Senior Brother Chu finally remembers!” Shangguan Shenghua immediately stepped forward, his tone becoming warm.

Chu Wuliu had studied at Tianmo Academy, and he was now a student there, making him Chu Wuliu’s junior brother; addressing him as senior brother was perfectly reasonable.

The two immediately exchanged pleasantries, with Chu Wuliu inquiring about Prime Minister Shangguan’s recent situation.

Shangguan Shenghua shook his head dismissively, saying the old man was living too comfortably; he'd taken a concubine last month.

"The Prime Minister is still going strong!" Chu Wuliu couldn't help but remark.

"Haha, it's just for show. He's so old, who knows if he can still manage?" Shangguan Shenghua said in a low voice.

Chu Wuliu was at a loss for words.

He thought to himself, "This filial son... I wonder if Prime Minister Shangguan will be so angry he'll sever their father-son relationship if he hears this?"

After a brief chat, Shangguan Shenghua turned to Chu Wuliu, asking in confusion, "Senior Brother Chu, you serve in Baijiang Prefecture, so what are you doing in Qingzhou? Don't you know that Qingzhou is no longer under the jurisdiction of the imperial court?"

"And... what's going on with your cultivation level? You've already reached the Yang God Realm! You broke through your cultivation level without reporting it to the capital, breaking through outside the capital without authorization—that's a serious crime!"

Shangguan Shenghua spoke, his brows furrowed deeply.

At this moment, the other prodigies who had noticed the commotion had arrived one after another.

"What's going on?" Wu Yuan asked Shangguan Shenghua.

Shangguan Shenghua then introduced Chu Wuliu to everyone: the governor of Baijiang Prefecture, the ruler of the prefecture, an official appointed by the imperial court!

"The governor of Baijiang Prefecture?"

The newly arrived people were quite surprised upon hearing this, especially after sensing the aura emanating from Chu Wuliu, their brows furrowing deeply.

He's actually at the Yang God Realm!?

Could it be that the people from Chu Prefecture, like them, have come to Qing Prefecture for training?

But that's impossible. Those who have come to Qing Prefecture for training are all geniuses like them, no older than thirty-five.

Chu Wuliu's age is probably approaching three digits.

Therefore, he couldn't possibly be here for training like them.

"I have important matters to discuss with you, but not on behalf of the court, but on behalf of Divine Mountain," Chu Wuliu thought for a moment, then finally stated his purpose directly.

"Important matters? Divine Mountain?"

Catching these two keywords, the expressions of the geniuses who heard this became strange.

"Ahem!" Shangguan Shenghua quietly took a step back, then said, "Senior Brother Chu, did you misspoke? What do you mean by 'on behalf of Divine Mountain'? We didn't understand."

"I didn't misspoke. I've come here on behalf of Divine Mountain to discuss important matters with you!" Chu Wuliu took a deep breath.

He understood in his heart that these words had created a rift between him and the court.

If the news reached the capital in a few days, the Chu Emperor would be furious, and his position as governor would be over.

But what did that matter? He had found his allegiance; by joining the Divine Mountain, his future was bright.

He even began to look forward to it.

“Governor Chu, are you serious? His Highness the Third Prince is also in the city. Do you know the consequences of your words?”

Shangguan Shenghua's expression turned serious. He had completely distanced himself from Chu Wuliu and sided with the other prodigies.

"The Third Prince is here too?" Chu Wuliu was slightly surprised, then solemnly said, "I am serious. Since His Highness the Third Prince is in the city, I will speak directly with him."

"Hmph! A traitor to the court dares not meet His Highness the Third Prince?"

A cold snort rang out, and Cha Xixing stepped forward, her gaze towards Chu Wuliu filled with disgust.

"The court provides for you, yet you turn out to be an ungrateful wretch. Representing the Divine Mountain to discuss important matters? What is the Divine Mountain? Another anti-religion like the Dragon-Slaying Sect? Chu Prefecture, you have quite the audacity!"

Cha Xixing spoke coldly.

"I have never betrayed the common people," Chu Wuliu said frankly.

He wasn't wrong; he had done his utmost to avert the demonic calamity in Baijiang Prefecture, but against a sixth-tier demon, he was simply powerless.

However, he had ultimately saved half the population of Baijiang Prefecture, which was already quite remarkable.

A prefecture that was afraid of death would have abandoned the city and fled, leaving its people behind.

"Heh, keep trying to argue! His Highness the Third Prince is here. Let's see how you try to justify yourself now!" Cha Xixing sneered.

Shangguan Shenghua looked at Chu Wuliu and sighed helplessly.


.
Chapter 211 The Third Prince Chu Yuan!
.

Facing the accusations from the assembled prodigies, Chu Wuliu remained utterly composed.

A royal carriage slowly approached and stopped before him. A moment later, a man with a striking appearance, wearing a bright yellow python robe, and possessing sharp eyebrows and bright eyes, alighted from it.

This was the Third Prince, Chu Yuan!

Chu Yuan exuded an imposing aura, his royal presence overwhelming everyone present. Furthermore, he was a late-stage sixth-tier Yang God Realm cultivator, practicing royal techniques, making him incredibly powerful.

He had overheard the conversation between Chu Wuliu and the others.

However, he remained outwardly calm, his expression perfectly composed.

"Your subject greets Your Highness, the Third Prince."

Chu Wuliu performed the proper courteous greeting to the Third Prince.

"Hmm, no need for formalities," the Third Prince replied calmly, then said, "I think I just heard you say you wanted to represent the Divine Mountain to discuss important matters with us?"

Chu Wuliu looked up at the Third Prince and said directly, "Yes, Your Highness, the situation is urgent; it concerns demons and monsters, and the safety of the people of Qingzhou!"

"These things are not urgent. First, explain to me and my fellow Daoists what this Divine Mountain you represent is."

The Third Prince looked at Chu Wuliu with a condescending gaze. Although his tone was calm, it revealed extremely strong dissatisfaction!

Chu Wuliu frowned, not expecting to encounter such trouble. He said in a deep voice, "Your Highness, this matter is urgent..."

"Explain!" The Third Prince's tone suddenly intensified, his expression darkening, and he said coldly, "I want you to explain what it means to represent the Divine Mountain!?"

Chu Wuliu glanced at the Third Prince, then nodded.

"Very well, then I will introduce the Sacred Mountain to Your Highness. Located in Liaoyun County, Qingzhou, it is a sacred mountain that has existed since ancient times. A deity dwells within, protecting all living beings and warding off demons. Anyone who lives at the foot of the mountain will receive the protection of the mountain god..."

Chu Wuliu slowly began, briefly introducing the Sacred Mountain to everyone.

However, after he finished speaking, many people began to scoff.

"What? Did I hear that right? A Sacred Mountain? And a deity?"

"No way, we're all cultivators, and someone still believes in deities?"

"As a prefectural governor, and a Yangshen realm cultivator, he shouldn't be so foolish!"

Shangguan Shenghua rubbed his forehead, feeling that Chu Wuliu had truly acted stupidly, actually believing such rumors and even betraying the court because of it!

He had heard a little about the Sacred Mountain, only from his father, who said it was a rather powerful force.

The court's abandonment of Qingzhou seemed to be because of this Sacred Mountain?

But no matter how strong Mount Shen is, can it be stronger than the imperial court? Chu Wuliu is truly incredibly foolish!

Cha Xixing sneered, thinking to herself that it was indeed a rebellious sect like the Dragon-Slaying Sect, only good at this kind of manipulative nonsense.

Most of the other prodigies never paid attention to events outside the capital, so they were completely unaware of news about Mount Shen.

They might have heard of it, but they wouldn't take it to heart.

The prodigies from Jinlong Temple and Xuanqing Temple, however, seemed to have some thoughts.

Especially Jinlong Temple; the four of them exchanged glances, their eyes flashing with golden light.

Before coming, their Second Master had specifically instructed them to pay close attention to this Mount Shen.

"If the opportunity arises, I will infiltrate it, retrieve the lost secret manuals of Jinlong Temple, and bring them back to the capital!"

The Third Prince's face was cold and grim.

He knew about the Divine Mountain; as a prince, he was extremely attentive to court affairs.

But what enraged him most was that a court official, even a prefectural-level official, would betray the court for the sake of the Divine Mountain.

Moreover, judging from Chu Wuliu's composed demeanor, he seemed to feel no remorse for betraying the court!

"Chu Wuliu! You betrayed the Great Chu Dynasty, aren't you afraid of being executed along with your entire clan?" the Third Prince said coldly.

Chu Wuliu frowned slightly. His family was in Baijiang Prefecture, but some of his relatives and clansmen were in the capital. If his entire clan was executed, it would inevitably implicate his relatives and clansmen in the capital.

However, he still cupped his hands and said, "Your Majesty is wise and will understand my good intentions. It's all for the sake of the people!"

The Third Prince stared into Chu Wuliu's eyes, but Chu Wuliu met his gaze calmly.

"Very well!" Finally, the Third Prince exhaled, his expression returning to calm, and said, "Since your mind is made up, there's no point in me saying anything more. I will report this matter to my father, and let him determine your guilt!"

"Thank you, Your Highness." Chu Wuliu bowed slightly, then said, "Now, shall we discuss this important matter?"

The Third Prince's face was cold, and he said indifferently, "Speak!"

Chu Wuliu carefully considered his words, then explained the purpose of his visit.

He represented Shenshan, that is, Qianshan County, to seek the help of everyone to unite against the demon army and protect the people of Qingzhou.

The demon army had already entered Qingzhou and was about to wreak havoc on Longyun County; the situation was urgent!

If we had been any later, the entire Longyun County would have become a living hell!

After speaking, Chu Wuliu's expression turned solemn, and he said in a deep voice, "Your Highness, tens of millions of people in Qingzhou are now only saved by us."

The Third Prince and the others frowned upon hearing this.

Someone exclaimed in shock, "A demon army of nearly 100,000, including experts above the sixth rank! How can we possibly resist them?"

Why didn't anyone mention before we came that the demons would mobilize such a large army? If a few more eighth-rank demons were to arrive, it would be comparable to the Battle of Tianshui three years ago!

"Man proposes, God disposes!" Chu Wuliu said in a deep voice.

"Your Highness, we don't need to agree. His Majesty sent us to Qingzhou for training, not to fight. To fight against overwhelming odds is an unwise choice!" The Zhao brothers, Bufan and Fuhu, hurriedly spoke up.

They didn't want to face the demon army; the thought of it was terrifying.

As for their earlier boastful words, they had long since forgotten them.

The other prodigies shared the same sentiment as the Zhao brothers, all urging the Third Prince not to agree.

Only a few frowned, their thoughts unreadable.

The Third Prince's expression, however, was constantly shifting, a glint in his eyes.

He suddenly realized something. He hadn't understood why his father had sent him to Qingzhou.

His performance was clearly superior to the other princes; why was he being sent to Qingzhou?

But now, he suddenly understood.

His father sent him to Qingzhou with a profound purpose!

"The demon army is eyeing us covetously, Qingzhou is in grave danger, and tens of millions of people are on the verge of death. If I can stand up now, lead the people to resist the demons, and protect the people of Qingzhou, this would be an unparalleled achievement!"

Thinking to himself, the Third Prince Chu Yuan's eyes shone brightly.

So, he was the prince most favored by his father!


.
Chapter 212 United Against Demons
.

"Very well! This prince agrees!" the Third Prince declared.

These words immediately stunned the others.

"Your Highness... how can you agree to this?" someone exclaimed, trying to persuade the Third Prince to change his mind.

"Are you trying to teach this prince how to do things?"

The Third Prince's icy gaze fell upon the man, causing him to tremble and dare not utter another word.

It was Cha Xixing who spoke in a deep voice: "Your Highness, please consider this carefully! Whether this sacred mountain is friend or foe is still unknown. If news of our cooperation reaches the capital, it may cause a stir in the court."

"I am aware of this."

The Third Prince spoke calmly.

His brothers, the emperor and his nephews, were closely watching his movements in Qingzhou.

If he did anything outrageous, news would quickly reach the capital, then spread through his brothers' hands throughout the court and even to their father, the emperor, in an attempt to smear his reputation.

But what did it matter?

Great men don't concern themselves with trifles!

If he could truly exorcise the demons and protect the people of Qingzhou—not necessarily all of them, but even half—that would be a great achievement!

And a real military exploit! In a time when humanity had suffered repeated defeats, military exploits were extremely valuable.

This one achievement alone would be enough for him to secure his position as crown prince and become the heir to the Great Chu!

The Third Prince grew increasingly excited, wishing he could immediately head to Longyun County and fight the demon army three hundred rounds!

However, he was intelligent enough to know the demons' power; directly confronting them would be tantamount to suicide.

Therefore, he still needed to discuss how to deal with the situation with Chu Wuliu and the others.

Hearing that the Third Prince agreed to join forces against the demons, Chu Wuliu breathed a sigh of relief.

In that case, the pressure on Qianshan County would be greatly reduced.

He looked up, his eyelids twitching slightly.

He hadn't paid much attention before, but now, upon closer inspection, he realized that among the geniuses from the capital, there were so many Yang Shen Realm cultivators.

Counting them carefully, including the Third Prince himself, there were a total of ten Yang Shen Realm cultivators.

Moreover, they were at the genius level, their combat power even stronger than ordinary Yang Shen Realm cultivators.

With so many sixth-tier cultivators, they would be much more confident in fighting the demon army.

Then, Chu Wuliu took out a Mountain Token, a newly crafted communication artifact from Qianshan County.

Forged in the Divine Weapon Forge, more than a dozen were made. Communication was possible over long distances, but it was limited to the Qingzhou region; beyond that, it would become ineffective.

According to the classification of magical artifacts, the Mountain Token falls between the third and fourth tiers.

To communicate with more distant places, the Mountain Token's artifact level needs to be upgraded.

Chu Wuliu focused his divine sense, activated the Mountain Token, and transmitted the message from Qingzhou City back.

"Wait, this prince has something else to say." Seeing Chu Wuliu transmitting the message, the Third Prince interrupted.

"Uh... what else does Your Highness have to say?" Chu Wuliu asked, puzzled.

The Third Prince said calmly, "In this cooperation, I will take the lead, and I will command and lead the team!"

"Hmm?" Chu Wuliu was slightly taken aback, but having spent so much time in officialdom and risen to the position of prefect, he quickly understood the implications.

Knowing the Third Prince wanted to seize the opportunity to gain merit, Chu Wuliu went along with it, smiling, "Since Your Highness has spoken, then I will agree on behalf of Qianshan County."

As long as they were jointly resisting the demon army, it didn't matter who was in charge.

Anyway, the credit wouldn't go to Qianshan County, so it was better to give it to the Third Prince and use it as a way to curry favor.

Upon hearing this, the Third Prince indeed smiled, and his gaze towards Chu Wuliu became much more amiable.

Divine Mountain, Qianshan County.

Bai Liang held the mountain token in his hand, having received the message from Chu Wuliu, and also received a message from Kong Wang.

"What's the situation?" Wu Ze asked in a deep voice.

Bai Liang first checked the message from Kong Wang, frowning slightly before relaxing.

“Kongwang has already made contact with the Dragon-Slaying Sect. They've also received news of a demon army of 100,000, including dozens of sixth-tier demons, and more importantly, two seventh-tier demons leading the charge!” Bai Liang said in a deep voice, his expression grave.

Wu Ze took a deep breath.

“Seventh-tier demons, this is troublesome! With our current strength, we simply can't contend with them!” he said.

Sanfu, Hu Zhengqiang, and the others were also nearby. Upon hearing about the two seventh-tier demons, their brows furrowed deeply.

“The difference between sixth and seventh tier is like an insurmountable chasm! Even if we have many methods, we are like ants before a seventh-tier Unity Realm cultivator,” Hu Zhengqiang sighed.

“Let's check the news from Chu Prefecture first,” Sanfu said.

Bai Liang nodded and then checked the message Chu Wuliu had sent back.

"Good news! Chuzhou Prefecture has dealt with the geniuses from the capital!" After reading the report, Bai Liang finally showed a hint of joy.

"The capital really went all out. This time, ten Yang God Realm experts and dozens of Yin God Realm experts have come to Qingzhou, all of them geniuses from the capital. Even the Third Prince has come!"

Bai Liang said, feeling somewhat surprised.

"Even the Third Prince has come?" Hu Zhengqiang asked, somewhat astonished.

As a general, he had heard about the affairs of the court.

The Third Prince was a strong contender for the throne, yet he had come to Qingzhou?

Isn't His Majesty worried about something happening to the Third Prince?

"Ten Yang God Realm geniuses—this force can greatly alleviate our pressure," Bai Liang said.

"As for those two seventh-tier demons, if it really doesn't work..."

He paused, glancing towards the next room.

Wu Ze understood Bai Liang's meaning and said, "Just act according to the situation."

Bai Liang nodded, indicating he understood.

"The demon army has arrived in Longyun County. The situation is urgent; we must set off!" he said in a deep voice.

Sanfu and the others nodded, already prepared for the battle against the demons.

"Brother Wu Ze, go and bring Xiaoxue here," Bai Liang said, looking at Wu Ze.

Wu Ze nodded emphatically and then walked towards the next room.

Bai Liang picked up the Mountain Token and sent a message to Chu Wuliu, instructing him to cooperate with the capital's prodigies and coordinate with Qianshan County.

He then sent a message to Kong Wang, who was still abroad, telling him how to proceed with the next steps.

"Kong Wang, if it becomes too dangerous, remember to prioritize your own safety and never take any risks!"

Bai Liang gave this final reminder.

Having completed all this, Wu Ze had brought Li Xue over.

Li Xue, dressed in a long, dark dress, wore a calm expression.

She was Qianshan County's trump card, to be used only as a last resort.

And now, it was a critical moment!

"Xiao Xue, are you ready?" Bai Liang asked, looking at Li Xue.

Li Xue nodded and smiled, "After receiving my ordination, I was ready and waiting!"


.
Chapter 213 Supernatural Powers Bless the Earth
.

Bai Liang remained silent, while Wu Ze looked at Li Xue with a complex expression.

He thought to himself, if he could exchange his divine talisman with Li Xue's, things would be much better.

But it was just a thought; if he possessed the divine destiny talisman, the result would be the same.

Bai Liang and the others wouldn't let him expend his lifespan in battle.

With everything prepared, the affairs of Qianshan County were entrusted to He Liyuan for temporary management, while the three Qilin remained to deal with any emergencies.

Xiao Nian was responsible for handling the remaining matters, maintaining order, and ensuring that Qianshan County wouldn't descend into chaos during this period.

This time, Bai Liang brought Du Tiandao, Er Qing, and other members of the patrol team, as well as students from the Hundred Refinements Hall.

There were several hundred people in total, the lowest level among them being at the Tongfa Realm.

Du Tiandao was only one step away from the Entry-Profound Realm, his cultivation progressing remarkably quickly, partly due to the assistance of Elder Zhou's pills.

"Among the demon army, there are low-level demons. Your mission is to snipe these weaklings!" Bai Liang looked at the crowd and said in a deep voice.

"Lord Bai, please rest assured, we will definitely not disgrace Qianshan County!" Du Tiandao and the others shouted in unison!

Among them, some looked tense, while others were excited and full of anticipation.

These people were cultivated by Qianshan County over the past year. Divine water, pills, spirit rice, and various other cultivation resources were continuously provided, which created this group of cultivators.

But how can cultivators only cultivate and not fight?

So this time, it was precisely the time for these people to see blood.

They would be left to deal with low-level demons on the periphery. Although there would be danger, it would also be a form of training for them!

"Then let's set off!" Bai Liang ordered.

The group set off in a grand procession, led by Wu Ze, heading towards Longyun County.

However, they were not going to find the demons first, but rather to meet up with Chu Wuliu's group.

Witnessing this scene, Li Yue was deeply moved.

Nearly two years ago, Wu Ze and his men were utterly powerless against the demons. Now, they were actively fighting them for the sake of the people of Qingzhou.

His approach remained the same: he wouldn't interfere, letting them face the situation themselves.

He would simply cover for them in the end.

How could they grow without bloodshed?

Li Yue didn't want his subordinates to be hothouse flowers, only able to stay at the foot of the sacred mountain, seeking his protection.

If that happened, and he were to become engrossed in comprehending the laws of heaven and earth, or fall into slumber for other reasons, unable to respond,

wouldn't they be slaughtered by the enemy?

Therefore, Li Yue hoped to cultivate his subordinates into capable leaders.

This way, even in his slumber, he could ensure the stable development of Qianshan County.

Previously, while comprehending the laws of heaven and earth, Li Yue sensed immense malice through the hole in the sky.

That malice was extremely unsettling; even he, sensing it, frowned.

This meant there were unknown beings beyond the heavens!

Moreover, these beings possessed terrifying power; after all, they were things that could make Li Yue feel their malice even across a vast expanse of space.

Because of the obstruction of the laws of heaven and earth, those things couldn't enter for the time being, but what about later?

Li Yue wasn't foolish enough to believe that those extraterrestrial beings were just idly creating a hole in the sky.

There must be a reason.

However, he wasn't yet aware of the deeper reasons, as he hadn't fully comprehended the laws of heaven and earth.

"After this advancement, perhaps I can try extending my divine sense through that hole to explore beyond the heavens?"

Li Yue thought to himself.

Then he looked at the panel, which showed 9.99 million merit points—just a little short.

Just as he was about to check how much more he needed, the panel suddenly changed.

【Merit points reached 10,000,000; realm advanced; promoted to fifth-tier Sacred Mountain!】

【Unlocked divine power; blessings bestowed upon the land.】

In an instant, his divine sense expanded hundreds of miles, stretching across the entire Liaoyun Prefecture!

Li Yue's spirits lifted, his consciousness appearing high in the sky, gazing at the land.

The entire Liaoyun Prefecture appeared in his mind, every river, mountain, tree, canyon, even the creatures floating in the pools were clearly visible.

A radius of three hundred miles, all within his sight!

At this moment, Li Yue saw the other counties of Liaoyun Prefecture.

Shishu County, where Chen Jianchi and the others were, was a small county, similar to Taohua County.

There were also Che County and several other counties, all small towns with populations of around one hundred thousand, now mostly inhabited by people from Qianshan County.

Looking up again, he saw Bai Liang and his group heading towards Longyun Prefecture.

The group of several hundred people, a grand procession, attracted considerable attention along the way.

Wu Ze and his companions, who were leading the group, suddenly sensed something and looked up at the sky in confusion.

"Why does it feel like the Mountain God is watching us?" Li Xue asked, frowning as she looked up at the sky.

"There are gods three feet above your head. The Mountain God is beyond the heavens; if he looks down, he's watching us. That's perfectly normal," Bai Liang explained softly.

Li Xue nodded, seemingly understanding.

The group then bowed respectfully to the sky before continuing their journey towards Longyun County.

Li Yue watched them travel, then shifted his gaze.

From the sky, the land of Liaoyun County looked like rows and rows of 'ants'.

These were people—commoners from all over Qingzhou who had come to the sacred mountain to seek refuge. Their numbers surprised even Li Yue.

The largest group came from Longyun County.

This wasn't because the demon army invaded Longyun County today, but because these people had fled Longyun County several days earlier.

Longyun County was located near the border, and previously, with imperial troops and powerful figures guarding it, everyone felt safe.

Now that the imperial court had withdrawn, and demons were eyeing it covetously, if they were to invade Qingzhou, the first to die would be the people of Longyun County.

Under these circumstances, who wouldn't flee?

"If so many people became my followers, how much merit would that accumulate?"

Li Yue sighed.

If he could protect the entire Qingzhou this time, his followers would likely increase dramatically!

This had to be done slowly, step by step, without making any rash decisions.

After expanding his divine sense to three hundred li and experiencing it firsthand, Li Yue withdrew his divine sense.

Then he sensed the changes in the divine mountain, which was his true body.

Inside the mountain, white crystals already occupied more than half of the surface, their inner spiritual energy extremely concentrated. It had begun to liquefy, condensing into a substantial pool of spiritual energy.

Li Yue's heart stirred. He guided the substantial spiritual energy within the pool, causing it to flow outwards and merge with the divine water.

The divine water and the spiritual liquid combined, undergoing a wondrous transformation, becoming even more divine.

The divine water, which originally only purified spiritual power and dispelled demonic obstacles, now possessed a richer spiritual energy.

Having done all this, Li Yue then turned his attention to the divine power he had unlocked after advancing in rank.

Blessing the Earth!


.
Chapter 214 To Take Its Place?
.

The new divine power after breaking through to the fifth-tier Sacred Mountain is called "Blessed Earth."

From the name of this divine power, Li Yue roughly understood its function.

【Blessed Earth: The earth's nature is receptive; a virtuous person, with broad virtue, bears all things. The earth, with its great virtue and generosity, carries all things and accommodates everything. After using this divine power, divine power envelops the earth, blessing all things, creating auspicious omens, and establishing a paradise on earth!】

【Note: The power of this divine power varies with the level of cultivation; currently, its coverage area is one state.】

After reading the description of Blessed Earth, Li Yue seemed to understand something.

This is still a support-type divine power, and its effects seem quite extensive.

Li Yue looked at it carefully and was quite surprised.

This Blessed Earth can do quite a lot.

It can instantly revitalize the earth, rapidly growing lush vegetation on barren land, resulting in abundant harvests and solving food shortages.

It can eliminate natural disasters on the land, such as earthquakes, floods, and droughts, ensuring favorable weather and allowing people to live and work in peace.

It can also improve soil fertility and resource output, enabling mines to produce more of their desired minerals, ensuring a continuous energy supply.

There are various other similar functions.

"This divine power is a wonderful thing!" Li Yue thought to himself.

If the Blessed Land is used on Qingzhou, then Qingzhou will become a blessed land, becoming incredibly prosperous in just a few years.

Of course, this is on the premise that there is no foreign invasion or disturbance.

"Excellent. It just so happens that the imperial court has now abandoned Qingzhou. Once Qianshan County takes over Qingzhou, I'll use the Blessed Land to elevate Qingzhou to a higher level."

Li Yue thought to himself.

With the blessing of the Blessed Land, prosperity can be restored in just a few years.

Perhaps Qianshan County can even compete for a higher position then?

What was that saying again?

"Are kings and nobles born with a special destiny?"

Of course, that's a long way off. Whether it's even possible is another matter entirely; thinking about that now is too early.

Li Yue looked at the panel again. As expected, the merit points for breaking through to the next realm had increased to one hundred million.

[Realm: Fifth-Order Sacred Mountain]

[Merit: 13010/100 million]

[Divine Abilities: Mountain God's Blessing, Manifestation of Heaven and Earth, Resumption of Incense Offerings, Gratitude for Water, Blessing the Earth]

[Spells: Manifestation of Avatar, Control of the Five Elements]

[Magic Weapons: Mountain-Suppressing Seal, Heart Mirror, Divine Weapon Furnace, Wind-Stabilizing Pearl]

The merit points had directly increased to one hundred million, or one hundred million.

Although it seems like a lot, as long as he could turn all the people of Qingzhou into his followers, he could quickly accumulate one hundred million merit points.

"Now my strength should be comparable to a saint, right?"

Li Yue sensed his own strength, feeling only the immense divine power contained within the sacred mountain—it was terrifying!

If it were fully unleashed, its power would shatter the heavens.

At the same time, his divine soul sensed a barrier between heaven and earth, hidden between yin and yang, utterly concealed.

Li Yue's heart stirred, and his divine soul immediately passed through the barrier, instantly arriving at a vast, empty void.

"This is... the Great Void?"

The moment he entered, Li Yue understood what this place was.

The Great Void, independent of heaven and earth, yet not detached from them, is a unique plane.

Only cultivators above the demigod level, those who have comprehended the Great Dao, are allowed to enter this place, and even then, only their true spirits can enter; the physical body cannot.

"However, it seems I can bring my body in."

Li Yue sensed the divine mountain outside the Void, completely unaffected and unimpeded.

He could even manipulate the entire divine mountain to hide within the Void, disappearing directly from the outside world.

"Interesting. Could it be because the divine mountain isn't inanimate?" Li Yue wondered.

Then he looked towards the Void. It was empty, illusory, and profound, like the vastness of space within the deepest void, without any sense of up, down, left, or right.

The divine soul could roam freely here, but Li Yue dared not stray too far, lest he get lost in the Void, which would be dangerous.

"Those are... coordinates?"

Far off, Li Yue spotted points of light in the void, in a very, very distant direction, estimated to be tens of thousands of miles away.

"The capital?" he wondered, sensing that direction corresponded to the capital.

Turning his head, he looked towards the outer reaches of the city, where there were also points of light, but more than one.

Scattered and scattered, he estimated at least three, with some coordinates even more distant, at least a hundred thousand miles away!

"So many coordinates beyond the borders?"

Li Yue was somewhat surprised. It seemed the waters beyond the borders were quite deep.

Coordinates meant traces of beings at the demigod level or higher. It seemed there were quite a few demigods existing in the mortal realm!

In the direction beyond the borders, two particularly dazzling coordinates shone brilliantly.

After a moment's thought, Li Yue realized those were two Saint-level beings from the demon realm, currently the only two Saints in the world!

"I wonder how bright my coordinates are?"

Li Yue walked a short distance, then looked back at his coordinates, only to find nothing.

There were no coordinates at all. This meant he had entered the Void without leaving a trace, and the Void was unaware of his arrival.

"Could it be because my soul is different?"

Li Yue wondered to himself.

It seemed he was quite different from cultivators, not only physically but also in his soul.

"That's good. Without leaving coordinates, no one else will notice."

Li Yue wandered around the Void for a while longer, then discovered that it wasn't entirely empty; there were other things there.

For example, scattered throughout the Void were Great Laws of Law, hidden within it, shimmering and functioning.

From Li Yue's perspective, this resembled the underlying code of a world, operating within the Void and maintaining its balance.

After observing several more Great Laws of Law, Li Yue's eyes were truly opened today.

Many of the confusions he had encountered while comprehending the laws of heaven and earth in the mortal world were resolved this time.

"Wonderful! Truly wondrous!"

Having grasped a portion of the essence of this world, Li Yue was filled with awe, only able to marvel at its grandeur as the Great Dao.

Divine Mountain, Thousand Mountain County.

The moment Li Yue broke through to the fifth-tier Sacred Mountain, the cultivators remaining in Thousand Mountain County all suddenly sensed a change.

The most obvious and direct change was the concentration of spiritual energy, which directly increased by a level, becoming incredibly dense.

"What's going on? What happened?"

The Black Dragon stood up, his brow furrowed deeply, his expression extremely wary.

The Qilin, on the other hand, suddenly sensed something. Gazing at the divine mountain, it seemed to have become increasingly mysterious and unpredictable, even its peaks appearing hazy when looked at directly.

It was as if the divine mountain didn't exist in this world, as if it were about to detach itself and ascend to the celestial realm at any moment?

"It shouldn't be anything bad," the Qilin said.


.
Chapter 215 Southern Frontier Tribes
.

These words temporarily reassured Black Dragon and Fire Phoenix, but they remained wary.

It wasn't until He Liyuan arrived and explained that this was normal, and that similar phenomena had occurred on the sacred mountain before.

After hearing this, the three of them finally relaxed.

"The spiritual energy is even more concentrated! This level of concentration is truly... terrifying!" Black Dragon exclaimed.

He had previously visited so-called blessed lands and sacred cultivation grounds, but the concentration of spiritual energy there was nothing special.

It was comparable to the concentration of spiritual energy in Thousand Mountain County.

But now, the concentration of spiritual energy had increased to a whole new level; this was something he had never encountered before.

He Liyuan simply smiled. He thought to himself, "You haven't been to the mountainside; the spiritual energy there is truly terrifying.

It's even materialized, turning into spiritual rain."

Just then, someone came to report that the divine water had changed.

He Liyuan's expression changed: "What? The divine water is acting strangely? What happened?"

The man quickly replied, "I don't know, but the divine water has started to vaporize, releasing a lot of spiritual energy, it's incredibly concentrated."

He Liyuan's expression shifted, and he immediately headed towards the divine spring.

The three Qilin followed.

When they arrived at the divine spring, they witnessed a miraculous scene.

Previously, the divine water flowing down was crystal clear, like a clear spring.

But now it had changed. As the divine water fell, it created wisps of mist that rose upwards, like celestial vapor.

The entire surface of the water was shrouded in a layer of mist, creating a hazy, ethereal atmosphere.

He Liyuan bent down, scooped up a spoonful of the divine water with a wooden ladle, and, disregarding whether the change in the divine water would bring any harm, drank it down in one gulp.

Then he closed his eyes, carefully sensing the divine water entering his body.

As before, the divine water felt cool and refreshing upon entering his throat, then transformed into a power that nourished his entire body, purifying his spiritual energy and dispelling his confusion.

However, He Liyuan had been drinking the divine water for a long time, and his spiritual energy was already thoroughly purified; there was no confusion left in his heart, so these effects were negligible.

But then He Liyuan's expression turned to surprise, because he discovered that the divine water had developed another effect: it contained incredibly powerful spiritual energy.

This spiritual energy was harmless and could be directly absorbed.

He had just drunk a ladleful, and his spiritual energy had increased by half a point.

"This..." He Liyuan was astonished.

The three Qilin members, watching from the side, quickly asked, "How is it? Has the divine water caused any problems?"

"You should try it too," He Liyuan said without answering, instead urging them to drink the divine water and try it themselves.

Without hesitation, the three Qilin members picked up wooden ladles, scooped up a spoonful of the divine water, and drank it down in one gulp.

Then, just like He Liyuan, they reacted. At first, everything seemed normal, but when their spiritual energy surged within them, they showed surprise.

"This... the divine water has an additional effect!?" Qilin exclaimed in surprise.

"Yes, that's right. It must be a change brought about by the strange phenomenon just now." He Liyuan nodded, looking up at the divine mountain with deep awe in his eyes, lost in thought.

After this ordeal, the three of them vaguely sensed that the strange phenomenon might have been caused by the divine mountain, and thus all looked at it with reverence.

After confirming the divine water was safe, He Liyuan and Qilin returned to the Mountain Tower.

The main force from Qianshan County had already headed to Longyun Prefecture. He Liyuan had initially intended to send a message about the strange phenomena at the sacred mountain to Bai Liang, but after thinking it over, he decided against it.

He decided not to disturb their operation yet and could deal with it upon their return.

"Sigh, it's such a pity they didn't call on us to fight demons this time," Black Dragon sighed, feeling deeply regretful.

Fire Phoenix and Qilin wholeheartedly agreed.

They had been rogue cultivators and had their own methods for dealing with demons.

Most importantly, the three of them loved fighting!

If they didn't, they wouldn't have become rogue cultivators.

"By the way, Qilin, last time you said the Second Prince committed suicide, what exactly happened?" He Liyuan suddenly asked curiously.

Qilin had mentioned this before, but Bai Liang and the others hadn't pressed for details, so it was left unresolved.

However, He Liyuan had always been curious: why would a prince commit suicide?

Upon mentioning this, the three of them, including Qilin, frowned immediately.

“We don’t know either. The situation was too urgent and extremely bizarre. Even I was frightened and didn’t think much about it at the time.”

“After escaping, thinking back carefully, it’s terrifying to think about.” Qilin said slowly, his expression grim.

No one would feel good being falsely accused.

“I even suspect that the Second Prince wasn’t human!” Black Dragon said in a deep voice.

How could someone quickly desiccate into a human skin after death?

To this day, the court still believes that it was the three of them’s cruel methods.

“Where is the Second Prince’s skin now? Has the court retrieved it?” He Liyuan suddenly asked.

Qilin thought for a moment and shook his head: “No, the court’s official response is that they couldn’t find the Second Prince’s body…”

Halfway through his sentence, Qilin also sensed something was wrong.

The human skin was right in front of the court's experts; how could they not bring it back?

"Is there a conspiracy here?" Huo Feng said, her face grim.

"Possibly." Based on the information Qilin provided, He Liyuan calculated and confirmed that there might indeed be something fishy going on.

"You've likely become scapegoats," he said.

At the same time, he had another guess he didn't voice: the Second Prince might not be dead yet.

He Liyuan had traveled the world, delivering goods across the land, and had seen many unorthodox methods.

In his youth, before the demonic chaos, he had delivered goods to a foreign power called the Southern Frontier Tribe.

The Southern Frontier Tribe possessed a secret technique that could dehydrate a person, turning them into a dried skin, which could then be rolled up and tucked into a book.

Through this unorthodox method, people could be smuggled into the Great Chu Dynasty.

However, this method was considered evil and had side effects, so it was not commonly seen.

Therefore, He Liyuan believed that the Second Prince was very likely not dead, but had used this evil magic of the Southern Frontier tribes to fake his death and escape.

But what was the point of faking death?

He Liyuan couldn't figure it out, so he thought he might be overthinking it.

The three of them, including Qilin, didn't think much of it either, only feeling that they had been tricked and made scapegoats.

Heilong grumbled bitterly, "If only I had just killed that Second Prince back then! He was going to take the blame anyway!"

Meanwhile, Wu Ze led the large contingent from Qianshan County into Longyun Prefecture.

Bai Liang communicated with Chu Wuliu using the Mountain Token, and the two planned to meet on a small mountain.

"Can you see the demon army?" Sanfu asked, looking into the distance.

"Too far away, can't see clearly, only the overwhelming aura of demons," Hu Zhengqiang said in a deep voice.


.
Chapter 216 Deaths are normal
.

The group stopped, gazing at the dark, heavy clouds in the distance.

Those weren't actually clouds, but rather a surge of demonic energy that had risen into the sky, obscuring the entire heavens.

"The Chu Prefecture forces are almost here. Let's wait for them here,"

Bai Liang said, putting away his Mountain Token.

This was a small pit, clearly once a lake, but now dried up, leaving this pit.

The group lay hidden in the pit, concealing their presence and waiting silently.

Sanfu approached Bai Liang and the others, informing them that the demonic army they faced was far too powerful; it would likely be a tough battle!

"A tough battle it is, but we'll take them down!" Wu Ze said in a deep voice.

However, a direct confrontation was impossible; that would be like throwing an egg against a rock.

The crucial strategy would have to be discussed after they met up with Chu Wuliu's group.

"With a seventh-tier demon in charge, it's going to be very difficult! Unless we have a way to deal with a seventh-tier demon, there's practically no chance of winning."

Bian Nong's expression was grave. His assessment of the odds of success was essentially zero.

"We'll play it by ear," Bai Liang said calmly, offering no further explanation.

If absolutely necessary, they would have to rely on Li Xue.

The situation in Longyun County was incredibly tense.

Although many people had fled long ago, a large portion remained; it was impossible for everyone to escape.

These people were now in grave danger.

According to the map, Longyun County had four counties, all large counties with populations of over two hundred thousand each, evenly distributed.

The county closest to the border was Longjiang County.

"Longjiang County has the largest number of fleeing people. Conservatively speaking, at least half of them have escaped," Bai Liang analyzed, holding the intelligence he had gathered.

He sighed slightly. He had already sent people to spread the word before the demon army even invaded.

It wasn't just Longjiang County, but all the counties near the border; the people there were to evacuate as soon as possible and not linger.

Some listened, others ignored him.

“Those who escaped are all penniless, the lowest of the low. Only wealthy farmers, merchants with businesses in the counties, and families will stay.”

Wu Ze glanced around; the ordinary people's thinking differed from that of cultivators.

In the chaos of Qingzhou, what mattered most to them were money and food. As long as they held onto them, they wouldn't starve or be plundered by rioters.

Of course, they were also afraid of the demons.

But what good was fear? They had nowhere to go if they wanted to escape.

Don't be fooled by their status as wealthy farmers and influential merchants in Longjiang County.

Outside Longjiang County, they were nothing!

Especially in Qingzhou now, without the imperial court to maintain order, they were highly sought after.

Many bandits specifically targeted the wealthy.

The poor, on the other hand, were alone and without fear, fleeing Longjiang County directly.

The bandits didn't bother with the poor, as most of them had been poor themselves.

Of course, many wealthy farmers and merchants disguised themselves as poor people to escape; some were recognized and killed, while others escaped undetected.

Those who remained in Longjiang County were likely already devoured by the demons.

“There’s no way to save them. The demon army is fast; they should have already reached Longjiang County.” Sanfu murmured a Buddhist chant, his expression unchanging.

Death is commonplace; he’s long been used to it.

Since the demons began wreaking havoc, at least six or seven hundred million people have died.

What are the lives of a few hundred thousand in Longjiang County?

“If Longjiang County falls, then Ning County will be next.”

Bai Liang looked at the map; Ning County was only about sixty li away from Longjiang County.

Fortunately, more people in Ning County evacuated; at least sixty percent of the population had escaped.

As for those who remained, Bai Liang chose to respect their fate.

They had done all they could; choosing to stay was their own choice.

As the old saying goes, you can’t reason with a ghost that’s already dead.

Wu Ze's expression was somewhat grave. Hu Zhengqiang stepped forward to comfort him, saying, "Don't be too depressed. Death is normal."

After confirming that the demon army had entered Longyun County, they were prepared to sacrifice the entire county, ideally confining the demon army within its borders.

As long as the demon army was stopped in Longyun County, the people of Qingzhou behind them could be protected.

In short, Longyun County was a quagmire; their goal was to trap the demon army there, preventing them from escaping.

"I understand all of this," Wu Ze sighed.

Understanding was one thing, but what one truly felt was another.

He had witnessed nearly 100,000 people in an entire county perish at the hands of demons.

"You were all too well protected by the Mountain God," Bian Nong said with a touch of melancholy.

The situation in Qianshan County was extremely unique; virtually no one's life had been threatened by the demons.

This was extremely abnormal. Even in Jingzhou, the heartland of the Great Chu Dynasty, where the capital was located, many demons lurked.

The people leaving the city lived in constant fear, terrified of encountering demons and suffering a terrible fate.

Otherwise, there would be no need for镖队 (escort caravans).

People were dying every day, and every month, entire counties were massacred; this had become the norm.

Only Qianshan County, protected by the sacred mountain, was virtually safe within a hundred miles, allowing even children to leave the city freely.

When Bian Nong and his group first arrived in Qianshan County, they were deeply shocked by what they saw.

"Yes, the Mountain God's grace is boundless. We are truly blessed to be protected like this," Wu Ze said with emotion, his eyes filled with reverence for the Mountain God.

The group waited in the pit for another quarter of an hour.

The mountain token in Bai Liang's hand emitted a faint glow. He quickly checked it, then said in a deep voice, "Chuzhou Prefecture has reached Longyun County, diagonally opposite us. We'll be there soon."

He immediately manipulated the mountain token to send a message to Chu Wuliu, informing him that the rendezvous point had changed from the small hill to the pit.

Meanwhile,

Chu Wuliu received Bai Liang's message and turned around, saying, "The situation has changed. The rendezvous point has been moved to this location."

As he spoke, he took out a map and pointed to a coordinate.

"Switching back and forth, what does this mean?"

A dissatisfied voice rang out. Chu Wuliu looked up and saw the Zhao brothers.

He frowned; from the very beginning, he had been extremely dissatisfied with these Zhao brothers.

In his old temper, he would have slapped him long ago.

But with the Third Prince present, he could only control his emotions.

The Third Prince had already changed out of his bright yellow python robe and into a white long gown.

His princely status was too conspicuous; if the demons found out, he would be in danger.


.
Chapter 217 Encountering the Enemy
.

The other Yang Shen realm cultivators did the same, all undergoing simple disguises.

After all, they needed to maintain a facade; if the demon army truly knew their identities, they might do unimaginable things.

However, Chu Wuliu's eyes twitched slightly as he looked at their disguises.

The four monks from Jinlong Temple donned wigs and Taoist robes, disguising themselves as Taoists from Xuanqing Temple.

They were trying to shift the blame onto Xuanqing Temple.

Seeing this, Wu Yuan Wu Hui was furious and didn't want to be outdone.

He used magic to conceal his hair, then donned a kasaya, transforming himself into a monk from Jinlong Temple.

Shangguan Shenghua, observing both sides' actions, had an idea. He then found a set of armor and transformed himself into the appearance of a military general.

Cha Xixing cursed Shangguan Shenghua for being shameless, then turned around and donned a scholar's white robe, becoming a bizarre and incongruous 'scholar.'

He even chuckled and uttered phrases like "Confucius said" and "classical Chinese."

On the other hand, the Imperial Preceptor's disciple, Miss Yun, appeared more normal, without much pretense.

Chu Wuliu truly couldn't understand why these prodigies' disguises were so abstract.

Even the Third Prince found it somewhat perplexing.

Finally, he simply turned his head away, out of sight, out of mind.

"How long will it take to reach this pit?" the Third Prince asked Chu Wuliu.

Chu Wuliu calculated the distance, thought for a moment, and said, "About a quarter of an hour."

"Alright, let's set off then!"

The Third Prince nodded, saying nothing more, and led the group onward.

Miss Yun, however, gradually fell behind the others.

Reaching the back of the group, her eyes flashed slightly. She flipped her hand, took out a paper figure, and silently affixed it to the back of a Yin Spirit Realm prodigy.

After affixing it, she immediately broke away from the group and headed in a certain direction.

The Third Prince at the front seemed to sense something, frowning as he glanced back, his gaze deep.

However, he didn't make a sound, instead withdrawing his gaze and continuing to lead the group towards the pit.

After leaving the group, Miss Yun quickly arrived at a mountainous area.

A person who had been waiting there for a long time visibly relaxed upon seeing Miss Yun appear.

"Senior, I have done as you instructed. The message has been sent out," Yue Qiu said in a deep voice, his face pale, clearly from excessive consumption of spiritual energy.

"Hmm, you did well."

Miss Yun nodded faintly, but her eyes were covered by a black cloth, obscuring her expression.

"But senior, I have some doubts. Will this really work? If we spread the news and let the demons know, won't it backfire and scare away all the geniuses from the capital?" Yue Qiu frowned, feeling uneasy.

Half a day ago, Miss Yun approached him, telling him about a way to save Qingzhou.

This way was to spread the identities of the Third Prince and the others, letting the demons know.

The prince's identity was a tempting prize for the demons, sure to cause them a stir.

Then, the Third Prince and his geniuses wouldn't be able to hide or avoid the situation.

The demon army would actively seek them out; annihilating the younger generation of human geniuses was far more appealing than slaughtering the common people of an entire prefecture!

When Yue Qiu first heard this method, he was shocked, feeling it was utterly rebellious.

Using the Third Prince and his group of prodigies as bait to attract the attention of the demon army seemed far too ruthless.

In the end, between the people of Qingzhou and the Third Prince and his group, Yue Qiu chose the people of Qingzhou.

Therefore, he accepted Miss Yun's method, leaving Qingzhou City directly and arriving in Longyun County ahead of time to spread the news.

The demon army would naturally receive the message then.

In that case, the Third Prince and his group's plan to impersonate the people of the Divine Mountain would be completely ruined.

"Since I asked you to do this, it must be effective. As for their attempt to escape..." Miss Yun said, smiling slightly, "Too late."

Yue Qiu sensed a chill in her tone.

Looking up at Miss Yun, he saw the excitement on her face, which startled him.

Could this senior be a madwoman?

He suddenly regretted his actions.

“You can go back to Qingzhou City and wait for good news,” Miss Yun said calmly, her excitement fading.

Yue Qiu nodded hesitantly, then turned and left.

Miss Yun made a hand seal, and her figure rippled as she slowly disappeared.

In the Third Prince's group, the paper figure behind the last prodigy suddenly moved, then fell to the ground, transforming into a lifelike little figure in a corner unnoticed by anyone.

The little figure slowly grew larger, finally taking the form of Miss Yun, who then returned to the group.

The group traveled for a quarter of an hour and arrived at their destination.

Chu Wuliu entered the pit and immediately saw Bai Liang and the others.

“Everyone, we have fulfilled our mission!” Chu Wuliu said, clasping his hands in greeting.

“Chuzhou Prefecture, thank you for your hard work!”

Bai Liang stepped forward, smiling.

Sanfu and the others also approached, looking at the group of prodigies brought by Chu Wuliu.

The Third Prince led the group of prodigies.

At this moment, the Third Prince and the others were also observing Bai Liang and his group, their brows furrowing and relaxing intermittently.

"Wu Ze of Qianshan County greets fellow Daoists!" Finally, Wu Ze stepped forward, representing Qianshan County to greet the prodigies.

"A mere Enlightenment Realm cultivator?"

The prodigies' expressions changed slightly upon seeing that the one who spoke was only an Enlightenment Realm cultivator, seemingly showing disdain.

Seeing this, Chu Wuliu quickly stepped forward and said, "Everyone, Fellow Daoist Wu Ze is the head of Qianshan County and a person favored by the gods, having received blessings from the Mountain God."

"Tsk, I thought the renowned Divine Mountain was something special. I didn't expect them to appoint an Enlightenment Realm cultivator as their head. It seems they're nothing special."

An untimely voice rang out, filled with sarcasm.

Even the Third Prince frowned slightly upon hearing this, coldly glancing at Zhao Fuhu, who had spoken.

But he didn't reprimand them, because he felt the same way.

Previously, the news about the sacred mountain was shrouded in mystery; even his father didn't want to interfere. Even the governor of Baijiang Prefecture, after becoming a Yang God Realm expert, willingly became a follower of the mountain god. This led him to have an extremely high opinion of the sacred mountain.

However, today, seeing the head of Qianshan County, whose cultivation level wasn't even at the Yin God Realm, made him somewhat doubt the veracity of the news he had heard.

When Wu Ze heard that familiar voice, he froze.

He would never forget that voice; it was that same voice that had sneered when his wife and daughter died.

"Just lowly commoners, dead is dead. Being run over by my Zhao family's carriage is their good fortune."

Chapter 218 A Gentleman's Revenge
.

Wu Ze's body trembled slightly, his fists clenched, as he slowly turned his head to look at Zhao Fuhu, who had spoken.

Upon seeing Zhao Fuhu's face clearly, the deepest memories buried in his heart were instantly stirred.

Scenes from the capital back then flashed through his mind like a series of images.

"It's...you!" Wu Ze's face darkened completely as he suppressed his anger.

"Hmm? You recognize me?" Zhao Fuhu was taken aback, scrutinizing Wu Ze closely, suddenly feeling a sense of familiarity.

Then, a flash of insight struck him, as if he remembered something, and he turned to look at Zhao Bufan.

Zhao Bufan's expression was one of surprise, which then turned into a cold laugh.

"Cousin, is this kid the soldier who caused trouble back then, the one who got you punished with confinement?" Zhao Fuhu asked.

"That's right! It's this brat. I'd recognize him even if he were ashes! If he hadn't caused trouble, why would I have been confined by the patriarch? My cultivation would have been delayed!"

Zhao Bufan gritted his teeth, his gaze venomous as he looked at Wu Ze.

"Fine, you managed to escape back then, but you didn't expect to run into me here today, did you?" Zhao Bufan said coldly.

The others frowned at this scene, unsure of what was going on.

Bai Liang knew what had happened back then, and he immediately stood beside Wu Ze.

His actions spoke volumes without a word.

He didn't try to persuade Wu Ze to prioritize the greater good; the hatred for the murder of his wife and daughter was irreconcilable.

Wu Ze had already shown enough restraint by not going to the capital.

Now that they were enemies, their eyes were filled with hatred. If Wu Ze wanted revenge today, then as Wu Ze's longtime friend, he would certainly be the first to help!

Chu Wuliu and the others sensed something was amiss, but without much hesitation, they positioned themselves around Wu Ze, ready for battle.

The Third Prince frowned, completely unprepared for this unexpected turn of events. He turned to the Zhao brothers with a cold gaze and said in a deep voice, "What exactly happened? Do you know him?"

"Your Highness, we know him more than we do! This brat has a grudge against our Zhao family!" Zhao Bufan replied, his eyes filled with venom as he looked at Wu Ze.

If it weren't for Wu Ze's military background back then, and the fact that the Zhao family's political enemies were secretly stirring up trouble, he would have long ago torn Wu Ze to pieces and fed him to the dogs.

In the end, due to public opinion, Wu Ze was only exiled to the borderlands.

He never expected that Wu Ze would rise to prominence and become the head of the sacred mountain, receiving the so-called favor of the 'mountain god'?

"A grudge against the Zhao family?" The Third Prince's expression remained unchanged, but his face had darkened.

Zhao Bufan was invoking the Zhao family to try and prevent Chu Yuan from interfering.

The Zhao family wielded immense power in the capital.

If Chu Yuan wanted to contend for the throne, he would need the support of powerful families like the Zhao family.

But who was Chu Yuan? Would he be hindered by a family like the Zhao family?

Seeing Zhao Bufan eager to attack Wu Ze,

the Third Prince coldly snorted, "You dare to move? This is Qingzhou, not the capital! We won't tolerate your insolence!"

This rebuke immediately frightened Zhao Bufan away.

He took a few steps back, his expression resentful, and inwardly furious at the Third Prince's obstruction.

Zhao Bufan thought bitterly, "Does he really think he's the heir to the throne? Once we return to the capital, I'll definitely tell Father about this. Want the Zhao family's support? Dream on!"

Zhao Fuhu's face also turned grim.

He hadn't expected the Third Prince to publicly reprimand Zhao Bufan for the sake of an outsider.

This was a direct slap in the face to the Zhao family.

"Your Highness, our Zhao family has a very good relationship with His Highness the First Prince. I don't think your previous suggestion was suitable for the Zhao family. I apologize," Zhao Fuhu said stiffly.

The Third Prince had previously spoken to him privately about some matters.

However, the Zhao family didn't want to take sides yet, so they hadn't immediately agreed.

But after today's incident, the Zhao family was destined not to support the Third Prince.

The Third Prince's expression remained unchanged. Before he even spoke, he already knew the Zhao family would completely turn their back on him.

But he couldn't care less about that now. The Zhao family's support was nothing compared to the unparalleled merit of resisting demons and protecting the people of Qingzhou.

If he could hold onto Qingzhou, the throne would almost certainly be his.

“It’s alright, I also don’t think the Zhao family is very suitable,” the Third Prince said calmly, then added, “We came to Qingzhou to train, to slay demons and monsters! Not for you to seek revenge or settle your personal affairs! If I discover any secret schemes you’re plotting, don’t blame me for not showing any mercy!”

Upon hearing this, the Zhao brothers’ faces darkened.

“Wu Ze, is it? A gentleman takes revenge even after ten years. I don’t care what grudges you have between you; now we are united to resist the demons, and we cannot afford to lose! I hope you can prioritize the greater good!”

The Third Prince then looked at Wu Ze and spoke slowly.

Wu Ze stared coldly at the Zhao brothers. Although he still harbored anger, his expression had temporarily returned to calm.

He understood the gravity of the situation. The demon invasion was a serious matter. He could avenge himself sooner or later; so many years had passed, what was a little more time?

Wu Ze took a deep breath: "The bigger picture is what matters. Let's deal with the demons first."

The Third Prince breathed a slight sigh of relief.

He was genuinely afraid Wu Ze was a reckless fool who might try to take revenge on the spot.

That would be difficult. Without the help of the Divine Mountain, he wasn't confident he could resist the demon army.

The Third Prince smiled: "Your Excellency is truly wise and righteous, worthy of being a chosen one. In that case, let's put this matter aside for now and get straight to the point: how to resist the demon army."

He was always very concerned about resisting the demon army, as it concerned his unparalleled achievement.

Wu Ze nodded slowly, and the atmosphere gradually eased from tense to relaxed.

As for the initial doubts, no one thought about them anymore.

Only the Zhao brothers, their faces grim and their eyes filled with venom, stared at Wu Ze, then glanced at the Third Prince.

They dared not show the Third Prince a disapproving look.

"Confucius said, 'Harmony is the most precious thing.'"

Cha Xixing, shaking his wide scholar's robes, swayed his head and spoke, his movements utterly comical.

Bai Liang and the others looked over and immediately felt that this 'scholar' was far too awkward.

He not only lacked scholarly air, but instead exuded a chilling aura.

Wu Ze, a former soldier, could clearly sense the unique military authority emanating from him.

"I... am Shangguan Shenghua from the Rentianmo Academy." Cha Xixing rummaged through his sleeves, unable to find a paper fan, so he could only offer a cupped-hand salute.

The real Shangguan Shenghua, his face darkening, then stepped forward.

His armor clanged against each other, and he simply and decisively bowed, saying, "Dragon Guard Army, Cha Xixing!"

However, to Bai Liang and the others, it was a thin, fair-skinned man dressed in heavy armor.

It looked utterly awkward!
