Chapter 219 Showing Off One's Edge
.

Are all the prodigies of the capital so abstract?

Besides Chu Wuliu, Bai Liang and his group were filled with bewilderment.

Not to mention the 'monks' and 'Taoists' who emerged later.

Among them, the old monk Sanfu stared deeply into thought at the two 'monks' who were making a Taoist gesture, their outer hand forming a Tai Chi symbol while their inner hand forming the Meridian Mudra.

Bian Nong and the others were also astonished, their jaws dropping as they watched the four 'Taoists' who almost chanted 'Amitabha' instead of 'Boundless Heavenly Venerable,' only correcting themselves halfway through.

What on earth happened in the capital? Have Buddhism and Taoism merged and become one?

The Third Prince, with a headache, could only step forward to explain that they had all been disguised.

The Yang Spirits do not leave the capital; if their identities were exposed, it would be very troublesome.

Upon hearing this, Bai Liang and the others nodded in understanding.

"Uncle-Master Sanfu, it's been a long time." A 'Taoist priest' stepped forward and bowed to Sanfu.

"You are... Wuyan?"

Sanfu finally recognized the 'Taoist priest' before him. He was somewhat surprised, then looked at the other three 'Taoist priests,' their identities self-evident.

"It's the four of you who have come to Qingzhou. It seems you have a heavy responsibility on this trip!" Sanfu said meaningfully.

The Four Vajras were arguably the most core disciples of the Golden Dragon Temple, the most promising talents being groomed.

Even their Dharma names were given separately.

To send all four of them at once meant they were no ordinary people.

A mere training trip in Qingzhou wouldn't warrant such attention.

Wuyan understood the 'deeper meaning' in Sanfu's words, assuming that Uncle-Master Sanfu had already gone ahead and infiltrated the sacred mountain. He immediately 'understood' and returned a meaningful glance.

Seeing this look, Sanfu was somewhat bewildered, thinking Wuyan was questioning him about why he had sided with the Divine Mountain.

"Amitabha, this humble monk has already found the sacred mountain in my heart. The karma within the temple is no longer my concern; my karmic ties have ended," Sanfu said calmly.

This directly indicated that he had officially left the Golden Dragon Temple and was now a member of the Divine Mountain.

"Uncle-Master, this disciple understands!" Wuyan said softly, using a secret technique to prevent outsiders from hearing.

He felt that his uncle was maintaining distance; only in this way could he better infiltrate the Divine Mountain and obtain the secret manual.

Therefore, it was best not to interfere with his uncle's plan.

Sanfu was even more confused.

You understand? What do you understand?

After thinking for a moment, Sanfu decided not to say anything more to Wuyan.

Meanwhile, Bai Liang was representing the Divine Mountain, discussing with the Third Prince how to resist the demons.

During this time, the Third Prince unexpectedly discovered that Bai Liang was actually from the Bai family of the capital.

The Bai family was already a declining aristocratic family in the capital. Without a cultivator at the sixth level or above, they would be relegated to a low-ranking family.

The Bai family patriarch was only at the peak of the Yin God Realm, and few of the Bai family disciples were outstanding. Even Bai Lihuo, the most talented, was currently only at the Yin God Realm.

They might have a chance to break through to the Yang God Realm in the future, but it was only a chance.

"What a coincidence, I remember Bai Lihuo seems to have come to Qingzhou as well," the Third Prince suddenly said with a smile.

So he ordered someone to summon Bai Lihuo.

Bai Liang hadn't expected to encounter someone from the Bai family. He naturally recognized Bai Lihuo, his cousin, who was highly valued by the Bai family patriarch. In fact, the Bai family's resources were almost entirely directed towards Bai Lihuo.

Bai Liang, lacking talent, had long been marginalized and left the Bai family.

Bai Lihuo approached, his expression somewhat awkward upon seeing Bai Liang. He forced a smile and said, "Clan...clan brother, long time no see. I didn't expect you to be in Qingzhou."

He and Bai Liang had a few unpleasant encounters in their youth.

Bai Liang nodded slightly. There was no deep-seated hatred between him and Bai Lihuo; it was all just youthful squabbles.

Now, they could easily let bygones be bygones, after all, they were both members of the Bai family.

"How is the patriarch's health?" Bai Liang asked.

Hearing this, Bai Lihuo knew Bai Liang didn't hold a grudge, so he breathed a sigh of relief and smiled, "The old master is still in good health; he even took a concubine last month."

Bai Liang was speechless.

Nearly two hundred years old, and still taking a concubine—truly, a man is a boy until death!

After a brief chat with Bai Lihuo, Bai Liang continued discussing serious matters.

"The demon army numbers a full one hundred thousand, including two seventh-tier demons and dozens of sixth-tier demons!"

He stated the situation solemnly.

The Third Prince and the others frowned deeply upon hearing this.

"Two seventh-tier demons—that won't be easy to deal with!" the Third Prince said in a deep voice.

Wu Yan stepped forward and said, "If we join forces, we can hold off one seventh-tier demon. As for the remaining one..."

Wu Yan then looked at Bai Liang and the others.

It was clear they wanted Mount Shen to demonstrate its strength.

They were holding back a seventh-tier demon, so Mount Shen should also engage and hold back a seventh-tier demon; that would be fair.

Equal strength was essential for cooperation!

The Third Prince and the others looked at Bai Liang and his group, awaiting a response.

They also wanted to know the true strength of Mount Shen.

Chu Wuliu and the others frowned slightly. With their current apparent strength, holding back a seventh-tier demon was simply a pipe dream, utterly impossible.

But seeing that Bai Liang and Wu Ze didn't show any fear, they gradually felt reassured.

Bai Liang and Wu Ze exchanged a glance, then nodded and said, "Alright, the remaining seventh-tier demon is ours!"

This immediate agreement surprised the Third Prince and the others.

Shangguan Shenghua asked curiously, "Among you, the strongest is only at the Yang God Realm, and there are only three of you. I'm not doubting you, I'm just very curious, with your strength, what do you intend to use to restrain a seventh-tier demon?"

The others shared the same thought.

The Divine Mountain currently only has three Yang God Realm cultivators: Sanfu, Chu Wuliu, and Hu Zhengqiang. With such strength, restraining a seventh-tier demon is simply impossible!

"We have our trump card!" Bai Liang replied simply, without elaborating.

The trump card was naturally Li Xue's Divine Fate Talisman.

Hearing Bai Liang's response, although the others still had doubts, they said no more.

If things went wrong, they would retreat immediately.

"The remaining sixth-tier demons are not to be underestimated either, there are at least twenty of them!" Bai Liang mentioned the sixth-tier demons again.

"We can ignore those. We're all at the sixth rank, but human cultivators are stronger than demons. We just need to avoid them."

The Third Prince spoke, then added gravely, "The most troublesome thing is the 100,000-strong demon army. Although they're all low-ranking demons, their sheer number makes them much harder to deal with! That's the real problem!"

Let alone 100,000 demons, even 100,000 pigs, if they just stood still, killing them all wouldn't be an easy task.

Chapter 220 Demonic Unrest
.

"There's no good solution. We can only contain them within Longyun County for now, preventing the demon army from spreading."

Bai Liang pondered.

If they could confine the demon army to Longyun County and then deal with the higher-level demons first, the rest would be trapped.

However, while the idea was there, putting it into practice was much more difficult.

Firstly, Longyun County spans several hundred miles. Preventing the demons from spreading is practically impossible with their current manpower.

"Let's conduct a probe first. What if there are more than two seventh-tier demons among them?" the Third Prince suggested.

The Third Prince didn't entirely trust the information Bai Liang provided.

He still trusted his own judgment; the situation had to be under his control.

"Alright," Bai Liang nodded, agreeing to conduct a probe first before making further decisions.

"On my side, all sixth-tier Yang Gods will be deployed." The Third Prince understood the principle of using full force even when hunting a rabbit.

Even if it's a test, all Yang Shen Realm cultivators must be brought along to avoid any unforeseen circumstances.

Then, the Third Prince looked at Bai Liang and the others, "Who will you send to test them? Make it clear first, they must have the strength to restrain seventh-tier demons; otherwise, if they encounter danger, this prince will not offer any assistance."

He spoke calmly; this was both a test of the demon army and a test of the Divine Mountain.

He still harbored doubts about the Divine Mountain's strength.

After all, the two in charge, Wu Ze and Bai Liang, were far too weak, both only at the fourth-tier Enlightenment Realm.

Such strength was only fit to guard his residence in the capital.

Bai Liang understood the Third Prince's intention, so he directly chose Chu Wuliu and two other Yang Shen Realm cultivators, then added himself and Wu Ze, and finally, the crucial Li Xue.

Seeing Bai Liang bring along a teenage girl, everyone frowned.

"Wait, why bring this little girl along?"

Cha Xixing asked casually, her tone slightly displeased.

The Divine Mountain's apparent strength was already weak; bringing along such a young girl seemed like a complete burden.

"She is also a person favored by the gods," Bai Liang said calmly, offering no further explanation.

Cha Xixing frowned; she had heard the term "favored by the gods" more than once.

The Third Prince and the others felt the same way.

At this moment, they all had the same thought: what exactly did "favored by the gods" mean, giving these few Enlightened God Realm cultivators such confidence?

But since Bai Liang and the others didn't explain, they naturally wouldn't understand.

In the end, the Divine Mountain selected six people: three at the Yang God Realm and three at the Enlightened God Realm.

The difference in strength was indeed somewhat captivating.

On the other hand, the Imperial Court's side was more uniform, all at the Yang God Realm.

Including the Third Prince, that made exactly ten people.

With their combined strength and hidden trump cards, holding off two seventh-tier demons wouldn't be a problem.

But they wouldn't do that. They were only supposed to hold off one seventh-tier demon, and that was it.

The remaining one would be dealt with by the people from Divine Mountain.

If the people from Divine Mountain couldn't handle the seventh-tier demon, they wouldn't offer any assistance.

"Since everything is ready, let's set off!" the Third Prince said calmly.

The demonic aura in the distance was growing increasingly intense, tinged with blood—blood had likely already been drawn.

This was the perfect opportunity to test the waters.

The Third Prince set off first, heading towards the demonic army.

The Four Vajras of Jinlong Temple, Wu Yuan Wu Hui of Xuanqing Temple, Shangguan Shenghua, Cha Xixing, and Miss Yun followed closely behind.

Miss Yun remained remarkably quiet throughout, almost unnoticed.

But the Third Prince dared not forget her; he knew all too well how mad this woman could be.

“Yun Lanxi, this matter in Qingzhou is of utmost importance. You'd better not cause any more trouble! Even your master won't be able to clean up your mess!”

The Third Prince warned Yun Lanxi telepathically.

“Don't worry, Your Highness, what could I possibly do?” Yun Lanxi replied.

Hearing Yun Lanxi's response, the Third Prince still wasn't entirely at ease and remained cautious, periodically observing her movements with his divine sense.

He remembered that Yun Lanxi had fallen behind once just now, only rejoining the group after quite some time.

Who knew what she had been doing during that time?

"Let's go, we'll catch up!"

Bai Liang spoke, leading Chu Wuliu and the others to follow behind the Third Prince's group.

Wu Ze activated the Divine Speed ​​Talisman, amplifying Bai Liang and Li Xue's speed, significantly increasing their speed.

Despite their Enlightenment Realm cultivation, their speed was comparable to that of a Yang God Realm cultivator.

The Third Prince's group ahead also noticed this scene, feeling somewhat surprised.

They had deliberately increased their speed to see how these Enlightenment Realm cultivators from Divine Mountain would react.

But their speed didn't decrease; no matter how fast they went, they could easily keep up.

"Indeed, they're not simple. No wonder they weren't intimidated by our cultivation level, even though they're at the Enlightenment Realm," Wu Yuan said, raising an eyebrow.

Wu Hui, however, noticed something and said, "It's that Wu Ze. He seems to have used some kind of 'secret technique,' a powerful force amplifying the speed of the other two."

This scene made them less dismissive of the Divine Mountain.

They did have something; they weren't just all talk.

..............................

Longjiang County.

Blood stained the sky, demonic energy soared, and monster energy permeated the air.

As the first stop of the demon army's invasion of Qingzhou, Longjiang County was a scene of utter devastation. Those who hadn't evacuated had become food for the demons.

Only now did they deeply regret not listening to those who claimed to be from the Divine Mountain and evacuating Longjiang County as soon as possible.

If they had listened to those people earlier, they wouldn't have become food for the demons.

"Battle, exhilarating!" The demon ripped open a man's chest, sucking blood and flesh with a wild, excited roar.

It had been a very, very long time since it had enjoyed such a hearty feast.

Although only tens of thousands remained in Longjiang County, it was enough to whet the appetite of the demon army.

The demons' slaughter created a pervasive aura of blood, forming a blood aura domain.

This was a naturally suitable environment for demons to cultivate; cultivating within a blood aura domain was ten times faster than usual!

This was why demons loved to slaughter—to create such an environment.

Meanwhile, at this moment, Quan Xiaotian and another seventh-tier demon were above the county town.

"Interesting! Interesting! I never expected the Great Chu Dynasty to secretly send so many prodigies to Qingzhou, even a prince!" Quan Xiaotian's eyes flashed with a sharp light as he threw the human man in his arms down.

However, before he even hit the ground, an eagle demon caught him and tore him apart, blood splattering through the air.

"Humans, they're getting bolder and bolder. The Holy Court hasn't even begun to disregard the Heavenly Water Pact, and they've already done it," a seventh-tier demon said coldly.

"Hmph! They think they can just come here for training? Since they're here, let them all stay!"

Quan Xiaotian's eyes were bloodshot, a restless energy stirring within him.

Chapter 221 Humanity Emerges from Adversity: A True Dragon
.

"Should we report this to the Holy Court? Emperor Chu has even sent his prince; this is a matter of great importance," the Demon Serpent said ominously.

"No rush," Quan Xiaotian waved his hand and said with a faint smile, "We'll send the news back to the Holy Court after we've captured the human prodigy and this prince."

"That's feasible!" the Demon Serpent smiled.

In that case, their capture of the human prodigy would be a great achievement.

This is the first time they've seen such a merit delivered to their doorstep.

"Remember not to be careless. Humans are treacherous and cunning. If they dare to let these prodigies out, they must have some kind of guarantee."

Quan Xiaotian spoke in a deep voice, not letting his guard down just because he was a seventh-tier demon king.

The serpent nodded in agreement, saying, "The human race is indeed powerful, and cunning. If it weren't for the arrival of the two Holy Venerables back then, we would have been driven to the margins long ago."

Speaking of this, both of them had a sigh in their eyes.

They were old demons who had lived through the era when demons and monsters had no saints.

At that time, demons of the same realm were no match for the human race's profound arts and techniques, suffering defeat after defeat. If it weren't for their extremely fast reproduction rate, they would have been wiped out long ago.

All of this changed when a crimson meteorite fell from the sky, landing in the demon's camp.

Immediately, the demon race's half-saints and the demon race's half-saints went to investigate the crimson meteorite, forbidding other beings from approaching.

This process lasted for a full forty-nine days. When the demon race's half-saints emerged, they had clearly transformed, becoming ninth-order saints.

No one knew what had happened inside, but the demon saints and monster saints who emerged as saints had undergone a drastic change in temperament, their eyes now devoid of emotion.

Below the level of a sage, all are but ants.

The birth of two sages among the demons immediately reversed their weakened state. Humanity's semi-saints were utterly incapable of reaching them, forced to rely solely on their own destiny to resist the sages' might.

The sages were powerful, but even they were powerless against the accumulated human destiny of the Great Chu Dynasty, built up over four hundred years.

They could only gradually diminish humanity's destiny before launching a final, decisive attack.

Quan Xiaotian vividly remembered a statement made by a demon sage at the time:

"Don't push humanity too far. Adversity breeds true dragons among humans; we must not repeat the mistakes of the ancient sages. Dealing with humanity requires a slow, insidious approach, giving them space to survive, thereby weakening their will to resist. Proceed gradually, and then annihilate them in one fell swoop!"

Quan Xiaotian didn't understand this statement then, and he still doesn't now.

Humanity breeds true dragons in adversity?

He wasn't entirely convinced, but he strongly approved of the latter part of the method for dealing with humanity.

Slowly, like a knife cutting flesh, they gradually eroded humanity, swallowing it whole in one gulp.

This strategy avoided depleting the Holy Court's strength and also prevented strong resistance from the humans—a brilliant plan.

The Holy Court signed the Treaty of Tianshui with the Great Chu Dynasty precisely for this reason.

"While the Demon Saint is powerful, he can sometimes misjudge. Will adversity produce a true dragon among humans? I doubt it!" Quan Xiaotian thought coldly.

He looked down; the demon army had completely occupied Longjiang County. Without exception, all the humans remaining in the county were dead.

But the demons did not leave; instead, they gathered in the Blood Qi Domain to absorb and cultivate, increasing their strength.

"This is just an appetizer. The real feast will be the blood energy domain formed by the condensed blood energy of the entire Qingzhou!" The demonic serpent's eyes gleamed as it greedily licked its lips.

For a seventh-tier demon king like him, a county-level blood energy domain was of little significance.

It needed to be at least at the prefecture level to be effective!

Once the demon army below had absorbed the blood energy, it would continue its advance to the next human county, until it had slaughtered the entire Qingzhou!

Meanwhile, thirty miles away from Longjiang County, on a lake...

Bai Liang and his companions, along with the Third Prince and his group, arrived late, now gazing solemnly at the distant horizon.

Above their heads was a Tai Chi futon, used to conceal their aura and deceive the heavens.

In the direction of Longjiang County, a crimson aura permeated the air, a dark, oppressive mass, carrying an extremely powerful aura.

"A blood energy domain has already formed; it seems everyone's dead."

The Third Prince said calmly, his tone devoid of any emotion.

Bai Liang frowned. He activated the Divine Sense Talisman within his body, maximizing the range of his divine sense.

In an instant, he saw the scene in Longjiang County.

The former county town was now a ruin, the ground littered with corpses, their flesh and blood devoured, resembling a ghost town.

Countless demons greedily absorbed the blood mist permeating the air, enhancing their own strength.

After investigating for a moment, cold sweat broke out on Bai Liang's forehead, and he immediately withdrew his divine sense.

Extending his divine sense thirty miles away was already his limit; the consumption was enormous. This was the first time he had attempted to fully activate the Divine Sense Talisman.

Seeing Bai Liang swaying unsteadily, Wu Ze quickly helped him up and asked, "Can you see Longjiang County?"

Bai Liang nodded, "Yes, everyone's dead. The demons are absorbing blood essence to cultivate."

Upon hearing this, the Third Prince and the others turned their gazes towards him.

"You can extend your divine sense thirty miles away?" Shangguan Shenghua asked in astonishment.

They, at the sixth-tier Yang God Realm and even as prodigies, could only barely see thirty miles away. Yet this person, at the Enlightenment Realm, could see thirty miles?

Bai Liang nodded calmly, offering no further explanation.

Cha Xixing, however, was somewhat skeptical and asked several questions about Longjiang County.

Bai Liang answered them all correctly, proving that he truly could extend his divine sense into Longjiang County.

"Impressive. It seems I underestimated you all," Cha Xixing said bluntly, beginning to take Bai Liang and his companions seriously.

The others did the same, no longer underestimating the group from Qianshan County.

Chu Wuliu and his two companions were somewhat embarrassed. Having just broken through to the sixth-tier Yang God realm, and not being top-tier prodigies, their divine sense only had a range of about twenty miles, preventing them from seeing the situation in Longjiang County.

“With the Blood Qi Domain formed, the demons won't leave Longjiang County anytime soon. This is the perfect opportunity for us to probe!” the Third Prince said calmly.

Wu Yuan nodded, saying, “There are two extremely powerful auras in Longjiang County; they should be those two seventh-tier demons. If we make a move, it will definitely alert them.”

“Probably not. We're only at the Yang God realm, and the demons don't know our identities. Seventh-tier demons have to maintain their armies; at most, they'll send sixth-tier demons to hunt us down,” Shangguan Shenghua analyzed, then smiled.

"Therefore, we can take this opportunity to kill some sixth-tier demons first. Once they realize something is wrong, we can quickly retreat and proceed with the next plan."

This strategy was quickly approved by the Third Prince and the others.

They went to probe, luring out some sixth-tier demons, and then killed them one by one.

By the time the seventh-tier demons realized what was happening, they had already killed quite a few sixth-tier demons.

Chapter 222 A Person Jealous of Heaven
.

"What do you all think?" the Third Prince asked, looking at Wu Ze and the others.

Since they were now in an alliance, they needed to consult the people from Qianshan County.

Bai Liang and the others exchanged glances, then finally nodded.

"We think it's feasible. Let's use this as a test to kill some sixth-tier demons and weaken the demon army," Bai Liang said.

However, at this moment, Hu Zhengqiang suddenly spoke up: "But what if the other side doesn't send a sixth-tier demon, but instead sends out a seventh-tier demon directly? How should we deal with that?"

The scene immediately fell silent. Then Shangguan Shenghua coughed twice and stepped forward, saying, "That is indeed possible, but the chances are slim. The demon hierarchy is strict, with a clear order of rank. If the enemy is only sixth-tier, a seventh-tier demon is unlikely to attack directly. There's a 90% probability they'll send a subordinate of equal strength."

"Shangguan Shenghua is right. Unless the demon knows our identities, but that's impossible. We kept it a secret before coming; apart from the accompanying prodigies, we didn't reveal anything to the outside world," the Third Prince said slowly.

As he spoke, Yun Lanxi, standing at the very edge, slightly turned her head away.

"Then I'm relieved."

After listening, Hu Zhengqiang nodded.

"In that case, let's act now, or it will be too late once the blood energy domain dissipates!" The Third Prince's tone was somewhat excited; this was his first time leading a team to fight against demons.

And against a demon army no less! If they succeeded, it would be his achievement, his battle record!

Bai Liang and the others nodded, their expressions serious, mentally prepared.

Wu Hui wielded the Tai Chi Futon with one hand. It was a sixth-tier magic artifact, but its concealment effect was almost equivalent to a seventh-tier one, capable of concealing itself from seventh-tier demons from ten miles away.

Thus, the group used the Tai Chi Futon to slowly approach Longjiang County.

However, unbeknownst to them, another gaze was secretly watching everything.

Li Yue projected a portion of his divine sense through the divine talismans within Bai Liang and the others, essentially sharing their perspective.

He had witnessed the horrific scene in Longjiang County, and even saw further than the Third Prince and the others.

"A dog, a snake, both only at the seventh-tier cultivation level."

Li Yue saw through the true nature of the seventh-tier demons, pondering that with such strength, Bai Liang and the others, combined with the Third Prince and his group, could hold their own.

"That blind woman, however, is quite interesting."

Compared to the demon army, Miss Yun attracted Li Yue's attention more.

Because when part of his divine sense descended upon the divine talisman within Wu Ze's body, this blind woman actually glanced at Wu Ze thoughtfully.

At first, Li Yue thought she had been detected, but later realized that wasn't the case.

This blind woman simply sensed that the heavenly secrets had been disturbed and felt something was amiss.

Thinking about it, it made sense. He was now a fifth-tier Sacred Mountain, comparable to a ninth-tier Saint. Apart from Saints of the same level and Half-Saints who had just glimpsed the Great Dao, other beings couldn't possibly detect him.

Yun Lanxi was able to sense something was wrong not because she had discovered Li Yue, but because Li Yue's divine sense had descended and disturbed the heavenly secrets of this place, thus being perceived by her.

"Those whom Heaven envy are often crippled or disabled; this woman must be an exception."

Li Yue, having comprehended the laws of Heaven and Earth, naturally understood what "those whom Heaven envy" meant.

This is the result of a variable created when the laws of heaven and earth are incomplete and damaged, giving rise to such a person.

The appearance of someone envied by heaven is a rejection by heaven and earth, hence the saying of physical disability. However, this envied person also gains unexpected abilities.

Yun Lanxi's ability should lie in her eyes.

"Blind in appearance, but not in spirit," Li Yue commented.

Then he turned his attention to the group. They had already reached a point ten miles outside Longjiang County using the Tai Chi Futon.

This location was the limit of the Tai Chi Futon's capabilities. Going any further, the futon would no longer conceal their auras, exposing them to the divine sense of seventh-tier demons.

"Everyone, I am preparing to withdraw the Tai Chi Futon. Are you ready?" the monk-like Wu Hui asked in a deep voice.

Bai Liang and the others nodded in unison, preparing to flee.

If any sixth-tier demons pursued them, they would scatter and escape, then kill the sixth-tier demons one by one.

Upon receiving the reply, Wu Hui no longer hesitated and immediately tucked the Tai Chi futon into his sleeve.

Instantly, everyone's aura was revealed!

Above Longjiang County, the previously composed Quan Xiaotian and Demon Snake suddenly opened their eyes and turned to look in a certain direction.

"What's going on? Human cultivators, sixth-tier Yang God Realm, more than a dozen of them!?" Demon Snake was surprised, even more astonished that they hadn't noticed them at such close range.

"Good! They've come to test us, truly audacious!"

Quan Xiaotian's face was cold as he sneered.

At this moment, the other sixth-tier demons also noticed the human cultivators ten miles away.

Immediately, a hot-tempered demon roared, "How dare some humans approach the demon army! They're courting death! I'll tear them to pieces!"

Soon, many sixth-tier demons approached Quan Xiaotian and Demon Snake, requesting permission to go out and kill the human cultivators!

"Heh, this is a human trick. You'll all die if you go out!"

Quan Xiaotian narrowed his eyes and sneered.

"My Lord Demon King, although our strength is inferior to that of the human cultivators, we have the advantage of overwhelming numbers and the enhancement of our Blood Qi Domain. Dealing with these humans is a piece of cake!" a sixth-tier demon retorted.

"Is that so? What if I told you that these human cultivators are all the pride of the capital, and some are even princes?" Quan Xiaotian said calmly, then countered, "Do you still think you can easily defeat them?"

"Human pride? Princes!?"

The sixth-tier demon was somewhat surprised upon hearing this, but remained fearless; on the contrary, his eyes turned bloodshot.

"Yes! So what if they're human prodigies? Our claws are no less effective! Demon King, let us go into battle and capture these human prodigies and that prince alive!"

With that, the group of sixth-tier demons were eager to try, their eyes filled with greed, all wanting to seize this 'great achievement'!

"Enough! I said you're no good, and that's final!"

Quan Xiaotian's tone was icy, and he said in a deep voice, "Humans are incredibly cunning, even the Holy Venerable has said so. Those are the new generation of human prodigies; if you go out, you'll only be throwing your lives away!"

"There's a saying among humans that goes, 'Even a lion uses its full strength to hunt a rabbit.' They dare approach the demon army; they must have something to rely on. Therefore, I've decided to personally take action and capture these human prodigies!"

None of the sixth-tier demons expected the Demon King to intervene personally.

"I will go with you!" the demonic serpent said coldly from the side.

Two seventh-tier demons attacked simultaneously!


.
Chapter 223 Unconventional Approach
.

"Something's not right. Why haven't the demons attacked yet?"

Having fully revealed their presence, Bai Liang and his group waited for the demons to emerge.

But after waiting for a while, the demon army remained motionless, which made everyone realize something was amiss.

The Third Prince frowned, a sense of unease creeping into his heart: "Something's wrong. Retreat."

But before he could finish speaking, a shrill, maniacal laugh echoed through Longjiang County.

"Hehehe, you think you can just come and leave? There's no such thing as a free lunch!"

The demonic serpent's body blotted out the sky. Now, it revealed its true form: a gigantic black serpent coiled high in the air, its head held high, its eyes looking down arrogantly at the group below.

Bai Liang and the others' expressions changed drastically, their faces instantly becoming extremely grave.

"Damn it... This is a seventh-tier demon!"

Shangguan Shenghua's face was grim, and he swallowed hard.

The hearts of the others sank as well.

They hadn't expected the demon army to be so unpredictable.

They were only at the Yang God Realm level; logically, given the hierarchical order among demons, they should have been met with a sixth-tier demon.

Who could have predicted that instead of a sixth-tier demon, a seventh-tier demon king would appear?

But that wasn't all. Another loud roar rang out, and the phantom of a giant dog strode through the air, with a human figure standing within it.

"A seventh-tier demon king, he's come out too!"

The Third Prince's expression darkened completely. With a slight flick of his right hand, a crimson spear appeared in his grasp.

He took a deep breath; this probing attack had ended in failure.

The demons didn't play by the rules at all, immediately sending two seventh-tier demons to attack.

"Haha, Emperor Chu is truly generous, actually sending human prodigies to Qingzhou. Is he trying to kill off the next generation of cultivators because he thinks humanity has been holding out for too long?"

Quan Xiaotian stood on the giant dog phantom, looking down at the crowd below, and coldly laughed.

Upon hearing this, the Third Prince and the others' expressions changed.

"You...how do you know our identities!?" The Third Prince's gaze sharpened as he looked up at Quan Xiaotian.

Bai Liang and the others also felt a chill in their hearts, not expecting that the Third Prince and his group's identities would be exposed.

"This is troublesome. The demons know their identities and will definitely hunt them down relentlessly. Retreat will be difficult."

Chu Wuliu said with a grim expression.

"Heh, the Holy Court's henchmen are everywhere. What news can be kept secret?" Quan Xiaotian sneered, looking at the Third Prince wielding a spear: "Overlord Spear Technique, it seems you're that prince."

Then he looked at the monk-like figures, one wielding a knife and the other a sword, Wu Yuan Wu Hui, and frowned slightly.

"Eh, I've never heard of any sword-wielding prodigies at Golden Dragon Temple? Could they be disciples secretly trained?"

He then looked at the four Taoist-dressed figures, their hands clasped together, solemn and dignified, with golden Dharma wheels emanating from behind their heads.

Quan Xiaotian's brows twitched. This...this is a Taoist?!

Looking at the scholars and soldiers next to them, something seemed off.

After a moment's thought, he understood completely.

As for Bai Liang and his group on the other side, he completely ignored them, guessing they were just ordinary prodigies from the capital.

"Hmph! Such a clumsy disguise thinks it can fool everyone? Laughable. You dare come to Qingzhou? Then you'll all stay here!" Quan Xiaotian sneered, casually waving his hand. The giant dog phantom roared and charged angrily towards the crowd below.

Shangguan Shenghua, dressed in armor and as a general, stepped forward. A surge of righteous energy emanated from his chest, dispelling the clouds and conjuring a white tiger phantom.

The white tiger roared, its body smaller than the giant dog, yet it still charged forward fearlessly.

But the moment it touched the giant dog, it instantly crumbled, utterly powerless to resist.

"Overestimating yourselves!" Quan Xiaotian sneered.

How could a casual strike from a seventh-tier Unity Realm cultivator be withstood by a mere sixth-tier Yang God Realm cultivator?

"Come at me!" the Third Prince said in a deep voice.

Then, Wu Yuanwu, Cha Xixing, and the other two joined forces. In an instant, swords flashed and spears gleamed. The four of them attacked with all their might, barely managing to withstand the giant hound's onslaught.

"Fight while retreating!" the Third Prince said gravely.

The group began to retreat, but it was too late.

Locked by two seventh-tier demons, their Yang God Realm strength made escape nearly impossible.

The only option was to fight!

Above, a seventh-tier demonic snake still lurked, its cold gaze fixed on the group below. It was a venomous snake, waiting for its chance to strike.

"We can't escape. What do we do now?" Chu Wuliu asked, his heart heavy.

The oppressive aura of the seventh-tier demons covered the entire area. He, a cultivator who had just advanced to the Yang God Realm, simply couldn't withstand it.

However, Bai Liang and the other two were only at the Enlightenment Realm, yet they seemed unaffected.

This surprised Chu Wuliu and the others.

Why could someone at the Enlightenment Realm easily withstand the power of a seventh-tier demon?

Bai Liang didn't explain; it was naturally the effect of the divine talisman within his body.

When the power of the seventh-tier demon pressed down on them, the divine talisman would emit a faint light, neutralizing the demon's power.

"Act according to the situation!" Bai Liang said in a deep voice.

Facing the seventh-tier demon, they also dealt with it with their strength.

On the other side, the four Vajras of Golden Dragon Temple were on guard against the demonic snake, but at this moment, the Third Prince and the others were struggling to withstand Quan Xiaotian's attacks, so he shouted, "Wu Yan, come and help quickly!"

Then he turned back to Bai Liang and the others, shouting, "The seventh-tier demon is yours to handle!"

After shouting this, the Third Prince gritted his teeth and fought back with all his might against Quan Xiaotian's attacks.

As for whether the people from Qianshan County could survive under the seventh-tier demon, he no longer cared.

It was agreed beforehand that each of the seventh-tier demons would restrain one.

If these people from Qianshan County were to die a gruesome death at the hands of the demonic serpent because of their boasting, then their deaths would be considered deserved!

“They’re here!” Upon hearing the plea for help, Wu Yan and the other three abandoned their guard against the demonic serpent, summoned their seventh-tier Maitreya Bell, and rode it towards them.

Feeling the power of the seventh-tier magic weapon, Quan Xiaotian’s expression changed slightly.

“So you had such a trump card. No wonder you dared to come to Qingzhou!” He sneered, but inwardly he became wary.

The power of a seventh-tier magic weapon was comparable to that of a seventh-tier cultivator. Although it couldn’t unleash its full power in the hands of a sixth-tier Yang God Realm cultivator, combined with ten Yang God Realm prodigies, it was enough to pose a threat to him.

However, he wasn’t without help.

"Demon Serpent, it's your turn now!" Quan Xiaotian shouted to the Demon Serpent.

The Demon Serpent began to move, its enormous head spitting out apricots, its eyes icy cold, and it cackled, "Fine, I'll devour these ants first, then I'll help you!"

Its body stirred up the wind and clouds, even obscuring the sky.

It immediately targeted Bai Liang and his six companions. In its eyes, these six were the weakest, utterly defenseless, and easily annihilated.

Chapter 224 The Little Girl Solo Kills a Seventh-Tier Demon King
.

In an instant, the serpent's body coiled around Bai Liang and his companions, trapping them completely.

"Will you walk into my mouth yourselves, or will I tear you apart and swallow you whole?" the demonic serpent sneered.

Its head was enormous, as large as a small hill.

The scales on it weren't normal scales, but rather skull fragments tainted with demonic energy, a truly gruesome sight up close.

The hearts of Chu Wuliu and his companions sank to the bottom.

The terrifying power of a seventh-tier demon king extinguished any will to resist.

But they gritted their teeth, drawing their magical artifacts, determined to fight to the death!

However, seeing the lack of panic on Bai Liang and his companions' faces, they were puzzled.

Could it be... they really had a trump card?

"Xiao Xue," Bai Liang glanced at Li Xue.

Li Xue nodded emphatically; she was ready.

The seventh-tier Unity Realm, calculated based on a cultivator's lifespan, generally lasts around 300 years.

Demons typically live a little longer, but not by much, at most ten or twenty years.

"Don't go too long, let's start with twenty years to test the waters. This demonic serpent might already be over a hundred years old," Wu Ze whispered.

Li Xue nodded, beginning to calm her mind and activate the Divine Life Talisman within her body.

The long-dormant Divine Life Talisman was activated again, now like a ravenous wolf, greedily absorbing Li Xue's life essence.

The little girl's face visibly paled; she could clearly feel the irreversible loss of life within her.

The Divine Fate Talisman was a bottomless pit, greedily absorbing Li Xue's lifespan until it had absorbed a full twenty years of her lifespan, only then reluctantly stopping.

"What are you dawdling for? If you don't respond, I'll tear you to pieces!" the demonic serpent said coldly.

Having finally escaped the Holy Court and arrived in human territory, it harbored the intention of toying with these people, but their delayed response was making it impatient.

"Don't rush, you'll soon know the answer."

Li Xue's voice was weak, but she still managed a smile.

The Divine Fate Talisman, having absorbed enough lifespan, began to emit a dazzling red light. This red light was incredibly scarlet, exuding an eerie and ominous aura, making those who approached it feel extremely uneasy.

The demonic serpent saw the red light and sensed something was wrong.

He actually sensed a faint danger within this red light.

"Stop! What is that?!"

The demonic serpent roared, its tail whipping wildly, attempting to crush Li Xue and the others on the spot.

But it was too late. The lifespan sacrifice of the Divine Fate Talisman, like the laws of heaven and earth, disregarded everything, drawing heavenly fire from the serpent's body and burning its life essence.

Feeling intense pain coursing through its body and its life force rapidly draining away, the demonic serpent was filled with terror.

"No... what the hell is this? A curse? A forbidden technique?"

In an instant, the scales on its skull began to peel away, its skin dulled, and its aura became tainted with death.

This bizarre scene left Chu Wuliu and the other two completely dumbfounded.

In their eyes, the demonic serpent resembled a dying old snake struggling before its demise.

The loss of its life force was truly terrifying!

Sanfu's eyes widened, faintly sensing the Dao of Life and Death Reincarnation within them.

This involved the Great Laws, things only saints could touch.

Yet, all of this was accomplished by a teenage girl.

At this moment, Sanfu's gaze towards Li Xue was filled with disbelief.

"You did it!"

The demonic serpent roared hoarsely, its once cold eyes now blurred with white spots.

But its eyes were fixed on Li Xue.

Now, its only thought was to kill this little girl, to stop the strange situation within its body.

It let out a deafening roar, spewing out a cloud of poisonous mist that swept towards Li Xue and the others.

As the mist passed, trees withered instantly, and the ground turned purplish-black, revealing its extreme toxicity.

Sanfu's expression changed. He immediately stepped forward, planting his staff in the ground and chanting Buddhist scriptures.

Countless golden Sanskrit scriptures appeared out of thin air, forming a large golden bell around the group, protecting them.

In the distance, the Third Prince and his companions, who were fighting Quan Xiaotian, heard the demonic serpent's furious roar.

They all turned to look, only to witness a scene that shocked them.

The once high and mighty seventh-tier demonic serpent was now frail and old, its scales falling off in a flurry, and it had shrunk considerably, clearly on the verge of death.

"How is this possible?" the Third Prince thought in shock!

A seventh-tier demon king, how could it have become like this in an instant? Who did this? Was it those people from Qianshan County?

"Hiss... Such powerful methods, draining the life force of a seventh-tier demon in an instant, somewhat like an evil cultivator!"

Shangguan Shenghua gasped, feeling a sense of unease despite his cultivation of righteous energy.

"They really have something. It seems they weren't lying earlier; Qianshan County does indeed possess strength," Wu Yuan exclaimed in surprise.

The four from Jinlong Temple exchanged glances, all sensing trouble.

"With Qianshan County possessing such methods, retrieving the secret manual will be even more difficult," Wu Shi transmitted telepathically.

Wu Yan replied, "It's alright. Uncle Sanfu has already infiltrated Qianshan County. He probably already has a plan."

Quan Xiaotian, who was waiting for the Demon Serpent to arrive as reinforcements, was terrified upon seeing its condition!

"How is this possible? The Demon Serpent is a late-stage seventh-tier cultivator, stronger than me, yet it has fallen to this state in less than a quarter of an hour!"

Quan Xiaotian was deeply shocked and couldn't understand what methods had transformed the Demon Serpent into this wretched state.

"Wait, Qianshan County... Divine Mountain!?" Upon hearing Wu Yuan's exclamation, Quan Xiaotian finally understood where those people came from.

It was none other than the legendary Divine Mountain, the very force that had killed the sixth-tier Blade Demon, broken the Heavenly Water Pact, and thus forced the imperial court to relinquish Qingzhou.

The demon army's mission was not only to slaughter Qingzhou, but also to nip this variable—the Divine Mountain—in the bud.

But unexpectedly, instead of seeking trouble with the Divine Mountain first, it came knocking on their door.

After spewing out its poisonous mist, the demonic serpent collapsed, its body limp, barely breathing.

It was clear it was nearing its end, with only a sliver of life remaining.

The golden bell constructed by Sanfu using Sanskrit was now being corroded by the poisonous fog. Although the demonic serpent launched its attack when it was on its last legs, it was still beyond the reach of Sanfu, who had just entered the Yang God Realm.

"This humble monk can't hold on any longer! This poisonous fog is extremely potent!" Sanfu gritted his teeth, his bald head covered in sweat.

"We'll help you!"

Bai Liang and the others also used their respective abilities to dispel and resist the poisonous fog.

Chapter 225 It's Not Our Responsibility
.

The poisonous fog was extremely potent; Bai Liang and the others dared not even extend their divine senses, as it could corrode even their spiritual power.

"No, we can't withstand it with just the few of us."

Chu Wuliu gritted his teeth, unleashing all his skills, but even that couldn't dispel the poisonous fog.

A seventh-tier demon is still a seventh-tier demon; even exhausted, it's beyond their ability to contend with.

At this moment, they were relying entirely on Sanfu's Golden Bell Shield for protection, but it seemed it wouldn't last much longer.

"Killing that demonic serpent should solve this poisonous fog."

At this moment, Li Xue, pale and bloodless, spoke with difficulty.

Having just sacrificed 20 years of her lifespan, her body was incredibly weak, feeling an emptiness as if all her strength had been drained.

But the situation was urgent; she couldn't afford to rest and activated the Divine Life Talisman again.

Twenty years was still too short; she couldn't kill the demonic serpent in one fell swoop, only leaving it in a state of utter exhaustion.

Now, adding another five years of lifespan should be enough.

Sacrificing five years would burn through 50 years of the opponent's lifespan, enough to kill the aging demonic serpent.

The Divine Life Scroll was activated once more, its crimson light blooming, shimmering with an ominous glow.

The extremely weakened demonic serpent, seeing the crimson light again, its turbid eyes widened suddenly.

It roared hoarsely, filled with unwilling fury, "No!"

Then it launched an even more ferocious attack, tearing out the last remaining scales from its skull and transforming them into incredibly swift meteors that hurtled towards Li Xue, attempting to stop it all.

But no matter how fast the scales moved, they couldn't outrun the laws of nature.

As Li Xue sacrificed her life, an ominous crimson flame instantly ignited within the demonic serpent's body. These flames weren't fiery red, but rather the color of raw flesh, like fresh blood, a somewhat eerie red.

The ominous crimson flames burned fiercely, instantly incinerating the demonic serpent's remaining life force. Just as it was completely burned away, the demonic serpent's life force ceased to exist.

The countless scales that had been flying towards Li Xue, now deprived of their power, fell from the sky, crashing to the ground.

The demonic serpent's body paused in mid-air for a moment before slamming to the ground with a loud thud, kicking up a cloud of dust.

Li Xue staggered, her face turning even paler, and collapsed to the ground.

Wu Ze quickly helped her up. Fortunately, Li Xue was only temporarily exhausted from consuming too much of her life force and was otherwise unharmed.

“Uncle Wu, I, Xiaoxue, have lived up to your expectations and have not disappointed the Mountain God’s blessing.”

Li Xue forced a beautiful smile and said.

Wu Ze nodded emphatically and said in a deep voice, “Xiaoxue, you did very well. The Mountain God will be very pleased to know. Now, you should rest well, and we will take care of the rest.”

With that, Wu Ze told Li Xue to go behind him to meditate.

As the demonic serpent’s aura dissipated, the poisonous fog, though not completely gone, was much weaker.

Seeing this, Chu Wuliu immediately went all out, using various methods to dispel the poisonous fog.

Finally, with the cooperation of the group, they managed to dispel the poisonous fog.

“Phew.” Hu Zhengqiang exhaled and said, “A seventh-tier demon, terrifying indeed. Just a casual attack when it was on its last legs made it so difficult for us to withstand. We only managed to dispel it after it died.”

Sanfu and the others deeply agreed.

They had thought a sixth-tier Yang God Realm cultivator was powerful enough, but now it seemed insufficient, far from it.

Then, all three turned their gazes to Li Xue, who was meditating. Their expressions varied, but their faces were all filled with shock.

Previously, they hadn't understood why Bai Liang had brought this young girl, Li Xue, along; they only knew she was also a chosen one of the gods.

But their understanding of chosen ones wasn't thorough enough. They assumed that even the most powerful among them were like Bai Liang and Wu Ze, capable of fighting beyond their level.

They hadn't expected her to possess such powerful and bizarre abilities.

A fourth-tier Enlightened God Realm cultivator, instantly killing a seventh-tier demon, and seemingly unharmed.

This was truly terrifying!

The three were incredibly shocked, and their gazes towards Li Xue changed.

Bai Liang and Wu Ze, however, didn't show joy at their victory; instead, their faces were filled with worry.

Bai Liang approached and inquired in a low voice, only to learn that Li Xue had consumed a total of 25 years of her lifespan this time. Adding to the 15 years she had sacrificed previously, Li Xue had already sacrificed 40 years of her lifespan.

"It's alright, Uncle Bai, it's just a few decades of lifespan. I still have over a hundred years left," Li Xue said with a smile.

The lifespan of a Divine Enlightenment Realm cultivator is 180 years. Consuming 40 years leaves 140 years, which does seem like a lot.

"Lifespan depletion is irreversible; even a year is incredibly precious!" Bai Liang sighed.

He didn't say anything to stop Li Xue from using the Divine Life Talisman.

Frankly, it was because their strength was insufficient. If they were strong enough, why would they let Li Xue sacrifice her life in battle?

Simply telling her to stop sacrificing her life is useless; they must work hard to improve their strength so that they are qualified to deal with any crisis, and Li Xue will naturally stop sacrificing her lifespan.

On the other side, the moment the demonic serpent's aura vanished, Quan Xiaotian was already shocked and retreated several hundred meters, distancing himself from the Third Prince and the others.

He was utterly stunned and filled with fear.

A late-stage seventh-tier demon king, fallen mysteriously without even exchanging a single blow, completely lifeless.

This scene was truly terrifying, utterly frightening.

Quan Xiaotian's eyes held deep apprehension as he looked at Li Xue.

But he also noticed a problem: the little girl had become extremely weak after killing the demonic serpent; it seemed that such bizarre methods came at a price.

However, he certainly didn't dare gamble with his life now.

"Fellow Daoists of Divine Mountain, why don't you press your advantage and capture this seventh-tier demon king as well!"

The Third Prince was incredibly excited. He hadn't expected his teammates to be so capable; in less than two exchanges, they had killed a seventh-tier demon king.

It seemed his unparalleled achievement on this trip to Qingzhou was secure!

Upon hearing this, Quan Xiaotian, who had already retreated several hundred meters, changed his expression.

“Humans, don’t go too far! Emperor Chu has already made a decision with my Holy Court: Qingzhou is ceded to the Holy Court. You are now on the Holy Court’s territory!” Quan Xiaotian said, his voice laced with bravado.

“Don’t listen to this monster’s nonsense. Father Emperor only ordered the court to withdraw from Qingzhou; it doesn’t mean he’s abandoning Qingzhou!”

The Third Prince spoke coldly, then urged the group from Shenshan to quickly kill Quan Xiaotian.

“I’m sorry, Your Highness, we will not intervene.”

Bai Liang spoke calmly, saying, "It was already agreed before. Each side would restrain one seventh-tier demon. This seventh-tier demon king belongs to you; it's not under our jurisdiction."

"What?" The Third Prince's expression darkened slightly upon hearing this.

He had forgotten that this agreement had already been made; he had simply been too excited.

"Fellow Daoist, there's only one seventh-tier demon left. How about we join forces to kill it?" the Third Prince continued.
