Chapter 226 A Great Victory
.

"Nothing much," Bai Liang replied coldly.

How could he possibly agree?

The kill of the seventh-tier demonic serpent was entirely due to Li Xue sacrificing her lifespan.

He couldn't possibly agree to let Li Xue continue attacking!

The arrangement had already been made: one side would restrain one seventh-tier demon, so Bai Liang couldn't possibly allow Li Xue to sacrifice her lifespan to help the Third Prince and the others.

He knew that if they didn't have the means to deal with seventh-tier demons, these geniuses of the imperial court wouldn't have come to their aid.

"Uncle Bai, I can still fight."

At this moment, Li Xue used her hands to support herself as she stood up and spoke.

"No!" Bai Liang refused without hesitation.

He wouldn't let Li Xue fight unless absolutely necessary.

The Third Prince, having suffered a setback, looked somewhat uneasy, but he understood that the other side was unlikely to offer assistance.

While their combined forces could easily contend with a seventh-tier demon, killing it was out of the question.

From afar, Quan Xiaotian witnessed this scene and suddenly smiled.

He surmised that these humans seemed to be divided, and that the little girl's methods likely came at a high price, preventing them from acting rashly.

In that case, he was relieved.

His expression darkened, and he immediately sent a message back to the demon army, ordering all sixth-tier demons to leave the city.

He wanted to gamble that the little girl from Shenshan Mountain couldn't kill so many sixth-tier demons.

The demons who had been stationed in Longjiang County had all witnessed the death of the seventh-tier demonic serpent and were all shocked.

But now that Quan Xiaotian had given the order, they had no choice but to grit their teeth and fight.

Of course, some were stubborn and eager to try their luck.

"All of you, charge! Leave all these humans here!"

Looking at the more than twenty sixth-tier demons, Quan Xiaotian spoke in a deep voice.

"Kill!"

The demons roared, their aura overwhelming, and charged directly at the Third Prince and his group.

As for the group in Qianshan County, they weren't stupid. They wouldn't go to their deaths before understanding the little girl's situation.

Quan Xiaotian sat at the rear, watching the group from Qianshan County with extreme vigilance. If the little girl made any move, he... would escape!

"Damn it, they're all attacking this way!" Cha Xixing cursed upon seeing this.

Dozens of sixth-tier demons, many of them powerful, posed a challenge no less significant than facing Quan Xiaotian.

"Form ranks! Fight them!" the Third Prince said in a deep voice.

Now they could only rely on themselves.

Wu Yan and the other three manipulated the seventh-tier Maitreya Bell, casting a defensive domain to protect everyone within.

A Tai Chi futon hung high above their heads, scattering faint rays of light to keep everyone's minds clear and their thoughts sharp, unaffected by mental attacks.

Shangguan Shenghua and Cha Xixing were also displaying their respective techniques.

Only Yun Lanxi, who had been almost invisible, was just going through the motions.

Even the Third Prince couldn't stand it anymore.

"Yun Lanxi! What are you doing? Hurry up and use your innate technique!" the Third Prince roared.

"The time isn't right yet," Yun Lanxi replied calmly.

Upon hearing this, the Third Prince almost vomited blood.

In the end, he could only ignore this madwoman and continue using the Overlord Spear to fight the demons.

"Should we go help?"

Chu Wuliu, watching the imperial prodigies struggling against the demons, suddenly felt a pang of guilt.

"Don't worry about them, they can handle it," Bai Liang said, shaking his head.

Although the Third Prince's group seemed to be struggling, being pushed back step by step, that was because they hadn't yet revealed their trump cards.

Bai Liang didn't believe these people weren't holding back.

"What we need to be wary of is the demon army!"

His gaze swept towards Longjiang County, where the demonic aura soared into the sky, obscuring the heavens.

If this army of 100,000 demons spread throughout Qingzhou, Qingzhou would be plunged into devastation, and the people would suffer annihilation.

Looking back at Quan Xiaotian, this seventh-tier demon king was still watching them intently, probably planning a sneak attack.

Therefore, they had to be extremely vigilant against this seventh-tier demon king.

"Amitabha Buddha, this test can be considered a great victory," Sanfu said slowly.

Killing a seventh-tier demon is a feat that would cause a sensation anywhere.

"They want to leave."

Wu Ze spoke, pointing to the Third Prince and the others.

They fought and retreated, gradually withdrawing over ten miles.

"We retreat too!" Bai Liang immediately ordered.

Thus, the people from Qianshan County, while remaining wary of the seventh-tier demon king, quickly withdrew.

Quan Xiaotian watched the humans retreat, his expression shifting, extremely grim.

He wanted to pursue, but the death of the demon serpent made him wary, and fear crept into his heart.

"We were careless! Lord Qingyuci also misjudged the situation. The strength of the Divine Mountain is not to be underestimated; it has become a major variable!"

Quan Xiaotian's tone was grave. He was already thinking about how to relay this news back to the Holy Court and absolve himself of responsibility, ideally placing all the blame on the Demon Serpent, since the Demon Serpent was dead and there was no way to verify the story.

Bai Liang and his group successfully withdrew from Longjiang County, while the Third Prince and his companions were still struggling.

Especially the demons, seeing that Bai Liang and his group, who had killed the seventh-tier Demon Serpent, were already far away, completely abandoned their guard and launched a frenzied attack on the Third Prince and his group.

This resulted in everyone being wounded and injured.

"They're leaving already? They have no loyalty whatsoever! Truly barbarians!"

Shangguan Shenghua turned around and saw Bai Liang and his group retreating, his tone somewhat displeased.

"Ignore them for now, let's get out of here quickly. We don't want to get entangled, or it will only cause more trouble!" the Third Prince said coldly.

He could already see Quan Xiaotian in the distance, ready to attack.

However, he was still wary of the people from Shenshan, worried about falling into a trap, so he hesitated.

"Fight!"

Shangguan Shenghua and the others gritted their teeth and launched another all-out attack. After successively killing two sixth-tier demons, and taking several blows and spitting out blood, they successfully retreated.

The demons wanted to continue the pursuit, but Quan Xiaotian called them back.

"Don't pursue a desperate enemy, come back!" Quan Xiaotian said coldly.

Hearing the order, the demons reluctantly returned.

In this battle, they lost three sixth-tier demons, while their opponents were only injured.

After Bai Liang and the others retreated, they quickly returned to the pit.

The people in the pit had been waiting anxiously. Seeing someone return, they immediately surrounded them.

"Brother Bai, you're finally back! How did it go?"

Du Tiandao and the other cultivators from Qianshan County stepped forward to inquire.

"A great victory!" Bai Liang said simply.

This immediately drew cheers from the crowd, significantly boosting their morale.

"Why are only you guys back? Where is the Third Prince? Where are they?"

However, the imperial court's prodigies were becoming restless.

They had expected that even if they returned, it would be the Third Prince and his entourage first, or all together.

Why did only a few people from Qianshan County return first?


.
Chapter 227 Yun Lanxi's Doubts
.

"They should be back soon," Chu Wuliu replied simply, offering no further explanation.

The prodigies harbored doubts, but given the current unclear situation, they refrained from commenting.

Zhao Bufan's eyes turned cold. He suddenly stepped forward and sneered, "Could it be that you encountered danger and abandoned His Highness and the others, fleeing on your own?"

These words immediately caused many to change their expressions, their gazes turning hostile towards Bai Liang and the others.

Bai Liang's eyes were icy as he looked at Zhao Bufan, coldly saying, "Are you looking for trouble?"

"Just stating the facts. Am I not allowed to question them?" Zhao Bufan sneered.

The other prodigies who were on good terms with the Zhao family echoed this sentiment, demanding an explanation from Bai Liang and the others.

Faced with this scene, Chu Wuliu frowned, about to speak,

when suddenly a commotion came from afar. Turning around, he saw the Third Prince and his entourage rushing back.

This silenced Zhao Bufan and his group completely.

Zhao Bufan's expression shifted, becoming somewhat grim.

He had originally intended to use this opportunity to pressure the group from Qianshan County, but now it seemed that wouldn't work.

But just as this thought arose, he heard Cha Xixing's disgruntled voice.

"Hmph! Abandoning us and withdrawing, Qianshan County truly has a sense of honor!"

Cha Xixing arrived at the pit, her face extremely displeased, wanting an explanation.

"Before the operation, didn't you already agree? Each of you would deal with your own troubles, without assisting each other," Bai Liang said calmly.

"That's what you said..." Cha Xixing was furious, wanting to refute, but unsure how.

"Enough, don't mention this again," the Third Prince said in a low voice, stopping Cha Xixing from speaking further.

He was also very dissatisfied, especially when he had asked Qianshan County to help kill the seventh-tier demon king, only to be directly refused.

He was the Third Prince, after all; not even this much face was a blow to his dignity.

However, it was difficult to say anything, since he had spoken first: each should deal with the demons individually, and they wouldn't assist if they encountered danger.

Similarly, if they encountered danger, Qianshan County could naturally stand by and do nothing.

It could only be said that his initial judgment was flawed. Seeing the Qianshan County group's weakness, he assumed they were weak and thus proposed not assisting each other.

Who would have thought they were playing dumb, hiding a powerful move that could easily kill even a seventh-tier demon?

He had underestimated them.

With no regrets, Wu Yan and the others looked at the Qianshan County group differently now.

Yun Lanxi, in particular, looked puzzled.

Her eyes, covered by a black cloth, frequently glanced at Bai Liang and the others. She felt as if something was there.

Puzzled, she channeled her inner power, swirling it around her eyes. Instantly, a faint light shone through the black cloth, illuminating Bai Liang and the others.

Then she witnessed a scene she had never seen before in her life.

A divine mountain rose abruptly from the ground, piercing the clouds, its height and width immeasurable.

The mountain was incomparably sacred, majestic, and awe-inspiring, inspiring involuntary reverence and a desire to submit.

Yun Lanxi was utterly horrified; she even saw a scene.

Unknown beings from beyond the heavens poured in through the gap in the sky, and they faced the incomparably sacred mountain.

The divine mountain rose from the earth, its peaks connecting to the heavens, like pillars supporting the entire world.

The final scene was incredibly blurry; all that could be seen was the constant trembling of heaven and earth, the shackles of the universe being obliterated, making it impossible to discern the final outcome or who had won.

Two streams of bloody tears slid down Yun Lanxi's cheeks, but she was oblivious, utterly stunned by the sheer power of the scene.

When she finally came to her senses, she found her eyes burning hot, consumed by raging flames of law that had reduced them to ashes.

This was beyond her limits, damaging her divine eyes, but she felt it no longer mattered.

Because she had seen the future.

A distance separated the two sides, and the prodigies gathered around the Third Prince and his group, inquiring about the demon army.

When they learned that a few people from Qianshan County had annihilated a seventh-tier demon king, they were all shocked.

"How...how is this possible!? Aren't they at the fourth-tier Divine Enlightenment Realm? How could they kill a seventh-tier demon? Are you sure you're not mistaken?"

Some of the prodigies were incredulous, completely unable to believe it.

"Impossible, absolutely impossible!"

Zhao Bufan's expression changed drastically, utterly unable to accept it.

"A gentleman's word is as good as his bond. How could a scholar like myself lie to you?" Shangguan Shenghua said calmly.

Everyone looked at the Third Prince again, wanting to confirm the truth.

The Third Prince nodded, and now all the prodigies were stunned.

Turning their heads to look at the group from Qianshan County, their eyes filled with disbelief.

"Could it be that the rumors about the Divine Mountain are true? Besides deities, who else could possess such bizarre methods?" someone exclaimed.

This reminded everyone that Qianshan County was located at the foot of the Divine Mountain, where the mountain god was worshipped.

Bai Liang and the others also claimed to be favored by the gods, having received the mountain god's protection.

Previously, they had dismissed it as a joke.

But now, the joke had become their own.

Zhao Bufan and Zhao Fuhu's expressions were extremely grim.

They had a blood feud with Wu Ze, one that was irreconcilable.

Now that Wu Ze had risen to power and obtained the opportunity of a god, he would surely seek revenge against the Zhao family.

"This matter must be reported back to the Zhao family, and preparations must be made as soon as possible!" Zhao Fuhu thought coldly.

As for killing Wu Ze in Qingzhou, he had indeed considered it before, but now he had to retract this immature idea.

If the opponent could even kill a seventh-tier demon, what chance did they, at the Yin God Realm, have?

Du Tiandao and the others were equally shocked upon hearing that Li Xue had killed a seventh-tier demon, and were speechless.

Bai Liang simply mentioned it without saying much.

Li Xue's body had almost fully recovered; after all, she hadn't been seriously injured to begin with, only weakened after sacrificing her lifespan.

“We’ve pretty much figured out the situation with the demons, and it’s largely consistent with Kong Wang’s intelligence. They’ve lost a seventh-tier demon, greatly reducing their strength, but that’s enough to hold back the demon army,” Bai Liang said.

This setback will certainly deter the demons from advancing further.

They might have to wait for more demon reinforcements to arrive before continuing their attack on the next human county.

“We can’t be careless. If the demon army disperses, it will be a huge problem for us,” Wu Ze said gravely.

Their biggest disadvantage right now is their lack of manpower.

The demon army numbered a full 100,000; if they were scattered, they would be utterly unable to cope.

At this moment, the Third Prince and his entourage also approached.

This time, Chu Yuan's expression was more serious, as if he were considering Qianshan County as an equal.

"Fellow Daoist Wu, Fellow Daoist Bai, there may have been some unpleasantness earlier; I hope you won't take it to heart," Chu Yuan said with a smile.


.
Chapter 228 Separate Operations
.

Hearing the Third Prince's words, Bai Liang understood that he wanted to ease tensions and cool things down.

The recent events had indeed created a rift between them.

But it wasn't that Bai Liang didn't want to help; if he were strong enough, he wouldn't have retreated first because of a grudge.

The main issue was their trump card: Li Xue's Divine Fate Talisman.

Asking Li Xue to sacrifice her lifespan to help others? Sorry, he wasn't that righteous.

Moreover, he could see that these prodigies had trump cards; dealing with a seventh-tier demon was more than enough, and they wouldn't be in danger.

"It's just a minor grudge, we didn't take it to heart," Bai Liang said with a smile.

"That's good."

The Third Prince nodded, and the relationship between the two sides eased somewhat.

"The demon's setback this time will surely raise its guard, and it might even bring more reinforcements. We must be even more careful," the Third Prince said in a deep voice.

Bai Liang wholeheartedly agreed. The demons' strength lay not in their raw power, but in their inexhaustible numbers, which utterly overwhelmed human cultivators!

"The strongest reinforcements should still be seventh-tier. Most of the demons' eighth-tier experts seem to have gone to the Wilderness, with the remaining few needing to remain at the Holy Court."

Bai Liang considered for a moment before deciding to share this information.

This was intelligence brought by Kong Wang.

"Oh?" The Third Prince and the others were surprised. Shangguan Shenghua couldn't help but say, "The Wilderness is still alive? Such a remote place; I thought it had long been ravaged by the demon army."

The Wilderness was incredibly remote, and even before the demonic chaos, it rarely contacted the Great Chu Dynasty.

The people, culture, and even cultivation system there are different from those of the Great Chu Dynasty.

The barbarians revere strength and excel in physical combat; their powerful warriors essentially overcome all techniques with brute force.

In the past, there was even a saying: never engage in close combat with a cultivator from the barbarians, because you simply cannot win against someone of the same cultivation level.

"It seems the barbarians still possess some strength, barely clinging to life. However, the Demon Sect sent so many eighth-tier experts, probably hoping to wipe them out in one fell swoop. What a pity," the Third Prince sighed, shaking his head.

With the destruction of the barbaric lands, only the Great Chu Dynasty remained of the remaining human race, struggling to survive.

Perhaps some were left behind in more distant regions, but these were at most small tribes, destined to be wiped out sooner or later.

"Hopefully, the wilderness can hold out for a while longer."

Bai Liang sighed.

Previously, he knew very little about the Wilderness; he only learned of it today from the information relayed by Kong Wang.

Who could have imagined that in the incredibly remote borderlands, there was another human force struggling to resist demons?

Although different races, they were all human, on the same side.

"In that sense, the Wilderness has actually helped us. With all the eighth-tier demons gone there, only seventh-tier demons will likely be coming to Qingzhou, significantly reducing the threat," the Third Prince said slowly, his eyes gradually brightening.

If it's only seventh-tier demons, then they still have hope of resisting.

Perhaps they could send a message back to the capital, persuading their father to rescind the decree abandoning Qingzhou and allow powerful court officials to re-enter Qingzhou, thus increasing their chances of successfully resisting the demons.

However, in the end, the Third Prince abandoned this idea.

The ownership of Qingzhou was a matter for those above the semi-saint level, even involving the saints; it wasn't something that could be changed so easily.

"If we back out today, demonic half-saints will come knocking on our door tomorrow."

"While the demonic army dares not act rashly, we must quickly evacuate all the people of Longyun County and prepare for what's to come," Bai Liang said in a deep voice.

"There are still quite a few people remaining in Longyun County. Previously, due to time constraints, we only informed them to evacuate, but most didn't listen, resulting in many deaths in Longjiang County."

"Now that we've arrived in Longyun County, we can force the people to leave."

"This matter is insignificant; dealing with the demonic army is more important."

The Third Prince was unconcerned. This kind of thing was thankless and extremely troublesome. There didn't seem to be many people remaining in Longyun County; even if they all died, it wouldn't have much impact."

Popular support wasn't that important to him.

Instead, cultivation level, merits, and achievements were what would have the greatest impact on his succession.

Seeing the lack of interest from the capital's elite, Bai Liang and his companions frowned.

Seeing that they truly didn't care about the people of Longyun County, they had no choice but to give up trying to persuade them.

"In that case, let's split up. We'll go and relocate the people of Longyun County, and you go and make preparations to deal with the demon army," Bai Liang said simply.

The Third Prince nodded indifferently.

Afterwards, the group from Qianshan County left, leaving only a group of the capital's elite remaining in the pit.

The Third Prince's face darkened. He turned to look at the group, his tone icy, and said, "I'll only ask once: who leaked our identities?!"

His identities being exposed and known to the demons was something he had kept in mind.

This meant there was a traitor among them.

Chu Yuan's first suspicions were the two from the Zhao family.

After all, there had been a conflict earlier, and it was very likely that the two from the Zhao family had leaked the information in retaliation.

Knowing their identities to the demons would trigger a series of troubles.

Yun Lanxi, standing at the edge, remained completely calm, as if nothing had happened to her.

...

Unbeknownst to Bai Liang and the others, the Third Prince was searching for a 'traitor' within the group.

After leaving the pit, he led the group towards the nearest county town.

Originally, they planned to split up to evacuate the people, but considering the extreme danger of Longyun County and the constant threat of encountering demons, they decided it was better to stay together.

Pu County was the largest county town in Longyun County.

It originally had a population of over 500,000; more than 100,000 had already left, but over 300,000 remained.

The group from Qianshan County arrived in Pu County, and their first act was to display the aura of cultivators, instantly shocking the townspeople.

They then mentioned the demon army's invasion of Longyun County, the fall of Longjiang County, and the deaths of all its inhabitants at the hands of demons.

The people were filled with terror upon hearing this.

They too could see the direction of Longjiang County; dark clouds obscured the sun, a gloomy and oppressive sight.

Many who had fled there recounted the horrific scenes of Longjiang County.

Bai Liang spoke, offering to help people relocate to a safer place.

Although most hesitated, under the threat of the demons, they ultimately chose to leave their hometowns where they had lived for decades and embarked on the journey to Liaoyun County.

For every person who wanted to leave, there was someone who didn't.

These were only a small minority, and Bai Liang had no time to waste on such stubborn individuals.

Leave if you want, or wait to die.

There were even those who didn't want to leave, spreading rumors and warning others not to be deceived, not to go to Liaoyun County, claiming these people were sent by demons to deliver everyone into their clutches.

As for these troublemakers, Bai Liang simply ordered Du Tiandao to kill them.


.
Chapter 229 Mutation
.

After a long and arduous journey through several counties, Bai Liang finally managed to relocate most of the people of Longyun County.

The remaining small number, stubborn and unwilling to leave, were simply ignored by Bai Liang.

"Finally, everyone's been sent out. But with so many people, can Liaoyun County handle it?" Wu Ze sighed, then asked with some worry.

Liaoyun County is already overcrowded, almost bursting at the seams.

People from all over Qingzhou are flocking to Liaoyun County. The population was already very dense, and now with nearly 800,000 more being relocated, the pressure Liaoyun County will face is unimaginable.

"It should be fine. Most people brought their possessions with them to Liaoyun County, so there won't be a food shortage problem in the short term."

Bai Liang wasn't worried. These days, fleeing famine had become a skill; everyone knew how to survive.

The real headache was the management issue.

"We can only hope that Xiao Nian's management team grows up quickly, otherwise they really won't be able to keep up with the development of Qianshan County."

"The demons haven't made any moves these past few days. The blood energy domain has dissipated, but they're still staying in Longjiang County, probably planning something," Chu Wuliu said in a deep voice.

Although they've been busy relocating the people these past few days, they've been paying close attention to the movements of the demon army.

Generally speaking, once the blood energy domain dissipates, the demons will move again, seeking out the next human settlement to slaughter and form a new blood energy domain.

But now the demon army hasn't done that; instead, they remain motionless even after the blood energy domain dissipated.

"They're probably waiting for the arrival of a powerful demon. We can't be careless."

Bai Liang's expression was solemn. Li Xue's actions were both a deterrent and aroused the demons' vigilance.

Even a seventh-tier demon was easily killed, so the demons certainly won't act rashly; they're probably waiting for a stronger demon to arrive.

“Hopefully it’s not an eighth-tier demon,” Wu Ze said.

The expressions of the others were all solemn.

Bai Liang had explained that Li Xue’s methods could only cross three major realms, and at a great cost.

Therefore, if there were an eighth-tier demon, Li Xue’s methods would be useless.

“It shouldn’t be. There aren’t many eighth-tier Venerable Realm demons,” Bai Liang said.

If an eighth-tier demon really came to Qingzhou, they could only retreat to the Divine Mountain and rely on its power for self-preservation.

“Any news from Kong Wang?” Wu Ze suddenly asked.

Bai Liang shook his head: “Not yet. He’s going deeper and deeper, and now we can’t contact him with the Mountain Token.”

As he spoke, his expression was somewhat worried.

Kong Wang’s last message mentioned that he had discovered restlessness among the demons, seemingly indicating an anomaly in the Wilderness.

Therefore, Kong Wang wanted to penetrate deep into the demon territory, even crossing over to the battlefield between the demons and the Wilderness to investigate.

The distance here is unknown, perhaps tens of thousands of miles, and the mountain token has long since lost its effect.

"May the Mountain God protect us." Wu Ze could only pray silently for Kong Wang.

Venturing deep into the demon realm is fraught with peril, far too dangerous.

"By the way, the Third Prince wants us to join them again on the journey to Longjiang County to jointly kill that seventh-tier demon king," Hu Zhengqiang said.

The Third Prince's side had been searching for traitors for several days, but ultimately found nothing.

There were suspects, but no concrete evidence, so they hadn't expelled them for the time being.

"Just refuse them," Bai Liang said without hesitation.

He knew all too well what the Third Prince and his men were thinking.

They simply wanted to use Li Xue again to kill the seventh-tier demon king.

"Hmph, they have a good idea, wanting to reap the benefits without lifting a finger?" Chu Wuliu sneered.

Ever since he learned the price Li Xue had to pay, he understood why Bai Liang had always said that Li Xue shouldn't be allowed to act unless absolutely necessary.

This was fighting with one's lifespan at stake; no one could withstand that.

"I heard the Third Prince has already sent the battle reports from a few days ago back to the capital, drawing praise from the court officials and the Emperor," Sanfu said calmly.

"The Third Prince came with ulterior motives; his heart isn't with the people of Qingzhou."

Chu Wuliu spoke, his face filled with disdain.

In his battle report, the Third Prince shamelessly claimed that he led the group to annihilate the seventh-tier demon king.

It was utter boasting. It was only because the people of Qianshan County didn't care about the opinions of the capital that he, as the governor of Baijiang Prefecture, would have submitted a memorial exposing the Third Prince's 'merit'.

"Ignore it. As long as they are resisting the demon army, that's enough," Bai Liang said slowly.

He didn't expect the geniuses of the capital to turn the tide or make any significant contributions; sharing some of the pressure would be sufficient.

Meanwhile.

In Longjiang County.

The blood energy domain dissipated, and the sky in the county returned to normal, but it remained dark and oppressively gloomy.

The once bustling county town was now a scene of ruins, its streets littered with corpses—a veritable hell on earth.

But to the demons, this was perfectly normal.

However, the dissipation of the blood energy domain and the Demon King's delay in ordering the army to move out again were causing restlessness among the demons.

In the central area, within a well-preserved mansion,

Quan Xiaotian's face was grim as he looked at the black command arrow in his hand, his divine sense having already read its information.

"Demon King, what is the situation? When will the Holy Court send powerful warriors?"

Below, a sixth-tier demon mustered his courage and asked.

Since witnessing the death of the seventh-tier Demon King a few days ago, the morale of these demons had been considerably dampened.

It was only after the pursuit of the Third Prince and his group, the blood and gore they had mustered, that they had somewhat recovered.

But they remained extremely wary of the methods of the humans in Thousand Mountains County.

Quan Xiaotian felt the same way.

Therefore, upon returning to Longjiang County, he immediately sent a message back to the Holy Court.

He reported the entire situation, naturally shifting all blame onto the Demon Serpent, claiming it was the Demon Serpent who had caused him such misery.

He also emphasized the despicable nature of the human race, who had disregarded the restrictions of the Heavenly Water Pact, openly sending a sixth-tier Yang God Realm expert to Qingzhou, even a prince.

Therefore, he implored the Holy Court to send more powerful experts to teach the human race a painful lesson, perhaps even using this as a pretext to annex half of the human race's territory.

After sending the message back to the Holy Court, Quan Xiaotian waited patiently for their response.

However, several days passed.

He assumed the Holy Court had received the message, become enraged, and prepared even more powerful experts and a stronger army, thus causing the delay.

Only after receiving the message from the Holy Court today did he realize he had been overthinking things.

"Stronger my ass!" Quan Xiaotian's face darkened as he slammed the black token to the ground, his demonic aura erupting as if suppressing his rage!

The demons below witnessed this and were all shocked.

What's going on? How could the Demon King be so enraged? Could it be that the Holy Court didn't send any powerful figures?


.
Chapter 230 The Time of Extreme Yin
.

"Demon...Demon King, what exactly is going on?"

Finally, a sixth-tier demon stepped forward and asked, steeling himself.

Quan Xiaotian's face was ashen, as if it were about to drip water, but he wouldn't take his anger out on his subordinates.

He coldly said, "Something has happened in the Wilderness. Lord Qingyuci has left the Holy Court and personally gone to the Wilderness, taking with him the few remaining eighth-tier Venerables. Even the Holy Venerable is paying attention."

"What!?"

Quan Xiaotian's words immediately caused the demons present to change their expressions drastically, eliciting gasps of shock.

"How is this possible? The Wilderness is weaker than Great Chu. Isn't it just a crippled Half-Saint struggling to hold on? How could it attract a Half-Saint to go there personally?"

"Seven eighth-tier Venerables went to the Wilderness before, and even that couldn't suppress that Barbarian Half-Saint?"

The demons all gasped. In their minds, the Wilderness was a level weaker than Great Chu.

In terms of the number of demigods, the Great Chu Dynasty has three, while the Barbarian Realm only has one.

Furthermore, the Great Chu Dynasty enjoys immense protection from its abundant fortune, while the Barbarian Realm does not. This is because the Barbarian Realm has never been unified; it consists entirely of scattered, self-contained tribes.

Only in recent years, under the influence of demons, has it slowly begun to band together and unite.

The Holy Court has not paid much attention to the Barbarian Realm, failing to annihilate it completely, instead intending to nurture it as food.

The Holy Court has always focused on the Great Chu Dynasty.

Unexpectedly, under this neglect, the Barbarian Realm has secretly risen to power.

This is also the reason for Quan Xiaotian's grim expression.

Almost all of the Holy Court's eighth-tier experts have gone to the Barbarian Realm, even the demon demigods have gone.

This means the Holy Court cannot send powerful reinforcements to Qingzhou; they can't very well summon the demon demigods who are in seclusion, can they?

As for the two Holy Venerables, they were untouchable. In the entire Holy Court, only Half-Saints could see them.

Therefore, the Holy Court's response was that they could send seventh-tier demons to assist, but eighth-tier demons were not allowed.

“The Wilderness has variables, but doesn't Qingzhou have them too!?”

Quan Xiaotian said, suppressing his anger.

In his view, the threat from the Divine Mountain was far greater than that of the Half-Saint in the Wilderness.

He had witnessed the methods of those people in Qianshan County; they were incredibly bizarre and beyond reason.

This meant that the mountain god mentioned in the rumors was very likely real.

A god still alive in the world!

If this wasn't the biggest variable, then what was?

But the Holy Court clearly didn't care about the Divine Mountain he mentioned. Perhaps they would have cared before the changes in the Wilderness, but now there were more pressing variables, and the issue of the Divine Mountain naturally took a backseat.

“Demon King, what do we do now?”

The other demons were instantly at a loss.

"What to do? Just let it be!" Quan Xiaotian's tone was icy. After his anger subsided considerably, he said in a deep voice, "The Holy Court will send three more seventh-tier experts. If that still can't resolve the problem with Divine Mountain, then we'll have to temporarily withdraw our troops and leave Qingzhou!"

"We'll deal with the Qingzhou problem after we've resolved things in the Wilderness!"

This was the Holy Court's response.

After reading it, Quan Xiaotian felt somewhat helpless.

What use would three more seventh-tier experts be?

He had a strong feeling that even this wouldn't solve the trouble the demon army was facing.

Thinking back to when he first entered Qingzhou, he chose Longyun County as his first stop, instead of Liaoyun County.

This made him feel fortunate. If he had directly attacked Liaoyun County, the main base of the Divine Mountain, his current situation would likely be much worse.

"That's settled then. Those three seventh-tier experts will arrive tomorrow. We'll launch the main force then," Quan Xiaotian said calmly.

.............

In a corner of Qingzhou.

The Nianmo, who hadn't shown herself for a long time, was now pale with fright.

She had fled from Longyun County and, without stopping, returned to her original hiding place.

She had thought the arrival of the demon army would make Qianshan County a problem.

But unexpectedly, she was just being sent to her death.

"This is terrifying! What exactly is this sacred mountain? Even its subordinates are so strange."

Nianmo was still shaken. Ever since she was slapped near the sacred mountain, she had become very timid.

After all, she was naturally cautious, so her timidity was understandable.

She dared not return to the demon army; as a sixth-tier demon general, she would only become cannon fodder in the battle.

Then, she wouldn't be able to escape.

"It seems I can only return through Baijiang Prefecture." Nianmo looked towards Baijiang Prefecture again.

That's how she entered Qingzhou.

However, she had a lingering fear of the sacred mountain, so she had never dared to approach it again.

But recently, she discovered that as long as she didn't get too close to the sacred mountain, within a hundred miles, she would generally not attract the attention of the mountain god.

"As long as I avoid the sacred mountain, maintaining a distance of a hundred miles—no, at least two hundred miles—I should be able to avoid being discovered."

"Illusion Demon muttered to herself."

She had already made up her mind: upon returning to the border, she would head directly to the Wilderness Battlefield.

Compared to Qingzhou, the Wilderness shouldn't be too dangerous, right?

Thinking this, she acted immediately, cautiously approaching Liaoyun County.

Divine Mountain, Qianshan County.

Li Yue withdrew his divine sense from Wu Ze's body.

He had witnessed everything that had happened these past few days.

To be honest, the demon army was indeed powerful, but they were frightened by Li Xue's methods and dared not make any rash moves.

“The demons have a constant supply of low-level combat power, while the human race has very few. No wonder they are at a disadvantage.”

Li Yue shook his head and thought.

Look at their demon armies, numbering in the hundreds of thousands at a time.

On the other hand, a human army of ten thousand cultivators is considered a large army.

It just goes to show how difficult cultivation is for humans, with very few possessing innate talent, unlike demons who are born as first-tier demons, or even beasts that have transformed into demons.

Humans' advantage in this regard is far too weak.

Lacking in numbers, they can only rely on quality to win.

This is how humans used to suppress demons in the past, using their top-tier combat power to suppress them and limit their numbers.

"We still need human saints," Li Yue thought to himself.

Putting these thoughts aside, night had fallen in Qianshan County.

Li Yue's divine sense gazed at the sky; a full moon hung high, its soft light illuminating the earth, yet containing wisps of yin energy.

"The extreme yin time!"

Li Yue's eyes flickered slightly; he noticed the moon tonight looked different from usual.

Meanwhile, in Qianshan County, the three members of the Qilin Clan, who had waited for this moment for countless years, were filled with immense excitement.

"It's finally here! The extreme Yin time, the creation of the Full Moon Yin God, begins tonight!" Qilin exclaimed excitedly.

"I've waited so long for this moment!"

The Black Dragon suppressed his joy, looking up at the moon, his eyes gleaming.


.
Chapter 231 Magical Artifact, Purple-Gold Gourd
.

The moon, bathed in a faint white light, illuminated the earth.

This made tonight's moonlight even brighter than usual.

Not only were Qilin and his companions waiting for this moment, but also those at the peak of the Enlightenment Realm across the capital and various states were filled with excitement and anticipation.

Including Wu Ze, who was in Longyun County.

"After suppressing my cultivation for so long, I can finally break through!" Wu Ze looked up at the moon and let out a heavy sigh.

Months ago, he could have broken through to the Yin God Realm.

However, for the sake of this Full Moon Yin God Realm, he had to forcibly suppress his cultivation, causing his progress to be slow.

"Congratulations, Brother Wu Ze, on your successful breakthrough tonight! You should be able to reach the mid-stage of the Yin God Realm!" Bai Liang said sincerely with joy.

He himself was currently only at the late stage of the Enlightenment Realm, still a short distance from a breakthrough.

Therefore, he had given up on the Full Moon Yin God Realm and planned to achieve the Heavenly Eclipse Yang God Realm when he broke through to the Yang God Realm.

"Breaking through now is perfect for achieving the Full Moon Yin Godhood. Fellow Daoist Wu has chosen an excellent time!" Chu Wuliu couldn't help but exclaim.

Whether one can achieve the Full Moon Yin Godhood doesn't depend on talent, but rather on fate.

Only those who are at the peak of the Enlightenment Realm and happen to encounter the extreme Yin energy, or those who are only a year or two away from achieving it, can forcibly suppress their cultivation level and delay until the opportune moment arrives.

As for those who are more than three years away, forget about it.

Firstly, their cultivation level cannot be suppressed for that long, and secondly, delaying it for too long would be counterproductive, doing more harm than good.

"We will protect you!" Hu Zhengqiang said forcefully.

Wu Ze nodded, went to a mountaintop, sat cross-legged, and faced the moon.

He began to calm his mind and concentrate, mobilizing his internal power, sensing the countless rays of moonlight falling from the sky with his divine sense, and began to absorb them into his body.

The moonlight entered his body, strengthening his primordial spirit and adding to his extreme Yin energy.

Watching Wu Ze silently absorb the moonlight, Bai Liang and the others positioned themselves around him to protect him.

Qianshan County.

The three Qilin were also entering a breakthrough state, protected by He Liyuan and several Yin God Realm cultivators who remained behind.

Li Yue witnessed this scene from above using his divine sense.

He watched as countless beams of moonlight fell, carrying an immense and overwhelming amount of extreme Yin energy that covered the entire world.

"Indeed, it's a treasure. No wonder someone would suppress their cultivation level for the Full Moon Yin God Realm, waiting for this moment," Li Yue thought to himself.

He tried to use his divine sense to touch the extreme Yin moonlight, hoping to capture it.

But as expected, he couldn't capture it at all.

The extreme Yin moonlight was formless and intangible; unless enveloped by divine sense, it would dissipate completely once a gap was opened.

Li Yue then tried to contain the extreme Yin moonlight in an ordinary container, but as soon as he released his divine sense, the extreme Yin moonlight would quickly vanish.

"What a pity, such good things can't be kept."

Li Yue sighed inwardly.

Looking down at the three Qilin beings breaking through, they had already absorbed more than half of the moonlight. Once fully absorbed, they would successfully break through to the Yin God Realm.

Then, his divine sense was directed towards Longyun County in the distance, where he saw Wu Ze also breaking through.

"This breakthrough will unlock a new magical artifact."

Li Yue suddenly felt a surge of anticipation, wondering what kind of artifact it would be.

So his divine sense waited quietly.

Wu Ze absorbed moonlight, continuously drawing it into his body. His primordial spirit was being constantly transformed by the extremely Yin moonlight, becoming increasingly powerful and refined.

His accumulated foundation was substantial. Having absorbed enough moonlight, he instantly crossed the threshold, shattering the barrier to his cultivation realm.

Countless bursts of extremely Yin energy erupted from his body, then coalesced again, being absorbed back into his body.

A different kind of Yin divine aura appeared on Wu Ze's body.

He stood up, a glint of light flashing in his eyes as he opened them. His face was filled with excitement, eager to release his Yin divine spirit from his body.

Instantly, an extremely refined Yin divine spirit emerged from his body, freely traversing the air, bathed in moonlight, utterly sacred.

"It's done!" Bai Liang exclaimed with joy upon seeing this.

Chu Wuliu and the others looked up and couldn't help but exclaim, "As expected of a Full Moon Yin Spirit, its refinement and purity far surpass ordinary Yin Spirits!"

Wu Ze was very excited, so he asked Bian Nong to summon his Yin Spirit for comparison.

Bian Nong was speechless, but ultimately his Yin Spirit left his body to allow everyone to see the difference.

Wu Ze's Full Moon Yin Spirit was incredibly refined, shimmering faintly in the moonlight, looking just like a real body.

Bian Nong's ordinary Yin Spirit, on the other hand, was quite ordinary, lacking the refinement of a real phantom, and didn't emit any faint glow.

"Is this alright?"

Bian Nong withdrew his Yin Spirit; in comparison, his own Yin Spirit was far inferior.

"It's fine, thank you, Brother Bian Nong!" Wu Ze expressed his gratitude.

Then his spirit returned to his body and flew down from the small hill.

"Congratulations, Fellow Daoist Wu, on achieving the Full Moon Yin Spirit. Your future achievements will surely surpass those of us old fogies," Chu Wuliu congratulated him.

"Thank you all for protecting me."

Uze also expressed his gratitude.

At this moment, Li Yue saw a change on his interface after witnessing Wu Ze achieve the Yin God Realm.

[The recipient has broken through to the Yin God Realm for the first time, obtaining the magic artifact *Purple Gold Gourd.]

[Purple Gold Gourd: A gourd that grows from a mysterious vine, born a supreme treasure, containing a world within it, capable of holding all things. After use, it can store items, including living beings, into its internal space. The size of the space and the level of the target increase according to the caster's own strength.]

"A gourd!?"

Seeing the information displayed on the panel, Li Yue was somewhat surprised.

He hadn't expected it to be a spatial magic artifact, and it seemed quite powerful, capable of being used on living beings.

With a thought, Li Yue's divine sense returned to the divine mountain, and he took out the Purple-Gold Gourd, examining it closely.

It was a gourd entirely purple, but with many golden patterns swirling around it.

He tapped it with his divine sense and discovered it wasn't made of metal, but of wood—a genuine gourd.

"It seems to be a truly natural treasure," Li Yue thought.

He then checked the size of the Purple-Gold Gourd's space and found it to be very large, roughly the size of a thousand-mountain county.

This space was more than enough.

Li Yue tried using the Purple-Gold Gourd to absorb items, his gaze settling on a pile of huge rocks on the back mountain.

He positioned the mouth of the purple-gold gourd towards the boulders, then gently activated it. Instantly, the gourd emitted an incredibly powerful suction force, drawing all the boulders into its body in the blink of an eye.

"Not bad, similar to the purple-gold gourd from Journey to the West, except I don't need to chant any incantations."

Li Yue was very satisfied with the gourd.

Chapter 232 Capturing Moonlight
.

After trying it on inanimate objects, Li Yue wanted to test its effect on living creatures.

However, almost all the demons in the vicinity had been extinct, and it wouldn't work on humans, so he could only use it on wild animals.

Many wild animals lived in the mountains and forests. Li Yue randomly found a silly roe deer, then activated the Purple Gold Gourd, directly sucking the deer inside.

The poor roe deer didn't even know what was happening before it was sucked in.

Li Yue probed the inside of the Purple Gold Gourd with his divine sense and saw the silly roe deer inside.

The silly roe deer lived up to its name; after being sucked into the Purple Gold Gourd, it didn't panic, but instead showed a foolish expression. Its large eyes were incredibly clear; after blinking, it looked around.

After finding no danger, it began chewing the tender grass it had just been holding in its mouth, eating with great relish.

After observing the silly roe deer's condition, Li Yue discovered that it wasn't affected by being inside the Purple-Gold Gourd.

"This silly roe deer."

Li Yue looked at the roe deer's foolish appearance and then released it.

The freed roe deer was still dazed, looking around with a hint of confusion in its clear, foolish eyes.

What just happened?

It couldn't figure it out. Well, what's done is done. It might as well eat first.

So the silly roe deer happily chewed on the tender grass in its mouth.

"What a silly roe deer."

Li Yue withdrew his gaze and tried the Purple-Gold Gourd a few more times, with results that satisfied him greatly.

If the space inside were a little larger, it would be a small world.

"Right, can this thing capture the extreme Yin moonlight?"

Li Yue suddenly thought of something, and a thought struck him.

He turned to look at the moon hanging high in the sky, which was still continuously showering down extreme Yin moonlight.

He glanced again at Qianshan County, confirming that the three members of the Qilin Clan had successfully broken through, and that absorbing the extreme Yin moonlight now would not affect them.

So Li Yue began, activating the Purple-Gold Gourd and drawing in the moonlight.

"Whoosh!"

The extremely strong suction created a powerful wind, drawing countless beams of moonlight towards the gourd's opening, only to be sucked inside.

Li Yue only absorbed a small amount first, then observed what changes would occur to the moonlight inside the Purple-Gold Gourd.

Unexpectedly, the extreme Yin moonlight, once absorbed into the Purple-Gold Gourd, did not dissipate.

After waiting a while longer, the moonlight still showed no sign of dissipating.

Seeing this, Li Yue smiled.

The Purple-Gold Gourd could indeed absorb extreme Yin moonlight and preserve it.

Even in times other than extreme Yin, he could still use the extreme Yin moonlight within the Purple-Gold Gourd to achieve the Full Moon Yin Godhood.

Thinking this, Li Yue released the Purple-Gold Gourd, suspending it high in the air and pointing it towards the moon, then began to suck in the air!

This time, he used all his strength, the suction even more powerful than before, instantly creating a fierce wind.

Even the residents of Qianshan County felt it; thinking it was about to rain, they quickly closed their doors and windows.

Countless rays of extremely yin moonlight surged wildly into the Purple-Gold Gourd, even dimming the moon itself.

The three members of the Qilin Clan, who had just broken through to the Yin God Realm, hadn't even had time to rejoice before sensing this change.

"Huh, why is this moonlight acting restless?" Qilin looked up, somewhat suspicious.

"I don't know, maybe it's about to disappear."

The black dragon shook its head, unconcerned. They had already achieved the Full Moon Yin God realm.

Even if the moonlight disappeared now, it wouldn't affect them.

"Maybe?" The Qilin checked for a while, finding no significant movement, and having already successfully broken through, it ignored it.

The Purple-Gold Gourd absorbed the moonlight for over an hour before it was completely full.

Li Yue retrieved the Purple-Gold Gourd and probed it with his divine sense. He saw it filled with incredibly dense, extremely Yin moonlight, so much of it that it completely filled the space.

Based on the amount of moonlight Wu Ze used for his breakthrough, the extremely Yin moonlight inside the Purple-Gold Gourd was enough for at least thirty or forty people to achieve the Full Moon Yin God realm.

"Not bad, enough is enough." Li Yue put the Purple-Gold Gourd away with satisfaction.

This extremely Yin moonlight could be preserved. Later, when his subordinates wanted to break through to the Yin God realm, they could enter the Purple-Gold Gourd and absorb the moonlight to break through.

Li Yue looked at the panel.

[Realm: Fifth-Order Sacred Mountain]

[Merit: 1.65 million/100 million]

[Divine Abilities: Mountain God's Blessing, Dharma Body of Heaven and Earth, Rebirth of Incense, Gratitude for Water, Blessing the Earth]

[Spells: Manifestation, Control of the Five Elements]

[Magic Weapons: Mountain-Suppressing Seal, Heart Mirror, Divine Weapon Furnace, Wind-Stabilizing Pearl, Purple-Gold Gourd]

The number of magic weapons has now reached five.

Merit points have increased relatively slowly recently, only a little over one million.

However, this is normal; usually, after a surge, it enters a stable period.

He needs to quickly assimilate the newly arrived Qingzhou people to gain more merit points.

Li Yue looked down; Qianshan County now accommodates over two hundred thousand people and is still expanding.

Outside the county seat, the population is rather alarming.

A rough estimate puts the number at seven or eight hundred thousand, all of whom have traveled thousands of miles to Liaoyun County recently.

Some have been absorbed by counties like Tianyue County, but the majority remain concentrated near Qianshan County, hoping to enter the county seat.

This number is already staggering. From Longyun County, and including people arriving from various other places, a massive influx is on its way.

The total population will eventually reach several million, putting immense pressure on Qianshan County.

Given Qianshan County's current makeshift administration, it's unlikely they can manage such a large population.

"Developing too fast isn't necessarily a good thing," Li Yue thought to himself.

However, he didn't want to concern himself with this, nor was he inclined to.

He'd leave it to his subordinates.

"Hmm?"

Suddenly, Li Yue detected a demon intruding within his divine sense range, approximately 250 li from the sacred mountain.

His divine sense instantly reached the demon's location, recognizing it at a glance.

"It's that guy again?"

Li Yue was somewhat surprised. Wasn't this the sixth-tier demon who had been slapped by him last time and then escaped?

It seemed she was with the sixth-tier Blade Demon, coming from Baijiang Prefecture.

"What's she doing? Trying to sneak back to Baijiang Prefecture?"

Li Yue immediately saw through Nianmo's intentions, noticing her furtive movements, concealing her presence, and even hiding her aura.

And she even went to great lengths, venturing into a ravine 250 li from the sacred mountain, hoping to use this route to enter Baijiang Prefecture?

"She has some brains, but not many," Li Yue thought to himself.

Below, Nianmo's heart pounded, her senses racing.

Her expression instantly changed; her intuition told her of impending danger, a feeling identical to when she encountered the mountain god!

Instantly, despair washed over her.

Even though she was already far from the sacred mountain, she still felt 200 li wasn't enough, deliberately adding another 50 li.

And yet, she was still discovered!

Chapter 233 A Wild Monster
.

Despair gripped her heart, and she longed to give up, but the will to survive compelled her to unleash her full power, attempting to escape once more.

“Since you’re already here, stay!”

Li Yue immediately summoned his Purple-Gold Gourd. The gourd instantly expanded, a powerful suction force pulling Nianmo inside.

Nianmo froze, a flicker of panic crossing her beautiful face. She cried out, “Mountain God, this lowly demon meant no harm! I was merely passing through and wished to return to my homeland. I promise I will never return to Great Chu! Please, Mountain God, spare my life!”

However, Li Yue ignored her.

Although this female demon didn't seem to have caused any bloodshed in Qingzhou, she was undeniably one of the main perpetrators of the demonic calamity in Baijiang Prefecture.

Nianmo continued to beg for mercy, even shamelessly displaying her alluring figure, resorting to such tactics.

But alas, it was all in vain.

Li Yue activated the Purple-Gold Gourd, instantly sucking her inside.

The Demon of Thought didn't even have time to scream before being drawn into the gourd.

When she opened her eyes, she saw a hazy space filled with countless beams of moonlight—and this moonlight was extremely Yin moonlight.

The Demon of Thought was utterly shocked. She recognized extremely Yin moonlight; it was the key to human cultivators achieving the status of a Full Moon Yin God.

It had never been able to be preserved since ancient times; even a half-saint lacked the means to intercept it.

But this mountain god of the sacred mountain had done it.

And it was an entire area filled with extremely Yin moonlight—how much of that was?

The Demon of Thought was so shocked that she briefly forgot her predicament.

When she came to her senses, she realized she was imprisoned.

She was helpless, with no one to turn to.

Utter despair filled her heart, and she became utterly distraught.

Li Yue shook the purple-gold gourd, but hearing no response, he put it away.

He wasn't worried that the demonic thought would damage the Yin Moonlight.

Because it couldn't be damaged at all. The Yin Moonlight was an ethereal thing, only absorbable by those breaking through to the peak of the Enlightenment Realm.

Others were useless and couldn't absorb or destroy it.

"Let's see how long the demon can stay in the gourd,"

Li Yue thought to himself.

If it could stay in the purple-gold gourd for a long time, then he could keep the demon for his subordinates to train later.

Haven't he collected so much Yin Moonlight?

If he wanted the moonlight to achieve the Full Moon Yin Godhood, that was fine!

He could go in and get it himself, but to get the moonlight, he had to defeat this sixth-tier demonic thought first.

Of course, a sixth-tier demon was too strong; he could consider suppressing it to fifth-tier strength later.

He'd place the Purple Gold Gourd halfway up the mountain, and those who desired the Full Moon Yin Spirit could only obtain it through their own strength; those who lacked the power could simply withdraw.

Li Yue thought his idea was excellent, saving him the trouble of bestowing moonlight upon each person one by one.

His attention returned to Qianshan County.

Looking at Qianshan County's development, it was nothing short of astonishing.

With a large population, the pace of development accelerated.

Xiao Nian, leading a group, had already begun planning a new county town nearby.

Right next to Qianshan County, it would be used to accommodate the constant influx of new refugees.

“Building a new county town is a massive project, not something that can be completed overnight.”

Xiao Nian spoke in a deep voice, his gaze sweeping over his colleagues.

The new county town was to be located south of Qianshan County, also adjacent to the sacred mountain.

The location was good, with rivers and forests, allowing for the use of local materials to build new houses.

However, Qianshan County's production of blue bricks had now increased, enough to supply the needs.

The most important element in building a new county town was the mountain god temple, a point Xiao Nian had never forgotten.

Qianshan County was also expanding, with each expansion involving the construction of a mountain god temple and the dispatch of a temple keeper to oversee it.

Now, Qianshan County had dozens of mountain god temples.

Countless people worshipped and offered sacrifices daily; previously, the thresholds of the mountain god temples had to be replaced every one or two months.

Later, they were replaced with bluestone thresholds, which proved much more durable.

The construction of the Mountain God Temple was of great importance, so Xiao Nian sought out He Liyuan to discuss it.

After all, it was the main temple of the new county town!

“What are your ideas?” He Liyuan asked after listening to Xiao Nian’s words.

Xiao Nian pondered for a moment, then replied, “I want to build it more magnificent and grander, so that it will be worthy of the Mountain God’s illustrious name.”

He produced blueprints, which depicted a temple complex covering nearly a hundred acres.

He Liyuan looked at it and shook his head.

“No, putting aside whether it’s appropriate, this scale already exceeds that of the main temple.”

“Then the main temple should also be expanded, made even more majestic and magnificent.”

Xiao Nian clearly had a backup plan, producing an even larger set of blueprints.

He Liyuan shook his head, solemnly saying, "Xiao Nian, formalism is unacceptable. We are loyal to the Mountain God, so as long as we are sincere, the Mountain God will naturally protect us. Back then, the temple was just a thatched hut."

"But if your heart isn't sincere, even if you build a temple higher than the sky, it won't help."

He Liyuan picked up two blueprints, frowning, "Your proposed new temple is too large. Not only is it a waste of resources and manpower, but Qianshan County's current finances can hardly support it."

"Instead of building such a large temple, it would be better to build more, so that people can worship the Mountain God every day and offer incense."

Xiao Nian listened attentively, then bowed his head, saying, "Thank you, Brother He, I have learned much."

"Hmm." He Liyuan nodded, then smiled, "However, your proposed new temple is very grand. You can keep the blueprints for now; if development progresses further, you can build it according to your ideas."

Xiao Nian nodded and solemnly put the blueprints away.

He realized he had indeed been too hasty.

While Qianshan County's development was rapid, its foundation was still insufficient.

If 80% of its productivity and financial resources were devoted to building a new temple now, development would stagnate.

In that case, building more temples might be more effective.

Understanding this, Xiao Nian bid farewell to He Liyuan and went to arrange the construction of the new county town.

As for the name of the new county town, that would have to be discussed together after Bai Liang and Wu Ze returned.

Liaoyun County border.

A rustling sound echoed through the forest, and after a while, a group of people appeared, all clad in black robes, their disguises adding to the mystery.

"Your Highness, the demon army has suffered a crushing defeat. Should we still circle around to their rear and ambush them?" one of them asked, his voice tinged with worry.

"Of course we should. We have to maintain the appearance."

Nangong Hu replied calmly, her gaze fixed on the direction of the sacred mountain, a question lingering in her mind.

Do gods truly exist there?

Has Kong Wang not deceived her?

Chapter 234 The Dragon Slayer Cult's Actions
.

“What if the demons suffer another crushing defeat? Won’t we be caught in the crossfire too?”

The man continued, clearly worried.

“Circling around to the rear doesn’t mean we have to intervene. We can just watch. If the Holy Court wins, then it’s not too late for us to act,” Nangong Hu said slowly.

In truth, she had never intended to help the demons fight against the humans.

Back in Luoshui Prefecture, she had already reached an agreement with Kong Wang.

Although Quan Xiaotian saw through her scheme and dismissed her, she still honored her promise to Kong Wang.

“But where is he?”

Nangong Hu frowned slightly, picking up the token in her hand. She couldn’t contact Kong Wang, meaning he was far away.

“Let’s go, let’s get going quickly, or we’ll be late.”

Nangong Hu said, continuing to lead the Dragon-Slaying Sect members forward.

They chose the mountains and forests to avoid being discovered.

It took the group a full half-day to finally circle around to the back of Longyun County, which was actually Qingyun County.

"Have all the people of Longyun County evacuated?"

Upon arriving, they saw a steady stream of people coming from the direction of Longyun County, all carrying families and large bundles.

The man's heart skipped a beat, he gave an awkward smile, and dared not speak further.

Nangong Hu took out her token for contacting the Holy Court and directly sent a message to Quan Xiaotian, informing him that they had arrived in Qingyun County.

Just four short words, nothing more.

But would she wait just because Quan Xiaotian told her to?

Of course not.

The others chimed in.

“With you all coming, that's what makes it dangerous.”

Nangong Hu's simple words immediately made the others feel embarrassed and ashamed.

Their strength was indeed lacking, but they were still at least sixth-tier Yang God Realm cultivators.

However, the Holy Maiden was still the Holy Maiden. Even with only the cultivation of the Yin God Realm, her mysterious and unpredictable methods, coupled with her innate demonic powers, allowed her to easily defeat them.

Leaving the others behind, Nangong Hu set off alone.

Avoiding the main road, she chose to enter Longyun County through the wilderness.

"Can this token contact the people of Qianshan County?"

Nangong Hu took out the Mountain Token that Kong Wang had given her, saying that it could be used to contact the people of Qianshan County.

Meanwhile, after Wu Ze broke through to the Yin God Realm, the people of Qianshan County had already set off, wandering around Longyun Prefecture.

In Longjiang County, Quan Xiaotian had released his demon army, letting them swarm into every corner of Longyun Prefecture to hunt on their own.

He wanted to use this method to disperse the human forces, and then defeat them one by one.

But the idea was beautiful, but the reality was harsh.

The people of Longyun Prefecture had already been relocated by Bai Liang and his group.

What remained were stubborn, unyielding people, numbering around 100,000 across several counties.

They had shown them a way out, and even arranged for carriages to take them away.

Chu Wuliu said calmly, already used to seeing such scenes.

They were currently on their way to rendezvous with the Third Prince's group.

Because three more seventh-tier demons had arrived in Longjiang County, making it four with Quan Xiaotian.

This was both bad and good news.

The bad news was that there were four seventh-tier demons, whom they were no match for.

The good news was that no eighth-tier demons had come, giving them a chance to resist.

On their way to rendezvous, they encountered many demon groups.

The weaker groups were led by third- or fourth-tier demons, numbering in the hundreds.

The stronger groups were led by fifth- or sixth-tier demons, numbering roughly a thousand.

At this critical moment, Du Tiandao and his hundred-plus cultivators came in handy. Wu Zechu and a few others dealt with the powerful demons, while the remaining weaker ones were left for Du Tiandao and his group to practice on.

These people had never seen blood before, and they suffered considerable losses in their initial battles against the demons.

Finally, Du Tiandao roared and tore off the head of a third-tier demon with both hands, blood splattering and igniting the bloodlust within the group.

After that, they fought with increasing ferocity, and whenever they encountered demons again, they were eager to fight without even needing Du Tiandao's orders.

True strength is forged in battle.

Although these people all sustained injuries of varying degrees, none were in mortal danger.

The more troublesome situation arose when they encountered a demon horde led by two sixth-tier demons.

Furthermore, there were a dozen or so fifth-tier demons, bringing the total number of demons to two or three thousand.

This posed a significant threat to the people of Qianshan County.

The number of demons was too great; Du Tiandao and his group simply couldn't hold them off.

In the end, Chu Wuliu and his companions had to quickly eliminate a sixth-tier demon, allowing Hu Zhengqiang to escape and personally deal with the majority of the demons, thus easing the pressure.

However, five or six people were still severely injured and rendered unable to fight.

Fortunately, no one died.

Longyun County was now in chaos, with demons roaming every corner, so it was impossible to send the wounded out first; they had to carry them along for now.

When they arrived at the rendezvous point, the Third Prince and his group had not yet arrived.

"They told us to come here, but where are they?"

Chu Wuliu frowned, his expression showing some displeasure.

Were they really going to have to wait for them here?

This was the border area between several counties, very close to Longjiang County, and their current location was on high ground, extremely conspicuous.

This place was too conspicuous; if they stayed any longer, the demons would definitely discover them!

Chapter 235: Find out the inner ghost
.

As expected, after waiting for half an hour, they were discovered by the demons.

He struck first. After breaking through to the Yin God Realm, his strength was no longer just at the early stage of the Yin God Realm; thanks to his previous accumulation and the consolidation from the battles of the past few days, he had directly reached the late stage of the Yin God Realm.

With the speed boost from the Divine Speed ​​Talisman, he was like a fish in water in the battle against the demons, easily dealing with the sixth-tier demons.

Du Tiandao's eyes were filled with murderous intent. The demon-slaying of the past few days had added a touch of killing intent to this burly, muscular man.

Relying on his innate divine strength, he was able to fight against fourth-tier demons with the strength of a late-stage third-tier demon—his combat power was extremely formidable!

This group of demons that attacked numbered in the hundreds.

But to the people of Qianshan County, this was nothing more than a piece of cake.
