Bai Liang immediately made the decision.

Chu Wuliu and the others nodded gravely. The number of demons was too great; if they didn't retreat, they would easily be worn down and killed.

However, they were now surrounded, and retreating wasn't so easy.

Bai Liang looked around and found an opening. He said in a deep voice, "Break through that way!"

So the group began to rush towards the opening. Demons tried to stop them along the way, but they were all killed.

The sixth-tier demons, wary of the strength of the people of Qianshan County, dared not directly confront them. They wanted to wear them down with lower-tier demons, but every demon they sent out died.

In the end, the people of Qianshan County managed to break through the encirclement.

Breaking out of the demon encirclement, Bai Liang and the others didn't stop, continuing to run for over fifty miles before stopping.

And it was at this moment that they happened to encounter the 'belated' imperial prodigy?

"Hmm? How did you all get here? Weren't we supposed to meet up ahead?"

The Third Prince asked, sounding surprised.

Bai Liang's expression darkened slightly. "You did this on purpose?"

He finally realized something was wrong. The Third Prince had told them to meet at the rendezvous point, but he had been hiding here, resulting in them attracting a bunch of demons. If they hadn't realized the situation was bad and retreated first, they would have been trapped.

"Fellow Daoist Bai, this is a huge misunderstanding. What do you mean we did this on purpose?"

The Third Prince frowned, looking completely bewildered.

Bai Liang, his face cold, recounted what had just happened, concluding, "You were the ones who gave us the location, and you were the ones who told us to wait there. And what happened?"

"What do you mean? Do you suspect we sent you to draw fire?"

Cha Xixing immediately flared up, her tone harsh.

"Isn't that right?" Hu Zhengqiang said in a deep voice.

The atmosphere between the two sides instantly became tense.

"Alright, there's been a misunderstanding. Please, fellow Daoists from Qianshan County, don't be angry."

Shangguan Shenghua stepped forward, first showing the bloodstains on his body, then explaining that they had also just encountered a demon attack.

Moreover, the demons were quite numerous, resulting in a fierce battle that prevented them from reaching the rendezvous point on time.

Hearing Shangguan Shenghua's words, Bai Liang and the others noticed that his aura was somewhat chaotic, and that he was also covered in demon blood.

This confirmed that Shangguan Shenghua's statement was true; they had indeed encountered a demon attack.

But how could it be such a coincidence?

Both sides went to the same rendezvous point, only to be attacked by demons.

Although Longyun County was only a county, it was still quite large.

Even if 100,000 demons were scattered throughout Longjiang County, they wouldn't be encountered easily, let alone in this wild mountainous terrain.

So what exactly was going on?

Bai Liang and Wu Ze exchanged glances, sensing something was amiss.

The Third Prince and the others also realized what was happening, their expressions instantly darkening.

Especially the Third Prince, who had already suspected a traitor among them, having exposed their identities last time, and despite a thorough search, hadn't found one.

His gaze immediately locked onto his suspects, the Zhao brothers.

The Zhao brothers, their faces grim, were now filled with malice.

Because they had seen Wu Ze, and sensed the aura emanating from him.

Late-stage Yin God Realm!

Considering the extreme Yin energy of a few days ago, they knew Wu Ze had achieved the Full Moon Yin God Realm.

They were already enemies, and seeing their enemy grow stronger filled them with a sense of crisis.

Because they could already sense the danger in Wu Ze's gaze.

Only then did the Zhao brothers notice the Third Prince's stare. They quickly realized what was happening, their expressions changing drastically.

They were being suspected again.

Damn it, they hadn't done anything wrong, so why were they being suspected?

"Your Highness, what do you mean by this?"

Feeling the gazes of the crowd, Zhao Fuhu's face darkened.

"What do I mean? Zhao Fuhu, you know perfectly well. If you confess now, out of respect for the Zhao family, I will let it slide."

The Third Prince's face was cold. He was eighty percent certain that the Zhao family was the hidden traitor.

Because the Zhao brothers not only had a falling out with him, but also a deep-seated grudge against Wu Ze of Qianshan County!

The motive was far too obvious; anyone with eyes could see it.

Bai Liang and the others also looked at the Zhao brothers. They too suspected the two, and also wondered if the Third Prince was putting on an act.

The Zhao brothers were immediately enraged; anyone wronged twice would be furious.

"Chu Yuan! Don't think that just because you're a prince you can arbitrarily accuse others of being traitors? Do you really think that just because we're in Qingzhou, everyone has to listen to you?"

Zhao Fuhu's face was grim.

Upon hearing Zhao Fuhu address him by his name, the Third Prince's expression turned completely cold.

"Whether it's true or not, you know the answer in your heart. Only the two of you here have the motive to be traitors!" he said coldly.

"Who said only we have the motive?"

Zhao Fuhu immediately objected, coldly replying, "Have you all forgotten that among us there is a restless one!"


.
Chapter 236 Deep into Danger
.

"A restless one?"

Only after Zhao Fuhu's words did the Third Prince and the others belatedly realize what was happening.

Then, everyone's gaze turned to Yun Lanxi, who stood at the back, seemingly unremarkable.

Yun Lanxi's expression was calm, her eyes covered by a black cloth, giving her a serene air.

Despite being stared at by so many, Yun Lanxi seemed oblivious, her expression remaining as calm as ever.

Zhao Fuhu sneered, "The Imperial Preceptor's personal disciple, with a glorious record—have you all forgotten?"

Of course, no one had forgotten.

As the Imperial Preceptor's only disciple, a prodigy envied by heaven, possessing exceptional talent and the ability to see through celestial secrets.

This series of titles instilled fear in many towards Yun Lanxi.

Of course, what impressed people most were Yun Lanxi's various achievements.

The title of "The Capital's Number One Madwoman" wasn't given lightly.

In the capital, she even managed to swindle and deceive several princes, tricking them into leaving the capital to exorcise demons. However, they encountered an eighth-tier demon, nearly causing the Chu Emperor to be childless.

This happened before the Tianshui Pact.

During the Tianshui Battle, she boldly led a group of aristocratic scions to flank the demons and cut off their retreat.

The result was disastrous; more than half of the second-generation elites were killed or wounded, causing heavy losses to the aristocratic families.

Similar incidents were countless.

Even the Golden Dragon Temple and Xuanqing Temple were tricked several times.

If it weren't for the Imperial Advisor always cleaning up her messes, Yun Lanxi would have been torn apart by the aristocratic sects long ago.

“Yun Lanxi, are you causing trouble in secret again!?”

The Third Prince's tone was low; he remembered Yun Lanxi's unusual behavior earlier.

It was just that Yun Lanxi's special destiny made people inadvertently overlook her, which was why they hadn't thought of her at the moment.

Yun Lanxi didn't deny it, nor did she need to; she readily admitted it.

"I did it," she said succinctly.

"It really was you!" Zhao Fuhu's voice was furious. He had been wronged twice, and the mastermind behind it all was this crazy woman, Yun Lanxi!

"Are you insane? Why would you do this?"

The Third Prince's face darkened, his gaze towards Yun Lanxi filled with hostility.

Shangguan Shenghua and the others also looked at Yun Lanxi with unfriendly eyes.

They all wanted to question her about her motives.

Bai Liang and his group followed the crowd's gaze, looking at Yun Lanxi, the instigator.

Chu Wuliu frowned: "I've heard of this woman; she truly is a madwoman."

"A disciple of the Imperial Preceptor, she was infamous back then. It's said that many prodigies were implicated and killed by her," Hu Zhengqiang said in a deep voice.

Sanfu, as if struck by a sudden thought, spoke up: "This old monk once heard the elders in the temple mention that the Second Prince's death seemed to be related to this woman."

"The Second Prince?"

Bai Liang was somewhat surprised upon hearing this.

Wasn't the Second Prince's death related to Qilin and the others? How did it get involved with this disciple of the Imperial Preceptor?

It seems there are many hidden secrets here.

"Since this woman is too crazy, we'd better not stay with them, lest we get implicated."

Wu Ze frowned as he spoke.

At this moment, Yun Lanxi finally responded, her tone very calm as she slowly said, "Isn't it good to simply strengthen your resolve and kill more demons?"

She spoke calmly, but those who heard her were seething with anger.

“You call this good? Exposing our whereabouts! If the attackers weren't sixth-tier demons, but seventh-tier demons, we'd all be dead here!”

The Third Prince roared.

“Who's stronger is still unknown. Perhaps it'll be a seventh-tier demon who dies here,” Yun Lanxi said calmly.

Everyone's faces darkened; they couldn't accept being manipulated by Yun Lanxi like this.

The Third Prince wanted to send Yun Lanxi away, but then he thought that if she left alone, who knew what trouble she might cause?

He couldn't punish her either; she was the Imperial Preceptor's personal disciple, and even his father valued her highly.

In the end, he could only give her a stern warning, order someone to keep an eye on her, and then let the matter drop.

“Your Highness, we won't travel with you,”

Bai Liang stepped forward and said.

With such a madwoman around, who would want them together? They might get sold out without even knowing it.

Her fate is special, so the best way is to avoid her.

The Third Prince's expression faltered. He wanted to persuade her to stay, arguing that fighting the demons together would give them a better chance of victory.

But Bai Liang and the others refused without hesitation.

Compared to the threat of the demons, they were more worried about Yun Lanxi, this unsettling element.

After hearing Chu Wuliu's account of Yun Lanxi's past, Bai Liang and the others became wary of this madwoman.

After saying goodbye, the people of Qianshan County turned and left.

Demonic monsters are now rampant in Longyun County, and they need to be constantly eliminated to prevent them from escaping. That would be a real problem.

There are also demons in Longjiang County; it seems several more seventh-tier demons have arrived there.

This is a significant threat, and they must avoid them for now.

......

Outside the border.

Thousands of miles away from the Great Chu Dynasty, Kong Wang lay dormant in a demon city.

This was the heartland of the demon realm, within the Holy Court's sphere of influence.

Most of the demons residing here had become 'civilized,' managing their cities with remarkable order.

However, there were still significant differences compared to human cities.

Kong Wang mingled in a camp that sold human slaves.

Many demons had come to buy human slaves, mostly choosing a male and a female to take back and breed, ensuring a continuous supply of food.

The captured humans all had lifeless eyes, like puppets.

"What?! Two human slaves for such a high price? Are you robbing me?"

A demon who had come to buy them shouted.

"Heh, you call that expensive? The front lines in the Wilderness are strained, and Qingzhou is bound by the Heavenly Water Pact. Human slaves are incredibly valuable these days. If you're so capable, go hunt in the Wilderness!"

The demon selling them chuckled sinisterly.

The demon blushed crimson. If he had the strength and guts to go to the front lines, he would have gone long ago.

Kong Wang listened to the demons' conversation, his brow furrowing slightly.

He had heard the news of the strained front lines in the Wilderness more than once.

At first, he was surprised, because before this, he had no idea that a human force existed in the distance.

Moreover, it seemed that this Wilderness was not much weaker than the Great Chu Dynasty.

As more news broke in the past few days, his curiosity about the Wilderness grew even stronger.

"A barbarian demigod seeks to prove his Dao through force, aiming to become the third Saint of Heaven and Earth!?"

This was a secret piece of information he had gathered from the demons' stronghold.

It was precisely because of this barbarian demigod that the Holy Court mobilized its demigod forces, along with ninety percent of its eighth-tier Venerables, all heading to the Wilderness.

Moreover, even the two demon Saints were alarmed.

"The Wilderness, I must go and see what's going on!"

Kong Wang thought to himself.


.
Chapter 237 Fierce Barbarians
.

The desolate wilderness is extremely remote; reaching it would take at least ten days to half a month.

Furthermore, Kong Wang would need to conceal his presence and avoid any potential encounters with powerful demons and monsters along the way, making his journey even slower.

It would take at least a month.

But this still couldn't deter Kong Wang's determination to go to the desolate wilderness.

After deciding to go, he set off.

However, before setting off, he spent a day retreating to Luoshui Prefecture and then used the Mountain Token to send a message back.

Only after doing this did Kong Wang begin his journey towards the desolate wilderness.

Li Yue noticed Kong Wang's movements and projected his divine sense onto him, watching him resolutely head towards the desolate wilderness.

Then he learned about the situation in the desolate wilderness.

"Proving the Dao through force? The third saint of heaven and earth?"

Li Yue murmured to himself, becoming somewhat interested.

The desolate wilderness, a region tens of thousands of miles away, actually possessed such a prodigious talent.

It seems we shouldn't underestimate the people of this world.

Look at the three semi-saints of the Great Chu Dynasty; they've wasted a century, their path to enlightenment still incomplete, and they haven't yet achieved sainthood.

But the semi-saint from the barbarian wilderness possesses extraordinary courage, directly declaring his intention to prove his Dao through force.

However, Li Yue isn't optimistic about this barbarian semi-saint.

First of all, with two demonic saints watching over them, how could they possibly tolerate a human reaching sainthood?

Moreover, achieving sainthood isn't so easy.

Just as the legends say, the Heavenly Path is severed, the path to enlightenment has ended.

For hundreds of years, no saint has been born.

This is related to changes in the rules of heaven and earth.

In Li Yue's view, as long as these so-called shackles of heaven and earth remain unbroken, it's impossible to successfully attain the Dao.

As for the two Saint-level beings from the demon realm, that's a variable.

Li Yue even suspects they might be outsiders.

The evidence is the large hole in the sky and the blood-red meteorite from beyond the heavens.

Thinking of the hole in the shackles of heaven and earth, Li Yue suddenly felt that the barbarian half-saint might actually have a sliver of a chance to succeed.

The shackles of heaven and earth sealed off the entire world and isolated the perception of the Great Dao.

But now a hole has appeared. Although the rules of heaven and earth are trying their best to repair it, the opening remains.

So, through this gap, perhaps there really is a chance.

"It seems that the barbarian half-saint has also seen this gap."

Li Yue thought to himself.

Now it all depends on whether the other side can succeed.

He suspected that the three semi-saints in the capital were also watching the movements of the barbarian lands.

Once the barbarian semi-saints truly succeeded in attaining enlightenment, they would quickly follow suit and become saints themselves.

Meanwhile, in the capital.

Li Yue's suspicions were correct. Emperor Chu, Tai Xuan, and Jia Pi were meeting in the Void.

"Aturu couldn't wait any longer and went ahead!" Jia Pi said with a faint smile, seemingly having anticipated this.

"The barbarian lands are too fragmented, with countless tribes, making unification impossible. Faced with the gradual encroachment of the demons, if Aturu hadn't taken this step, the barbarian lands would have been completely annihilated."

Emperor Chu spoke slowly, clearly well-versed in the situation of the barbarian lands.

Although they were tens of thousands of miles apart, in the Void, a thought could travel tens of thousands of miles, so for semi-saints, distance was irrelevant.

"It's too hasty. Taking this step now will make it less likely to succeed than originally planned."

Tai Xuan sighed softly, now appearing as a young man.

“The barbarians are fierce and brave; it's understandable that they've taken this step prematurely. The demons have pushed them too far. Aturu is going all in; success means the barbarians' survival, failure means their annihilation.”

The Chu Emperor spoke with a touch of melancholy. Compared to Aturu, he was more hesitant.

In truth, he had a better chance of succeeding in attaining the Dao than Aturu, but he dared not take that step for the time being.

The path to enlightenment, once embarked upon, offers no turning back.

Either succeed and become a saint, mastering a great Dao.

Or fail, and the body and spirit are annihilated within the Dao, with no chance of resurrection.

He was the emperor, ruling over a dynasty; how could he dare to take that step lightly?

Even Tai Xuan and Jia Pi dared not.

Because they knew the chance of success was extremely slim; countless predecessors had fallen at that step.

The Heavenly Path was severed; this was no joke.

“Let’s wait. Aturu has paved the way for us. If he truly succeeds in attaining sainthood, then humanity will be saved,” Tai Xuan said.

“Heh, it’s not that easy. The demons won’t just watch him break through,” Jia Pi shook his head.

The two demon saints usually don’t show themselves, but if a human tries to attain sainthood, they will definitely intervene and kill them beforehand.

Therefore, he didn’t have high hopes for Aturu.

“We’ll act according to circumstances, but the agreement with the barbarians must be honored,” Emperor Chu said.

Great Chu had an agreement with the barbarians: if one side had someone attain sainthood, the other side would help protect them and block the demons’ harassment and obstruction.

This was an agreement between the two humans. Although they hadn’t been in contact with the barbarians for a long time, this agreement must be honored.

“We can block a half-saint, but if a saint intervenes, we can’t withstand it,” Tai Xuan said, frowning.

"At most, they can hold them off for ten breaths. That's more than enough; we haven't broken our promise."

Jiabi spoke calmly.

The power of a Saint is immeasurable.

Especially two Saints.

Even if he and Taixuan summoned their Ancestral Artifacts, and Emperor Chu summoned the portrait of the Great Ancestor, they could only hold them off for a moment, not fight them off.

Inanimate objects can never compare to true Saints.

"Give the Barbarians ten breaths; let's see if Aturu can live up to the hype."

Emperor Chu said, having already decided to intervene and help Aturu this time.

This was not just a promise, but also a ignited flame of hope for humanity.

If this flame can be lit, then humanity has hope.

If it cannot be lit, then they can only wait for the next chance to ignite it.

They weren't the only ones concerned about the Barbarians.

Headquarters of the Dragon-Slaying Cult.

The Dragon-Slaying Cult Leader, clad in black robes, appeared in the void.

He gazed towards the desolate wilderness, where a few lights shimmered brilliantly.

"To attain sainthood, what immense courage!"

His tone was calm, devoid of emotion.

"Unfortunately, it's a certain death."

He slowly shook his head and sighed heavily.

As fellow humans, their hearts naturally sided with Aturu.

But the demons were far too powerful; two Saint-level beings were enough to suffocate the entire human race.

Aturu dared to be the first, taking this step first—truly a display of courage.

But what were the chances?

"Very well, we're all of the same race. If you truly have a chance, this leader will certainly lend a hand."

The Dragon Slayer Sect Leader's eyes burned brightly, gleaming with light.

At this moment, the spotlight shone on the desolate wilderness, all the semi-saints were watching Aturu's actions.

They were all waiting for the right opportunity.

Dragon Cloud County.

When Bai Liang received Kong Wang's message, he was utterly shocked.

Chapter 238 Defying Fate
.

In his message, Kong Wang mentioned that a Half-Saint of the Wilderness was about to attain enlightenment, and all the powerful demons had already gone to the Wilderness.

He too had already set off for the Wilderness to gather intelligence.

“Hiss! Attaining enlightenment and becoming a Saint! This Half-Saint of the Wilderness is truly a powerful being!”

After informing everyone of the news, Chu Wuliu couldn't help but exclaim in surprise.

The others shared the same shock.

This was the first time since the chaos caused by demons that a powerful human had attained enlightenment and taken this step.

If he truly succeeded in attaining enlightenment, he could reverse the current situation of the human race and stop them from being gradually devoured by demons.

“I hope this senior can succeed in attaining enlightenment and successfully reverse the situation of the world!”

Du Tiandao said.

Everyone nodded in agreement.

Although they weren't from the Great Chu Dynasty, they were all human; what difference did it make?

However, after calming down, everyone felt that the hope of success was extremely slim.

After the Heavenly Path was severed, achieving sainthood became a distant dream; the realm of saints ceased to exist.

Throughout history, countless prodigies have attempted to attain sainthood on that path, only to suffer crushing defeats.

None succeeded.

The legends of saints all exist from before the Heavenly Path was broken.

Now, everyone refers to them as ancient saints.

Because so much time has passed, the world has forgotten the realm of sainthood; it exists only in records.

"It's a pity I couldn't witness the barbarian semi-saint's ascension to sainthood firsthand."

Wu Ze's tone was tinged with regret.

At this moment, Bai Liang finally understood why the demons hadn't sent any eighth-tier experts to Qingzhou.

They had all been sent to the barbarian battlefield.

Only seventh-tier demons remained in Qingzhou to keep them company.

Without the threat of eighth-tier demons, they could do even more.

Therefore, Bai Liang led the group to continue their wanderings in Longyun County.

This time, they no longer hid. Upon encountering demons, they attacked and killed them directly.

If they encountered a large-scale attack, they would temporarily avoid direct confrontation and then seek an opportunity to strike.

In this way, within a few days, they annihilated several thousand demons, including four sixth-tier demons.

Then they encountered the Third Prince and his entourage.

Their group was much more cautious, not openly appearing to kill demons, spending most of their time in hiding.

Bai Liang told them about the barbarian semi-saints. The Third Prince and his companions were all shocked and astonished.

They hadn't expected the barbarians to be the first to achieve sainthood; their audacity was truly remarkable!

After the initial shock, they, like Bai Liang and his group, became reckless.

Without eighth-tier demons, the threat posed by them was greatly reduced. They could simply retreat if things went wrong.

Even if surrounded, with their hidden trump cards, survival was assured.

Thus, the demons of Longyun County, who initially savagely hunted and plundered human flesh, became trembling and hiding in fear.

Demons were constantly being slain, while in Longjiang County, Quan Xiaotian remained calm and unperturbed, ignoring all the news.

The three seventh-tier demons who had come to assist were furious at this sight.

"Quan Xiaotian, what do you mean by this? Are you shirking your duties, watching the demons below being hunted down by humans while you do nothing?"

Mo Teng spoke coldly, his eyes filled with extreme hostility.

"Heh, I already said, go if you want to go, little did you know what fate awaited the demonic serpent?"

Quan Xiaotian sneered.

His original plan was to have the Holy Court send an eighth-tier expert to assist him, but instead, three seventh-tier experts arrived, greatly disappointing him.

Thinking of the methods used by those people in Qianshan County, he didn't want to go out and fight.

Although the little girl's methods seemed to come at a price, he dared not gamble.

Who knew who she would use them on?

If she used them on him, and he died, then the credit would go to other demons, wouldn't he be at a huge loss?

So he simply decided not to fight, let things take their course.

In any case, the Holy Court had already sent word that he should prepare to withdraw his troops; the barbaric front was in dire straits, and he needed to go to support them at any time.

As for Qingzhou, he could put it aside for now.

"Good! Very good! I will definitely report what you've said to the Holy Court when we get back!"

Mo Teng laughed in anger. He hadn't expected Quan Xiaotian to be so cowardly, so easily frightened by humans.

"Gentlemen, since this coward doesn't dare to fight, then we'll take matters into our own hands!" Mo Teng looked at the other two demons.

But only one responded.

The other was clearly on good terms with Quan Xiaotian and naturally wouldn't side with Mo Teng.

Seeing this, Mo Teng's face darkened.

Finally, he snorted coldly and turned to leave.

Watching Mo Teng and the other seventh-tier demon leave, Quan Xiaotian sneered.

"Truly ignorant, obsessed with merit!"

"Brother Quan, why didn't you fight?" the remaining seventh-tier demon asked, puzzled.

"You saw the demon serpent's corpse, didn't you?"

Quan Xiaotian said calmly.

Then he recounted the strange methods the demon serpent had encountered—it died without any contact.

The seventh-tier demon shuddered upon hearing this.

What kind of bizarre method was this?

To be able to instantly kill a seventh-tier demon at the Enlightenment Realm was truly terrifying.

No wonder Brother Dog dared not easily engage in battle.

“If the Demon Vine wants to seek its death, let it be. Let us prepare; in a while, the Holy Court will summon us to the Wilderness,” Quan Xiaotian said calmly.

As for the mess in Qingzhou?

He completely ignored it. Divine mountains and mountain gods—these were none of his concern.

What did it matter how many variables there were? He couldn't interfere anyway; let the Holy Court's experts deal with it.

He'd rather pack up and go to the Wilderness to collect some merit.

Meanwhile, Bai Liang and the others were still slaying demons when they suddenly encountered an unexpected guest.

The Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Sect, Nangong Hu.

However, Nangong Hu did not reveal her identity, only showing the token given to her by Kong Wang, indicating that she was Kong Wang's friend.

"So you're Kong Wang's friend, then you're a friend of Qianshan County."

Bai Liang smiled. Kong Wang had mentioned Nangong Hu in his message.

So he knew Nangong Hu was the Dragon-Slaying Cult member Kong Wang had contacted, but he didn't know she was the Holy Maiden of the Dragon-Slaying Cult.

"Kong Wang sent me here, but where is he?"

Nangong Hu's first question was about Kong Wang's whereabouts.

"He ventured deep into the demon territory and has now headed to the Wilderness," Bai Liang said slowly.

"Ah? The Wilderness!?"

Nangong Hu was slightly surprised, not expecting Kong Wang to go to such a far place.

"Kong Wang mentioned that you can contact the demons?" Bai Liang suddenly asked.

Nangong Hu hesitated for a moment, but nodded nonetheless, saying,

“The Dragon-Slaying Sect has an alliance with the demons, so they are in contact with each other.”

Bai Liang’s eyes lit up, and he quickly said, “Then can you contact the demons now and try to gather some information?”

“Probably not, I’ve already been suspected by the demons,” Nangong Hu said regretfully, shaking his head.

Chapter 239 Encounter Battle
.

Hearing Nangong Hu's words, Bai Liang felt a pang of regret.

If only he could gather more information about the demons, things would be much better.

"I can try to contact them," Nangong Hu hesitated for a moment, but still said.

"Then I'll trouble you, fellow Daoist," Bai Liang immediately expressed his gratitude.

Nangong Hu nodded slightly and took out the black token used to contact Quan Xiaotian.

She hadn't used this token yet, mainly because she was already suspected, so contacting them wouldn't be effective.

She probed the token with her divine sense, activating it to send a message.

The black token flashed slightly, indicating that the message had been successfully transmitted.

After waiting a while, a response came quickly from the other end of the token.

Nangong Hu's heart skipped a beat, and she quickly received the message. After reading the message from Quan Xiaotian, her expression became somewhat strange.

"How is it?"

Bai Liang asked suspiciously, seeing that Nangong Hu hadn't spoken yet.

"They actually responded to me."

Nangong Hu spoke slowly, her tone somewhat incredulous.

It wasn't just a response; they also informed her of some information, and it seemed very deliberate, as if they were specifically telling her this.

This made her suspicious—was it a trap?

"What did they respond to?" Bai Liang quickly asked.

Nangong Hu took a deep breath and said, "They said the Holy Court has sent three more seventh-tier demons, and two of them have already come out to hunt you. They told me to prepare from behind, to launch a coordinated attack from both sides and take them down in one fell swoop."

Hearing this, Bai Liang and the others were surprised.

Mainly because this news was too suspicious.

The demons no longer trusted Nangong Hu, so why would they still share this information?

There was definitely something wrong with this. The most likely explanation was that this news was a trap, intended to lure them in.

But the problem was, this news couldn't possibly lure anyone in.

Only two seventh-tier demons would be foolish enough to be tempted.

So, what was the purpose of releasing this news?

Was it to intimidate them? Or was there some other purpose?

Bai Liang and the others racked their brains but couldn't figure it out.

"Is it possible that the message they received is genuine? And there's no other hidden meaning?"

Finally, Wu Ze speculated.

The others were slightly taken aback.

After thinking about it carefully, they realized it was indeed possible; the message might be true.

Bai Liang sought confirmation from Nangong Hu.

However, Nangong Hu herself wasn't sure.

In short, Quan Xiaotian had indeed seen through her problems long ago; that was undeniable.

But if he knew she had problems, why would he tell her the real information?

Could there be internal problems on their side?

This was one reason Nangong Hu could think of.

After hearing Nangong Hu's speculation, Bai Liang and the others also thought it was very likely.

If there were internal problems, then it would explain things clearly.

"Regardless, caution is paramount. After all, we're still facing two seventh-tier demons. With such a threat, we'd better find the Third Prince and his group to reunite. That way, we'll have enough resources to protect ourselves."

Bai Liang spoke slowly.

Just as he mentioned the Third Prince and his group, the token in his robes flashed violently.

Picking it up, he saw it was a plea for help from the Third Prince and his companions.

"Encountering seventh-tier demons, the situation is critical! Come to our aid immediately!"

The short sentence, though brief, was urgent enough to illustrate the threat the Third Prince and his companions faced.

Bai Liang's expression changed slightly; he hadn't expected to encounter a seventh-tier demon so soon.

He quickly informed the others of the situation, and everyone present was immediately alarmed!

Chu Wuliu's expression darkened, and he said, "A seventh-tier demon is far too powerful. Too many of us going won't suffice. Perhaps the three of us should go to assist the Third Prince and his group."

He knew that Li Xue's actions came at a price, hence his suggestion.

"I want to go too!" Li Xue stepped forward immediately, her eyes resolute.

Chu Wuliu looked at Bai Liang.

Bai Liang sighed slightly and said, "Let's have the six of us go. The rest of you stay behind and hide for now to ensure your safety."

"As for you..." He looked at Nangong Hu, sensing the other's Yin God Realm aura, and hesitated slightly.

"I'll go with you too. Although I can't be of much help, I can at least analyze the situation," Nangong Hu said slowly.

“Alright!” Bai Liang nodded.

So Bian Nong and the other Yin God Realm cultivators, along with Du Tiandao and the others, temporarily went into hiding.

Two seventh-tier demons, and incredibly powerful ones at that, presented an immense pressure.

.

This was an incredibly reckless act. Previously, they would at least conserve their strength; now, they were completely disregarding it.

Regardless of age or gender, anyone capable of fighting was sent to the battlefield.

They were willing to risk their lives to buy time for their demigods.

Even sacrificing their lives was worthwhile.

Because this was their only hope, the only hope for the barbarian bloodline to continue!

Therefore, the Holy Court encountered the fiercest resistance. The barbarian experts deployed all their forces, fighting to the death, causing the Holy Court considerable losses.

The Holy Court was enraged and decided to use all its power to completely crush the barbarian wilderness and exterminate the entire barbarian race!

Therefore, Qingzhou had no choice but to strategically retreat and head to the barbarian wilderness to provide assistance.

As for the Divine Mountain, the Holy Court ultimately paid it no heed.

Quan Xiaotian was filled with worry. He always felt that the threat posed by the Divine Mountain was greater and more unpredictable than that of the Wilderness.

Unfortunately, the Holy Court wouldn't listen to him at all, only coldly responding that he should simply obey orders.

"Forget it, none of this concerns me."

Quan Xiaotian shook his head; he was merely a seventh-tier demon king.

Chapter 244 Triumphant Return
.

The demon army arrived in a mighty mass, and retreated in a similarly mighty mass.

Witnessing the demon army withdraw from Qingzhou, the weight lifted from everyone's hearts, and they breathed a heavy sigh of relief.

Shangguan Shenghua's tone was somewhat incredulous.

Before resisting the demons, he had never imagined a complete victory.

His best-case scenario was to tie the demon army down in Longyun County, slowing their advance.

That would have been the best possible outcome.

But he never expected such a pleasant surprise.

Everyone's faces lit up with joy and excitement.

The battle was over; the next step was to clean up the mess.

Although the demon army had retreated, many demons remained in Longyun County.

These demons weren't strong, but they were still a potential threat.

The Third Prince and his group no longer cared about this threat and had no intention of eliminating it.

These prodigies were already preparing to return to the capital.

Hu Zhengqiang said slowly.

Wu Ze's tone was resolute, and Hu Zhengqiang, seeing this, could say nothing more.

This was a new use Bai Liang had just discovered, limited only to the talisman's recipient.

It couldn't be done for others.

After watching Wu Ze leave, Bai Liang and the others focused on eliminating the demons.

Her task was complete; next, she needed to lead the Dragon-Slaying Sect members to evacuate Qingzhou.

The Holy Court's large-scale action had caused a great stir within the Dragon-Slaying Sect.

The Sect Leader had ordered her to return to the sect immediately, as there were important matters to discuss.

Nangong Hu roughly guessed that the Sect Leader might send her to the Wilderness.

It took the people of Qianshan County a full day to completely eliminate the remaining demons.

However, Longyun County was no longer suitable for human habitation.

The original city had long since fallen to demons, leaving only ruins and crumbling walls.

Not to mention the farmland and ponds, all destroyed and polluted.

The number of survivors was extremely small.

Looking at the deserted Longyun County, Bai Liang sighed.

Then they began their return to Liaoyun County.

This operation could be considered a triumphant return.

Although there were casualties in the ranks, overall it was still a great victory!

Because the demon army had completely withdrawn.

News of the demon army's defeat spread throughout Qingzhou at an incredible speed.

However, the main focus of this news was the elites of the capital.

The Third Prince deliberately spread the news to enhance his reputation.

When Bai Liang learned of this, his brows furrowed immediately.

Du Tiandao was indignant.

Li Xue slew the demonic serpent, and they, along with the Third Prince, played a crucial role in killing the demonic vine.

Not to mention cleaning up the mess and relocating the people—that was all their work.

And in the end, the Third Prince reaped all the benefits.

Although the Third Prince mentioned Qianshan County in his disseminated message, it was only briefly.

Bai Liang's expression was somewhat unpleasant, not because his credit and reputation had been stolen—he didn't care about that at all.

It was because the Third Prince's actions would directly impact the spread of the Mountain God's faith in Qianshan County.

He didn't care how the Third Prince tried to steal credit in the capital, but in Qingzhou, he wouldn't allow it.

Fortunately, he had prepared a contingency plan to prevent this situation.

"Don't worry, I was prepared," Bai Liang said slowly.

Du Tiandao was surprised to hear this; he hadn't expected Lord Bai to be so prepared.

He was completely relieved.

He was just a little curious about what method Lord Bai would use to deal with it.

When they returned to Liaoyun County and were about to reach Qianshan County, news suddenly reached their ears.

"The Zhao brothers have been brutally murdered in Qingzhou City!"

This news came from Qingzhou. Upon hearing it, Bai Liang knew that Wu Ze had succeeded.

Upon further inquiry, it was indeed as he had suspected.

After arriving in Qingzhou City with the group of prodigies, Wu Ze lay in wait, biding his time.

Once he found an opportunity, he used the speed of his Divine Travel Talisman to swiftly approach the Zhao brothers.

Although both were at the Yin God Realm, Wu Ze was far superior.

With a swift stroke of his sword, Mo Dao struck, and before the Third Prince and the other Yang God Realm cultivators could even intervene, the Zhao brothers' heads flew high into the air, their lives utterly extinguished!

Chapter 245 The Power of the People
.

Truly, these are just unseen cultivators from the wilderness, unaware of the true dragon's strength.

Outside Qingzhou City.

He was only responsible for maintaining order; he didn't really know the specifics.

Inside the Mountain Tower.

Bai Liang, who had just returned, hadn't even had time to celebrate his triumphant return before starting to deal with the accumulated affairs.

These were all decisions Xiao Nian couldn't make, so he had to leave them for him to handle upon his return.

The temple expansion has been rejected; there aren't enough funds to support it at the moment, so it has to be postponed.

They were mostly miscellaneous matters, nothing major.

There were simply too many people coming from all over Qingzhou.

With so many people, things easily become chaotic and problems can arise.

At first, everyone was obedient.

But as time went on, some people began to get impatient and started causing trouble.

Initially, they could be suppressed, but as the number of troublemakers increased, you can't suppress everyone, can you?

Public opinion and the will of millions cannot be silenced.

However, they still lingered around Qianshan County, refusing to leave.

Asking hundreds of thousands of people to do hard labor was harder than climbing to heaven for him.

He turned and whispered a few words to He Liyuan.

He Liyuan’s expression was strange, then he nodded and turned to leave.

Soon, news spread from Qianshan County.

The Mountain God, in his great compassion, could not bear to see the people suffer, and thus bestowed divine water upon all who came to the sacred mountain.

Drinking this divine water would dispel illness and evil, purify the mind, and even offer a chance to awaken one's talent for cultivation.

Upon hearing this news, the people gathered around Qianshan County gasped in astonishment!

No one doubted the authenticity of the divine water, for they had long heard of its miraculous effects.

The residents of Qianshan County had all drunk it.

Every time they heard the residents of Qianshan County boast about the divine water's efficacy, they were filled with envy.

Now they too could enjoy the legendary divine water?

Wu Ze had just arrived at the entrance of the Mountain Tower when he heard this news.

His expression was somewhat astonished; he hadn't expected Bai Liang to solve the problem so simply and directly.

However, this method was indeed very effective!

Upon learning of Wu Ze's return, Bai Liang and the others immediately came out to greet him, exchanging pleasantries.

When the matter of the Zhao brothers was mentioned, Wu Ze simply replied that the matter had been resolved without any unexpected incidents.

Upon hearing this, Bai Liang and the others breathed a sigh of relief and smiled.

He had received the Third Prince's message shortly after learning that Wu Ze had killed the Zhao brothers.

The Third Prince was furious, stating that Wu Ze was lawless and dared to kill even members of the Zhao family in the capital.

Little did they know that the Zhao family wielded considerable power in the capital; even the Third Prince had to show them respect.

Furthermore, Zhao Bufan and Zhao Fuhu were the two most outstanding members of the Zhao family's younger generation.

Wu Ze's move had effectively wiped out the Zhao family's younger generation.

The Zhao family would undoubtedly be furious and would certainly seek revenge, otherwise they would lose all face.

"Let the Zhao family come!" Wu Ze said coldly.

Chapter 246 A large amount of merit is on its way
.

Completely forgetting about Qianshan County.

It took several days to ensure that hundreds of thousands of people had access to the divine water.

Those who drank the divine water became much more stable, no longer as agitated as before.

At this moment, Bai Liang stepped forward again, delivering an inspiring speech, praising the Mountain God once more, before instructing everyone to proceed to the planned site for the new county town and work together to build it.

No one objected; everyone followed Bai Liang's instructions, and the large group headed towards the planned construction site for the new county town.

There will be no barriers between Xin County and Qianshan County.

In the future, as they develop, they can be directly merged to become Qianshan City.

Meanwhile, the people of Longyun County began to play their part.

In a tavern somewhere in Qingzhou.

"Hey, have you heard? The Third Prince led the capital's elite to drive away the demon army that wanted to annex Qingzhou, saving the people of Qingzhou. Truly a great deed!"

A commoner, unaware of the details, exclaimed in admiration.

A merchant from Longyun County immediately retorted.

"Who am I? I'm the one who escaped from Longyun County! It was the cultivators from Qianshan County who personally protected us as we evacuated Longyun County that day!"

The merchant slammed his hand on the table and sneered.

The crowd exclaimed in surprise.

This scene unfolded throughout Qingzhou, spreading rapidly, one person telling ten, ten telling a hundred.

Shenshan.

Li Yue quietly observed the unfolding events.

From Bai Liang's group's return, to the construction of the new county, to the spread of public opinion among the people of Longyun County, he had witnessed it all.

The result was a rapid and continuous increase in his merit points.

And it seemed a large amount of merit was on its way.

This campaign to expel the demon army had been incredibly successful.

The number of believers had increased significantly.

However, Qianshan County was developing too quickly and couldn't immediately accommodate such a large population.

It still needed time to settle down, and the shortage of manpower was also a problem.

"It seems I need to bless a few more people," Li Yue thought to himself.

Chapter 247 My heart is too narrow; it can only accommodate two people.
.

Li Yue already had someone in mind to choose as the recipient of the new blessing.

Qianshan County had been under his observation for a long time, and he naturally knew perfectly well who was most suitable to receive the blessing.

Looking around Qianshan County, it was evening, and the streets were bustling with people, merchants hawking their wares, creating a lively atmosphere.

Patrol teams were also patrolling, preventing any riots.

The residents were already used to the presence of the patrol teams. At first, they might have been somewhat afraid of the patrol teams because of the yamen runners.

But as time went on, they discovered that the patrol teams wouldn't harass, extort, or exploit them like the yamen runners; on the contrary, they were eager to help them.

Sometimes, if someone lost something, they could ask the patrol teams, and they would even help find it.

In short, the patrol teams of Qianshan County were completely different from the yamen runners of the imperial court.

People would greet the patrol teams with genuine smiles, not feigned ones.

As the patrol leader, Du Tiandao was highly respected by the residents of Qianshan County.

Especially after his triumphant return, Du Tiandao had achieved remarkable results. Although the strongest demon he killed was only at the fourth level, the sheer number of demons he killed was a significant advantage, and he also brought along cultivators from the patrol, honing their combat skills.

Bai Liang rewarded Du Tiandao handsomely, praising his formidable strength, especially his brute force—truly a man of superhuman strength!

Du Tiandao was flattered and overwhelmed with excitement!

How long ago was it that he had wandered the land as a mere laborer for many years? He never imagined such a day would come.

Now, he was content with everything he had and dared not ask for more.

For example, to receive the blessing of the Mountain God, like Lord Bai and Lord Wu.

He didn't want much; he only wanted to be able to cultivate. As for becoming a powerful figure, he dared not ask for too much.

His current strength, at the Profound Realm, was already sufficient.

Once Elder Zhou and Master Bian Nong refined even better pills, he could advance further, his future bright.

Besides cultivation, he was also the commander of a patrol team, managing a patrol of over a thousand people.

Beyond his career, there was also love.

Recently, Du Tiandao had been frequenting the restaurant, naturally owned by Long Qianqian.

Later, Du Tiandao came into her sights again.

Born into this world, a woman like Long Qianqian desperately longs for a safe haven.

A vulnerable woman finds it difficult to walk alone.

That's why she and Du Tiandao hit it off.

As for the past, he had long forgotten it.

His heart was small, only able to hold two people, one big and one small.

Du Tiandao and Long Qianqian's relationship warmed up, but he still didn't forget his mission and didn't indulge in romance.

Every day, he diligently led his men on patrols throughout the county, nipping any potential crises in the bud.

Li Yue observed everything Du Tiandao did.

Firstly, his talent was average, but he possessed innate divine strength and a formidable physical body.

Moreover, this divine strength increased with his cultivation level, not stagnating at the mortal stage.

This was something Du Tiandao only recently discovered.

Previously, he thought the increase in strength was due to his improved cultivation level.

However, after sparring with a friend at the same cultivation level, he discovered that his strength was several times greater, and only then did he understand the reason.

Then there's his character, which is beyond question.

When he first joined Qianshan County, he trusted Bai Liang without question.

Later, he was diligent and dedicated, wholeheartedly serving Qianshan County.

Of course, most importantly, Du Tiandao was extremely loyal to the Mountain God!

That alone was enough!

“You’re the one.” Li Yue looked down at Du Tiandao, and with a thought, cast the Mountain God's Blessing.

In their eyes, any change in the sacred mountain was a manifestation of the Mountain God.

"This golden light...is..."

Bai Liang was utterly astonished, then his heart pounded with excitement. His divine sense seemed to sense something, becoming extremely active.

He quickly realized that this golden light was the Mountain God's blessing!

Would someone in Qianshan County receive another blessing?

Wu Ze and Li Xue also sensed the change and came out, looking at the sky with astonished expressions.

Chu Wuliu and the others were utterly astonished, thinking it was a manifestation of the Mountain God.

"Amitabha! The Mountain God has manifested! Is this a decree?"

Sanfu asked slowly, his eyes filled with piety.

Bai Liang shook his head, saying, "It shouldn't be."

He then told the others that the golden light might be a blessing from the Mountain God, but he didn't know who would receive it.

Upon hearing that it was a blessing from the Mountain God, everyone's expressions changed, and their breathing became rapid.

They had all witnessed blessings before.

Among them, Li Xue's blessing was the most terrifying. For a little girl at the fourth level of the Divine Enlightenment Realm to slay a seventh-level Demon King, one could imagine how powerful the blessing was.

Naturally, everyone hoped to receive a blessing themselves.

But the chances were extremely slim. Chu Wuliu and the others dared not even dream of it, after all, they had only recently become the Mountain God's followers.

Chapter 248 Divine Power Talisman
.

A group of people nervously looked up at the sky.

They were all wondering who would receive the Mountain God's blessing.

Just as they were speculating,

a golden light suddenly burst forth, then transformed into a golden beam and fell upon Qianshan County.

Du Tiandao, who was busy maintaining order in the streets, trying to calm the residents who were overwhelmed by the Mountain God's manifestation, was frantically trying to manage the situation.

Suddenly, he noticed the crowd staring at the sky in astonishment and paused for a moment.

"Gold...golden light is coming towards us!"

They rushed forward, wanting to touch the golden light.

In their hearts, the golden light appearing on the sacred mountain must be something good, and couldn't possibly harm them.

The golden light was incredibly fast, arriving before Du Tiandao in the blink of an eye.

Before Du Tiandao could react, the golden light completely entered his body.

Instantly, his body shimmered with light, and his internal energy surged.

Already at the third level of the Profound Realm, he felt a dense power flowing into his body.

He quickly reacted, immediately sitting cross-legged and closing his eyes to absorb the power entering his body.

The most precious part of the Mountain God's blessing is naturally the divine talisman.

Divine talismans are incredibly powerful, exceeding the limits of ordinary people's imagination.

The mere fact that he could ignore the limits of talent was already terrifying.

Beside him, Chu Wuliu and the others watched Du Tiandao in the arena, filled with envy.

The crowd waited quietly. After a full quarter of an hour, Du Tiandao finally unleashed a surge of spiritual energy, officially entering the fourth-tier Enlightenment Realm.

"This...he broke through already?"

Hu Zhengqiang looked on in disbelief. Du Tiandao, who had just been in the Profound Realm and only at the mid-stage, had broken through to the Enlightenment Realm in less than half an hour.

Even the breakthrough process was skipped, as simple as drinking water—this was unbelievable!

Du Tiandao slowly opened his eyes, his expression excited, though still somewhat dazed.

He had actually received the Mountain God's blessing, and his cultivation level had directly broken through to the Enlightenment Realm.

Before, he had only dared to dream of it; he never expected to actually be chosen by the Mountain God.

"Disciple Du Tiandao kowtows in gratitude for the Mountain God's blessing!"

Du Tiandao immediately trembled with excitement, bowing deeply towards the sacred mountain, kowtowing dozens of times.

He stopped only when his forehead bled.

His heart was filled with both excitement and boundless joy.

"Congratulations, Tiandao, on receiving the Mountain God's blessing."

At this moment, Bai Liang and the others stepped forward to congratulate him.

The others also offered their congratulations.

Du Tiandao quickly turned around and replied, "This is all thanks to the Mountain God's blessing!"

Bai Liang and the others nodded in deep agreement.

They owed everything they had today to the Mountain God!

Some things were better left unsaid, so Bai Liang brought Du Tiandao back to the Mountain Pavilion.

The others sensed that Bai Liang and the others had something to discuss, so they tactfully took their leave.

Once only Bai Liang, Wu Ze, Li Xue, and Du Tiandao remained in the room, Bai Liang finally asked, "Tiandao, what is the name of the divine talisman you received?"

Du Tiandao replied solemnly, "Divine Power Talisman!"

"Divine Power Talisman?"

Bai Liang and the others looked surprised upon hearing this.

"Judging from the name, it's related to power?" Wu Ze asked curiously.

Because Du Tiandao himself possessed innate divine strength, and the divine talisman he received was precisely the Divine Power Talisman, it was indeed a coincidence.

Du Tiandao nodded, a smile appearing on his face. "That's right, it's related to power!"

Then, he explained the effects of the Divine Power Talisman to Bai Liang and the others.

Simply put, just like its name suggests, activating the Divine Power Talisman amplifies power, granting divine strength capable of splitting mountains and sweeping across lakes and seas.

"I'll give it a try," Wu Ze said eagerly.

He then had Du Tiandao activate the Divine Power Talisman and compete with him in strength.

He was a fifth-tier Yin God Realm cultivator, and a Full Moon Yin God at that, far stronger than ordinary Yin God Realm cultivators.

Du Tiandao, on the other hand, was only at the Enlightened God Realm, and had only just entered that realm. Normally, he shouldn't be stronger than Wu Ze in terms of power.

But after Du Tiandao activated the Divine Power Talisman, the result was astonishing. In terms of pure strength, Wu Ze was actually no match, his face flushed red from Du Tiandao's suppression.

"Hiss, this power is incredible!"

After experiencing Du Tiandao's power, Wu Ze gasped.

It's no wonder these are divine talismans bestowed by the Mountain God; none of them are to be underestimated.

Du Tiandao hadn't expected the divine power talisman to be so powerful, and his excitement grew even stronger.

After casting the Mountain God's Blessing, Li Yue quietly watched this scene.

Now, including this divine power talisman, he had bestowed five talismans.

He wanted to bless another person, but unfortunately, the other recipient had been sent out on a mission by Bai Liang.

He would have to wait until the recipient returned before bestowing the blessing.

"If I continue bestowing blessings, I wonder if another blood-red talisman will appear?"

Li Yue suddenly wondered.

Of the five divine talismans bestowed, only Li Xue's Divine Fate Talisman was blood-red; the others were gold.

The blood-red talisman seemed more powerful than the gold one, but it also had significant side effects.

It could only be said that there were advantages and disadvantages.

As for why the blood-red talisman appeared, Li Yue had some guesses.

However, he wasn't quite sure yet and would need to see the result of the sixth blessing to know the reason.

Chapter 249 Steady Development
.

The matter of the divine talisman can be set aside for now.

Li Yue's divine sense hovered high in the sky, overlooking the entire Liaoyun Prefecture.

His divine sense now covered a full three hundred li, encompassing almost seventy to eighty percent of Liaoyun Prefecture's territory.

It included several county towns, as well as newly established counties.

"The population is increasing rapidly, two to three times more than before."

Li Yue silently surveyed the scene. Since the imperial court announced the abandonment of Qingzhou, the number of people coming to Liaoyun Prefecture had indeed increased dramatically.

They were arriving almost continuously. The population of Tianyue County, which had been declining, had been forcefully reversed.

Now, not only had it reversed, but it had increased by hundreds of thousands, almost breaking the million-person mark.

The personnel responsible for managing Tianyue County were already overwhelmed.

To prevent any unexpected incidents, Bai Liang had already dispatched a patrol team to maintain order.

Besides ordinary people coming to Liaoyun County, low-level cultivators neglected by the imperial court also flocked to join them, providing Qianshan County with ample manpower.

Most of these cultivators were trustworthy; even those who weren't, once they drank the divine water, were considered one of their own.

Bai Liang and his group were now increasingly adept at using the divine water.

Anyone involved in Qianshan County affairs, regardless of their origin or background, was boldly employed.

With the divine water, they felt completely at ease.

Xiao Nian had been incredibly busy lately, handling a multitude of affairs.

Qianshan County now indirectly controlled the entire Liaoyun County.

The remaining counties had also been brought under control.

Personnel had already been dispatched to take over and directly manage them.

Preliminary estimates put the population of Liaoyun County at four million.

This population size was quite alarming; the previously stored food supplies were already running low.

Therefore, Bai Liang had no choice but to send He Liyuan, the former head of the escort agency, back into the world of martial arts to procure more grain.

Fortunately, He Liyuan hadn't lost contact with his old friends and directly contacted grain merchants in Chizhou, gathering several grain traders to collect a large quantity of grain.

He Liyuan is now heading to Chizhou to receive the grain.

Because the other party wouldn't provide escort, saying that Qingzhou had broken away from the imperial court's protection and was too dangerous, they didn't want to take the risk.

With Liaoyun County under control, the most important task at hand was to build more mountain god temples so that all the people of Liaoyun County could worship the mountain god.

This task was difficult and stressful.

It was hard for one person to handle alone.

Therefore, Bai Liang assigned this task to several people:

Qian Ke, Chen Jianchi, and Qilin.

In addition, Bian Nong and other Yin Shen Realm cultivators from Baijiang Prefecture also expressed their willingness to contribute to the sacred mountain.

Bai Liang was more than happy to agree.

Having a Yin God Realm expert handle this matter would only yield better results and reduce the difficulty.

"By the way, in the other counties, there are still temples and Taoist monasteries remaining. These are remnants of the Golden Dragon Temple and the Xuanqing Temple. They have many followers. When you promote the Mountain God, be careful not to provoke them."

Bai Liang spoke up, offering a warning.

He didn't want to incite any conflict between believers.

Whether it was Buddhism or Taoism, Qianshan County wouldn't deliberately denigrate them; the best approach was to subtly change people's perceptions.

As long as they saw that the Mountain God could truly protect them, they would naturally follow him.

Bian Nong and the others understood; they were seasoned veterans and knew exactly what was going on.

After arranging these matters, Bai Liang took out the Mountain Token and tried to contact Kong Wang, but there was still no response.

"It seems Kong Wang has truly ventured beyond the borders to the Wilderness."

Bai Liang frowned, a hint of worry in his expression.

The borders were far too dangerous, and with the current tense situation in the Wilderness, even the saints of demons were paying attention.

Even with the Divine Concealment Talisman's assistance, it was difficult to guarantee that Kong Wang wouldn't encounter danger.

He was actually quite curious about the situation in the Wilderness, wanting to know what stage it had reached.

Will the barbarian demigods ultimately succeed in attaining enlightenment and breaking the current stalemate?

He couldn't fathom it, nor could he ascertain the situation in the barbarian wilderness.

It was simply too far away, hundreds of thousands of miles apart.

At such a distance, perhaps only a demigod-level being could descend to earth through the void with their true spirit and intervene.

Even an eighth-tier Venerable couldn't do that.

Shaking his head, Bai Liang stopped thinking about it; the distance was too vast, and thinking about it was pointless.

He'd rather consider the situation in Qingzhou.

Yesterday, a man named Yue Qiu came to Qianshan County and found him.

He also informed him of the situation in Qingzhou City.

He said that the geniuses of the capital were gradually returning to the capital.

Only a few remained in Qingzhou, saying they wanted to continue their training.

Among them were the Four Vajras of the Golden Dragon Temple, these four were genuine Yang God Realm experts.

Bai Liang recognized Yue Qiu; he was the cultivator who had accompanied Kong Wang last time. However, knowing someone's face doesn't guarantee their true intentions, Bai Liang remained wary of Yue Qiu.

Only after offering Yue Qiu a cup of tea brewed with divine water did he gradually lower his guard and greet him with a smile.

"So, there are at least seven or eight of the capital's prodigies remaining in Qingzhou City?"

Bai Liang pondered for a moment before speaking slowly.

Yue Qiu nodded, his tone solemn. "Yes, the Third Prince was eager to return to the capital to claim credit, so he went back early. Most of the prodigies followed him back. Those who stayed, I think, all have ulterior motives, like that blind woman."

"That Yun Lanxi? The Imperial Preceptor's disciple?" Bai Liang asked in surprise.

"Yes! That's her!" Yue Qiu nodded emphatically, then solemnly recounted the task Yun Lanxi had secretly asked him to do last time: to spread the news that the capital's prodigies were coming to Qingzhou for training.

Bai Liang was utterly astonished upon hearing this.

"So, you're the one who spread this news," he said, somewhat speechless.

Last time, the Third Prince had thoroughly investigated the traitors within the ranks because of this matter, eventually suspecting the Zhao brothers, though no evidence was found.

"There's another man named Bai Lihuo who asked me to deliver this letter to you, Lord Bai."

Yue Qiu took out another letter and handed it to Bai Liang.

Bai Liang's expression remained unchanged. Bai Lihuo was his cousin, with whom he had previously had little interaction.

He took the letter, opened it, and glanced at it.

The letter didn't mention anything important; it was just a younger cousin sending greetings to his elder cousin. It ended by mentioning that next year would be the 200th birthday of the Bai family's patriarch, and asked if Bai Liang would return.

After reading it, Bai Liang felt no particular emotion.

Chapter 250 Plotting in Qingzhou
.

Although he shared the surname Bai, Bai Liang felt no sense of belonging to the Bai family.

Aside from his sister, whom he cared about, everything else seemed indifferent to him.

After thinking for a while, Bai Liang decided that if he had time next year, he might return to the Bai family.

“By the way, Lord Bai, where did Master Kongwang go?”

Yue Qiu suddenly asked.

“He went abroad,” Bai Liang replied simply.

“Ah?” Yue Qiu was quite taken aback. Abroad? That was such a dangerous place!

Master Kongwang had actually gone there.

He felt a little disappointed; he had been thinking of seeking out Master Kongwang to ask him some questions.

“What are your plans now?” Bai Liang suddenly asked Yue Qiu.

Yue Qiu was startled, then hesitated for a moment before saying, “I want to go back to Qingzhou City. That’s my hometown. I don’t want to see Qingzhou City become more and more chaotic, losing its former prosperity.”

His tone became increasingly firm as he finished speaking.

Bai Liang chuckled upon hearing this: "What can a mere Tongfa Realm cultivator like you do in Qingzhou City?"

He felt Yue Qiu's ambitions were high, but his abilities were completely inadequate.

Yue Qiu looked slightly embarrassed; he knew his abilities were lacking.

But his lack of cultivation couldn't stop his desire to do something.

"Then go ahead. If you need any help, just contact Qianshan County," Bai Liang said slowly.

Yue Qiu's heart stirred, his expression moved: "Thank you for your support, Lord Bai!"

"Don't rush to thank me. If you don't have the ability, Qianshan County won't offer you any assistance. So tell me first, what's the first thing you'll do after returning to Qingzhou City?" Bai Liang took a sip of tea and then said.

"Before I came here, I already had some ideas."

At this, Yue Qiu became a different person, more invigorated.

“Currently, there are very few cultivators in Qingzhou City. The strongest are just a few of the capital's prodigies who remained. But they completely ignore the affairs of Qingzhou City, only caring about their own comfort, so we can disregard them.”

“Qingzhou City is currently fragmented, with over a dozen gangs occupying sections of the city streets. They fight and brawl every day, vying for territory, with absolutely no order. The people are suffering terribly, always fearing danger when they go out.”

“Therefore, what I want to do is, using the influence left behind by Master Kongwang, unite all the people to drive out these gangs and then completely control Qingzhou City.”

Yue Qiu became increasingly excited as he spoke. In the past, this kind of thing would have been beyond his capabilities as a mere Tongfa Realm cultivator.

But the situation in Qingzhou is different now. The high-level cultivators have all left, leaving only low-level cultivators to wreak havoc, thus giving him an opportunity.

“Is that so?”

Bai Liang pondered slightly. He hadn't expected Qingzhou City to have fallen into such chaos.

Last time in Qingzhou City, with Kong Wang in charge, things hadn't fallen into disarray. But now that Kong Wang is gone, Yue Qiu, who remained, could initially keep his men in check, but over time, things completely spiraled out of control.

"It seems now is the perfect time to extend my reach into Liaoyun County!" Bai Liang thought to himself.

Originally, he planned a steady, step-by-step approach, first absorbing Liaoyun County, then gradually conquering the entire Qingzhou.

However, it now seems that a steady development plan can't keep up with the changing circumstances. If he waits until he's absorbed Liaoyun County before targeting the whole of Qingzhou, then Qingzhou will likely be in complete chaos by then.

Now is the best time to intervene in other parts of Qingzhou.

But Qianshan County simply doesn't have enough manpower; even taking over the entire Liaoyun County is difficult now.

So, for now, it seems he can only hope that Yue Qiu will first scout the situation.

"Alright, then go back to Qingzhou and implement your plan!"

Bai Liang slammed his hand on the table, but then changed his tone, saying, "However, I also have a condition: you must spread the fame of the Mountain God in Qingzhou City, establish a Mountain God Temple, and develop followers!"

"This is my duty, I will not forget!" Yue Qiu solemnly promised.

He knew the Mountain God's power and felt a deep respect for him.

Bai Liang nodded in satisfaction upon seeing this.

He then saw Yue Qiu off, but before leaving, Yue Qiu expressed his wish to visit the main temple to pay homage to the Mountain God.

Bai Liang readily agreed without much hesitation.

For the Mountain God's followers, the main temple was now a place of pilgrimage.

It was located at the foot of the mountain, the first temple dedicated to the Mountain God, and the site of grand sacrificial ceremonies.

Being able to visit the main temple to pay homage to the Mountain God was an immense honor.

However, due to the temple's sensitive location—near a sacred spring and the only path leading to the sacred mountain—it was inaccessible from other places, blocked by an invisible barrier.

Forcing one's way up the mountain would only result in death; one would be reduced to ashes, leaving not even a corpse behind.

Therefore, the Main Temple is now a vital location in Qianshan County, guarded by a Yin God Realm expert, and unauthorized personnel are forbidden from approaching.

Only with permission can one go to the Main Temple.

Upon receiving Bai Liang's permission, Yue Qiu was overjoyed.

Being able to visit the Main Temple to worship the Mountain God before returning to Qingzhou City meant he had no regrets.

It is said that worshipping the Mountain God in the Main Temple offers a chance to receive a response from the Mountain God!

Unfortunately, Yue Qiu did not receive the Mountain God's attention, but he was already very satisfied.

After leaving the Main Temple, Bai Liang enthusiastically poured him several large jugs of divine water, as if afraid he hadn't drunk enough.

Yue Qiu was deeply moved, thinking that since Lord Bai cared for him so much, he must work even harder for Qianshan County and for the Mountain God!

After seeing Yue Qiu off, Bai Liang let out a breath and muttered, "Having given so much divine water, there shouldn't be any problems."

He had only just met Yue Qiu and didn't know his character.

So, giving him divine water was the best course of action.

After finishing his miscellaneous tasks, Bai Liang went to his cultivation cave halfway up the mountain. The spiritual energy there was becoming increasingly dense.

His cultivation had already reached the late stage of the Enlightenment Realm, while Li Xue had reached the peak of the Enlightenment Realm and was about to break through to the Yin God Realm.

Their progress was incredibly rapid, making the old Yin Gods like Bian Nong extremely envious.

Wu Ze, on the other hand, buried himself in cultivation, striving to break through to the Yang God Realm as soon as possible.

"It's a pity I couldn't break through to the Yin God Realm during the extreme Yin time; I missed the Full Moon Yin God Realm." Bai Liang felt a pang of regret.

Missing the Full Moon Yin God Realm meant waiting twelve years, but twelve years was simply too long. Therefore, he could only hope for the Heavenly Eclipse Yang God Realm.

Calculating the days, the extreme Yang time was still more than six years away.

At their current cultivation speed, they could break through to the Yang God Realm in two years.

This meant waiting four years for nothing, or simply forgoing the Heavenly Eclipse Yang God Realm and directly achieving the ordinary Yang God Realm.

But he was somewhat unwilling to accept that.
