Chapter 251 The Realm of Trials
.

"It's alright. Ordinary spirits are no bad either. Anyway, we have all been blessed by the Mountain God. The path is endless, and sainthood is the ultimate goal. There's no need to worry about the mud on the way."

Uze spoke up.

"Yes, I was too impatient."

Bai Liang nodded after hearing this, and he understood.

The Full Moon Yin God and the Heavenly Eclipse Yang God only possess slightly more combat power than ordinary Yin and Yang Gods; there's no qualitative leap.

This is an incredibly rare opportunity for other cultivators.

But for those who have received blessings from the Mountain God and possess divine talismans within them, it's merely icing on the cake.

After all, they have no cultivation barriers and can directly cultivate to the Saint realm.

Their talent is limitless; they can be considered the prodigies of this era.

Just as Bai Liang had come to terms with it and begun to abandon the Full Moon Yin God and the Heavenly Eclipse Yang God, Li Xue suddenly cried out in surprise from inside the cave.

"Huh, what's that?!"

Li Xue's exclamation rang out, tinged with astonishment.

Upon hearing this, the two quickly rushed deeper into the cave.

Then, they saw a huge and deep cave entrance before Li Xue.

The cave entrance was utterly dark, and they couldn't see what was inside.

"What's going on? How did a cave entrance appear here?" Wu Ze frowned, looking utterly bewildered.

He was extremely familiar with this cultivation cave.

It should have been a stone wall; how did it suddenly turn into a cave entrance?

He immediately questioned Li Xue.

Li Xue didn't know either, shaking her head. "I don't know either. I was cultivating here just now when I suddenly heard a strange phenomenon, and then when I opened my eyes, I saw this cave entrance."

"It appeared suddenly?"

Wu Ze was surprised, but remembering that this was a sacred mountain, he calmed down.

This must be the work of the Mountain God; nothing serious.

"Look quickly! Behind this cave entrance is a giant gourd!"

Bai Liang exclaimed in surprise, his face filled with disbelief.

Wu Ze and Li Xue quickly went to Bai Liang's side and looked behind the cave entrance.

They saw that the extension of the cave entrance was a bottleneck-like structure, beyond which was a huge, round object, connected to an even larger round object.

From a distance, it looked like a gourd lying on its side.

The cave entrance they saw was actually the mouth of the gourd.

This giant gourd was a purplish-gold color, incredibly dazzling and brilliant.

The three were all stunned by the sudden appearance of the gourd, unable to understand why one would appear in a cave.

Anything that appeared on the sacred mountain must be the work of the Mountain God.

Bai Liang boldly reached out and touched the giant gourd. The gourd began to tremble, an aura connecting with the divine talisman within him, and then a message entered his mind.

After a dozen breaths, Bai Liang read the message, gradually understanding its meaning. His eyes shone brightly, his excitement overflowing.

“A purple-gold gourd, the light of the extreme yin moon!? Is this the Mountain God's grand gesture!?”

Bai Liang exclaimed, his voice filled with utter shock.

Wu Ze and the other two, somewhat puzzled, also touched the purple-gold gourd.

The same message was immediately transmitted to their minds.

A moment later, they understood the function of the Purple-Gold Gourd.

Wu Ze exclaimed in amazement, "The Trial Realm! This Purple-Gold Gourd actually contains a whole world within it, filled with countless rays of extremely Yin moonlight! Truly worthy of the Mountain God; only a deity could possess such power!"

Li Xue was incredibly excited. If this meant she could also become a Full Moon Yin God?

This made her eager to enter the Trial Realm, absorb the extremely Yin moonlight, and attempt to break through to the fifth-tier Yin God realm.

"Don't go in yet. There's a sixth-tier demon within this Trial Realm. Although it's been suppressed to fifth-tier strength by the Trial Realm, it's still not to be underestimated. We must be fully prepared before entering."

Bai Liang stopped the eager Li Xue, urging her to calm down.

The message just transmitted by the Purple-Gold Gourd explained the requirements of the Trial Realm.

To enter and absorb the extreme Yin moonlight to achieve the Full Moon Yin Godhood is possible, but only if you can defeat the demon trapped inside the Purple-Gold Gourd alone.

Defeating it allows you to absorb the extreme Yin moonlight and break through your cultivation level.

If you can't defeat it, you'll have to retreat.

Li Xue is currently at the peak of the Enlightenment Realm. Defeating a demon whose cultivation has been suppressed from the sixth rank to the fifth rank alone won't be easy.

This is because Li Xue has very little combat experience and primarily relies on the Divine Life Talisman in battle.

However, the Divine Life Talisman is unnecessary and cannot be used inside the Purple-Gold Gourd.

Therefore, they can only rely on Li Xue's own strength, as does Bai Liang.

"I can do it, Uncle Bai," Li Xue said resolutely.

"I know you can, but be careful. Go back and prepare first, then go in and test yourself," Bai Liang said with a smile.

In the end, Li Xue could only nod in agreement.

Bai Liang and Wu Ze observed the Purple-Gold Gourd again and found that it had been fixed deep within the cave.

Unmovable and unshakeable.

"Truly worthy of the Mountain God's handiwork, transcending the mortal realm, a true miracle," Bai Liang exclaimed in admiration.

Wu Ze nodded in agreement, his eyes filled with reverence.

A few days later, Li Xue, having made ample preparations, arrived at the cave entrance again, led by Bai Liang and Wu Ze.

"Are you ready?" Bai Liang asked Li Xue.

Li Xue nodded firmly: "Ready!"

"Then let's go in!" Bai Liang said.

Li Xue nodded and stepped into the deep, dark gourd-shaped opening without hesitation.

As soon as she stepped inside, she found herself in a hazy little world.

There was no sun, moon, or stars here; all she could see was a gray sky.

The soil beneath her feet was exactly the same as outside, nothing special.

But Li Xue's mouth was wide open.

Because within this place, she sensed an incredibly dense, almost overwhelmingly intense, yin moonlight.

The sheer quantity of this moonlight was terrifying.

Floating in the air, it was like countless fireflies shimmering and dancing in the air.

Li Xue's spiritual energy began to surge, sensing the yin moonlight and yearning to absorb it.

However, she quickly suppressed this impulse, her expression becoming alert as she surveyed her surroundings.

The message conveyed by the purple-gold gourd stated that a ferocious demon guarded the place.

To absorb the extreme yin moonlight, one must first defeat the demon!

Therefore, Li Xue immediately focused her attention, frantically scanning her surroundings with her divine sense to avoid a surprise attack.


.
Chapter 252 Merit Soars
.

But after surveying her surroundings and waiting for over fifteen minutes without seeing the demon, Li Xue hesitated.

"That demon is so good at hiding? Is it that patient?"

This was her first thought.

She didn't doubt the accuracy of the information.

Since the demon was so patient, she would just wear it down! Let's see who outlasts whom!

Having made up her mind, Li Xue began to regulate her breathing, while remaining vigilant, preparing for a sneak attack.

Deep within the Purple-Gold Gourd, the Demon of Thought, trapped for several days, had lost all will to live.

She had transformed into human form, her body curled up in a corner, looking utterly pitiful.

She had sensed Li Xue's entry into the Purple-Gold Gourd.

Initially, she was overjoyed, thinking she had a chance to escape, or perhaps a companion had come to share her time in prison.

However, the Purple-Gold Gourd's will revealed that the demon had come for training, and she had now become its subject.

The opponent needed to defeat her before absorbing the Yin Moonlight and breaking through to the Yin God Realm.

Upon learning this, Nianmo felt utterly hopeless.

Didn't this mean she would never have a chance to leave, destined to be used as a punching bag for training for the rest of her life?

In that instant, she wanted to commit suicide.

But inside the Purple-Gold Gourd, even self-harm was difficult, let alone suicide.

Her final fate was to be manipulated by the Purple-Gold Gourd, becoming the first demon to be used as a trial subject in the Trial Grounds.

"Why? I just wanted to go home, how did I end up like this?"

Nianmo's voice was trembling with tears, filled with immense grievance.

Feeling wronged, she wanted to go on strike on her first day at work.

But Li Yue, noticing this, wouldn't let her strike so easily.

If she really went on strike, his Trial Realm would be ruined.

Therefore, Li Yue directly used the Purple Gold Gourd to issue a decree to the Mind Demon, exerting pressure.

The gist was that if it didn't cooperate, the consequences wouldn't be as simple as death.

The Mind Demon felt this will and its body trembled.

Utterly terrified, despite its reluctance, it gritted its teeth and got up, beginning its first day of 'work'.

Li Xue, who had been waiting for a long time, finally saw the demon.

It was an extremely alluring female demon, its strength nearing the mid-stage of the early fifth rank.

She charged straight at her, carrying a powerful aura. Li Xue's expression was solemn as she began to use her swordsmanship, which she had learned from Chu Wuliu.

Although it was only a very ordinary third-rank swordsmanship, her proficiency was high, and combined with her own agility, its power was no less than that of a fourth-rank swordsmanship.

The Mind Demon felt aggrieved and wanted to vent its anger on Li Xue, but the more it fought, the more terrified it became!

This young girl, only at the peak of the Enlightenment Realm, was actually able to withstand her attacks without falling behind!

She was a sixth-tier demon! Although suppressed by a major realm, her insights and techniques far surpassed those of fifth-tier demons.

Even among fifth-tier demons, she was among the strongest.

But now, she was being held back by a young girl at the peak of the Enlightenment Realm.

After a dozen or so rounds of fighting, the Demon of Thought began to fall into a disadvantageous position because she could no longer unleash stronger spells.

The Purple-Gold Gourd was merely a trial stage, so Li Yue naturally wouldn't put too much pressure on his subordinates. After all, besides Li Xue and other recipients of the talisman, others could also enter the trial stage.

Therefore, if the difficulty was too high, others might not be able to pass.

After persisting for several dozen more rounds, the Mind Demon finally succumbed.

Li Xue was also in bad shape, bruised and battered, pale-faced, and utterly exhausted.

She exhausted all her strength to barely defeat her opponent.

"Luckily, I listened to Uncle Bai beforehand and didn't rush in, otherwise I would have really suffered."

Li Xue felt fortunate.

She had come fully prepared this time, and even so, she barely passed the trial.

If she hadn't been prepared, she probably would have had to flee in a sorry state.

After defeating the demonic thought, she could absorb the Yin Moonlight and break through to the Yin God Realm.

Thinking of this, Li Xue became excited again.

Ignoring her exhaustion, she quickly sat cross-legged on the ground and began to sense the circulating Yin Moonlight, slowly guiding it and absorbing it into her body.

With the Yin Moonlight entering her body, she began to attempt to break through her realm.

Outside, Wu Ze and Bai Liang waited anxiously.

Three hours had passed since Li Xue entered the Purple-Gold Gourd.

Both were worried, wondering if Li Xue could defeat the demon and successfully break through to the Yin God Realm.

Suddenly, a noise came from the cave entrance, and the two quickly looked over.

They saw Li Xue emerge from within.

She was now a completely different person from when she had just entered the Purple-Gold Gourd.

She exuded an aura unique to the Yin God Realm, her skin bathed in a faint moonlight, her demeanor distinctly different.

With just one glance, Bai Liang and the other knew Li Xue had successfully broken through.

"Congratulations, Xiao Xue! You've achieved the Full Moon Yin God Realm!" Wu Ze exclaimed sincerely.

Bai Liang smiled slightly, "Your Uncle Bai is really getting old; even your cultivation has been surpassed by you."

The two approached Li Xue and began inquiring about the situation inside the Purple-Gold Gourd.

Li Xue answered everything without hesitation.

Bai Liang listened intently, for he too would soon break through to the Yin God Realm.

Li Yue withdrew his gaze. Having witnessed the scene within the Purple-Gold Gourd, he knew his trial setup was effective.

From now on, his subordinates who wished to achieve the Full Moon Yin God Realm could undergo trials within the Purple-Gold Gourd.

The extreme Yin moonlight was abundant, and even if it was depleted, it could be replenished during the extreme Yin season in 12 years.

As for the 'victim' Nianmo inside the Purple Gold Gourd, Li Yue felt no pity for her.

She bore half the responsibility for the deaths of tens of millions of people in Baijiang Prefecture.

A demon is a demon after all; let her atone for her sins within the Purple Gold Gourd.

Looking at the panel again, his merit points were now close to breaking 20 million.

[Realm: Fifth-Order Sacred Mountain]

[Merit Points: 18.45 million/100 million]

[Divine Abilities: Mountain God's Blessing, Dharma Body of Heaven and Earth, Resumption of Incense Offerings, Gratitude for the Source of Water, Blessing the Earth]

[Spells: Manifestation of Avatar, Control of the Five Elements]

[Magic Weapons: Mountain-Suppressing Seal, Heart Mirror, Divine Weapon Furnace, Wind-Stabilizing Pearl, Purple Gold Gourd]

The population of Liaoyun County was increasing, providing him with even more merit points.

This was before the temples were even built. Once all the mountain god temples in various places were completed, the merit points would surge again.

And if this were expanded to the entire Qingzhou, it was unimaginable how quickly the merit points would increase.

Li Yue suddenly felt that advancing in rank had become easier, not so difficult after all.

"By the way, I haven't used the Blessed Earth ability yet. What would happen if I used it now?"

Li Yue looked at the Blessed Earth ability.


.
Chapter 253 Causes a Stir
.

Currently, the area covered by the Blessed Earth is the territory of one prefecture.

That is to say, it covers the entire Qingzhou.

Logically, casting Blessed Earth now wouldn't yield much benefit.

Because Qingzhou hasn't been completely conquered yet, if Blessed Earth covered the entire prefecture, then the regions outside Liaoyun County would also be blessed by its power, flourishing and transforming into paradises.

In that case, would people still flock to Liaoyun County and become followers of the mountain god?

Therefore, casting Blessed Earth at this moment wouldn't be very beneficial.

However, Li Yue didn't think so.

Qingzhou is currently experiencing a huge upheaval. The imperial court has withdrawn, and demons have invaded. Although the demons have withdrawn, the people of Qingzhou have fallen into panic, and order has been disrupted.

Murder, arson, looting, and rape are happening every moment.

These are just the prelude. It should be noted that due to the chaos, vast amounts of farmland have been destroyed.

They can still survive on stored grain for now, but once that grain is depleted and the crops in the fields fail, a human tragedy will ensue.

Countless people will starve to death!

Some merchants, sensing the crisis, have begun stockpiling grain, buying up the remaining food on the market.

Mountain bandits and thugs have also descended the mountains, directly looting the few remaining crops in the fields.

These actions have further exacerbated the situation in Qingzhou; the fire has already been ignited.

Therefore, Li Yue wants to extinguish these flames before they become a raging inferno.

Blessing the Earth is the best way to put out the fire.

Looking at the description of Blessing the Earth, it is a supernatural power with extremely wide effects.

It can bless all things and revitalize life.

“The court doesn’t want these people, but I do!”

Li Yue’s divine sense slowly rose into the sky, reaching the edge of the heavens.

He looked down upon the entire Liaoyun Prefecture, extending beyond to Qingzhou.

Although his divine sense was limited to a distance of three hundred miles, he could now survey the entire outline of Qingzhou.

Qingzhou stretched for thousands of miles, a vast area inhabited by tens of millions of people.

This world, due to its spiritual energy, had a higher food production than Earth.

This allowed it to support such a large population despite its lower productivity.

But now, Qingzhou was out of order, food production was failing, and without intervention, many were destined to die.

These people were all his potential followers.

Saving them, even for his own sake, was something he couldn't bear to see with corpses piled high and starving.

Li Yue's eyes narrowed, and he immediately cast Blessing the Earth.

A pillar of light shot into the sky from the divine mountain.

This astonishing phenomenon immediately attracted the attention of countless people.

The people of Liaoyun County, and even the entire population of Qingzhou, witnessed this earth-shattering spectacle!

A golden-red pillar of light, dazzling and radiant.

A pillar of light pierced the sky, shattering the gloom that had shrouded Qingzhou for days, bringing clarity to the world.

"Hiss! What is that? How can a pillar of light appear?"

The people outside Liaoyun County exclaimed in disbelief.

"A miracle! This is a miracle! A deity has descended to earth to save us poor people!"

An elderly man, his eyes filled with tears, hurriedly knelt and kowtowed repeatedly, begging for divine protection.

Similar scenes unfolded throughout Qingzhou.

In Qingzhou City.

The remaining geniuses of the capital city flew into the air, their expressions solemn as they gazed at the pillar of light piercing the clouds.

"What is that?" Wu Yuan asked in a deep voice, puzzled.

Even from thousands of miles away, he could feel a tremor emanating from that pillar of light.

This was utterly illogical, indicating that the pillar of light was the work of a supreme being.

Wu Yan, his face grim, stared intently in the direction of the pillar of light.

He remembered this direction clearly; it was the direction of the divine mountain.

Such an astonishing phenomenon could only be the work of the mountain god.

Shangguan Shenghua noticed this too. After his initial surprise, he looked at the four from Jinlong Temple with interest and teased, "Your staying in Qingzhou seems to be related to the Divine Mountain. What are your thoughts now?"

Wu Yan's face darkened. He replied indifferently, "What's it to you? If you stay in Qingzhou, Prime Minister Shangguan will likely be displeased."

Shangguan Shenghua remained calm. "The old man is living too comfortably. It's good to annoy him now and then; it helps to soothe his mind and body."

"Heh, Prime Minister Shangguan really has a good son," Wu Yan said unkindly.

Shangguan Shenghua didn't seem to care, but secretly observed the four of them.

Originally, he had intended to return to the capital with the Third Prince.

But unexpectedly, the four of them decided to stay in Qingzhou.

Later, Wu Yan, Wu Hui, and Yun Lanxi also expressed their desire to stay in Qingzhou.

This completely baffled him.

After a moment's hesitation, he decided to stay and observe the situation.

Since he chose to stay in Qingzhou, Cha Xixing, who considered Shangguan Shenghua's mortal enemy, naturally also chose to stay.

Shangguan Shenghua remained silent, letting Cha Xixing do as he pleased, ignoring him.

Turning his head to look at the pillar of light in the direction of the sacred mountain, his expression gradually became solemn.

Since arriving in Qingzhou, his understanding of the sacred mountain had evolved from initial indifference to later appreciation, then to being moved, and finally to being utterly awestruck.

The image of the sacred mountain had begun to become mysterious and unpredictable.

"To create such a massive commotion, the name of the deity must be truly real,"

Shangguan Shenghua thought to himself.

The others, including Cha Xixing, were equally stunned by the scene before them.

Everyone looked towards the direction of the sacred mountain, their hearts heavy, wondering what this extraordinary phenomenon meant.

Somewhere in the city, Yue Qiu, who had just returned to Qingzhou City, stared in astonishment at the sacred mountain.

His expression was one of overwhelming excitement; in his view, any change in the sacred mountain must be a good thing.

Meanwhile, in Liaoyun County, Bai Liang and his companions, who had just descended the mountain and returned to the Mountain Pavilion, were completely stunned by what they saw.

Everyone looked up, gazing at the sky in disbelief.

"What...what happened?"

Hu Zhengqiang, who had lived most of his life, had never witnessed anything like this before, and was utterly astonished.

Qianshan County was located at the foot of the sacred mountain, which made them feel the terrifying aura emanating from the pillar of light even more acutely.

The pillar of light shot into the sky from the peak of the sacred mountain; without a doubt, it was the work of the Mountain God.

The people of the county, including those planning the construction of the new county, were already completely terrified.

Until someone shouted, "The Mountain God has manifested!"

A cacophony of shouts rose and fell, echoing across the sky.

Countless people knelt and kowtowed towards the sacred mountain, their expressions sincere and devout, all praying for the mountain god's protection and the safety of their families.

Bai Liang and Wu Ze were the first to take to the air.

They activated their divine talismans, carefully sensing the power of the pillar of light, and the results astonished them.

Chapter 254 Blessing All Things
.

"This pillar of light is affecting the entire Qingzhou!?"

Bai Liang exclaimed in surprise.

The golden pillar of light had already dispersed the dark clouds that had gathered in the sky above Qingzhou, bringing a clear and bright sky.

But this wasn't the end. As the dark clouds were dispelled, various auspicious signs began to appear one after another.

Purple aura rose from the east, illuminating the summit of the sacred mountain, like divine light descending from heaven.

Golden clouds shaped like dragons and phoenixes hung high in the sky, a symbol of good fortune.

Furthermore, heavenly rain fell, dispelling the filth of the world, nourishing all things, and bringing endless vitality.

The farmland that had been trampled and destroyed began to sprout tender green shoots and grow vigorously.

Dried-up rivers began to gather rainwater, trickling streams becoming rivers that flowed slowly.

Finally, the rivers formed lakes, where fish, shrimp, and crabs began to frolic.

Everything began to flourish.

Such a tremendous transformation was witnessed by everyone in Qingzhou, from cultivators to beggars on the street.

"This...this is a miracle! A miracle!"

A farmer knelt in his field, tears streaming down his face as he gazed at the newly sprouted, still-growing shoots.

They had sprouted, they had grown; they would no longer go hungry.

Similar scenes unfolded throughout Qingzhou.

The people of Qingzhou, who had previously clung to despair and dread, were now reborn.

And they all knew perfectly well that the source of all this change in Qingzhou was the place from which the pillar of light emanated.

Some began inquiring about the direction of the pillar of light.

The reply they received was always that of Liaoyun County.

And mentioning Liaoyun County inevitably brought to mind the recently rumored sacred mountain.

Connecting the two, some guessed that the pillar of light might very well have been created by the sacred mountain.

This conjecture was quickly confirmed.

People from Liaoyun County excitedly cried out that the Mountain God had manifested, blessing the land and protecting Qingzhou.

The news spread like wildfire, and soon everyone knew that the pillar of light was the work of the Mountain God, who had brought new life to Qingzhou.

So, some people began to bring wooden tablets and build simple shrines for the Mountain God, placing them in their homes as a way to pray for his protection.

More and more people followed suit, and for a time, worshipping the Mountain God became the mainstream practice among the people of Qingzhou.

Li Yue never expected that using the Blessing the Earth technique would bring such unexpected benefits.

Countless people in Qingzhou worshipped him in their homes, which also brought him merit.

Looking at the rapidly increasing merit points on his panel, at this rate, breaking through to the sixth-tier Sacred Mountain was not far off.

However, Blessing the Earth was a powerful divine ability; it consumed a considerable amount of energy, and not in a one-time event, but continuously.

As long as he maintains the Blessed Earth divine power, his power is constantly being depleted.

The larger the area covered, the more power is consumed.

Fortunately, it currently only covers one province, so the power consumption is still within his tolerance range, only about five percent per day.

However, his power recovery speed is very fast, recovering to one hundred percent in a day.

Therefore, this consumption is almost negligible for him.

The impact of Blessed Earth is far greater than Li Yue imagined.

The simultaneous appearance of auspicious omens, rarely seen in a century, in Qingzhou immediately stirred other thoughts in the hearts of some of its people.

Rumors began to circulate subtly.

"Hey, have you heard? Ten auspicious omens have appeared in Qingzhou! This is a sign of a sage's birth! Our Qingzhou is about to rise!"

"What sage's birth? That's all rumors! I'm telling you, don't spread them around. It's said that a true dragon has appeared in our Qingzhou!"

"Hiss! A true dragon? Doesn't that mean it will replace..."

"Be careful what you say! Be careful what you say! Keep it to yourself, don't say it out loud, and definitely don't spread it around."

"What's there to be afraid of? The imperial court has already abandoned Qingzhou. Why bother being careful? That pillar of light came from Liaoyun County. Maybe the true dragon is there too. I plan to go there now; maybe I can even get some credit for following the dragon's rise."

"Take me with you! I want to go too!"

"..."

Conversations like these filled the dark corners of Qingzhou.

Various ambitious individuals were all excited.

After discussing it, they began flocking to Liaoyun County.

Bai Liang was unaware that a group of 'rebels' were on their way to Qianshan County. At this moment, his face was radiant with joy. The blessing bestowed upon the land brought new life to Qingzhou, and even more so to Qianshan County and Liaoyun Prefecture.

Moreover, because of its greater proximity to the sacred mountain, Qianshan County benefited even more.

For example, increased grain yields and the eradication of all diseases.

These changes were quite obvious.

And there were many more potential changes.

"The Mountain God truly has compassion for all living beings, creating such a grand gesture to save the people of Qingzhou. I deeply respect him!"

Sanfu's eyes burned with fervor, like that of a fanatical believer.

The others were the same.

The changes brought about by the blessing upon the land were far beyond their imagination.

Even the two saints on the demon side couldn't have accomplished such a grand gesture, could they?

Only a legendary deity could create something so casually.

"The construction of the new county can be accelerated. It won't be long before the outline of the county seat is established."

Xiao Nian came to Bai Liang, excitedly reporting the news of the new county.

Originally, the construction of the new county wouldn't have been so fast.

After all, it was an entire county seat; building it would be a massive project, taking several years.

But unexpectedly, after Bai Liang had hundreds of thousands of people drink the divine water, he mobilized hundreds of thousands more to participate in the construction.

Those were hundreds of thousands of people! Even if each person laid a brick, they could build a small section of the Great Wall in a month, let alone the foundation of a mere county seat.

Although hundreds of thousands of people were too many and difficult to manage, with Chu Wuliu, the prefectural governor, managing and allocating resources was still easy.

Additionally, the patrol teams maintained order, and the construction of the new county quickly got on track.

"Excellent! Try to connect the new county with Qianshan County; don't separate them. Prepare for future integration," Bai Liang said with a smile.

There's no need to make such a clear distinction between Qianshan County and Xin County.

They'll be merged into one eventually anyway, becoming a single large city, like Qingzhou City, a city that can accommodate millions of people.

“I’ve already made plans,” Xiao Nian nodded in reply.

While Qianshan County was undergoing rapid development and construction, on the other side, Kong Wang, outside the border, was currently being hunted by demons and was almost besieged.

In a dark underground river, Kong Wang sat cross-legged on the dried-up riverbed, meditating and healing.

After seven or eight days of travel, he had now penetrated deep into the demon's territory, where demons appeared more frequently than before.

And just two hundred miles ahead lay the demon's stronghold—the Holy Court!

Chapter 255 Encirclement and Suppression
.

Kong Wang exhaled slowly. His internal injuries had mostly healed, but they still significantly hampered his performance.

He was no longer as strong as he had been in his prime.

"So many powerful beings lurking in this demon-infested land. It seems I underestimated the demons."

Kong Wang murmured softly, his eyes deep and unfathomable.

His pupils flashed with golden light, piercing through the rock wall above him and allowing him to see directly onto the ground.

Countless demons patrolled the ground, their auras blazing, frantically searching every corner, trying to find the human blood sacrifice that had infiltrated.

"Whoever finds that human monk will be rewarded with three thousand blood sacrifices!"

A deep, furious roar rang out, coming from a massive figure in the air.

It was a vulture with a human face and a beast's body, its appearance quite hideous, yet none of the surrounding demons dared to underestimate it.

Because the Vulture Demon's strength was incredibly formidable, ranking among the top in the seventh rank, and even capable of contending with average eighth-rank demons.

Now, with him personally leading the pursuit and encirclement of Kong Wang, setting up an inescapable trap, Kong Wang was caught in a dilemma.

Faced with such overwhelming power, even the Divine Concealment Talisman was difficult to use effectively.

The Vulture Demon possessed eagle-like eyes that could see through illusions; any method of concealment would be exposed to its gaze.

Kong Wang was initially unaware of this, and thus careless, not fully activating the Divine Concealment Talisman to maintain its concealment effect, only ensuring the minimum level of concealment, as this conserved spiritual energy.

The saved spiritual energy could be used for travel.

As a result, upon approaching the Demon Holy Court, he was spotted by the patrolling Vulture Demon, suffering a hidden loss and sustaining a significant injury.

Now, due to his injuries, even fully activating the Divine Concealment Talisman is difficult.

"It cannot be moved; if it moves, it will definitely be detected."

Kong Wang felt a heavy weight in his heart.

He can continue to remain hidden and undetected thanks to the effects of the Concealment Talisman.

However, he cannot move. Any movement will change the situation, and the Vulture Demon will immediately notice.

He could only remain where he was, waiting for his spiritual energy to recover and slowly heal his injuries until he was back to his peak condition. This way, he could fully activate the Divine Concealment Talisman and avoid the vulture's gaze.

However, according to the information he had gathered, the barbarian demigod was likely to attain enlightenment in about ten days.

It would take him at least seven or eight days to recover from his injuries.

This meant he would miss the opportunity to travel to the barbarian battlefield and witness the barbarian demigod's enlightenment in person.

So, what other way is there to avoid the vulture demon's eyes?

Kong Wang frowned in thought.

Searching demons kept passing by, but none of them could spot him.

Suddenly, a figure appeared beside him, tentatively asking, "Are you a friend from Great Chu?"

The tone was strange, as if carrying a hint of irony.

This greeting almost made Kong Wang break out in a cold sweat.

He instantly turned around, his gaze grave as he looked at the dark figure to his side.

This figure was pitch black, making it impossible to tell if it was human or ghost.

The figure was still looking around, and demons passed right through it, unable to touch it at all, as if it were just a phantom.

"Friends of Great Chu, do not worry, I am also human, but from the Southern Frontier tribe, currently under the command of the Barbarians."

The dark figure seemed unsure of Kong Wang's exact location, only vaguely sensing that he was hiding there.

So he began to call out softly to his surroundings.

Kong Wang frowned, a slight thought stirring within him.

He had learned about the Southern Frontier tribe before.

He seemed to have heard about it from the Qilin.

The death of the Second Prince of Great Chu seemed to resemble some of the witchcraft practices of the Southern Frontier tribe.

The rumored Southern Frontier tribe was a mysterious tribe, possessing certain unique forbidden arts.

However, due to their small population and rather strange methods of transmission, they had not flourished.

In the former Great Chu Dynasty's territory, the Southern Frontier tribe was actually within Great Chu's borders, but a portion of the tribe had emigrated, naturally becoming an external tribe.

Unexpectedly, after the chaos caused by demons, the tribes of the Southern Frontier had become subordinate to the Wildlands.

Kong Wang hesitated, wondering if the other party was trustworthy.

The shadowy figure, receiving no response, felt a pang of regret, then called out unwillingly, "Friend of Great Chu, I am not your enemy, but your friend. I am the holy son of the Southern Frontier tribe, ordered to investigate within the demon realm. I happened to witness the Vulture Demons' rampant search and discovered that some of my compatriots were in distress, so I came to help."

"If you trust me, you may simply show yourself. I have a way to escape from the Vulture Demons' eyes!"

This was merely a final attempt. If the other party still did not appear, he would have no choice but to leave.

Shortly after the shadowy figure finished speaking, Kong Wang considered for a few moments, then gradually concealed his Divine Concealment Talisman, releasing a wisp of aura before quickly disappearing again.

"This humble monk is here," Kong Wang said softly.

Hearing the response and sensing the presence of another human, the shadowy figure's spirits lifted. He immediately looked in Kong Wang's direction, excitedly exclaiming, "So you really are human! Are you truly a friend of Great Chu? How did you infiltrate the demon realm? Does this mean Great Chu is finally no longer cowardly and is considering a change of tactics?"

The shadowy figure's tone was light and cheerful, seemingly excited to encounter a human, becoming quite talkative.

Kong Wang hesitated for a moment, then finally shook his head, saying, "This humble monk is not a member of Great Chu."

"Ah? Not from Great Chu!?" The shadowy figure was somewhat bewildered, then seemed to realize something, his expression suddenly darkening, his tone becoming hostile.

"Not a friend of Great Chu? Then you are a traitor of the Dragon-Slaying Sect!?" The shadowy figure's words were now laced with killing intent.

At the same time, a dark mist began to emanate from his body. If the other party was a member of the Dragon-Slaying Sect, then he had fallen into a trap.

Everyone in the world knew that the Dragon-Slaying Sect and the demons were allies.

How could allies engage in such a scene of encirclement and pursuit?

Therefore, the conclusion is obvious: this is a charade orchestrated by the Dragon-Slaying Sect and demons to lure him into a trap.

"Damn traitors, colluding with demons, may you die a horrible death!" the shadowy figure cursed, attempting to flee.

"Wait, I am not a member of the Dragon-Slaying Sect," Kong Wang hurriedly explained, but how could the other party believe him so easily?

"If you're not from the Dragon-Slaying Sect, then where do you come from?"

The shadowy figure scoffed.

"This humble monk comes from the Divine Mountain," Kong Wang said slowly.

"The Divine Mountain? What's the Divine Mountain? I've never heard of it before."

The shadowy figure was taken aback, his tone puzzled.

"The Divine Mountain is located in Qingzhou. You should know something about Qingzhou, shouldn't you?" Kong Wang said.

"Qingzhou!?"

Hearing Qingzhou, the shadowy figure seemed to remember something.

Chapter 256 Witch Gu Goddess
.

"You mean, the Qingzhou that the demons sent an army of 100,000 to attack a while ago?"

The shadowy figure asked in surprise.

Hidden deep within the demon territory, he had naturally heard the news.

Initially, when he heard that the Chu Emperor had abandoned Qingzhou and withdrawn all the court's powerful officials, he cursed the emperor, calling him a fool.

Ceding a province to the demons, treating the lives of tens of millions of people like dirt, was utterly incompetent, a complete tyrant.

But what followed and the outcome left this holy son of a southern tribe completely bewildered.

The demon army of 100,000 had barely arrived in Qingzhou when they encountered resistance and ultimately suffered a setback.

Even more unbelievable was that the Demon Serpent, one of the seventh-tier demon kings leading the army, was actually killed by the people of Great Chu.

The shadowy figure hadn't gathered much information, only knowing that the Demon Serpent had been killed by human cultivators from Qingzhou.

Since they were human cultivators from Qingzhou, they must be from the Great Chu, the shadowy figure assumed.

Upon hearing this, the shadowy figure was already utterly shocked.

But what followed was even more unbelievable.

Having suffered a setback and lost a seventh-tier demon king, the demon army was unwilling to be stopped so easily.

Therefore, they dispatched three more seventh-tier demons to Qingzhou.

It is said that initially, Quan Xiaotian of Qingzhou strongly requested the Holy Court to send an eighth-tier Venerable to Qingzhou.

But at this time, the battle on the barbarian front was intensifying, and the eighth-tier Venerable was simply unavailable. Thus, only seventh-tier demons could fill the gap.

Three seventh-tier demons, plus Quan Xiaotian, made a total of four seventh-tier individuals.

Such a force should have been more than capable of taking over Qingzhou, which had long been abandoned by the Great Chu.

But the result was still incredibly unpredictable: another seventh-tier demon king died in Qingzhou, and a powerful one at that.

Rumors even circulated that the human cultivator who killed the seventh-tier demonic vine was apparently not even at the sixth-tier Yang God realm.

Such an extraordinary feat initially made the shadowy figure doubt the veracity of the news, wondering if it was just a joke spread by demons.

However, the subsequent withdrawal of the demon army from Qingzhou gradually convinced him of the rumor.

So he wondered, what kind of monstrous talent had the Great Chu Dynasty cultivated? How could someone with less than a sixth-tier Yang God realm cultivation kill a seventh-tier demon king?

Although the rumor was exaggerated, even removing the exaggerated parts, it was still a case of a lower-level cultivator killing a higher-level one.

It was definitely something to boast about as a peerless genius.

As for his knowledge of Divine Mountain, it was only by chance that he heard the human cultivator who killed the seventh-tier demon king seemed to come from Divine Mountain.

However, he assumed that Divine Mountain was merely a sect or force under the Great Chu Dynasty, like Golden Dragon Temple and Xuanqing Temple.

The hidden figure before him claimed to be from the Divine Mountain, yet denied being from the Great Chu.

What was going on?

The shadowy figure directly asked this question.

Kong Wang, of course, answered truthfully, without any need for concealment.

“The Divine Mountain is an independent force, protected by the Mountain God…”

Kong Wang slowly began, recounting the information about the Divine Mountain to the shadowy figure.

The shadowy figure listened, completely bewildered.

What Divine Mountain? Deities? Mountain God?

He had never heard of these terms before. Originating from a tribe in the Southern Frontier, he knew very little about the culture of the Great Chu.

However, he could still understand the concept of deities.

Because the tribes of the Southern Frontier continued the ancient system of divine worship.

They worshipped the great and sacred Goddess of Sorcery.

After Kong Wang's explanation, he gradually understood.

It seemed that this sacred mountain, like their Southern Frontier tribes, was an independent force that worshipped deities.

Perhaps the sacred mountain, like their Southern Frontier tribes, was once subordinate to the Great Chu, but now, having grown powerful, it had broken away.

"I see. So the human cultivator who killed the seventh-tier demon king was from your sacred mountain?"

The shadowy figure asked the question that had been on his mind most.

Kong Wang slowly nodded: "Yes, it must have been Li Xue who acted. She is a talisman recipient, equivalent to a divine messenger. With the power bestowed by the Mountain God, she was able to kill a seventh-tier demon king, even though she was of a higher rank."

He knew about Li Xue's divine destiny talisman.

"Wow, truly a land of geniuses!" the shadowy figure exclaimed, gradually beginning to believe Kong Wang's words.

The main reason was his unique ability to judge character, a gift from the Goddess of Sorcery.

The shadowy figure casually asked about Li Xue's cultivation level, guessing it was around the Yang God Realm, though the rumors were exaggerated, claiming she was below it.

However, to be able to kill a seventh-level Returning to One Realm cultivator while only at the sixth-level Yang God Realm was still terrifying.

At least for now, he couldn't do that; at most, he could only defeat her, not kill her.

"When I left the Divine Mountain and entered the foreign lands, she was only at the Enlightenment Realm," Kong Wang said calmly.

"Yang God Realm, huh? Just as I guessed... What? What did you say, Enlightenment Realm!?"

The shadowy figure nodded at first, then realized something was wrong, his voice rising several notches, thinking he had misheard.

"Yes, Enlightenment Realm!" Kong Wang affirmed.

"Impossible, absolutely impossible!"

The shadowy figure's tone was resolute, utterly disbelieving Kong Wang's words.

He scoffed, "Do you think I'm easily deceived? A fourth-tier Enlightenment Realm cultivator killing a seventh-tier Unity Realm cultivator—a difference of three major realms—what kind of absurd fantasy is this? Even with the aid of divine power, it's absolutely impossible to achieve this!"

He had reason to say this.

Because he had once received the blessing of the Goddess of Sorcery, he knew the terror of divine power, and also the limitations of divine power.

"A monk does not lie. What I have said is absolutely true."

Kong Wang said calmly, but he added that to kill a seventh-tier demon king three realms above one would naturally come at a great cost.

He didn't specify what that cost was.

Even so, the shadowy figure remained unconvinced. He assumed that Li Xue, as Kong Wang described, might be at the peak of the Yin God Realm, and that she had used some forbidden technique with severe backlash, along with a powerful magic weapon, to barely manage to kill the seventh-tier demon king.

If that were the case, it would be somewhat plausible.

Kong Wang didn't care whether the other party believed him or not; he had simply answered the question casually.

The shadowy figure knew he had strayed from the point. Now was not the time to debate this; the immediate issue was to escape the sight of the Vulture Demon with the man from Shenshan.

"Without further ado, if you trust me, come with me. I can lead you out of the demon's heartland. But let me make this clear: I can only take you towards the wilderness, not towards the Great Chu. If you want to return to the Great Chu, you'll have to find your own way around the demon's heartland, traveling hundreds of thousands of miles back."

The dark figure spoke in a deep voice, his tone quite serious.

Kong Wang smiled slightly and said, "There's no need to go towards the Great Chu, because this humble monk is going to the wilderness battlefield."
