Chapter 257 Escape
.

"You're also going to the Wilderness?"

The Southern Frontier Holy Son's tone was surprised. He had initially thought the other party was merely venturing into the heart of the demon realm.

How could he have imagined that the other party's ambition was far greater than he had anticipated? To traverse hundreds of thousands of miles, passing through the perilous demon realm, to reach the Wilderness.

"You have great courage, daring to cross the demon realm. The Great Chu has no warriors like you!" the Southern Frontier Holy Son said sincerely, admiring him.

He could tell that Kong Wang was only at the Yin God Realm. With such strength, let alone venturing into the demon realm, even getting close would be difficult.

It could only be said that this monk from the Divine Mountain was something special.

Kong Wang simply smiled slightly, saying nothing more.

"Follow me, and I can make that Vulture Demon, despite its piercing eyes, unable to see us," the Southern Frontier Holy Son said confidently.

Kong Wang nodded solemnly, following behind the shadowy figure, but still maintaining a distance.

It was still necessary to be cautious; one could never be too careful.

The Holy Son of Southern Xinjiang understood this as well and deliberately distanced himself from Kong Wang.

The two, one after the other, arrived directly at the entrance to the underground river.

Hundreds of demons guarded this place, each one fierce and menacing, all possessing strength of at least the fifth rank.

A vulture demon floated above, its eagle-like eyes scanning every corner of the ground like torches.

Kong Wang gazed upwards, currently activating his Divine Concealment Talisman at full power to avoid being seen by the vulture demon.

There was no way around it; the opponent's eagle eyes were simply too sharp, detecting the slightest movement.

"Don't be nervous, this vulture can't see us now."

At this moment, the Holy Son of Southern Xinjiang suddenly laughed.

"How so?" Kong Wang asked suspiciously, then he noticed several wisps of black smoke separating from the shadowy figure and beginning to swirl around him.

Instantly, he sensed an unusual aura.

These auras were unusual, completely unlike the various spells and techniques cast by cultivators of the Great Chu using spiritual power.

They were also different from the demonic, monster-like, and blood-like auras of demons; they were a completely independent power.

However, Kong Wang sensed a power within them that was both familiar and strange.

“Hehe, how about it? Never seen such an aura before, have you?” The Southern Frontier Holy Son spoke smugly, completely disregarding the demons around him, and said confidently, “This black smoke is divine power, a gift from the Goddess. With the Goddess’s divine power, no matter how powerful that vulture demon is, it cannot see through its mysteries.”

“Divine power?”

Kong Wang pondered. He tried to dispel the effect of the Divine Concealment Talisman and found that the demons indeed could not detect him, including the vulture demon above.

He had just felt a sense of familiarity because he had also sensed a similar aura on the Divine Mountain. Now he understood; this black smoke originated from the hand of a deity.

“Yes, it’s divine power!” the Southern Frontier Holy Son said proudly.

She then added, "Since that divine calamity, very few gods survived, and our Goddess is one of them, ranking among the strongest."

"Gods cannot manifest in the mortal realm; they can only send divine messengers to act on their behalf. But how many in the world are qualified to become divine messengers and borrow divine power? They are as rare as phoenix feathers!"

Kong Wang listened quietly, and then casually asked, "So, you are the messenger of the Southern Frontier Tribe Goddess?"

"Indeed!" the Southern Frontier Holy Son replied proudly.

"This black smoke is a gift from the Goddess, blessed by divine power. This mere vulture demon is nothing but a blind eagle. So you need not worry; with the Goddess's protection, you will be safe and sound."

He wanted to ask if this was more powerful than the mountain god you worshipped, but then decided against it, fearing it might cause an unpleasant situation.

Kong Wang, however, was preoccupied with other matters. He hadn't expected to gain so much today.

The news brought by the Southern Frontier Holy Son stirred a slight ripple in his heart.

It was that besides the mountain god, other deities existed in this world.

And it seemed there were quite a few. He wondered why there had been no news of these deities before.

It seemed he would have to ask the Southern Frontier Holy Son for more details later.

Right now, the most urgent matter was to leave this place as soon as possible.

The two walked blatantly past a large group of demons, even right under the nose of the Vulture Demon, and remained undetected.

Above, the Vulture Demon frowned. "Strange, where has that human brat gone?"

He was confident no one could escape his eagle eyes, but this time he truly found no trace of the human.

"My lord, Quan Xiaotian has returned, saying he wants to catch up with you." Suddenly, a minor demon came to report.

The Vulture Demon frowned and coldly snorted, "A defeated general dares to ask me to find him? Let him come to him himself!"

The minor demon retreated tremblingly.

While he spoke, Kong Wang had already followed the Southern Border Holy Son, escaping the demons' search area.

"Phew, finally out!"

Having escaped a hundred miles away, the Southern Border Holy Son let out a heavy sigh.

Maintaining the black smoke had consumed too much of his energy; even with his formidable strength, he was drenched in sweat.

"Thank you for your help. If you ever need anything in the future, please feel free to come to this humble monk."

Kong Wang expressed his gratitude solemnly.

Although he could use the Divine Concealment Talisman to hide himself and avoid any danger,

that would delay his journey to the Wilderness. Therefore, the Southern Frontier Holy Son's intervention was timely help.

"As fellow human beings, it is only natural to help those in danger. No need for thanks!" the Southern Frontier Holy Son said calmly.

At this moment, he also dispersed the black smoke swirling around him, revealing his true appearance.

A rugged appearance, dark skin, wearing peculiar Southern Frontier clothing, and a green mark on his forehead, a rather sinister mark.

Kong Wang also revealed himself, showing his true face.

"You're actually so young?"

The Southern Frontier Holy Son looked surprised upon seeing that Kong Wang was a handsome young monk.

He had initially assumed that someone with such courage and strength must be at least thirty or forty years old.

But now, judging by appearances, he was probably not even twenty.

Kong Wang simply smiled and didn't answer, after all, he had no age.

If he had to say, he was only a few years old, but who would believe that?

He changed the subject, saying, "Your Southern Frontier tribe's goddess, I've never heard of her before?"

“How could I not have heard of it? Our Southern Frontier tribes have always worshipped the God of Sorcery. You want to ask why the Goddess never manifested before, right?” the Southern Frontier Holy Son said calmly.

Kong Wang nodded.

“That question involves secrets, and I don’t know the answer myself. You won’t get it from me,” the Southern Frontier Holy Son said, then added, “Aren’t you from the Divine Mountain? Could it be that your Mountain God only recently manifested?”


.
Chapter 258 First Clash
.

Upon hearing the Southern Frontier Holy Son's question, Kong Wang was at a loss for words.

He first asked the Southern Frontier Holy Son how long "recently" meant.

The Southern Frontier Holy Son replied, "Half a year."

Kong Wang was slightly silent. Leaving aside the age of the sacred mountain, the Mountain God had already been manifesting for several years.

That was naturally unlike the Southern Frontier's Witchcraft God, who had only recently manifested.

The Mountain God seemed different from the Witchcraft Goddess.

As for the secrets involved, that was beyond his knowledge.

"Our Mountain God also only recently manifested," Kong Wang said ambiguously, naturally not revealing the entire situation of the sacred mountain.

"That's it! As far as I know, many deities have begun to manifest within the last six months," the Southern Frontier Holy Son said slowly.

"Many deities?" Kong Wang caught the key phrase.

“Yes, but this news is extremely secretive. Even the barbarian demigods, and even the saints of the demon realm, haven't known it.”

The Southern Frontier Saint Prince spoke, then looked at Kong Wang, smiling, “As for why I'm telling you this, it's because you're now acting as a divine messenger on behalf of the gods. Sooner or later, you'd have to find out.”

Kong Wang's brows furrowed, feeling he'd fallen into the Southern Frontier Saint Prince's trap.

He said coldly, “What do you mean by that?”

“Don't worry, I have no intention of harming you. As a divine messenger, I must carry out the goddess's will. Our goddess is very interested in the mountain god you mentioned; perhaps we'll have some cooperation in the future,” the Southern Frontier Saint Prince said with a smile.

However, Kong Wang sensed a deeper meaning in the other's smile.

“The deity you mentioned is stronger than a saint?” Kong Wang asked.

“No, not at all.” The Southern Frontier Holy Son shook his head, his voice deep. “To survive the divine calamity is already an immense feat. How much divine power could one possibly retain? If one were stronger than a saint, they wouldn’t have tolerated the demons running rampant.”

“What is a divine calamity?” Kong Wang asked again.

“Don’t ask me, I don’t know either, but I can tell you, the divine calamity is related to the ancient saints.” The Southern Frontier Holy Son revealed a secret.

Kong Wang’s heart stirred slightly.

He had heard of the ancient saints; they were actually the saints from a thousand years ago, separated by a millennium, hence the current term.

This millennium wasn’t a year ago, but several thousand years ago!

“I’ve said too much. That’s enough. I need to go back and report. If you want to go to the Wilderness, you can come with me first, and we’ll separate when we reach the Wilderness front.” The Southern Frontier Holy Son said.

Kong Wang nodded, suppressing his doubts, and began to follow the Southern Frontier Holy Son towards the Wilderness.

Divine Mountain.

Li Yue withdrew his divine sense from the Divine Concealment Talisman he had placed within Kong Wang's body, a sense of unease stirring within him.

He had only intended to check on Kong Wang's situation, but he hadn't expected such a tremendous gain.

"The goddess worshipped by the Southern Frontier tribes... there really are other deities in this world!"

Li Yue thought to himself.

In truth, he had sensed something before, feeling that there shouldn't be no deities in this world.

After all, with the merit of offering incense and prayers, it shouldn't be difficult to cultivate a deity.

But not a single deity manifested their power, leading Li Yue to wonder at one point if he was the only deity in the world.

"But what exactly is this divine calamity?" Li Yue wondered.

The Southern Frontier Saint Son hadn't explained clearly, likely because he didn't know much, only that divine calamities were related to ancient saints.

This reminded Li Yue of the shackles that sealed the world, also related to ancient saints.

"The severing of the Heavenly Connection, and now a divine calamity—this ancient saint is extraordinary!"

Li Yue was astonished. Could a saint really accomplish such a feat? Could a saint possess such power?

Even with his current strength as a fifth-tier saint, he wouldn't dare claim he could set up the shackles of the severing of the Heavenly Connection. Such a grand undertaking even daunted him, let alone someone at the mere ninth-tier saint level.

Perhaps only a realm far above sainthood could accomplish this?

Then there was this divine calamity. Li Yue wasn't sure what it meant, but judging from the literal meaning, it probably wasn't much weaker than the "Severing Heaven and Earth" calamity.

It could only be said that these ancient saints were ridiculously powerful!

But why had all these powerful ancient saints disappeared? Had they perished, or where did they go?

Li Yue didn't know, only that he needed to improve his strength as soon as possible.

Thinking this, he directly looked at the panel.

【Realm: Fifth-Order Sacred Mountain】

【Merit Points: 31.2 million/100 million】

【Divine Abilities: Mountain God's Blessing, Dharma Body of Heaven and Earth, Rebirth of Incense, Gratitude for the Source of Water, Blessing the Earth】

【Spells: Manifestation of Avatar, Control of the Five Elements】

【Magic Weapons: Mountain-Suppressing Seal, Heart Mirror, Divine Weapon Furnace, Wind-Stabilizing Pearl, Purple-Gold Gourd】

His merit points had already reached over 30 million, and the growth rate was still increasing. At this rate, he wasn't far from advancing to the sixth-order Sacred Mountain.

I wonder what comes after the seventh tier, a sacred mountain?

Li Yue wondered to himself.

Suddenly, a malevolent force struck, the void trembled, and wisps of black smoke billowed forth, attempting to break through the void and touch the divine mountain.

"Hmm?"

Li Yue was utterly astonished. This was the first time someone had bypassed Qianshan County to test him.

"What is this thing?"

At this moment, he felt neither anger nor panic at being offended.

On the contrary, Li Yue was excited.

Since becoming a mountain, his days were incredibly boring, because no one would ever consider attacking a mountain.

Even if there was malice, they would have attacked Qianshan County first. If they couldn't get past Qianshan County, they naturally wouldn't have been able to reach him.

But now, someone dared to directly test him, and from the void no less! This meant the opponent's strength was above that of a half-saint.

The black smoke carried unique divine power, attempting to probe him.

Li Yue showed no mercy. How could he test the divine mountain so easily?

He immediately unleashed his oppressive aura, endless golden light radiating from it, almost turning the black smoke into golden smoke itself.

Li Yue's golden light carried his oppressive aura.

The black smoke initially tried to resist, but it was utterly powerless.

Seemingly surprised by the divine mountain's power, the black smoke was somewhat taken aback and began to try to escape.

"Trying to escape? Stay here!"

Li Yue showed no mercy, directly unleashing the Mountain-Suppressing Seal, instantly suppressing the black smoke.

Then, the Mountain-Suppressing Seal gave the black smoke no chance to struggle, shining brightly and beginning to slowly refine it.

"It can even refine this?" Li Yue was somewhat surprised, then quickly realized what was happening and stopped the Mountain-Suppressing Seal from refining the black smoke.

He still wanted to find out the true identity of the other party from this black smoke!

In his heart, Li Yue already knew who the other party was.

It was also black smoke, and it had followed the traces of his divine sense that he had just withdrawn; all of this indicated that the other party was the witch god of the Southern Frontier tribe.


.
Chapter 259 The God Who Escaped the Net
.

This witch goddess actually dared to test the sacred mountain?

She seems quite confident in her own strength.

Li Yue retrieved the black smoke from beneath the Mountain-Suppressing Seal and carefully sensed it with his divine sense. He was surprised to discover that the black smoke indeed contained divine power.

Divine power differs from the power of cultivators. The most obvious difference is that divine power usually contains a trace of incense offerings and merit.

This usually indicates that this witch goddess also achieved her divine status through incense offerings.

"Her strength is considerable," Li Yue mused.

The opponent can traverse the void; her strength must be above that of a half-saint.

However, the strength of a deity cannot be judged by ordinary standards.

"It's a pity the black smoke disappeared before it could leave a trace, otherwise I could have followed it and seen who this witch goddess is."

Li Yue felt regretful.

But this was enough. Through the black smoke, he had gained a general understanding of the deities in this world.

This one was slightly different from the other, because Li Yue sensed a slight incompleteness in the other's power.

According to the Southern Frontier Holy Son, the Goddess of Witchcraft survived the divine calamity a thousand years ago, presumably at a great cost.

"If the other deities are in the same situation, then it seems there's nothing to fear," Li Yue thought to himself.

With his current strength as a fifth-tier Holy Mountain cultivator, he could easily handle them.

Therefore, in Li Yue's eyes, these seemingly dying deities were less threatening than the two Saint-level beings from the demon realm.

After all, they were true Saints.

There's a saying in the world that there are only two realms for cultivators: one is the Ant Realm, referring to ranks one through eight, even including half-saints.

The other is the Saint Realm!

Below the Saint, all are ants; this is not just a saying.

Ancient sages were capable of defying the gods, causing divine calamities and leaving the world without gods for millennia.

Therefore, this shows that some sages were no less powerful than the gods, and perhaps even stronger.

Thinking of this, Li Yue felt an unprecedented sense of urgency.

This world is full of mysteries, rife with secrets.

Leaving aside distant examples, just consider the hole in the sky; it remains unrepaired, and Li Yue constantly senses the deep malice emanating from its opening.

That malice is incredibly malevolent, eerie, and ominous, causing extreme discomfort.

Li Yue doesn't know what it is, but it's certainly not good.

The ancient sages' severing of the connection between heaven and earth, the shackles they set up, were perhaps intended to isolate those things from beyond the heavens.

Therefore, while the shackles of heaven and earth must continue, it's crucial to prepare for the future and improve one's strength as soon as possible.

And what about the barbarian wilderness? He wonders if the barbarian half-saint has successfully attained enlightenment.

Li Yue suddenly felt a sense of unease. Because of the shackles of heaven and earth, the Heavenly Path was severed, and no cultivator could attain sainthood.

If the barbarian semi-saints succeeded in attaining sainthood this time, wouldn't that indirectly prove that the shackles of heaven and earth were gradually losing their effectiveness?

Without the constraints of the shackles, those things from beyond the heavens could enter without any hindrance.

"Is this a blessing or a curse?" Li Yue's heart was heavy.

Meanwhile, hundreds of thousands of miles away from the divine mountain, within the desolate wilderness...

In a damp rainforest, where roots intertwine, hundreds of villages are nestled.

Among these villages live many mountain dwellers; this is the Southern Frontier Tribe.

“It’s terrible, Chieftain! Something’s wrong with the shrine! The goddess’s… her statue…”

An old man rushed in, trembling, and spoke to the Southern Frontier Chieftain, who was kneeling on the ground pounding herbs.

“What’s with the panic? Has the goddess manifested again, issuing a decree?”

The old chieftain remained calm, even dipping his rough hand in the herbal juice, sucking on it, and tasting it carefully.

“No… no manifestation…” The old man swallowed hard, his expression filled with fear.

“No manifestation? Then what’s going on?”

The old chieftain frowned, turning to look at the old man.

"The goddess's... her idol... it's cracked!" The old man finally uttered these words, then collapsed to the ground as if all his strength had been drained.

"What did you say!"

With a loud bang, the medicine-pounding jar crashed to the ground, shattering into more than a dozen pieces, spilling medicinal liquid everywhere.

The old chieftain's eyebrows shot up, his face grim as he stared at the old man who had collapsed to the ground.

"Take me there immediately!" the old chieftain said in a deep voice.

The old man hurriedly got up and led the way.

A moment later, the two arrived at the center of the village, a natural cave.

Entering through the cave entrance, they traversed winding passages before reaching the most sacred place of the Southern Frontier tribe—the altar where the witch goddess was worshipped.

The altar was constructed from the seven-colored rocks naturally formed in the cave, its appearance extremely sacred and magnificent, befitting the status of a deity.

A massive shrine stood on the altar, carved from a single hollowed-out tree.

Within the shrine was the statue of the goddess.

The statue of the Witchcraft Goddess was placed there, but unlike before, the once sacred statue was now split open from the top of its head all the way to its chest.

Its appearance was grotesque and terrifying.

Below the altar, the guards were already prostrate on the ground, trembling with fear.

They chanted the Witchcraft Goddess's incantations, begging for her forgiveness.

The old clan chief felt a chill run down his spine upon seeing this.

He rushed forward and knelt before the shrine, his expression one of utter trepidation.

"This old servant is terrified. Please forgive me, Goddess."

Since the Witchcraft Goddess manifested, he had never encountered anything like this before.

This statue was once the very image of the goddess, and changes in such a statue can generally indicate the state of the deity.

The fact that the statue of the Goddess of Sorcery had cracked meant that the deity had encountered a calamity.

He was unaware of the situation and dared not know.

Therefore, all he could do was prostrate himself on the ground, kowtowing repeatedly.

The cracked statue of the Goddess of Sorcery shimmered with a faint light.

Meanwhile, in the vast emptiness, a temple was floating.

However, this temple was extremely dilapidated, many parts were broken, and it bore the marks of battle; blood still clung to it, vividly fresh.

Within the temple, a figure sat on a bronze chair.

She was shrouded in black smoke, obscuring her features; only a vague outline could be discerned.

From the outline, it was clear she was a woman, and even more bizarrely, she possessed six arms.

Moreover, her arms were slender and moved with remarkable agility.

From within the black smoke, the woman let out a muffled groan, seemingly injured.

"Mount Shen, whose old friend is this?"


.
Chapter 260 The Situation in the Wilderness
.

From within the black smoke, murmurs emanated, seemingly in remembrance.

"A deity associated with mountains... could it be the Black Mountain God?"

The Goddess of Sorcery recalled something, but then shook her head.

"The Black Mountain God perished in the divine calamity; even his true spirit was annihilated. It's impossible for him to have survived."

If not the Black Mountain God, then who could it be?

The Goddess of Sorcery considered several other deities, but ultimately rejected them all.

Either had already perished, or their strength didn't match.

"Perhaps this person has nothing to do with mountains at all?"

The Goddess of Sorcery considered this again.

If she didn't associate it with mountains, then there were many more deities that could fit the bill.

First and foremost was the other person's strength, which was what truly shocked the Goddess of Sorcery.

"To survive the divine calamity and retain so much divine power, one must be extraordinary."

The Goddess of Sorcery pondered, "And using a large seal as a magical artifact... could it be the Crimson Sun God?"

She thought of several deities who fit the description, but these alone weren't enough proof.

"This mountain god has recovered at least forty percent of his strength. How did he manage that?"

The Goddess of Sorcery couldn't understand.

After the divine calamity, all these deities suffered extremely severe injuries.

Some even suffered damage to their divine essence, falling directly from their divine positions to the mortal realm.

Becoming mortals, losing their eternal lifespan, no longer able to live as long as the heavens, they turned to dust within a few hundred years.

Not dying in the divine calamity, but falling to the mortal realm, dying of old age—a truly tragic fate.

Which of the surviving deities wasn't in a deep slumber?

It was only in the last six months, with changes in the rules of heaven and earth, that they gradually awakened.

Upon awakening and understanding the current state of affairs,

these deities all shared a question:

"Where did these two ninth-tier Saints come from?"

But when they saw the large hole in the sky, everything became clear.

After understanding the situation, they all began to heal their wounds and restore their divine energy.

The Goddess of Sorcery spent half a year barely recovering twenty percent of her peak strength.

She recovered relatively quickly because she had a base of followers.

Those without a base of followers would find it much more difficult to recover their strength.

That's why the Mountain God's strength had shocked her.

How exactly did the other side recover its strength?

"I won't provoke them for now. I'll settle this score when I'm strong enough," the Goddess of Sorcery muttered to herself.

Her recovery was slow, not weak.

If both were at their peak, she didn't believe the Mountain God would be stronger than her.

Even before the divine calamity, the number of deities who could surpass her was only a handful.

Through the void, she looked at her trembling subordinates below the sacrificial altar.

The Goddess of Sorcery simply issued her decree.

The old chieftain of the Southern Frontier tribe, as if granted a pardon, repeatedly kowtowed in gratitude.

Then he hurriedly arranged the offerings and summoned professionals to repair the statues.

The Savage Front.

After several days of travel, Kong Wang finally arrived here.

The Holy Son of Southern Xinjiang spoke, “Go in that direction; that’s the direction of the Savage Battlefield. Right now, the Savages are engaged in a fierce battle with demons, the fighting so intense it changes the very fabric of the world. If you want to go, you’d better be careful.”

“By the way, you’d better say you’re from the Great Chu. There’s no need to say you’re from the Divine Mountain, otherwise it’ll be very troublesome to explain. Those barbarians aren’t exactly brainless.”

Kong Wang nodded, noting these words down.

“Take care,” he said to the Holy Son of Southern Xinjiang.

“Until we meet again!”

After saying this, the Holy Son of Southern Xinjiang turned and disappeared.

Kong Wang watched him leave before turning and heading towards the Savage Battlefield.

The land here was completely barren, devoid of any life.

The sand was purplish-brown and emitted a foul, stench.

It was soaked in blood—human blood, demon blood, and monster blood.

A few miles further on, countless irregularly shaped piles of bones lay like mountains.

A deathly aura permeated the air, making even Kong Wang, a Yin Spirit Realm cultivator, feel uneasy.

Staying there too long would affect his soul.

Kong Wang's expression was solemn as he gazed at this carnage, a sigh escaping his lips.

Almost entirely composed of human corpses, making up over 80%.

The barbaric human race had suffered devastating losses.

Just as he was pondering this, a tremendous cracking sound erupted from the center of the distant battlefield, staining the sky crimson.

The earth trembled, sending countless grains of sand and stones flying, accompanied by roars of resentment.

Then silence fell, and rain began to fall from the sky—blood rain.

Kong Wang suddenly felt a profound sorrow, overwhelmed by the scene before him, which filled him with utter astonishment.

"What has happened?"

He was filled with doubt, but upon approaching the battlefield, he was shocked by what he saw.

The battlefield stretched as far as the eye could see, densely packed with figures—countless barbarians locked in a bloody battle against demons.

The barbarians' cultivation methods differed from those of the Great Chu.

They enhanced their strength through physical training, so their fighting style was exclusively close-quarters combat.

The barbarians' methods of fighting were extremely primitive; their bare hands were their weapons.

Once they captured a demon, they would tear it to pieces.

In the very center of the battlefield, the area had been cleared, leaving a massive crater.

A gigantic corpse lay there, clearly humanoid.

Countless powerful barbarians gathered around the giant corpse, their expressions filled with grief and despair.

"Third Elder... Third Elder..."

The crowd cried out in anguish, but no matter how they called, the giant would not awaken.

The Third Elder, an eighth-tier Venerable Realm expert, was dead.

"Fight those beasts!"

A young barbarian shouted angrily, but was quickly silenced by an older barbarian powerhouse.

"Nonsense! Stop right there! The Third Elder sacrificed himself, exchanging his life for three eighth-tier demons, all to carve a path for us. Turning back now only makes the Third Elder's death meaningless!"

The older barbarian spoke in a deep voice.

These words immediately calmed the group of young barbarians.

Their eyes reddened as they looked at the Third Elder's corpse, and they immediately knelt on the ground, bidding farewell with their unique barbarian ritual.

After doing this, they resolutely turned and left.

Because from the direction where the demons had been, more powerful demons had heard the commotion and were coming.

If they didn't leave now, they would never be able to leave again.

As for the Third Elder's body, they couldn't take it with them.

"Retreat!"

The barbarian warriors began to retreat. The main force obeyed orders without question, instantly starting to withdraw.

The demon army tried to stop them, but several powerful barbarian warriors stepped into the air, repelling the demon army with a few powerful hand seals.

Taking advantage of this opportunity, the main barbarian force successfully withdrew from the battlefield.

Kong Wang, seeing the situation was dire, also mingled with the barbarian force and retreated to the wilderness.

The moment after the barbarian warriors withdrew, a powerful aura swept in, and two more eighth-tier demons appeared.

They arrived on the battlefield and immediately recognized the giant barbarian corpse on the ground.


.
Chapter 261 Glory to My Great Barbarian Tribe
.

"That's the Third Elder of the Barbarian Clan, a late-stage Eighth-Rank Venerable!"

One of the Eighth-Rank demons recognized the giant corpse on the ground.

Another demon looked around and saw the scattered demon flesh and blood everywhere, incredibly gruesome.

This flesh and blood still exuded a terrifying aura, causing lower-ranking demons to fear and dare not approach.

"Stone Demon and the others are dead," the Eighth-Rank demon said with a grim expression.

"Killed by this Barbarian Third Elder?"

Another demon gasped. In a three-on-one situation, he had actually managed to take down three Eighth-Rank demons.

This Barbarian Third Elder was truly powerful!

"Be careful. The Barbarians are now fighting for their lives, disregarding all costs, even their own. So don't get too carried away. If things look bad, retreat quickly, lest you suffer the same fate as Stone Demon and the others."

The Eighth-Rank Demon Venerable with the bird's beak spoke coldly.

"Trying to buy time for their king? It's nothing but a futile struggle."

The Mirage Demon sneered.

If becoming a saint were so easy, then in the past thousand years, besides their two saints from the Holy Court, why hadn't a single saint appeared?

Therefore, in his eyes, the barbarian half-saint Arturu was nothing more than following in the footsteps of his predecessors.

"Lord Qingyu Thorn is already summoning us. Let's go now; the Holy Court is probably about to make a big move!"

The Blood Cuckoo Demon spoke calmly.

The Mirage Demon nodded, and they merely glanced at the Third Elder's corpse on the ground, then casually struck, completely annihilating the Third Elder's body, leaving no trace.

Having done all this, they turned and left, flying towards the demon's main camp.

In the distance, hundreds of miles away, the barbarian warriors, seeing the Third Elder's corpse destroyed through secret techniques, were filled with grief and rage.

But there was nothing they could do. They could only wait for things to calm down before sending men back to the battlefield to see if they could still collect the Third Elder's ashes or anything like that.

"Warriors, the Third Elder has gone to see our late king to ensure our safe retreat. But we must continue fighting, battling the demons to the very end, until the last barbarian!"

"Long live our Great Barbarians!" the leading barbarian warrior shouted resolutely.

Tens of thousands of barbarian warriors ahead, their blood boiling, raised their arms and shouted in unison!

"Long live our Great Barbarians!"

"Long live our Great Barbarians!"

Each barbarian warrior carried the will to die. From the moment they stepped onto this battlefield, they had no intention of leaving alive.

They wanted to fight, they wanted to slaughter, they wanted to die here.

And all of this was to buy their king enough time.

If their king could successfully attain enlightenment, then they would all die without regret.

Not only them, but countless powerful barbarian warriors, even the nine elders of the barbarian tribe, were all mobilized.

Three elders have already fallen: the Fifth Elder, the Seventh Elder, and the recently deceased Third Elder.

These were the barbarian tribe's core strength; even in past conflicts with demons, they wouldn't easily mobilize to prevent unforeseen circumstances.

The primary responsibility of the barbarian warriors was to protect the tribe's vital forces.

But all of that had changed at this moment.

Their king sought to prove himself through force, achieving the realm of a saint!

If he succeeded, their barbarian tribe would no longer be subdued by demons, nor would they face the threat of annihilation.

This was why their barbarian tribe fought so relentlessly, risking their lives to battle the demons.

At this moment, a tragic atmosphere permeated the barbarian army.

Even Kong Wang, mixed among them, was moved by this atmosphere.

He hadn't expected the barbarians to be so united; this was something the Great Chu could never match.

After Kong Wang delved deeper, he gradually understood the reason.

The barbarian tribe's unity could all be attributed to their king, their only demigod, Aturu.

The king's deeds were nothing short of a miracle.

Aturu rose from humble beginnings, merely an orphan in a small barbarian tribe.

From a young age, Aturu was ridiculed for his weak physique, told he didn't resemble a barbarian and was more like a frail scholar from the Great Chu.

It's important to know that barbarian cultivation focuses on physical training, so physique is essentially the standard for judging talent and potential.

As a child, Aturu was so thin and frail, practically skin and bones, that he could basically be considered a cripple.

But Aturu, an orphan from childhood, possessed an indomitable spirit. Lacking physical strength, he trained several times harder than others.

While others ate, he trained; while others slept, he trained; while others played, he trained.

Through this tenfold effort and with an unwavering will, Aturu finally distinguished himself.

People began to respect and value him.

They thought Aturu's success was the result of hard work.

But little did they know, this was just the beginning.

Having gradually gained strength, Aturu was not content to stop there; he wanted to become even stronger.

In the sacred land of the barbarians, Aturu saw with his own eyes the statue of the first barbarian king.

This was a great man of the barbarians; it was the first barbarian king who led the barbarians to forcefully carve out their habitat on this land, allowing the barbarians to thrive and multiply.

After seeing the statue of the first barbarian king, Aturu seemed to have found his goal.

He began to engage in an even more terrifying method of cultivation, the kind that would make even a glance from onlookers tremble.

But Aturu persevered through it all; his willpower was extremely strong.

Throughout this process, Aturu's cultivation level steadily broke through to the eighth-tier Venerable Realm.

And it was precisely at this moment that a blood-red meteorite appeared from beyond the heavens, shattering the sky and falling upon the demons' habitat.

Thus, demons wreaked havoc, and the barbarians faced annihilation.

At this time, Arturu stepped forward. With his formidable strength, he gradually led the barbarians against the demons. Although they suffered more defeats than victories, they persevered and preserved most of the barbarian forces.

Finally, Arturu broke through to become a demigod and was revered as the King of the Barbarians.

The position of Barbarian King, vacant for over a hundred years, was finally filled again.

This is the story of Arturu, the demigod of the barbarians.

Actually, this is a simplified version. Legendary figures like Arturu have countless versions and versions of their stories.

There are even versions of the story that could be told for three days and three nights.

After hearing about Aturu's deeds, Kong Wang felt genuine admiration.

He truly deserves to be called a semi-saint who dared to be the first to attempt to attain sainthood in the current situation.

Such courage could only be possessed by a legendary figure like him.


.
Chapter 262 The Lone Ranger
.

Being the first to attain sainthood would inevitably attract the attention of all forces.

Kong Wang believed that not only demons were watching the Wilderness, but also the Great Chu, and even forces like the Dragon-Slaying Sect were waiting for Aturu to achieve sainthood.

The eyes of the world were all focused on the Wilderness.

One could imagine the immense pressure Aturu would face.

Yet he still resolutely made this decision.

Kong Wang deeply admired him and hoped that Aturu would succeed in attaining sainthood; otherwise, the fate of the Barbarians would likely be tragic.

Although all the Barbarians were prepared for either survival or annihilation.

In this world, Aturu was like a lone traveler.

No other half-saints supported him; he was always alone.

There was no way back; he could only move forward step by step, without any companions along the way.

Kong Wang followed these Barbarian warriors, retreating together to the Barbarian stronghold.

Here, there were powerful barbarian warriors.

If he continued to use the Divine Concealment Talisman, he would only be discovered. After some hesitation, Kong Wang chose to reveal himself.

The sudden appearance of a person startled many barbarian warriors.

“Who are you? How did you get in here?!”

A junior barbarian general, a fifth-tier cultivator, quickly stepped forward upon seeing Kong Wang's sudden appearance, staring at him with extreme vigilance.

If it weren't for the fact that Kong Wang was human and didn't seem to have any ill intentions, he would have already captured him.

“Amitabha, this humble monk is Kong Wang, from the Great Chu.”

Kong Wang clasped his hands together and bowed slightly in greeting.

The man looked somewhat surprised upon hearing that Kong Wang came from the Great Chu.

However, he naturally wouldn't believe him easily, so he summoned an elderly barbarian who had been to the Great Chu. The two first communicated in the barbarian language, discussing something.

Then the barbarian elder stepped forward and asked in the common language of this world, "You claim to be from the Great Chu, but the Great Chu is hundreds of thousands of miles away from our wilderness, and there are demons blocking our way. How did you arrive?"

Kong Wang slowly replied, "This humble monk has a method of concealment, which allows me to avoid demons. I traveled along many side paths, and with the help of others, I was able to reach the wilderness."

"Oh? Others? Who are they?" the barbarian elder pressed.

Kong Wang answered, "They are from your wilderness, from the Southern Frontier tribe. They claim to be the Holy Son of the Southern Frontier."

"Hmm? The Holy Son of the Southern Frontier?"

The young general paused slightly upon hearing "Holy Son of the Southern Frontier," clearly recognizing him.

He then began to press Kong Wang for details about the Holy Son of the Southern Frontier.

Kong Wang thought for a moment and answered each question.

The barbarian elder then asked a few more questions, finally confirming that Kong Wang was indeed a human from the Great Chu.

The barbarian general finally smiled, stepped forward, patted Kong Wang on the shoulder, and laughed, "So you really are a friend of Great Chu!"

The general was very enthusiastic, and the other barbarian warriors also lowered their guard, curiously examining Kong Wang.

This was the first time they had ever seen a cultivator from Great Chu.

Before the chaos caused by demons and monsters, Great Chu and the barbarian lands had frequent exchanges, so seeing someone from Great Chu back then wasn't unusual.

But after the chaos caused by demons, communication between the two sides ceased. Demons stood as an insurmountable obstacle, insurmountable for ordinary cultivators, let alone commoners.

Therefore, Kong Wang's arrival was a great surprise to the barbarian tribes.

The junior general asked Kong Wang how long it had taken to travel from the Great Chu to the Wilderness.

Kong Wang thought for a moment and replied, "Traveling non-stop, excluding delays, it took a total of twenty-four days."

"Hiss!" The junior general gasped, not expecting such a long journey.

He knew that cultivators from the Great Chu were much faster than their barbarian body cultivators.

This didn't even include the speed-enhancing spells.

If it took cultivators from the Great Chu over twenty days, wouldn't it take them more than a month?

"Impressive!" The junior general gave Kong Wang an approving look.

Then he asked Kong Wang what business he had in the Wilderness.

Deep down, he understood that the other party had come to the Wilderness because they had heard the news of the King's impending ascension to sainthood.

Kong Wang didn't hide anything and directly stated his purpose.

"The news of the Barbarian King's impending ascension to sainthood has already spread throughout the Great Chu. This humble monk has traveled hundreds of thousands of miles to witness the Barbarian Half-Saint's ascension to sainthood!" Kong Wang spoke slowly.

"Haha, you're quite sincere!" The young general laughed heartily, quite satisfied with Kong Wang's last sentence.

"The news of the King's impending ascension to sainthood has indeed spread throughout the world. That's why all the powerful demons have come out to stop the King and hinder his ascension."

The young general sneered, then added, "But we barbarians have never been pushovers. If those demons dare to come, we dare to fight. What is there to fear even in death?"

Kong Wang fell silent after hearing this.

Compared to the barbarians, the Great Chu was far too weak.

The Great Chu had three Half-Saints!

"The barbarian tribe only has one Saint."

Seemingly sensing Kong Wang's thoughts, the young general patted him on the shoulder and said, "You don't need to feel ashamed because of Great Chu. The barbarians have their difficulties, and Great Chu has its difficulties. Everyone says Great Chu is weak and incompetent, only able to retreat and cede territory in the face of demons, constantly making concessions."

"But you must know that the two Saints of the demons have been closely watching Great Chu. It can be said that Great Chu has indirectly restrained the two Saints of the demons."

"Otherwise, our barbarian tribe wouldn't have been able to hold out for so long. Our king is powerful, but ultimately, he cannot withstand the might of a Saint."

Hearing the young general's words, Kong Wang was somewhat surprised.

He had never heard of these things before. Although he had some guesses, they were very vague.

But this barbarian, isn't he just a young general? How does he know so much?

Kong Wang asked, "You said that Great Chu has indirectly restrained the two sages of the demons. What does that mean?"

The young general smiled and said, "You come from Great Chu, don't you know about the human destiny of the Great Chu Dynasty?"

"Destiny?" Kong Wang murmured. He certainly knew about destiny.

The Great Chu Dynasty has only been able to survive to this day because of destiny.

"Luck is mysterious and profound; it's something intangible, close to the Great Dao. Don't you have some interesting stories about Great Chu? People with extraordinary luck can find magical treasures just by going out, or even break through to higher levels by drinking water."

"That's a bit of an exaggeration, but there's some truth to it."

The young general spoke slowly. In short, Great Chu was able to restrain the two demon saints, preventing them from acting freely, precisely because of the luck of humanity.

Great Chu isn't actually as weak as everyone says.

In fact, Great Chu has already unleashed its ultimate move; it just hasn't used it yet.


.
Chapter 263 Humanity and Fortune
.

Kong Wang was utterly astonished upon hearing this for the first time.

In truth, he knew very little about the concept of destiny.

Mainly because he had remained on the sacred mountain since birth, and his subsequent travels hadn't drawn much attention to destiny.

Therefore, his understanding was limited.

However, he had heard of a general overview of destiny.

“As far as this humble monk knows, the destiny of the Great Chu is steadily declining, and it's far from what it used to be. If this continues, can it still restrain the two Saint-level beings of the demon realm?”

Kong Wang frowned.

“Definitely not. Not to mention the future, even now it's barely holding on. It's just a struggle.”

The young general sighed. Once upon a time, at the peak of the Great Chu Dynasty, its destiny was so abundant it could shatter the heavens.

Even a little over ten years ago, the Great Chu's destiny was incredibly prosperous.

But in order to deal with the two Saint-level beings of the demon realm, its foundation had already been shaken, and its destiny had declined.

The young general sighed, "The demons are waiting for their chance. When the Great Chu's fortune is completely exhausted, and there's no hope left, then humanity will face annihilation."

Hearing this, Kong Wang's heart grew heavy.

If their fortune is completely depleted, is death the only fate for humanity?

Without a sage to protect them, they ultimately cannot withstand the overwhelming tide of the demon army.

"Do you feel despair? Do you feel that humanity has no future?"

The young general gave a smile, a smile tinged with sorrow.

He understood this feeling of despair; it could be said that every human cultivator felt this despair.

That feeling of being powerless to resist, yet unwilling to accept humanity's demise.

"No."

Kong Wang didn't reveal the expression the young general wanted to see. His expression was calm, yet tinged with a glimmer of hope.

The Great Chu and the Wildlands' inability to resist didn't mean the Divine Mountain was powerless.

With the Mountain God present, Kong Wang firmly believed that even the two powerful demon saints couldn't eradicate the human race.

Seeing that Kong Wang didn't show despair, but instead had hope in his eyes,

the young general was astonished.

He was puzzled. He thought that when he said these things, regardless of who the other person was—an unknown low-level cultivator or a powerful eighth-tier Venerable—

their face would darken and their heart would fill with despair because of his words.

Those with unstable Dao hearts might even suffer a cultivation deviation.

But this man could step forward, pat the other person on the shoulder, and offer words of comfort.

Then he could casually recount the king's legendary deeds, describing the king as humanity's only hope, the most promising human to attain sainthood.

This tactic would completely captivate the other person with the king's charisma, turning them into his follower.

This trick had never failed him.

After all, the King was indeed a legendary figure; anyone who heard of his legendary deeds would feel admiration.

But today, his tactic had failed.

What was going on?

Was the other party completely unmoved? Or did he lack any compassion for humanity?

The young general, unwilling to accept defeat, continued speaking, painting a picture of an incredibly bleak future for humanity, utterly devoid of any hope.

A definite, utterly hopeless future.

Then he stared intently at Kong Wang, wanting to see his expression change.

But Kong Wang still disappointed him.

Kong Wang remained resolute, showing no change in emotion despite the young general's words.

In fact, Kong Wang even comforted the young general, saying, "Benefactor, there's no need to despair. Human effort can overcome anything."

"This humble monk believes that no matter how strong the demons are, they cannot possibly bring about the extinction of humanity. Know that there are always stronger beings, and the world is not limited to demons and saints."

Kong Wang wanted to mention the Mountain God's reputation, but having already stated he was from the Great Chu, explaining now would be too troublesome, so he gave up.

The young general was speechless. He had wasted so much breath, only to end up with nothing, and even received a consolation.

What was this all about?

Suddenly, he didn't want to talk to this monk from the Great Chu anymore and turned to leave.

But at that moment, some barbarians approached.

Upon seeing the young general, he exclaimed with delight, "Artur, you're here! I just happened to have something to discuss with you!"

Hearing the young general addressed by his name, Kong Wang glanced at him thoughtfully.

The name Artur differed from the name of the barbarian demigod Arturu by only one character.

What was this guy's relationship with the barbarian demigod?

Artur frowned, looking at the barbarian who had come to find him.

"Meng En, weren't you on the western front? What are you doing here?" he asked in a deep voice.

Meng En's face was flushed, and he lowered his head, saying, "I'm utterly useless. The western front has collapsed and fallen, and we've retreated another fifty li."

"The Second Elder sent others to fill in, abandoning me, and sent me to find you."

He finished speaking, his eyes pleadingly fixed on Artur.

Hearing Meng En's words, Artur's heart sank even further.

The western front has collapsed again, and the barbarians are retreating steadily; the situation is dire.

“Why are you looking for me? I don’t want you either. You’re too weak. Go to the rear and do logistics,” Artur said calmly.

Upon hearing this, Meng En immediately panicked and said, “No, Artur, you’re my good brother. Everyone else doesn’t want me, but you’re the only one who can take me.”

“Brotherhood is one thing, but on the battlefield, that doesn’t apply. Meng En, you’re not strong enough. Going there would only result in your death. It’s better for you to go to the rear. Logistics is a crucial part of the fight. We’re all fighting for the barbarians!”

Artur said earnestly.

Meng En, looking unwilling, said, "A barbarian man shouldn't hide in the rear! I don't care, I want to go to the front lines and fight the demons! Even if I'm just a soldier, cannon fodder, I'm willing!"

Artur's face darkened: "No, you have a younger sister to take care of. Your parents are dead. If you die too, who will take care of her?"

"My sister is already five years old and can live independently," Meng En stubbornly replied.

"You bastard!" Artur cursed, but he couldn't do anything about Meng En.

They'd been brothers for decades, growing up together; he knew Meng En's temper all too well.

"Since you want to go to the front lines, then go."

In the end, Artur could only compromise.

"Good brother, I knew you wouldn't abandon me! You truly are the king's younger brother!"

Meng En exclaimed joyfully, then tried to hug Artur.

But Artur pushed him away in disgust.

"You stink like a log, and you still want to cling to me? And stop saying I'm the king's younger brother!" Artur said with an unfriendly expression.

Meng En chuckled sheepishly, sniffing himself and finding he indeed smelled terrible.

Right, he hadn't bathed in over a month; it would be strange if he didn't smell.

"Huh? Who's this bald guy? He looks different from us."

At this moment, Meng En finally noticed Kong Wang standing to the side, and asked with surprise.


.
Chapter 264 Sparring
.

“He’s a friend from the Great Chu. And besides, he’s not called Baldy, he’s a monk!”

Artur explained, recounting Kong Wang’s situation to Meng En.

Meng En was quite surprised after hearing this.

Then he began circling around Kong Wang, repeatedly contradicting himself.

He clicked his tongue in amazement, repeatedly saying, “So this is what people from the Great Chu look like!”

Kong Wang smiled, not paying much attention to Meng En’s scrutiny.

However, it was the slip of the tongue from Meng En that surprised him.

Kong Wang glanced at Artur a few times, not expecting that the other party was actually the younger brother of the Barbarian Half-Saint.

No wonder their names differed by only one character.

“You are actually the Barbarian King’s younger brother; this truly surprises this humble monk,”

Kong Wang said.

He was indeed surprised; the other party, as the younger brother of the Barbarian Half-Saint, was only a minor general in the army.

Moreover, judging from his appearance, he seemed to be working quietly behind the scenes, yet he was at the forefront—this was incredibly dangerous.

Atur, realizing that Kong Wang already knew his identity, felt a pang of reproach for Meng En's carelessness.

But since Kong Wang already knew, he readily admitted it.

"What's so surprising about that? As the king's younger brother, to be unable to follow in his footsteps, and instead to hold him back—I only feel ashamed."

Atur spoke calmly, but his tightly clenched hands betrayed his emotions.

Kong Wang noticed, but didn't point it out.

From Atur's previous words, it was clear he was a fervent follower of his brother.

However, his brother was a half-saint, while he only possessed the cultivation of a fifth-tier Yin God. This caused him a sense of defeat and unbearable shame.

Kong Wang didn't know how to comfort him, so he could only remain silent.

Fortunately, Artur quickly composed himself and smiled, saying, "You flatter me."

Kong Wang shook his head, indicating that he wasn't.

At this moment, Meng En had also finished sizing up Kong Wang, the guest from Great Chu.

He found that the other party was just like their barbarian tribe, with only one nose and two eyes, nothing special.

If there was anything special, it was his cultivation system.

Meng En was somewhat eager to try, and said, "Hey, friend from Great Chu, would you like to spar? Let me see the methods of cultivators from Great Chu."

Artur looked at Kong Wang; he was also very curious about Great Chu's cultivation system.

Although he had heard of it, he had never seen it with his own eyes.

Kong Wang pondered; he also wanted to see the barbarian cultivation system.

So he nodded and said, "Sure, but it seems you are only at the fourth level of cultivation, I'm afraid you can't withstand my attacks."

"No problem, bring it on!" Meng En waved his hand.

Barbarians never beat around the bush; whether they can succeed or not, they'll know after a fight.

"If he can't handle it, I'll take over!" Artur said from the side.

He was also eager to try.

"Alright!" Kong Wang nodded in agreement.

As soon as he finished speaking, Meng En threw a punch directly at Kong Wang's face, his movement incredibly swift.

Kong Wang didn't immediately intervene; instead, his pupils glowed with golden light as he observed Meng En's attack method.

He noticed a fluctuation of blood energy in the opponent's attack.

Blood energy was the source of power for barbarians.

The stronger and more refined one's blood energy, the greater one's strength.

Meng En's blood aura was intensely hot, but not particularly dazzling.

This was likely due to his opponent's cultivation level being only fourth-tier.

After a brief observation, Kong Wang immediately raised his hand, his spiritual energy forming a barrier in front of him.

Meng En appeared carefree, but he was far from complacent.

His eyes narrowed upon seeing Kong Wang's spiritual energy barrier.

He changed his fist into a palm and struck the barrier directly.

But how could a fourth-tier cultivator possibly overcome a fifth-tier one?

Moreover, Kong Wang was no ordinary fifth-tier Yin God Realm cultivator.

Instantly, Meng En was thrown back dozens of meters, kicking up a cloud of dust.

"Damn, how is he so tough?" Meng En exclaimed in astonishment, not expecting Kong Wang to be so strong.

He had thought he could at least make a move against his opponent.

But his opponent remained completely unmoved.

"I'll go!"

Artur watched with burning desire. He could tell Kong Wang possessed genuine skill, and his thirst for battle instantly ignited.

Kong Wang's expression turned serious as he looked at Artur, his face slightly grave.

This was the younger brother of a Barbarian Half-Saint; he couldn't afford to underestimate him.

"Ready? Here I come!"

Artur roared, unleashing the Barbarian's signature Great Wilderness Handprint.

This technique was incredibly powerful, renowned for its overwhelming force.

It involved no fancy moves, just pure power suppression.

The stronger you were, the more powerful the technique would be.

Kong Wang dared not be careless. He chanted scriptures, instantly forming a large golden bell that enveloped him.

"Hmm?" Artur noticed Kong Wang's change in attack and increased his force.

The Great Wilderness Handprint slammed directly into the golden bell.

A heavy, muffled thud resounded.

A crisp cracking sound followed, and Kong Wang's eyes narrowed.

The golden bell shattered into pieces under Artur's Great Wilderness Hand Seal.

A hint of joy flashed in Artur's eyes, but it didn't last long before his expression changed.

Kong Wang's expression only changed briefly after his golden bell was broken, but he quickly recovered.

He formed a hand seal, instantly creating a lotus seal.

This was something the monk Sanfu had secretly taught him back in Qingzhou.

It was called the Lotus Seal.

This was one of the secret techniques of the Golden Dragon Temple.

It could be used by anyone from the eighth-tier Venerable to even a half-saint, and its power was extremely formidable.

Each tier unlocks one lotus petal.

The more lotus petals, the greater the power.

Kong Wang, with his fifth-tier Yin Spirit Realm cultivation, could condense a five-petaled lotus.

Artur had thought that breaking Kong Wang's golden bell would defeat him.

However, unexpectedly, Kong Wang hadn't even made his move yet.

The five-petaled lotus appeared, its light dazzling, and a Buddhist aura enveloped Artur.

Artur felt his movements slow down.

Sensing danger, he unleashed his blood energy, barely managing to break free from the mud.

But the five-petaled lotus had already locked onto him; it was too late to retreat.

So Artur could only grit his teeth and forcefully withstand the lotus hand seal.

The Great Wilderness Hand Seal clashed with the Lotus Hand Seal.

Instantly, spiritual energy and blood energy erupted and merged together.

The enormous commotion immediately attracted the attention of the surrounding barbarians.

Even some eighth-tier barbarian powerhouses turned their gazes towards the scene.

When they saw that it was the king's younger brother, Artur, fighting someone, they all showed expressions of astonishment.

Some of the powerhouses recognized that the person Artur was fighting was different; he was a cultivator from the Great Chu.

"Why is Artur fighting someone? Didn't the king forbid him from sparring with others at will?"

A barbarian asked in surprise.


.
Chapter 265 The Timing of Proving the Dao
.

"The one fighting Artur seems to be a cultivator from the Great Chu?" a powerful figure slowly spoke.

This remark immediately caused a stir among the barbarians.

Everyone crowded around, looking at Kong Wang, who was sparring with Artur.

Most of the barbarians were seeing a Great Chu cultivator for the first time, their eyes filled with curiosity.

Meanwhile, another group began to shout: "Artur, don't disgrace the barbarians! Defeat your opponent to prove your strength!"

"Yes! This is a contest between the barbarians and the Great Chu people. Artur, as the king's younger brother, you mustn't embarrass yourself!"

Some barbarians began to jeering.

This directly increased the pressure on Artur, who was fighting Kong Wang.

He had already felt immense pressure when facing Kong Wang's lotus seal, and when he used the Great Wilderness Hand Seal to counter it, he teetered on the brink of collapse, almost unable to hold on.

"You're very strong, not an ordinary cultivator from the Great Chu!"

Artur looked at Kong Wang with a strange expression.

You must know that his strength is among the best in the Wilderness; among the fifth-tier cultivators, he can even overpower many!

Kong Wang nodded slightly, tacitly acknowledging Artur's words.

Having achieved the Full Moon Yin God status and with the blessing of the Mountain God's Divine Concealment Talisman, Kong Wang's strength is naturally much stronger than that of an ordinary fifth-tier Yin God.

Moreover, the Lotus Seal technique is a closely guarded secret of the Golden Dragon Temple, inherently no less powerful than the Great Wilderness hand seals.

"Again!" Artur refused to believe it; he simply couldn't believe that a cultivator from the Great Chu could surpass him in strength.

He roared, his veins bulging, countless streams of blood surging, directly activating Blood Rage.

This is a unique talent of the Barbarians; activating Blood Rage multiplies their power, unleashing it instantly.

However, afterwards, one would feel physically exhausted, and in severe cases, even suffer physical injury, affecting cultivation.

Seeing that Artur was serious, Kong Wang's expression also turned solemn, and he dared not be careless.

He cast the Lotus Seal again, and at the same time, golden light flashed in his eyes as he used his pupil technique to observe Artur's movements.

Artur's strength was indeed formidable; even Kong Wang felt the pressure.

If he didn't use the Divine Concealment Talisman, it would be difficult for him to defeat his opponent.

Entering a state of Blood Rage, Artur's attacks became even more ferocious, and Kong Wang's Lotus Seal was directly shattered by the Great Wilderness Handprint.

But Kong Wang's pupil technique had already predicted the location of Artur's fist and dodged in advance.

Artur's attack missed, but he quickly recovered and continued to attack Kong Wang.

Kong Wang felt the pressure; the Lotus Seal could not withstand the Great Wilderness Handprint in its Blood Rage state.

He didn't want to activate the power of the Divine Concealment Talisman, so he could only keep dodging until Artur's Bloodrage state subsided.

Artur stopped, the bloodlust emanating from his body began to dissipate, and even his slightly reddened eyes gradually returned to normal.

He turned to look at Kong Wang, unable to resist praising, "You are indeed very strong, and you can even see through my attacks. Is it a pupil technique?"

Kong Wang nodded: "It is a pupil technique."

"Truly worthy of being a cultivator from the Great Chu, your methods are numerous."

An aged voice rang out, followed by a giant striding forward, its body over three meters tall.

Kong Wang looked up at the giant, his brow twitching.

He sensed an immense, overwhelming power emanating from the giant before him.

And that blood aura, soaring skyward, was incredibly intense.

This must be a true powerhouse among the barbarians!

“Ninth Elder? What brings you here?” Artur asked, surprised to see the giant.

“With all this commotion, if I hadn’t come, I could have resigned from my position as overseer!”

The Ninth Elder of the barbarian tribe spoke in a booming voice.

Meng En, being so close to the Ninth Elder, nearly went deaf from the noise and quickly covered his ears and ran away.

Then, the Ninth Elder looked at Kong Wang, his eyes gleaming, and said, “Boy, you are very strong, and it seems you still have some tricks up your sleeve. Are you trying to show Artur some mercy?”

“No, this humble monk has already used all his strength,” Kong Wang replied, shaking his head.

"Hahaha, the people of Great Chu haven't changed a bit. They're still so good at social maneuvering, always saving face for others," the Ninth Elder laughed heartily.

Artur looked slightly embarrassed.

The Ninth Elder's words were practically a declaration of his defeat.

Kong Wang hadn't gone all out; he still had other methods up his sleeve. Artur, on the other hand, had even used his trump card, Blood Rage.

By that calculation, Kong Wang was definitely the winner.

Fortunately, being a barbarian, Artur was also very decisive. Since he had lost, he had lost; there was nothing to be ashamed of.

The other barbarians, seeing this result, all focused their attention on Kong Wang.

They hadn't expected this Great Chu cultivator to be so powerful.

Didn't their elders say that the strength of Great Chu cultivators was about the same as theirs?

How come it seemed like everyone who came was so strong?

Even the king's own brother, Artur, had been defeated.

"Artur, go to the camp and receive your hundred lashes." The Ninth Elder suddenly said calmly.

"Huh?" Artur looked reluctant, but ultimately obeyed, head bowed, and walked towards the camp.

Kong Wang watched this scene, somewhat puzzled.

Meng En watched his brother leave with sympathy, then whispered in Kong Wang's ear, "The King forbids Artur from sparring with others on the battlefield. Violation will result in a whipping. Hehe, this isn't a military order, but a family rule!"

Kong Wang looked at the somewhat gloating expression on Meng En's face and suddenly couldn't help but suspect that Meng En's suggestion of sparring was just a ploy to get Artur to participate and get whipped.

This seemingly simple-minded fellow with thick eyebrows and big eyes shouldn't be trusted so easily!

"Young friend, please come inside for a chat."

The Ninth Elder's voice boomed like thunder, and Kong Wang quickly nodded solemnly.

He was then invited into the barbarian camp.

The military camps here were constructed using ironwood, a species unique to the wilderness.

At the joints, the ironwood was directly burned with intense heat, turning it into a glue-like substance. Then, cold water was poured on top, and after it completely solidified, the bond was firmly established.

Kong Wang, seeing this, thought it was quite convenient.

The barbarian camps were enormous, reflecting the barbarians' robust physiques.

The stronger they were, the larger they were.

Even a powerful figure like the Ninth Elder, a strong eighth-tier Venerable, was over three meters tall in his normal state—a veritable giant.

Of course, they could revert to the size of a normal human.

But there's absolutely no need for that. Among barbarian giants, strength is a symbol of power; no one would compress or shrink their size.

On the contrary, there are countless examples of people increasing their size to show off.


.
Chapter 266 Focus of Attention
.

As soon as Kong Wang entered the barbarian camp, several giants who had been waiting for a long time turned their gazes towards him.

"So this is a cultivator from the Great Chu? Spanning hundreds of thousands of miles of demon territory?"

A large, bearded giant spoke with interest, standing up to scrutinize Kong Wang.

"Are you a monk from the Golden Dragon Temple?" the bearded giant suddenly asked.

"No," Kong Wang shook his head directly.

"No? Then why were you able to use the Lotus Seal just now? As far as I know, that's a closely guarded secret of the Golden Dragon Temple!"

the bearded giant said coldly.

Kong Wang explained, "That was privately passed down to me by a senior from the Golden Dragon Temple. This humble monk comes from the Divine Mountain of Qingzhou, not from the Golden Dragon Temple."

"The Divine Mountain of Qingzhou!?"

the bearded giant was surprised, feeling that the name Qingzhou sounded familiar.

It wasn't until the Ninth Elder reminded him that he remembered.

"Hmm? So it's that great state recently abandoned by the Great Chu court. How come it hasn't been conquered by the demons yet?"

The bearded giant stroked his beard and said.

Kong Wang directly replied that the demon army had been driven out of Qingzhou, and Qingzhou had now returned to peace.

"You've got something," the bearded giant said.

At this moment, another giant began to interrogate Kong Wang, asking about his purpose in coming to the Wilderness.

Kong Wang's answer was naturally the same as what he had told Artur: he had heard the news that the barbarian demigod was about to attain sainthood, so he had traveled a great distance to investigate.

"You're lying. The court wouldn't send anyone to investigate. In the vast void, a journey of ten thousand miles is instantaneous. For the Chu Emperor to know the situation in the Wilderness, it would only take a few breaths," the bearded giant said, narrowing his eyes.

Kong Wang remained unmoved and continued, "This humble monk never said I came to investigate on behalf of the Great Chu Dynasty."

"Oh? Not the Great Chu Dynasty? Then who do you represent? The Dragon-Slaying Sect?"

The bearded giant's tone suddenly turned cold.

An invisible pressure, unique to an eighth-tier Venerable, began to emanate from him, pressing down on Kong Wang.

Kong Wang remained calm, the Divine Concealment Talisman within his body flickering slightly, naturally dissipating the bearded giant's pressure.

"This humble monk has already stated, I come from the Divine Mountain of Qingzhou, so naturally I represent the Divine Mountain," Kong Wang said slowly.

A hint of surprise flashed in the bearded giant's eyes.

Not because of Kong Wang's words, but because the pressure he had just released had no effect on the person before him.

How could the other party so easily dissipate his pressure?

Could it be that he possesses a supreme treasure?

"Heh, you're probably not simple, and the Divine Mountain you represent is no simple matter either!" the bearded giant said in a deep voice.

The person behind the other party, who could so easily dispel his pressure, must be at least an eighth-tier Venerable.

They might even be a Half-Saint!

But aren't there only five Half-Saints among humans?

Three from the Great Chu, one from the Dragon-Slaying Sect, and one from the Wilderness.

Could there be another cultivator secretly achieving Half-Saint status, unknown to anyone?

The bearded giant was filled with doubt and suspicion. He then looked at Kong Wang and said in a deep voice, "Since you come representing the Divine Mountain, you didn't come to the Wilderness just to witness the King's enlightenment, did you?"

"Yes!" Kong Wang nodded seriously.

The bearded giant exchanged glances with the other giants.

Finally, the Ninth Elder spoke up: “Since that’s the case, then you should go find Artur first. As for the King’s attainment of enlightenment, don’t even think about it. This matter is beyond your control. Whether you can witness it firsthand depends on your fate.”

Having said that, he dismissed Kong Wang.

“Grand Elder, is this person trustworthy?” The Ninth Elder looked at the bearded giant.

The bearded giant was silent for a moment, then shook his head, but then nodded, saying, “Even if he’s not trustworthy, do we have any other way out?”

“The Southern Frontier tribes…”

The Ninth Elder was interrupted halfway through his sentence by the Grand Elder: “The Southern Frontier tribes are entangled with the Old Gods, the cause and effect are unclear, it’s not advisable to get too involved with them, this path is not feasible.”

“Grand Elder, I have a question, why can’t we entangle ourselves with the Old Gods? The Old Gods are so powerful, wouldn’t it be much easier to deal with demons with their assistance?”

At this moment, the Eighth Elder stepped forward and spoke up.

“You don’t understand.” The First Elder shook his head. He hadn’t originally intended to say more, but after some thought, he spoke up: “The Old Gods were abandoned by the previous era. They should have perished in the divine calamity, but who knew something would go wrong, allowing some of them to survive. Perhaps this was their only chance.”

“But even if they survived, they were still abandoned by the era, rejected by the rules of heaven and earth, and ultimately destined for destruction!”

“That’s all I know. There are far too many secrets involved, even the King knows very little. Perhaps only those ancient saints of that time would know the truth.”

The elders were hearing these secrets for the first time, their hearts filled with doubt and uncertainty.

The Old Gods were rejected by the rules of heaven and earth; no wonder they dared not show themselves, only hiding in the void, using other means to collect the merits of faith to heal their wounds and recover their strength.

“If the Southern Frontier tribes are no good, who else can we choose? The sacred mountain that the monk mentioned?” the Ninth Elder asked in a deep voice.

“Mount Shen, I’ve never heard of this before. Will Artur be able to go with them?” The Eighth Elder asked worriedly, as this involved the continuation of the barbarian bloodline!

“Whether it’s possible or not, they have to go. Artur and the others are just one path; there are others. As long as one path leads to survival, the barbarian bloodline won’t be severed!” The First Elder said gravely, “The King is going all in, but he is the King of the barbarians. He can’t allow the barbarian bloodline to be extinguished and the entire race wiped out. That’s why he’s prepared so many backup plans.”

“As for Artur, you must never let him know. He will have to leave.”

The Ninth Elder and the others nodded in agreement: “We all know that. Artur is incredibly talented, possessing the potential of the King in his prime. His survival is hope. Let us old bones accompany the King and fight to the very end!”

“It’s too early to talk about this now. If the King succeeds in attaining enlightenment, these backup plans will be unnecessary,” the First Elder said slowly.

However, their hearts remained heavy.

To attain sainthood and achieve enlightenment is incredibly difficult!

Wang was a genius of unparalleled talent, a legend of his time.

Yet, throughout the millennia, countless brilliant individuals have emerged, yet not a single one has ultimately succeeded in attaining sainthood.

In short, regardless of whether they succeed in attaining enlightenment, they must prepare for the worst.

Therefore, they prepared several backup plans, selecting talented and promising young members of the barbarian tribes to send away at the last minute.

This included the king's younger brother, Artur.

However, Artur's destination was still undecided, but Kong Wang's arrival gave the elders some ideas.

Perhaps sending Artur to Qingzhou wouldn't be bad?
