Chapter 267 Heavenly Cave
.

After Kong Wang left the military camp, Meng En immediately approached him and asked the elders what business they had with Kong Wang.

"Nothing much, just a few simple questions," Kong Wang replied simply.

Meng En curled his lip, disbelieving in his heart.

However, since several elders were involved, he dared not ask any further questions. Instead, he became gloating and began to complain to Kongwang about how terrible Artur had been after being whipped.

"Luckily, I'm alright!" Meng En said cheerfully.

Seeing his reaction, Kong Wang became even more suspicious that this guy was deliberately setting Artur up.

But before Meng En could finish celebrating, he saw Artur walking over with a dark expression.

The clothes on his back were tattered from being whipped, and blood was seeping through them.

“Meng En!” Artur said coldly.

Meng En was startled, and when he turned around and saw that it was Artur, his expression immediately changed.

"Good brother, you're finally back! I just saw you being whipped, and it really upset me. If it weren't for the strict military rules, I would have taken the punishment for you!" Meng En said casually.

"Heh, I heard everything you just said!" Artur sneered.

Meng En looked embarrassed, then smiled sheepishly, indicating that he knew nothing.

"Get out of here now, or I'll beat you up right now!"

"Alright!" Meng En ran off decisively.

Artur's expression softened a little as he watched Meng En leave.

He then looked at Kong Wang, curious about what the elders had said to him.

"Elder, did you ask me to do anything?" Artur asked with a strange expression.

"What to do?"

Kong Wang was somewhat puzzled and shook his head, saying, "No, the seniors only asked a few questions, and that was it."

Artur stared at Kongwang's expression and, seeing that he didn't seem to be lying, nodded in relief.

"That's good. In any case, I will never leave the wilderness!" he said firmly.

"Huh?" Kong Wang looked surprised, not understanding what Artur meant by saying that.

But seeing that he was unwilling to talk about it anymore, I didn't press him further.

Then Artur asked Kongwang whether the elders wanted him to leave or stay.

Kongwang replied, "The seniors have agreed to let this humble monk stay. As for whether I can witness the Barbarian King attain enlightenment, that depends on my fate."

“Well then, you can stay in the barbarian camp. But let me make this clear first: you won’t be staying in the barbarian camp for nothing. If the demons attack, you must help them!” Artur said.

Kong Wang nodded solemnly; he was naturally aware of this.

Seeing Kong Wang nod in agreement, Artur also smiled.

She went up to Kong Wang and put her arm around his shoulder, which made Kong Wang feel very uncomfortable.

And so, Kong Wang remained in the barbarian camp.

During this time, Artur also began to inquire about Kong Wang and learn about the various situations in Da Chu.

Kong Wang naturally speaks freely, but he only answers questions about things he is aware of.

It was only at this moment that Artur learned that Kongwang was not a person of the Great Chu, but belonged to the Divine Mountain.

This sacred mountain is located within the territory of Qingzhou, which is under the jurisdiction of the Great Chu Dynasty.

When Artur first heard about the sacred mountain, he was very curious.

I wonder what kind of power this is, and why it can cultivate a genius like Kong Wang?

He had fought against Kong Wang before and knew Kong Wang's abilities.

He was far stronger than him; in the Great Chu, such a person would probably be considered a top-tier genius.

Kong Wang explained briefly that the Divine Mountain was the Mountain God, and he could be considered the Mountain God's messenger.

Beneath the sacred mountain, there are millions of people who are all protected by the mountain god.

Demons dare not attack, and the people live in peace and contentment. It can be said to be the only paradise in the world today.

Artur frowned slightly after hearing this.

He didn't have much affection for the gods' words, simply because he had also heard the Grand Elder mention rumors about the old gods.

The mountain god mentioned by Kong Wang is very likely an ancient god that survived from thousands of years ago.

But he had some doubts in his heart. Didn't the Grand Elder say that it was impossible for the Great Chu to have ancient gods?

What's going on here?

Artur then went to find several elders and reported the matter to them.

Upon hearing this, the elders all looked surprised.

They hadn't questioned Kong Wang before because they had already assumed in their hearts that there couldn't possibly be any ancient gods in the Great Chu.

But who would have thought that the sacred mountain behind the illusion was actually inhabited by deities?

"How is that possible? After the divine calamity, would any old god still dare to approach the Great Chu? Wouldn't that be courting death?" The First Elder was the first to disbelieve.

Even if the old gods survived, they hid in various remote corners of the world. How could they dare to approach the vicinity of the Heavenly Cave?

The Heavenly Cave is located in the sky above the Great Chu Kingdom, and the entire Great Chu Kingdom is within the Heavenly Cave's radiation range.

If any of the old gods dare to approach, their only fate will be death!

The Ninth Elder frowned. If the sacred mountain was an old god's power, then they couldn't send Artur there.

As the king's younger brother, Artur was their greatest hope for the future.

"Are we sure that mountain god is an old god? Could it be that a new god is about to be born?" the Fourth Elder speculated.

"That's even more impossible. After the ancient sage severed the connection between heaven and earth, there were no more gods in the world."

The Grand Elder shook his head decisively, never having considered the possibility of a new god being born.

His expression was solemn, if he was neither an old god nor a new god.

Then there's only one other possibility: Outer Gods!

Beyond the Heavenly Cave, there exists an unknown entity.

Those things were called Outer Gods by the Old Gods and Ghost Spirits by the Ancient Saints.

If it's one of those things, then it's even more terrifying!

The elder felt a cold sweat break out on his back.

However, after calming down, he realized that his thoughts were a bit too exaggerated.

If things outside the Heavenly Cave could enter so easily, then this world would have been destroyed long ago.

So it probably isn't.

But if it's neither an old god nor a new god, nor an external god, then what is it?

The elders couldn't figure it out and eventually had to give up their guesses.

At the very least, the other side appears to be on the side of humanity, and that's enough.

Nowadays, the barbarians don't have the energy to care about these things; the king's path to enlightenment is the most important thing.

"Let's leave this matter aside for now. Since they've come with good intentions, let's consider them friends for now and not be too wary, but we still need to be cautious."

Finally, the Grand Elder made the decision.

The others nodded, and the matter was put aside for the time being. They still needed to prepare the things that Wang Zhengdao would need.

That is the most crucial and important link.

This concerns the very survival of the barbarian tribe!

"Elder, I want to go too," Artur said hurriedly.

"No, your cultivation level is too low. Go back. There will be things you need to do then," the Grand Elder said calmly.

Chapter 268 Dialogue in the Void
.

Artur looked unwilling, but he seemed to understand what the elders were thinking.

But he refused to be a deserter; he wanted to stay here and live or die with the barbarians!

The Ninth Elder could see Artur's feelings; both sides were aware of it, but neither of them said it aloud.

He sighed, “The chance of attaining sainthood is less than one in a hundred. The king has already gone all in and cannot afford to be unprepared.”

"Artur, as the king's younger brother, you must shoulder this responsibility. Preserving the barbarian bloodline and ensuring its continuation is the only hope!"

After saying this, the Ninth Elder patted Artur on the shoulder and left.

Artur was left alone, looking dejected.

Kong Wang stayed in the wilderness for several days, during which time he observed the wilderness's specific strength.

After investigating, he was quite surprised.

The strength of the barbarians was actually not much weaker than that of the Great Chu.

Because of the different cultivation system in the wilderness, every member of the barbarian race can be a cultivator. As long as they train their physical body and temper their blood and qi, they can all step into the first level and become a cultivator.

However, for realms beyond the first level, talent is required.

The talents of barbarians are rather arbitrary; as long as your physical talent is strong, you can go further in the future!

Originally, the barbarian tribe had nine eighth-level Venerables, but three of them have died, leaving only six.

There were dozens of other seventh-tier experts, a considerable number, which is why they were able to hold off the demon army for so long.

The number of lower-level cultivators is even greater.

More importantly, Kong Wang discovered that after killing demons, barbarian cultivators could devour them to restore their blood energy, which made them stronger in battle and provided a continuous supply of blood energy.

The fighting scenes were extremely bloody and barbaric.

Moreover, sometimes they may even consume the flesh and blood of demons that they cannot ingest, leading to poisoning or various abnormalities.

But this is only a minority; most people will not encounter it.

Moreover, the barbarians are not stupid; they will not eat anything whose flesh is obviously poisonous.

"No wonder the barbarians have been able to hold out until now with this fighting style."

Kong Wang thought to himself that the barbarians were more like demons than demons.

Meng En added, "Demons can eat people, but humans can't eat demons?"

Kong Wang thought it made a lot of sense; it was a case of "if Kou can go, so can I."

"The demons have launched another massive attack!"

On this day, the sound of drums resounded in the barbarian army camp, and soldiers shouted, their voices echoing throughout the entire camp.

In an instant, countless barbarian warriors surged forth, each one bursting with bloodlust and power.

"Where are the demons?" Artur stepped out of the air and scanned the distance.

"To the east, right where the Third Elder perished last time."

Upon hearing the name of the Third Elder, a trace of grief flashed in Artur's eyes, which then turned into resentment!

"Follow me into battle!" he shouted.

Then, his thousands of barbarian warriors quickly gathered around him.

Kong Wang and Meng En also came to his side.

In addition, several other barbarian legions are also beginning to gather, and seventh-tier powerhouses have already arrived.

"kill!"

As the word "kill" rang out, the barbarian army marched in a mighty torrent towards the demons.

This was Kong Wang's first time following the barbarian army in a battle against demons.

Upon arriving at the battlefield, his expression turned slightly grave.

Artur said from the side, "Don't be nervous. You'll be with me in a bit, a powerful warrior who will behead demons!"

There are tactics on the battlefield; soldiers fight soldiers, and generals fight generals.

For fifth-tier powerhouses like them, the ones they need to deal with are fifth-tier demons.

More powerful demons will naturally be dealt with by the strong barbarian warriors.

"I heard that the demon demigod has also come?" Kong Wang asked in a deep voice.

“Yes, he is a half-saint of the demon race, named Qingyu Ci. He is the royal family member of the demon race, Tianqing Hu, and his strength is extremely powerful. However, with the king present, he dares not act rashly,” Artur explained slowly.

Kong Wang nodded, no wonder the demons' demigods haven't made a move yet.

"They're here. Get ready!" Artur's tone became serious.

On the opposite side, countless demons gathered, black mist obscured the sky, demonic energy soared into the heavens, and evil energy permeated the air.

But the barbarians were not afraid at all, and they stood firm against the arrogance of the demons.

"Kill!!" The sounds of battle rang out, and the two sides clashed completely.

Upon contact, thousands of barbarians and demons were instantly torn to shreds.

The barbarians tore the demons apart with their bare hands and devoured their flesh and blood. The demons did the same, and the battlefield became a scene of gluttonous cannibalism.

Kong Wang directly confronted two fifth-tier demons, but they could not last more than ten rounds against him before being killed one by one.

"Well done!" Artur exclaimed in admiration upon seeing this, and then resumed his battle with the demons.

At this moment, the Divine Concealment Talisman within Kong Wang's body emitted a faint light.

When Li Yue's divine sense descended, he witnessed the great battle between the barbarians and the demons.

"They've started fighting?" Li Yue asked in surprise.

He had already projected his divine sense when Kongwang arrived in the wilderness.

Therefore, he learned a lot about the wilderness, and even when Kong Wang was talking with several barbarian elders, he was watching silently.

He was quite surprised by the situation with the barbarians.

It is truly remarkable that they have survived to this day.

It was only thanks to the strength of the barbarian demigod Arturu that the barbarians were able to lead the tribe to persevere for so long.

Looking at the battlefield now, it seems that the demons have launched a large-scale attack, but upon closer inspection, the demons have not deployed many powerful warriors, only a larger number of them.

"Is this an attempt to exhaust the barbarian forces through sheer numbers?" Li Yue wondered to himself.

The demon race's half-saint was clearly being held back by the barbarian race's half-saint, and dared not make any rash moves.

There aren't many eighth-tier Venerable Realm cultivators among the demons, and if they were all mobilized, they might encounter a Barbarian Half-Saint.

Unless a demon demigod also comes in person, but the other party has not yet appeared and we don't know where he is.

Perhaps the barbarian demigod saw this opportunity and decided to attain sainthood?

Li Yue thought to himself.

Even so, the barbarians couldn't hold out for long.

The number of demons is simply endless; as soon as one batch dies, another batch is sent in.

"When exactly is the time for a barbarian demigod to attain enlightenment?" Li Yue asked, somewhat puzzled.

We can't wait until all the barbarians are wiped out before we try to become saints, can we?

By then, it would be too late.

Suddenly, Li Yue's divine sense detected an anomaly in the void. Without much hesitation, he activated the void and sent his divine sense into it.

The void remained a vast emptiness, utterly desolate.

But at this moment, Li Yue saw specks of starlight—no, those weren't starlight, but two true spirits.

"Arturu, stop dreaming. Give up resistance, and the Holy Court might spare a bloodline of your barbarians, making you second-class servants!"

A voice rang out, its tone laced with sarcasm.

Li Yue concealed his aura and slowly approached, then he clearly saw the appearance of the two true spirits.

Chapter 269 Daring to be the First
.

One of the true spirits resembled a giant, towering and resolute, with unwavering eyes.

Li Yue recognized it at a glance as Aturu, the half-saint of the barbarian race.

The other true spirit, though human in appearance, had eight swaying blue fox tails faintly visible behind it.

Li Yue even saw a newly sprouted tail, though only half-grown.

This must be Qing Yuci, the half-saint of the demon race.

This was Li Yue's first encounter with a half-saint level cultivator, and he was astonished.

Since he only sent a portion of his divine sense, he didn't approach too closely to avoid being discovered.

“Barbarians will never be slaves! What can you do to me?” Aturu said calmly.

"How about it? I can annihilate your barbarian race!"

Qing Yuci sneered, continuing, "Aturu, do you really think you can succeed in attaining sainthood? No one has succeeded in a thousand years, and you think you can?"

"Whether I succeed or not, we'll only know after we try!" Aturu replied coldly, his tone leaving no room for argument.

"Hahaha, I'm not afraid to tell you, the two Holy Venerables warned me never to attain sainthood, or I would surely die. Don't you even consider your own strength? You, a barbarian, do you think you know more than the Holy Venerables?"

Qing Yuci laughed derisively, completely disregarding Aturu.

Arturu remained unmoved, calmly stating, "If the path ahead is truly blocked, why are you so afraid? You've even abandoned the Great Chu, betting all your strength on the Barbarian Lands?"

"Isn't this fear of me? Those two won't let you attain sainthood, saying the consequence will be certain death, yet why are they still focusing their attention on me? Isn't this out of concern?"

"Is it possible that your Holy Venerable is preventing you from attaining sainthood because he fears your success will threaten their positions? You've been fooled, kept in the dark!"

Qing Yuci's expression changed slightly, then erupted in fury!

Let me tell you, it's useless! If you remain obstinate and ungrateful, then the only fate for the barbarians will be annihilation!"

"Heh," Arturu chuckled softly, "Am I being so cunning? Qingyuci, you are supposed to be a half-saint of the demon race, why don't you have a brain?"

Qingyuci's face darkened.

"The Half-Saint realm is already the limit of the mortal realm; beyond that is only the Saint realm. You say the Demon Race Half-Saint is in seclusion, what is she cultivating?"

"Fellow Daoist of the Demon Race, she is in seclusion cultivating divine abilities. Arturu, don't speculate about us with malice!" Qing Yuci frowned, but his thoughts were somewhat chaotic.

But he wasn't a pushover either; he was a half-saint, after all!

Qing Yuci spoke coldly, then stepped forward and decisively attacked.

In an instant, the void trembled, and countless mosquito-like starlight converged, swirling around Qing Yuci.

His true spirit transformed into its original form, a hundred-foot-long, eight-tailed blue fox suddenly appearing, its head raised high, letting out a roar.

At the same time, an incredibly terrifying semi-saint's aura emanated, causing immense trepidation!

Li Yue, watching from afar, also felt this semi-saint's aura.

He was slightly surprised: "Something's got something. This aura is quite strong!"

Of course, it was only "quite strong."

It was the strongest aura he had ever seen.

"Hmph! Daring to swagger around with only eight tails? I'll tear them off!"

Aturu said calmly.

He merely raised his hand slightly, and countless forces gathered in it.

Facing the hundred-foot-tall blue fox, Aturu didn't even glance up. With a light leap, he landed right in front of the fox.

"Die!" Qingyu Thorn swung its giant claw, carrying a terrifying aura. Where the claw passed, ripples appeared in the void.

Seeing the power of this claw, Li Yue estimated that one strike could probably wipe out a county, a large county like Tianyue County at that.

However, this was the Void, an incredibly special space, which is why the claw attack didn't seem very powerful.

"Is that all the strength you've got?" Aturu sneered, casually throwing a punch to meet it head-on.

The force of the punch swept through, whistling like a typhoon.

The fist and claw collided, creating a violent explosion within the Void.

Ripples spread, causing the Void to tremble.

Further, boundless power erupted, filling the Void, then being absorbed by the Void itself, returning to emptiness.

Qingyuci flew several miles, his massive body heaving, clearly having suffered a significant impact.

Aturu, on the other hand, stood calmly in place, only his chest rising and falling slightly as he breathed lightly.

"How about it? Want to continue?" Aturu said indifferently.

Qingyuci's expression grew even more grim.

He hadn't expected to be defeated even with his full strength, which made him doubt his own abilities.

Was he too weak? Or was Aturu too strong?

“Fighting now is meaningless! When you attain sainthood, let's see if you still have the same spirit you have today,” Qingyuci said coldly.

“Haha, then you're welcome to come and witness my sainthood then.”

Aturu laughed heartily, completely unconcerned about Qingyuci's threat.

If he were afraid, he wouldn't have dared to be the first to attain sainthood before everyone else.

He was determined to take that one in a hundred chance!

"You won't succeed. Just wait for the barbarian race to be wiped out!" Qingyuci left these words before his true spirit withdrew from the void.

Aturu watched Qingyuci leave calmly, then his expression gradually turned serious.

Finally, he sighed softly.

Chapter 270 Aftermath
.

“I’ve prepared my escape route; this is all I can do.”

Aturu muttered to himself.

He knew all too well that the chance of attaining sainthood was nearly nonexistent.

But he had no choice but to take this step.

If he didn’t, the barbarians would be doomed.

Li Yue watched from the shadows; the outcome of their duel was exactly as he had predicted.

Aturu’s strength was greater than Qingyu Ci’s.

This was also the first time he had witnessed a battle between half-saints, albeit in the void, but it had given him a rough idea of ​​their strength.

It was roughly equivalent to his own strength at the fourth-tier Saint Mountain level, perhaps even weaker.

Now that he was a fifth-tier Saint Mountain, he could easily crush these two without even using his physical body; his divine sense alone was enough.

“Saints are even stronger, but it seems I can handle them too.”

Li Yue felt much more confident.

Previously, lacking a comparison of strength, he always felt inadequate, suffering from a "lack of firepower syndrome."

Now he felt much better.

"However, I still haven't witnessed the power of a Saint, so it's best to advance to the sixth-tier Saint Mountain as soon as possible."

As Li Yue thought this, he noticed that Aturu hadn't left the Great Void; instead, he was waiting in the same spot.

"Who is he waiting for?" Li Yue wondered.

Those who can enter the Great Void are at least at the Half-Saint level, so the person Aturu is waiting for must be incredibly powerful.

Is he waiting for some Half-Saint?

Suddenly, from the depths of the Great Void, a mysterious bronze palace slowly floated out.

This palace exuded an ancient aura, its surface riddled with various pitted and scarred sword marks.

Li Yue was incredibly astonished when he saw this bronze palace.

Because this palace wasn't a spiritual object, nor was it something on a mental level; it was a real, tangible object!

This was the first time Li Yue had seen a real object in the Great Void.

"How were the actual objects brought into the Void?"

He was puzzled, and at the same time, he sensed a familiar aura emanating from the bronze palace.

The bronze palace slowly floated over, finally stopping before Aturu.

A majestic female voice echoed from within the bronze palace: "Aturu, have you made up your mind? Follow me, and I can give you hope for the continuation of your barbarian bloodline!"

"No!" Aturu's tone was firm, his voice deep and resolute: "I am not on the same path as you. The fate of the barbarians will be decided by the barbarians themselves. I cannot accept your conditions."

"Hehehe." Laughter echoed from within the bronze palace, seemingly mocking him.

"Mere mortal ants, do you really think you can attain sainthood and defy fate?" The female voice was disdainful, completely skeptical of Aturu's ability to achieve sainthood.

"Even mortal ants can defy heaven and defeat gods!" Aturu said calmly.

The bronze palace fell silent instantly.

The person inside seemed enraged, sneering, "Ants are just ants, utterly foolish. Since you wish to die, then go."

"Don't blame me for not warning you, under those guys' absolute power, no one can attain sainthood, including those guys!"

With that, the bronze palace began its ascent into the depths of the void.

Aturu gazed deeply at the bronze palace, ultimately saying nothing more.

Deep down, he knew his chances of attaining enlightenment were zero.

"Even if it's a dead end, I must go and see!" Aturu whispered.

Then, he too withdrew from the Void.

In the distance, Li Yue watched for a long time, finally recognizing who was inside the bronze palace.

Wasn't this the deity who had tested him from the Void last time?

It seemed to be called some kind of witchcraft goddess, a deity worshipped by the tribes of the Southern Frontier.

So this thing was hiding in the Void, with a bronze palace to conceal its presence; no wonder he couldn't find it.

"That bronze palace must be some kind of treasure. To be able to roam the Void, it's no ordinary object."

Li Yue pondered. It was just that the palace was too dilapidated; otherwise, it could serve as his palace.

Currently, the only physical objects he knew of that could appear in the Void, besides his own divine mountain, were this bronze palace.

"The score from last time hasn't been settled yet. Now that it's delivered to our doorstep, let's settle it properly!"

Li Yue's gaze sharpened as he watched the bronze palace disappear into the distance.

Although he only possessed a portion of his divine sense, it was more than enough to deal with a remnant god.

With a thought, Li Yue immediately followed.

Inside the departing bronze palace, the Goddess of Sorcery sat high on her platform, her eyes utterly calm, yet a hint of anger could be discerned.

How could she possibly be rejected by a mere mortal ant?

Even if Aturu truly became a saint, in her eyes, he would still be no different from a mortal.

Those who truly earned her respect were the powerful individuals who had made significant strides on the path to sainthood.

Only those individuals could truly transcend the mortal realm and defy fate.

Like the ancient saints.

"Ignorant of destiny, such foolish mortals are useless to us."

The Goddess of Sorcery shook her head and murmured, showing no regret.

She was merely a half-saint; if she wanted to nurture them, she could cultivate several half-saints in ten years.

If it weren't for the current urgent situation, requiring someone to help her navigate the mortal realm, why would she, a goddess, speak with mere ants?

Truly, times have changed; even gods must bow their heads.

The Goddess of Sorcery deeply felt the difficulties of the present, which fueled her even greater desire to restore her strength.

If she could recover even half of her peak power, she could easily face the current situation.

"The barbarians are ignorant; then we can only set our sights on the Great Chu."

The Goddess of Sorcery frowned: "But the Heavenly Cave is over there in Great Chu; it's too dangerous..."

The Heavenly Cave was actually the hole from which the crimson meteorite had fallen.

However, this hole wasn't created by the crimson meteorite; it already existed, only temporarily sealed by the ancient saints using great divine power.

Finally, the crimson meteorite broke the seal, reopening the Heavenly Cave.

The thought of the Heavenly Cave filled the Goddess of Sorcery with involuntary fear.

The Heavenly Cave was too terrifying; she dared not approach it.

"I still need to find someone to travel in the mortal realm in my place."

The Goddess of Sorcery frowned. The people of the Southern Frontier tribes were still too weak.

The strongest among them were only at the seventh rank. This strength was considerable in a small tribe, but it was insufficient in the Great Chu.

"I must restore my divine source as soon as possible, or perhaps going to the Dragon-Slaying Sect wouldn't be a bad idea."

The Goddess of Sorcery thought to herself.

Unbeknownst to her, a figure of light was already approaching the bronze palace, attempting to enter.

"This bronze palace...it's actually a divine artifact?"

Li Yue was blocked from entering the bronze palace, and sensing the power emanating from it, he was slightly astonished.

This was divine power, something only gods could possess!


.
Chapter 271 Remnants of the Old Era
.

Li Yue tried to ignore the bronze palace's obstruction, but it was utterly futile.

The power emanating from the bronze palace was not much weaker than his divine power; on the contrary, due to its ancient age, it carried an ancient aura that subtly suppressed his power.

"It seems sneaking in won't work; I'll have to use force," Li Yue muttered.

He stopped suppressing his power, and his divine source erupted, dazzling like a blazing sun.

The immense impact caused the bronze palace to shake violently, even reopening old wounds.

Within the bronze palace, the Goddess of Sorcery's expression changed, and she roared, "Who dares to offend me?"

Suddenly, she vanished from her spot, reappearing above the bronze palace.

The Goddess of Sorcery radiated a brilliant light, appearing incredibly divine, especially her six jade arms, which moved with a graceful beauty.

She gazed in the direction the bronze palace had been attacked, where a blurry figure stood, its face indistinct, yet exuding composure.

"This aura..."

The Goddess of Sorcery's expression shifted slightly; she immediately recognized the figure.

Wasn't this the mountain god she had tested in the Great Chu a while back, traveling through the Void?

The opponent's strength had exceeded her expectations, forcing her to flee in disarray.

At that time, she had planned to return to the Great Chu and settle the score once her strength returned to its peak.

But now was not the time. Her strength hadn't even recovered to thirty percent; she was no match for the opponent!

Sensing the danger, the Goddess of Sorcery's first instinct was to flee.

But within the Void, the opportunity had already been locked onto; how could she escape?

Escape was impossible unless she abandoned the bronze palace, but she couldn't bear to.

"Fellow Daoist, what is the purpose of this?"

The Goddess of Sorcery asked calmly.

"What brings you here? Your Majesty seems to have a poor memory; you just visited the sacred mountain a few days ago, how could you have forgotten already?"

Li Yue spoke slowly, while simultaneously scrutinizing the Goddess of Sorcery.

To be honest, this was his first time seeing a deity in person, and he was very curious about the differences between gods and cultivators.

Therefore, Li Yue showed no restraint, his divine sense brazenly scanning the Goddess of Sorcery.

This incredibly offensive act enraged the Goddess of Sorcery!

Although she had long since become a goddess and no longer cared about physical appearance, such unbridled probing was utter disrespect and insult to a deity!

The Goddess of Sorcery simultaneously formed hand seals with her three hands, and the lights of the sun, moon, and stars flickered simultaneously in the void.

Instantly, they transformed into a robe of light, draping over her and blocking Li Yue's divine sense probe.

However, Li Yue had already seen through it all. Physical appearances were secondary; he didn't care about them at all.

The most important thing was that he discovered the difference between deities and cultivators.

The witch goddess before him actually only possessed a true spirit, without any physical body or other external manifestation.

It was only because the witch goddess's true spirit was so solidified that it appeared as if she had a physical body.

This inevitably raised a question in Li Yue's mind: Are all deities only spirits, without physical bodies?

"I was wrong about what happened last time," the Goddess of Sorcery said, calming down. "Fellow Daoist, we are all deities, and it's not easy for us to survive a divine calamity. I was only thinking of my former colleagues, and my actions were a bit hasty; I had no intention of offending you."

Li Yue's eyes flickered slightly upon hearing her words.

She mentioned colleagues? Could it be that these deities had all worked together before?

Could there be some kind of divine palace or heavenly court?

Looking at the massive bronze palace before him, Li Yue suddenly felt this possibility was very high.

"Your actions don't seem like thinking of former colleagues; they're more like you've spotted some prey," Li Yue replied calmly.

The Goddess of Sorcery's expression changed slightly, giving Li Yue a deep look.

However, Li Yue's figure was blurry, making him impossible to discern.

A sudden unease gripped her; she was unsure of the true nature of this mountain god before her.

The sorcerer goddess tentatively asked, "Which deity are you from the past?"

"Guess?" Li Yue didn't answer directly.

Not receiving an answer, the sorcerer goddess frowned deeply.

She carefully sensed the divine aura emanating from Li Yue. He was indeed a deity, but why was this divine aura so unfamiliar?

Back then, she wasn't exactly aloof; she had interacted with almost all the deities, none of whom possessed an aura like this mountain god before her.

As for the God Lord and the three deities of the Sun, Moon, and Stars, she hadn't even considered them.

Because the auras of these four were far more familiar.

The Sun, Moon, and Stars were in the sky, the easiest to identify. And the God Lord's aura was unique, the most distinctive.

The aura of this mountain god before her was completely unfamiliar; she had never encountered it before.

"You are not an ancient god!?" The sorcerer goddess uttered a startling statement, her eyes fixed intently on Li Yue.

Li Yue didn't answer; he felt he couldn't glean much information from the Goddess of Sorcery.

"You don't need to know who I am. I've met you today to settle scores!" Li Yue said calmly.

The Goddess of Sorcery's expression darkened slightly: "Fine, then let's have a match!"

Without hesitation, she raised her hand, and six sorcery dolls flew out, landing on the ground. The dolls, which had been lifeless, instantly came to life.

The sorcery dolls opened their eyes, revealing a sinister light.

Each one exuded the aura of a half-saint!

However, they were still different from true half-saints, being inanimate objects.

"Not bad." Li Yue raised an eyebrow, surprised that she could summon six half-saints with a single move; this method was simply heaven-defying.

He dared not underestimate her. He formed hand seals, unleashing the divine power of "Dharma Body of Heaven and Earth."

In an instant, Li Yue's body swelled, transforming into a towering giant.

The six witchcraft dolls were as small as his toes.

"Divine power!? Your divine source has recovered more than 50%!?"

The witchcraft goddess's expression changed drastically. She recognized at a glance that Li Yue was using divine power, not an ordinary method.

She hurriedly used her silk threads to control the witchcraft dolls, trying to resist them with overwhelming force and using her extreme speed advantage to restrain Li Yue.

However, Li Yue's attack was swift and decisive, instantly killing them.

The six witchcraft dolls, with their half-saint strength, were like weeds before him.

With a thought, Wu Ze, who was cultivating outside the void, suddenly felt a disturbance in his Divine Travel Talisman. Thinking something was wrong, he was startled when the talisman vanished into nothingness.

This startled Wu Ze, completely losing his focus on cultivation.

The Divine Travel Talisman didn't disappear; instead, it appeared in the void and entered Li Yue's body.


.
Chapter 272 The True Power of the Divine Travel Talisman!
.

Summoning the Swift Travel Talisman back into his body, Li Yue felt its joyful exuberance, as if it had finally returned home.

"Let me show you what true speed is!"

Li Yue said calmly.

The Swift Travel Talisman finally unleashed its true power in his hands.

Li Yue simply took a step, and his figure vanished instantly. The witch dolls' attempt to restrain him with their extreme speed failed completely.

Before they had gone far, they were firmly grasped by a pair of large hands.

The witch dolls' faces instantly showed terror. They tried to struggle and unleash their power, but Li Yue gave them no chance, crushing them on the spot.

The witch dolls exploded instantly, countless threads and cotton flying in all directions before turning to ashes.

"How is this possible!?" The witch goddess's face was filled with horror.

Her witchcraft dolls were renowned for their incredible speed. Despite only possessing a half-saint cultivation level, even a saint might not be able to catch them.

Yet this mountain god captured her witchcraft dolls with such ease.

And it seemed she hadn't even used her full strength.

"Damn it, I refuse to believe you have any advantage in speed!" The witchcraft goddess's face turned cold.

Six hands, thirty fingers in total, each bound with a silk thread, connected directly to the witchcraft dolls through the void.

This time, she would control them herself; she refused to believe she could be captured again.

Li Yue stood in the center, his Divine Speed ​​Talisman now unrestricted, granting him ultimate speed.

So now, a single step he took covered a thousand miles, achieving the ability to shrink distances to an inch.

The remaining five witchcraft dolls experienced another surge in power. The witchcraft goddess poured even more divine energy into them, bringing them closer to true half-saint levels!

"A mere trick." Watching the witch dolls flicker around him, constantly seeking an opportunity to attack,

Li Yue found it laughable. The speed the witch goddess prided herself on was, in his eyes, like a slowed-down fly.

He could easily catch them with a single grab.

The true power of the Divine Speed ​​Talisman was fully unleashed upon him.

Li Yue gently reached out, his extreme speed seemingly locking down space itself, directly grabbing at the witch dolls.

Grab, crush!

Three more witch dolls were destroyed on the spot.

The witch goddess's expression changed drastically. At this moment, she finally realized that the strength of the mountain god before her was beyond her current capabilities.

His speed had completely reached the realm of Dao, a return to simplicity.

Unless she recovered her peak strength, she simply couldn't contend with him.

The Goddess of Sorcery was quite clever. Seeing she couldn't defeat Li Yue, she immediately cut her losses, retrieving the remaining two sorcery dolls that hadn't yet been harmed.

"Fellow Daoist, I concede!" she quickly said, lowering her stance.

However, she felt a hesitant weight in her heart, unsure if her opponent would pursue her to the bitter end.

Although she was confident he couldn't kill her, it would come at a heavy price!

"It's good that you concede. Then we must discuss your offense against me," Li Yue said, withdrawing his Dharma Body and looking at the Goddess of Sorcery.

The Goddess of Sorcery's expression turned grim. She spoke in a deep voice, "What does Your Excellency desire?"

"It's not about what I want, but what sincerity you offer," Li Yue retorted.

However, his gaze remained fixed on the bronze palace.

The Goddess of Sorcery immediately understood Li Yue's meaning.

She quickly shook her head, saying, "Impossible! Fellow Daoist, this palace is my temporary residence; I cannot give it away!"

A divine palace?

Li Yue was surprised and scrutinized her again.

He hadn't expected someone of her strength to possess a palace; it seemed she had held a considerable position in the past.

The Goddess of Sorcery blushed under Li Yue's gaze and explained, "Fellow Daoist, in the previous era, I was a renowned deity. Having my own palace is perfectly reasonable."

"Since it's your palace, then forget it," Li Yue said with slight regret.

Although he was very interested in the bronze palace, the Goddess of Sorcery's palace was essentially her home.

Taking her home directly was indeed inappropriate.

"Fellow Daoist, this Divine Lord is skilled in witchcraft. How about I offer these two witchcraft dolls as an apology?"

The Witchcraft Goddess spoke, taking out the two remaining witchcraft dolls.

Li Yue's heart stirred as he looked at the two witchcraft dolls.

These were valuable items. Each one possessed the power of a half-saint. In the Void Realm, it might not seem like much, but what about in the outside world?

In the outside world, the power of a half-saint would be unstoppable.

"Indeed, can these be used indefinitely?" Li Yue asked.

"No," the Witchcraft Goddess shook her head. "These witchcraft dolls were crafted by me using divine source. Unless I personally control them, they cannot be used indefinitely. Once the divine source within is exhausted, the witchcraft dolls become ineffective."

"Is that so?" Li Yue felt a pang of regret.

If they could be used indefinitely, they would be invincible.

However, thinking about it, it was unrealistic. If it were possible, the Witchcraft Goddess would probably have already been using this method to stir up trouble in the outside world.

"But one use is enough. He doesn't need it himself, but he can throw it into Qianshan County for the people there to use."

"Alright, this one will do. But I still have a question for you," Li Yue said calmly.

"As long as I know it, I will tell you everything!"

The Goddess of Sorcery breathed a sigh of relief, thinking that thankfully the other party wasn't a bloodthirsty person, otherwise she would have suffered a great loss today.

Li Yue accepted the Sorcery doll and casually asked the Goddess of Sorcery how long it could last.

The Sorcery doll replied, "The divine energy inside is enough to last for two hours. If the Sorcery doll uses its full power, the divine energy consumption will be too great, and it will only last for one hour."

Li Yue nodded, indicating that he understood, and then asked his question.

"Tell me about the divine calamity," he said slowly.

He was very curious about this divine calamity, which involved the ancient saints and the Heavenly Cave.

"A divine calamity?" The Goddess of Sorcery frowned, glancing at Li Yue thoughtfully.

However, she finally answered, "The divine calamity occurred more than three thousand years ago and lasted for over a thousand years before finally ending."

"Over a thousand years!?"

Li Yue was surprised; he hadn't expected a divine calamity to last so long.

Over a thousand years—the cultivators must have changed several times over.

Even Saints can be replaced in waves, after all, a ninth-tier Saint only has an eight-hundred-year lifespan.

However, the lifespan of gods is an unknown quantity; at least so far, there's no known case of a god perishing due to their lifespan running out, except for those who have fallen from their divine positions.

Falling from their divine positions and becoming mortals means they are no longer gods.

"Yes, it lasted for over a thousand years. Those thousand years were a dark age for gods!" Fear flashed in the eyes of the Goddess of Sorcery, clearly still haunted by that period.

Chapter 273 The Six Realms of a Saint
.

"How dark is it?" Li Yue inquired.

What he was actually more curious about was whether a saint's power was truly that formidable, capable of instilling fear even in the gods.

"Utter darkness!" The Goddess of Sorcery took a deep breath and slowly said, "During that time, an irreconcilable conflict arose between the gods and the ancient saints, escalating into a tense standoff."

"Then something happened, and that became the catalyst, sparking a thousand-year-long war between humans and gods, also known as the Divine Calamity!"

Li Yue listened intently and asked, "And you gods lost?"

The Goddess of Sorcery remained silent for a long time before sighing, "We lost, a crushing defeat! Otherwise, it wouldn't have been called the Divine Calamity. In the war between humans and gods, the ancient saints defied the heavens and attacked the gods, launching a massive assault on the Void, sinking even the temples and shattering them into countless pieces."

"Hundreds of gods, including the lords and deities, died at the hands of the ancient saints. Those who survived all paid a heavy price."

She recounted her story slowly, as if recalling a scene from thousands of years ago.

A dozen ancient saints, wielding sacred artifacts, shattered the void, attacking the Great Void with divine might. At that time, the Great Void wasn't called the Great Void, but rather the Divine Realm.

As the name suggests, it was the world inhabited by these gods, and the divine temple stood within it.

The ancient saints, armed with their sacred artifacts, attacked the Divine Realm, instantly shattering the boundary walls. Countless divine lords fought back, but all perished at the hands of the ancient saints.

Finally, the Divine Lord intervened, leading three divine venerables and several powerful divine lords in a great battle against the ancient saints.

This battle even shattered the heavens, the Divine Realm trembled, and countless divine relics were reduced to ashes by the aftershocks of the battle.

Only the main body of the divine temple remained, shattered into countless fragments drifting throughout the Great Void.

That great battle lasted for countless years.

Gods perished, the divine realm wept blood, and strange phenomena descended.

Ancient saints achieved ultimate sublimation, transforming into the Great Dao, and thus perished.

At that time, the Goddess of Sorcery could not participate in such a great battle; she could only observe from afar.

She only remembered that everything outside the Heavenly Cave was alarmed by the battle between the ancient saints and gods, and retreated a great distance.

In the end, the great battle ended, but only three ancient saints returned.

The rest disappeared without a trace, but the Goddess of Sorcery and the other gods already understood that they had lost.

The ancient sages began to settle scores with them; gods were constantly being slaughtered, and ultimately only a handful survived.

The Goddess of Sorcery was fortunate enough to be one of the surviving gods, and she even found a palace that had flown out after the temple shattered.

This allowed her to slumber in the vast void without getting lost.

"What about the last three ancient sages?" Li Yue pressed.

"They've all perished," the Goddess of Sorcery said calmly.

"What happened?"

Li Yue was surprised. Weren't all the gods already destroyed? How could these last three ancient saints have fallen?

The Goddess of Sorcery explained, "The 'Severing the Heavenly Connection' was a grand scheme orchestrated by these last three ancient saints. When they returned, they were likely severely injured."

"At this time, the Heavenly Cave grew larger and larger, and the things outside began to threaten the realm within. The three ancient saints had no choice but to transform themselves into the Dao, using their life's cultivation to seal off heaven and earth, severing the connection between heaven and earth, setting up shackles on the heavens and earth, and simultaneously patching up the Heavenly Cave."

After listening, Li Yue gradually understood.

He finally understood the origin of the shackles on heaven and earth. However, while the ancient saints isolated those things outside the Heavenly Cave, they also blocked the path of cultivators within the realm.

After them, no new saints emerged in the world!

Didn't the ancient saints consider this?

They probably did. Perhaps in their eyes, even if humanity had no more saints, it was far better than those things outside the Heavenly Cave entering.

That's why they were willing to pay such a high price to seal off the Heavenly Cave.

But now another question arises: are saints really that strong?

To be able to defy the heavens and defeat gods, and even sever the connection between heaven and earth—this is clearly a great divine power. Even Li Yue in his current state might not be able to do it.

Li Yue raised this question, and the Goddess of Sorcery glanced at him suspiciously, though she still answered:

“Even saints have varying strengths. The ancient saints I just mentioned were the most elite saints, having reached the pinnacle of their paths, their cultivation reaching the heavens, stronger than ordinary gods!”

“How are they distinguished?” Li Yue asked curiously.

“There are six realms of sainthood, each realm having a Heavenly Gate!” the Goddess of Sorcery explained, continuing, “Every ancient saint capable of defying the heavens and challenging gods was a powerful figure at the sixth realm of sainthood, having passed through the six Heavenly Gates, achieving immortality!”

“Upon first entering the sainthood realm, one is at the first realm, just like the two current saints of the demon realm; they are at the first realm of sainthood. At my peak, they were like ants.”

The Goddess of Sorcery said calmly.

“I see.”

This was the first time Li Yue had heard of the six realms of sainthood, and he had a general understanding of it.

In short, saints were divided into six realms.

The sixth realm of a sage is the limit of the Dao, with a lifespan equal to that of heaven, meaning immortality.

This also explains the previous contradiction: the 800-year lifespan of a sage likely referred to the first realm.

Further breakthroughs can increase lifespan even further.

“These are the cultivation paths of human cultivators; they have no effect on gods like you and me,” the Goddess of Sorcery said again.

“Then how are the realms of gods distinguished?” Li Yue asked humbly.

Hearing this, the Goddess of Sorcery gave Li Yue a deep look before replying after a long silence: “Gods only have divine positions, not realms!”

“Huh? No realms?” Li Yue was surprised; this was the first time he had heard such a statement.

“Yes, there are no realms. A god is a god; what realm is there to speak of? Strength is simply a matter of the amount of divine source one possesses.”

The Goddess of Sorcery explained, “Powerful gods naturally receive divine titles. There are only three types of divine titles in the temple: God-King, God-Emperor, and God-Lord!”

“You are a God-King?” Li Yue looked at her.

“Yes, my divine title is God-King!” The Goddess of Sorcery said with a slightly arrogant tone.

“A lowly god, doesn’t seem so impressive.”

Li Yue shook his head. He had thought she was a high-ranking god, but it turned out she was just a lowly one.

The witch goddess, her face slightly embarrassed, said, "The position of God-King is already quite remarkable within the temple. Know that there are only three God-Venerables and only one God-Lord!"

Li Yue was speechless. So few?

This God-Lord is really stingy; couldn't they grant more divine positions?

So, besides the three God-Venerables and one God-Lord, everyone else is a God-King?

Li Yue asked this question.

"That's not true," the witch goddess slowly said. "The position of God-King isn't something one can simply accept. There are countless gods without divine positions."

Chapter 274 What exactly is outside the Heavenly Cave?
.

From the witch goddess, Li Yue finally understood the specific situation of the Old Gods.

Thousands of years ago, the Great Void was not called the Great Void, but was known as the Divine Realm.

At this time, the divine realm contained countless deities, all under the rule of the temple.

Powerful deities can obtain a divine position conferred by the temple.

That's the general situation of the Old Gods.

It looks quite like the Heavenly Court in the mythology of my previous life, only this temple is a bit simpler.

Most of these deities were born from the void and are considered to be naturally born gods.

The remaining small portion either attained deity status through incense offerings or through other means.

Li Yue was quite surprised to hear that deities could be born from the Great Void.

However, the witch goddess sighed and said that it was the former Taixu.

The Great Void of today has long been shattered by the ancient sages, and its order and laws have been lost, making it no longer what it once was.

She also stated that the void in the past was bright and sacred, unlike the desolate emptiness and lifelessness of today.

Moreover, if a true spirit stays in the void for too long, there is a risk of getting lost.

The witch goddess was able to slumber in the void thanks to the bronze palace.

Li Yue, however, had no particular feeling about this.

Anyway, he didn't think he would get lost in the Great Void. On the contrary, he felt very comfortable in the Great Void, much more comfortable than in the outside world.

After all, to the outside world, he is like a mountain, unmovable.

Having come to Taixu, at least he can still be a human being.

"You are neither an ancient god nor a cultivator, so who are you?!" The goddess of witchcraft finally asked the question that had been nagging at her heart.

She knew there was something wrong with Li Yue from the question he just asked.

How could a deity like her forget divine calamities? And not even understand the divine ranks and realms of deities?

"You don't need to know that," Li Yue replied calmly.

The witch goddess felt somewhat unwilling.

She always felt that Li Yue had a big problem.

How could such a deity possibly exist in this day and age?

At first, she thought the other party was an ancient god who had just recovered his strength faster.

But now she realized that the other party was not an Old God at all, and that their strength did not seem to have been affected.

More importantly, she realized that the mountain god before her was only a part of his divine consciousness, meaning that this could only be considered a clone of him.

How can a clone possess such power?

Could it be that a new deity has truly been born in the world?

The witch goddess was plunged into deep confusion.

Li Yue didn't care what she was thinking; he still had many questions to ask.

Having finally caught an old god, how can we not drain her dry?

"How did the Heavenly Cave appear? What's outside the Heavenly Cave? Did the ancient sages and old gods have a conflict because of the Heavenly Cave?" Li Yue asked the key question.

The witch goddess hesitated for a moment before replying, "I don't know where the Heavenly Cave came from. I only know that it appeared a long time ago, but initially it was just slowly eroding without any abnormalities. Moreover, the rules of heaven and earth were constantly being repaired, which is why the temple didn't pay much attention to it."

"It wasn't until the speed at which the rules of heaven and earth could repair themselves couldn't keep up with the speed at which the celestial cave eroded them that it caught the attention of powerful figures from all sides. But by then, it was too late."

Listening to this, Li Yue felt as if the patient had initially discovered some minor health problems but then ignored them.

In the end, it developed into a terminal illness, and only then did they regret it.

Sure enough, it was too late to mend the fence after the sheep were lost.

"As for what lies outside the Heavenly Cave, no one knows what it is. They only know that it is dangerous, evil, strange, and ominous. Saints and gods have gone to test it, and their fates were all extremely tragic."

The witch goddess said with lingering fear.

"Furthermore, I don't know about the conflict between the ancient saints and the gods; only the gods and deities can know that. But it is indeed related to the Heavenly Cave."

Li Yue nodded slightly, feeling that asking this question was as good as not asking it at all.

This witch goddess knows nothing and cannot answer many questions.

However, this also provided him with a lot of information.

That is, it seems that everything is related to the Heavenly Cave.

Li Yue wanted to look up at the sky, but then realized that he was in the Great Void and couldn't see the sky.

"Tell me anything else related to the Heavenly Cave," Li Yue said.

The witch goddess began to recall, and then revealed a few more secretive pieces of information.

Li Yue listened silently and took notes.

"Fellow Daoist, may I leave now?"

After revealing everything she knew, the witch goddess exhaled.

Li Yue wanted to say no, but after thinking about it carefully, it seemed there was nothing left to ask.

It wouldn't be right to kill the god to silence him, would it?

So he said, "Okay, but if you offend me again next time, it won't be just a small punishment like today."

Then it released a powerful aura, making the other party feel immense pressure.

"How dare this humble goddess offend you?" The witch goddess lowered her head and said with some shame.

She desperately wanted to avenge her humiliation, but she simply couldn't.

The other party's origins were mysterious, their background unknown, and their strength was unexpectedly powerful, beyond her comprehension.

"Okay, that's fine then." Li Yue nodded lightly and waved his hand, allowing the witch goddess to leave.

The witch goddess breathed a sigh of relief, bowed again, turned and disappeared. Then the bronze palace flickered and vanished into the void in the blink of an eye.

"They run really fast," Li Yue said in a low voice.

Then he looked at the witchcraft doll, a gift from nature. It was a good thing. If he threw it into Qianshan County, he wouldn't have to keep a close eye on it all the time.

"What exactly is outside the Heavenly Cave?" Li Yue frowned deeply again, his heart filled with unease.

After his last immersive experience, he realized that the Heavenly Cave was no ordinary place.

Unexpectedly, this celestial cave spans thousands of years, and even ancient saints and old gods could not solve it.

Li Yue used his divine sense to exit the void and return to the divine mountain, at the same time returning the Divine Travel Talisman to Wu Ze.

Wu Ze, who was filled with anxiety because the Divine Travel Talisman had disappeared, breathed a sigh of relief when he saw it return.

The message then conveyed by the Divine Traveler's Talisman informed him what had happened.

It turned out that the Mountain God had used the Divine Travel Talisman, which instantly dispelled his worries.

Even when the mountain god invoked the divine travel talisman within him, Wu Ze felt honored.

But now it seems that what the rules of heaven and earth have repaired is just a thin barrier.

That was to repair the shackles of heaven and earth that the ancient sage had severed the connection between heaven and earth; it was to mend and patch up what the ancient sage had patched up, and then mend it again.

The actual hole was not repaired, or rather, it was repaired, but the repair speed still could not keep up with the erosion speed.

Chapter 275 Half-Saint Level Guardian
.

In other places, no matter how strict the supervision, corruption is still unavoidable.

Sure, let's finish this cup of holy water first.

This is why Bai Liang is so confident in employing various talents.

All officials worked together as one, which is why Qianshan County has developed so rapidly.

Chapter 276 Establishing a New Empire
.

On the contrary, the demons' extinction would force Great Chu into direct contact with the barbarians, losing any buffer zone, inevitably leading to conflict.

Chapter 279 Preventing a Collapsed Building
.

They had been planning and preparing for years, and now they could finally take the first step?

However, when Song Yihe asked when they would raise the flag, Bai Liang said there was no rush.

“The situation is critical right now. A demigod among the barbarians wants to attain sainthood. We can wait for news from there. If the barbarian demigod succeeds, then we'll have to change our current strategy,” he said.

“Aturu wants to attain sainthood, but the chance is extremely slim; it's just a desperate struggle,”

Song Yihe seemed to know something, shaking his head and sighing.

Bai Liang was surprised and pressed for the reason.

Song Yihe remained silent for a long time before finally saying, "The Ancient Sage's 'Severing Heaven and Earth' technique is a great divine power capable of sealing off heaven and earth. Now that the 'Severing Heaven and Earth' technique has not yet been eliminated, the chance of becoming a saint is almost zero."

"Then why do the barbarian half-saints still want to prove themselves as saints?" Bai Liang frowned, puzzled.

"That's because the barbarians are already on the edge of a precipice. If they don't make a final attempt, they will be gradually devoured by the demons," Song Yihe said in a deep voice.

"They've persisted for over ten years, how come they can't hold on now?"

Chu Wuliu asked curiously, a question shared by most of those present.

"Because the remaining power of the barbarian ancestor has been exhausted," Song Yihe sighed.

"Unlike the Great Chu Dynasty, the barbarians don't have the protection of human destiny. Perhaps due to their internal divisions, they can only rely on their own strength to resist the demons. If they can't resist, they can only rely on the power within the statue of their barbarian ancestor."

"The demons have toiled away for many years to completely erode the power of the barbarian ancestor, leaving only a single half-saint to support the barbarians."

Bai Liang and the others suddenly understood, not expecting this reason.

No wonder the barbarian half-saint was so eager to attain sainthood.

With only him supporting the barbarians, it was indeed insufficient to resist the demons.

If two demon half-saints joined forces, or if a demon saint emerged, the barbarians would be like fish on a chopping block, completely at their mercy.

Therefore, the barbarian half-saint wanted to strike first, proving his Dao through force.

"Once Aturu fails to attain Dao and the barbarians are destroyed, the demons will focus all their attention on the Great Chu. At that time, the pressure on us will be even greater," Song Yihe said in a deep voice.

Bai Liang and the others frowned upon hearing this.

With one less barbarian, the demons could then focus all their efforts on the Great Chu.

The last encounter between Longyun County and the demons was merely a minor skirmish. Once the demon army truly sets foot on Qingzhou, they will be utterly powerless to resist, let alone raise a banner and establish their own independent territory.

"So, before the barbarian semi-saints attain enlightenment, should we seize power and establish our own independent state?" Bai Liang asked solemnly.

"Not necessarily, but we must take Qingzhou first!"

Song Yihe spoke earnestly.

"Qingzhou is now free from the control of the imperial court, making this the best time to take it. I came from Chizhou specifically to seize this opportunity."

"We have also been planning this, but progress has been slow, as there are still some of the capital's top talents stranded in Qingzhou City."

Bai Liang said, briefly outlining his plan to send Yue Qiu to Qingzhou City.

After listening, Song Yihe pondered for a moment: "The prodigies of the capital are not worth paying attention to; they're all juniors, strong in their own right, but lacking in understanding of the bigger picture. However, that disciple of the Imperial Preceptor needs our attention; she has a significant problem."

"Those who remain all have ulterior motives. Although I don't fully understand their purposes, I can roughly discern that they are all related to the Divine Mountain."

"Your proposed plan is too slow. I happen to have a solution," Song Yihe suddenly said.

Bai Liang's eyes flickered slightly. He asked, "What solution?"

"The celestial phenomena!" Song Yihe said.

Bai Liang was taken aback, then immediately recalled the celestial phenomena that had appeared on the Divine Mountain recently, phenomena that had practically covered the entire Qingzhou.

Hearing Song Yihe's explanation, he gradually understood Song Yihe's idea.

"You mean, we can utilize the celestial phenomena that appeared on the Divine Mountain?" Bai Liang asked.

“That’s right!” Song Yihe laughed. “Such a celestial phenomenon appearing on the sacred mountain is an auspicious omen! With a little manipulation and propaganda, it will win the hearts of the people of Qingzhou. Then everyone will worship the Mountain God, and naturally, the world will be united!”

“Feasible!” Bai Liang nodded, finding the plan excellent.

However, it would take time for public opinion to build up, and it would be difficult to achieve this before the barbarian semi-saint attained enlightenment.

“Don’t worry about that. I have my own methods. And it doesn’t necessarily have to be before the barbarian semi-saint achieves enlightenment. As long as we take Qingzhou before the demons shift their focus to the Great Chu, that’s enough!”

Song Yihe said confidently.

But at this moment, Bai Liang asked a crucial question.

"After taking Qingzhou, how do we defend against the demons?"

This question is crucial.

Qingzhou is vast, and the Divine Mountain occupies only a very small portion of it.

Bai Liang can ensure the area around the Divine Mountain remains safe from the demons' attacks, thanks to the protection of the Mountain God.

But what about the areas outside the Divine Mountain?

Those areas are not within the Divine Mountain's protective range!

Chapter 280 New Fortune
.

"Luck!"

Faced with Bai Liang's question, Song Yihe answered decisively.

"Luck?" Bai Liang was even more puzzled.

Isn't luck something only the imperial court possesses now?

And most of the time, it can't even withstand powerful demons; it can only barely protect itself.

"That's right, it's luck!" Song Yihe said excitedly, his eyes shining. "You may not see it, but I can. Above Qianshan County, luck has begun to gradually condense. This luck isn't the decaying, dilapidated luck of the Great Chu. It's luck brimming with the vitality of new life!"

"Right now, this luck is still very weak, but it represents new life. As long as we can continuously strengthen this luck and spread it throughout Qingzhou, a single spark can start a prairie fire. Then, using this newfound luck to resist demons is entirely feasible!"

His eyes shone with hope as he looked out the window at the sky.

There, countless faint golden lights were converging—that was the newborn luck!

Ever since he witnessed the emergence of new fortune in Qianshan County, he knew he had come to the right place.

“Qianshan County is beginning to generate new fortune!?”

Song Yihe’s words astonished Bai Liang and the others.

They hadn’t actually noticed this, mainly because Kong Wang, the only one with the pupil technique, wasn’t on the Divine Mountain; otherwise, he would have discovered it long ago.

“Yes, this newly generated fortune is extremely vigorous, brimming with vitality!” Song Yihe said, his voice trembling.

Xu Cang and the other cultivators who had come with him were also deeply shaken.

They knew very well what newly generated fortune represented!

The Great Chu Dynasty had long since stopped generating new fortune. Since the chaos caused by demons and monsters, it had relied on the fortune accumulated over hundreds of years to resist them.

Without production, only consumption, it was no wonder it was declining!

Unexpectedly, decades later, the first wisp of new fortune would be born in Qianshan County!

"Can we withstand an army of demons with just luck?" Bai Liang asked.

He had witnessed the power of luck before; it was indeed incredibly strong, but against powerful demons, it still seemed somewhat lacking.

"Absolutely! New luck is far superior to the decaying luck of the Great Chu!"

Song Yihe said excitedly. He wanted to explain, but the way of luck was profound and indescribable.

In the end, he could only describe it simply: new luck is ten or even a hundred times stronger than decaying luck.

As long as they could strengthen the luck of Qianshan County and fill the entire Qingzhou, they would have the confidence to face the demons!

"Then how do we strengthen luck?" Bai Liang asked.

Song Yihe calmed down and said, "This old man doesn't know either. We need to figure out why a new life force has emerged in Qianshan County. Once we understand the reason, we should be able to find a way to strengthen that life force."

Bai Liang and Wu Ze exchanged glances, and after further discussion, they decided to follow Song Yihe's plan.

First, they would take over the entire Qingzhou, and then find a way to strengthen the new life force.

However, to take over Qingzhou, they would need Song Yihe's help.

"I have deep roots in Qingzhou, and I have many channels and connections, mostly with merchant families and schools in the mortal realm. They are the most useful for spreading rumors."

Song Yihe slowly spoke, beckoning Xu Cang to come out. After giving him a few instructions, Xu Cang retreated with a serious expression.

"Don't worry, they won't get involved in these matters," Song Yihe said confidently.

Bai Liang didn't say much, taking out his Mountain Token and sending a message to Yue Qiu.

Next, they discussed various details, including those for the other prefectures.

When Song Yihe learned that the governor of Baijiang Prefecture and the general were none other than Chu Wuliu and Hu Zhengqiang, who had been standing nearby, he was completely dumbfounded.

What was going on?

The top official of a prefecture and the top military commander had both come to Shenshan, and it seemed they had been staying for quite some time. No wonder his men hadn't found them recently; they had come here.

"Mr. Song, I never imagined we'd had dealings before," Chu Wuliu said with a smile.

Hu Zhengqiang also stepped forward with a smile to greet Song Yihe.

Song Yihe looked at the two men and finally let out a heavy sigh.

"It seems I overestimated myself and underestimated you all."

They'd colluded long ago; wasn't this a premeditated rebellion?

"Now that you two are here, Baijiang Prefecture is no longer a problem," Song Yihe began to discuss serious matters.

He mentioned his connections in several other prefectures, including the army in Yuelu Prefecture.

Many of the magistrates and prefects in Chizhou and Fengjiang Prefectures were his men.

These men could coordinate with them from within and without.

As for Yuelu Prefecture, Song Yihe's influence was even more ruthless, because the commander-in-chief of Yuelu Prefecture was his man, controlling the entire prefecture's army.

Now, with the addition of Baijiang and Qingzhou, if they were to raise their banners directly, they could instantly control three prefectures, enough to establish their own independent regimes.

However, there are too many variables, so it's better to take Qingzhou first.

Baijiangzhou is already in their grasp, so there's no need to rush.

As for Yueluzhou, although they already have an army, taking it won't be easy.

As for Chizhou and Fengjiangzhou, they'll be even more difficult.

Especially Chizhou, as the court officials and powerful figures who withdrew from Qingzhou are still stranded there.

"For now, let's take Qingzhou first!"

Finally, Bai Liang made the decision: first take the entire Qingzhou, then strengthen the new Qi!

As his decision was made, everyone left the Mountain Tower.

Everyone left with excitement and anticipation.

Because they had all experienced a historic moment today.

Perhaps in the near future, today's conversation will be recorded in history.

Everyone was full of energy, eager to contribute to the cause of Qianshan County.

Li Yue's divine sense witnessed the entire process.

He had considered that Qianshan County might eventually follow this path, but he hadn't expected it to happen so quickly.

Song Yihe, however, seemed to know quite a bit.

Li Yue wondered if this guy had been possessed by some kind of being?

But after examining him with his mental mirror, he found that Song Yihe was perfectly normal.

So he stopped worrying about it; after all, he had drunk the divine water and wasn't going to get off the boat.

"New fortune?"

Li Yue looked up at the sky above Qianshan County. He had previously seen a considerable amount of fortune gathering.

Back then, there was very little, but now there was much more.

It was roughly twice the amount seen in Peach Blossom County.

This new fortune arose invisibly, its origins unclear, making it impossible to discern its source.

However, Li Yue noticed some clues, sensing a connection between this fortune and merit, a link to something more.

Chapter 281 They've Got a Brain
.

Now, Qianshan County's influence has spread throughout Liaoyun Prefecture.

Mountain God Temples are also being built in various locations.

The most obvious change brought about by this is Li Yue's merit points skyrocketing, at a rate many times faster than before.

Li Yue looked at the panel.

[Realm: Fifth-Order Sacred Mountain]

[Divine Abilities: Mountain God's Blessing, Dharma Image of Heaven and Earth, Resumption of Incense Offerings, Gratitude for Water, Blessing the Earth]

In just a few dozen days, his merit points have increased by tens of millions.

Such a speed is truly astonishing.

Li Yue guessed that Liaoyun Prefecture alone probably couldn't have contributed this much.

It's likely that other places outside Liaoyun Prefecture, namely Qingzhou, also contributed a portion.

It seems the extraordinary phenomenon created by his previous act of blessing the earth has affected quite a few people.

Once he reached the sixth rank, he would be able to handle the incredibly complex situation ahead much more easily.

Qingzhou City.

The capital's elite talents who had stayed here had been here for quite some time.

Shangguan Shenghua was very puzzled. He had stayed to see what these four monks from Jinlong Temple were up to.

But they hadn't made any moves at all. They just stayed calmly in the city all day, cultivating when they had nothing to do, and occasionally gathering to debate Buddhist scriptures when the mood struck them.

This completely baffled him.

"What's the point of you staying in Qingzhou? This remote place doesn't have any government institutions to maintain order; it can't compare to the prosperity of the capital."

Shangguan Shenghua was puzzled, and even more so by Cha Xixing's presence.

After waiting for over ten days with no action from Shangguan Shenghua, he grew increasingly impatient.

The straightforward Cha Xixing immediately confronted Shangguan Shenghua, her face grim as she questioned, "Shangguan Shenghua, what's your purpose in staying in Qingzhou City?!"

Shangguan Shenghua, looking bewildered, retorted, "I haven't even asked you what your purpose is in staying in Qingzhou City, and you're asking me this?"

"I'm staying to keep an eye on you, of course!" Cha Xixing stated matter-of-factly.

"..." Shangguan Shenghua was speechless, unsure how to respond.

He didn't doubt Cha Xixing's words, because given his intelligence, it was entirely possible he would do such a thing.

Shangguan Shenghua was already used to it.

"I'm staying, naturally for the same reason you are!" Shangguan Shenghua said.

Cha Xixing was astonished: "So? You want to stay and keep an eye on me!?"

Cha Xixing glared at Shangguan Shenghua with anger.

A glint of shrewdness flashed in Cha Xixing's eyes as she said with a hint of smugness.

Shangguan Shenghua simply ignored Cha Xixing; he was afraid he was starting to get a brain too.

"Didn't you ever consider that we were both being tricked?"

Shangguan Shenghua suddenly spoke.

"You see, I stayed because I knew you would stay too. Have you ever thought about why I stayed?" Shangguan Shenghua said slowly.

A surge of anger rose within him: "So, the mastermind is those bald monks from Jinlong Temple! No wonder something felt off!"

Cha Xixing, hot-tempered, immediately wanted to cause trouble for Jinlong Temple.

Shangguan Shenghua wholeheartedly agreed; he too was getting impatient.

They would use Cha Xixing as a pretext to probe what the four from Jinlong Temple were up to.

In a pavilion in the western city.

The four sat facing each other, two playing chess, two observing.

Wushi suddenly spoke: "Why hasn't that martial uncle contacted us yet? Has something happened to him?"

The martial uncle he referred to was Sanfu, who was staying in the sacred mountain.

"Don't rush. The sacred mountain is guarded by deities; obtaining the secret manual won't be easy. Let's wait patiently for good news."

Wu Yan said calmly.

They had also verified Sanfu Shishu's identity with the temple.

He was indeed a third-generation disciple of their Golden Dragon Temple, and of considerable status, responsible for commanding the Golden Dragon Temple's forces in Baijiang Prefecture, entrusted with important duties.

This was a problem, but not a major concern.

After all, right now, the matter of the secret manual was more important than anything else!

Before he could stand up, Cha Xixing broke open the window and forced his way in.

Upon hearing this, the four members of Jinlong Temple were immediately stunned. They exchanged glances, their eyes asking, "Did you provoke this guy?"

Chapter 282 Shangguan Shenghua's Awareness
.

Qingzhou had been abandoned by the court, but the Third Prince's return to the capital had invigorated the court.

Could Qingzhou be reclaimed?

This proposal had not yet been adopted by the Chu Emperor, and there was considerable opposition.

However, there were also many supporters.

Supporters believed that reclaiming Qingzhou would greatly boost the morale of the Great Chu soldiers.

Therefore, the capital city hadn't withdrawn its attention from Qingzhou and continued to monitor the situation closely.

"Could they really be serious?"

Seeing Yue Qiu's further actions, Shangguan Shenghua felt a chill run down his spine, filled with worry.

If things really went as he suspected, the Great Chu Dynasty would face a violent upheaval!

It might even jeopardize the very survival of the human race.

Shangguan Shenghua gazed solemnly towards the direction of the sacred mountain, muttering to himself, "I hope you prioritize the human race and don't make a foolish mistake at this critical juncture, becoming a sinner for all eternity!"
