The Wilderness.

This was Kong Wang's twelfth day in the Wilderness.

The battle between the barbarians and demons continued unabated; in just these twelve days, over a hundred battles, large and small, had occurred.

Kong Wang had participated in over thirty battles and now felt utterly exhausted.

The barbarians suffered more defeats than victories, and another elder had fallen.

Kong Wang's expression was somber as he gazed at the colossal giant's corpse on the central altar.

Countless barbarian warriors, their faces etched with sorrow, surrounded the giant's body, offering prayers.

Since Kong Wang was not a barbarian, he did not participate, but instead mourned from afar.

Artur was wounded, and seriously wounded at that.

During a bloody battle with a sixth-tier demon, he unleashed a surge of blood rage, managing to kill the demon despite being of a higher rank.

However, after the blood rage subsided, he became completely weakened.

Taking advantage of this opportunity, the demons swarmed him, intending to kill Artur.

But Kong Wang arrived in time to save him.

Entering the tent, Kong Wang looked at Artur, who was sitting cross-legged healing his wounds. After a moment's hesitation, he said in a deep voice, "Sixth Elder, he has fallen."

Artur, who was healing, trembled slightly, then opened his blood-red eyes.

Chapter 283 Ancestral Bones
.

"Sixth...Sixth Elder...he's gone too?"

Atur's eyes were red, and he murmured hoarsely.

Kong Wang remained silent, then nodded slightly: "He died in yesterday's battle."

He recounted to Atur the circumstances of the Sixth Elder's demise.

Yesterday, a barbarian warband was trapped in a stalemate. Seeing this, the Sixth Elder didn't hesitate to plunge into the battlefield to rescue them.

But unexpectedly, this was a demonic scheme, luring the enemy deep into their territory.

The Sixth Elder may have known this, but he couldn't bear to watch thousands of barbarian warriors die before his eyes, so he stepped forward.

Single-handedly, he broke the stalemate, buying the barbarian warband a chance to retreat.

But while the barbarian warband retreated, the Sixth Elder was surrounded by eighth-tier demons who had been lying in ambush for some time.

The enemy had deployed three eighth-tier demons, including a powerful dragon demon whose strength reached the peak of the eighth tier, approaching the level of a half-saint!

Facing three opponents single-handedly, and with such formidable strength, the Sixth Elder was utterly outmatched. Even with his all-out resistance and using all his trump cards, he was forced into a steady retreat.

The other elders wanted to come to his rescue, but it would take time.

By the time they arrived, the Sixth Elder had already perished at the hands of the dragon demon.

The First Elder was enraged and launched a fierce attack, engaging the dragon demon in battle.

The other elders also fought a bloody battle against another eighth-tier demon.

The battle was earth-shattering and awe-inspiring, ultimately ending in the demons' defeat, with one eighth-tier demon falling.

The First Elder fought with all his might, but ultimately failed to subdue the dragon demon, which escaped.

The Ninth Elder was wounded; one of his arms was corroded away, requiring a period of recuperation for it to regrow.

The Sixth Elder's body was brought back and will be buried in the ancestral land of the barbarians in a few days.

Artur's eyes turned even more bloodshot after hearing this.

He clenched his fists tightly, then relaxed them, letting out a heavy sigh.

"I'll go see the Sixth Elder," Artur said, standing up and heading straight for the altar, ignoring his still-healing injuries.

Kong Wang followed closely behind.

Upon reaching the altar, Artur's tense expression finally crumbled, tears streaming down his face and falling to the ground.

He knelt down abruptly and kowtowed several times before the altar.

Kong Wang watched silently, his emotions of grief and indignation affecting him as well.

During his time with the barbarians, he had built strong bonds with them.

In his eyes, the barbarians were actually quite simple.

Although they appeared savage, that was merely an outward appearance.

Their hearts were, in fact, remarkably simple.

They worshipped power, pursued power, and their entire lives were dedicated to the struggle for power.

Now, they fight for the continuation of the barbarian bloodline.

Barbarian warriors continuously come to pay their respects to the Sixth Elder, the most special among them being the battle group he had saved with his life.

The barbarian warriors of the battle group wept bitterly, their faces filled with remorse.

If it weren't for them, the Sixth Elder wouldn't have died.

The First Elder arrives, his face displeased as he rebukes the weeping barbarian warriors.

"Blood debts must be repaid with blood! What's the use of crying here? Go back and cultivate!" the First Elder roared.

After being reprimanded, the barbarian warriors' faces hardened, and they turned and gradually left.

Then the Grand Elder approached Artur, sighing slightly, "Go back. Come back in a few days to carry your sixth uncle's coffin when Sixth Son is buried in the ancestral land."

Artur nodded in agreement, giving the Sixth Elder a deep look at him on the altar before turning to leave.

"Young friend, wait a moment."

Kong Wang intended to follow Artur, but was stopped by the Grand Elder.

"Senior, is there anything you wish to instruct me?" Kong Wang stopped, looking at the Grand Elder, who stood three zhang tall.

The Grand Elder's eyes were deep. He glanced at Artur's departing figure before speaking in a deep voice, "It's not an instruction, but this old man has something I'd like to trouble you with, young friend."

"What is it?" Kong Wang asked, surprised, not expecting the Grand Elder to speak like this.

"If the King fails, I hope you, young friend, can take Artur away, away from the barbaric wilderness, to the Great Chu, to the sacred mountain you speak of!"

The Grand Elder spoke with utmost solemnity.

"Take Artur to the sacred mountain!?" Kong Wang's expression changed slightly as he looked at the Grand Elder, saying, "Senior, why go to such lengths?"

He understood the deeper meaning in the Grand Elder's words.

It was the same as entrusting the future to someone; this was about securing a way out for the barbarians.

"Young friend, do you think attaining sainthood is simple? If it were, why hasn't a saint emerged in nearly a thousand years?"

The Grand Elder spoke in a deep voice, his tone grave.

"The King is very strong, exceptionally talented, but even the King himself isn't entirely confident in attaining sainthood. Therefore, it's essential to prepare a way out for the barbarians."

Kong Wang listened, his heart also growing heavy.

"Does Artur know this?" he asked.

The Grand Elder said, “He will know. You will receive the signal, and then you can take Artur with you on your journey. However, in that case, you will miss the chance to witness the King's ascension to sainthood.”

Kong Wang remained silent. He had traveled thousands of miles to the Wildlands to witness the barbarian semi-saint's ascension to sainthood.

Now the Grand Elder was telling him that he might not be able to see it.

After much deliberation, Kong Wang finally said solemnly, “Senior, I accept your request.”

Upon hearing this, the Grand Elder's expression relaxed, revealing a slight smile.

“Thank you, young friend. Taking Artur with you gives our barbarian tribe another way out,” the Grand Elder said sincerely.

Then he took out a bone and handed it to Kong Wang.

“This is a gift for you, young friend, as a token of my gratitude,” the Grand Elder said slowly.

“A leg bone?” Kong Wang asked doubtfully, accepting the 'leg bone' from the Grand Elder with both hands.

The bone was over a meter long and incredibly thick, unlike anything a normal human could have.

"This isn't a leg bone, but a finger bone," the Grand Elder corrected.

"A finger bone!?"

Kong Wang was astonished, staring at the meter-long bone.

This thing is a finger bone? Over a meter long?

Then how enormous must the owner of this bone have been!?

They could practically be described as a giant.

"What kind of being is this bone?" Kong Wang asked, somewhat shocked.

"The bone of the Barbarian Ancestor," the Grand Elder replied softly.

But this simple reply truly startled Kong Wang.

The Barbarian Ancestor? That's the ancestor of the barbarian race! The Grand Elder actually gifted him such a rare treasure?

"Senior, isn't this inappropriate?" Kong Wang asked hesitantly.

"What's inappropriate about it? The barbarian race is in dire straits. Leaving the ancestral bones in the wilderness will only subject them to the trampling of demons. It's better to disperse them; that way, the ancestral bones can be preserved."

The Grand Elder said calmly, his decision having been carefully considered.

Most importantly, this was also the King's intention; otherwise, how could he have so easily taken out the ancestral bones?


.
Chapter 284 The Barbarian King Proves His Way
.

Seeing the Grand Elder's words, Kong Wang could only put away the ancestral bone.

The Grand Elder spoke with Kong Wang for a few more minutes before waving him away.

"I wonder if sending Artur to the Divine Mountain was the right thing to do?"

The Grand Elder murmured softly, turning to look at a small hill in the distance.

The King was on that mountain; judging by the days, the time for his enlightenment was approaching.

...

Kong Wang returned to his tent and found Artur not there to heal his wounds, but sitting and waiting for him.

"The Grand Elder told you everything?"

Artur looked up at Kong Wang, his eyes somewhat dim.

"Yes, he did. Senior instructed me to take you away, to take you to the Great Chu." Kong Wang didn't hide anything; he felt Artur understood the gist of it.

"Why...why can't I stay? I can still fight!"

Artur clenched his fists, his expression filled with resentment, but even more so with grief and helplessness.

"Fifth-tier strength can't change the course of the battle," Kong Wang sighed easily.

Atur remained silent; he naturally understood this as well.

But he was unwilling to accept it, especially since he was the king's younger brother.

To leave like this was no different from deserting.

Kong Wang understood Atur's feelings but didn't offer any comfort, letting him figure it out himself.

Somewhere on the front lines of the Savage Lands.

A small human squad suddenly appeared.

All of them were clad in black robes, completely concealing their faces.

"The wilderness lies ahead. The barbarian demigod will attain enlightenment in seven days. We can simply observe from here."

An aged voice rang out.

"King Yang, we've arrived, but where is the Sect Leader?" King Gu frowned, asking in confusion.

This time, all four Heavenly Kings of the Dragon-Slaying Sect had come together for the momentous event of the Barbarian Half-Saint's ascension to Sainthood.

However, they were appearing on the side of the Holy Court.

"Is the Sect Leader's whereabouts something we could possibly know?" King Yang's tone was calm, yet it silenced King Gu's question.

"This time, the Barbarian King's ascension has alarmed the world's powerful figures, causing turmoil in all directions. Something terrible is likely to happen!"

"Zhao Tianwang's tone was chilling," she said.

Then, she suddenly turned to look at Nangong Hu behind her, coldly saying, "For such an important matter, why did Yang Tianwang bring along a junior who just broke through to the sixth rank? With such strength, he's not even qualified to be cannon fodder in the Dao Proving Battle!"

Nangong Hu, once again targeted by Zhao Yu, frowned deeply.

Her expression remained slightly cold as she calmly replied, "I was sent here by the Sect Leader. If Zhao Tianwang has any dissatisfaction or doubts, he can go directly to the Sect Leader."

"Using the Sect Leader to intimidate me?" Zhao Tianwang's expression suddenly turned cold, her aura as an eighth-rank Venerable directly pressing down on Nangong Hu.

"Zhao Yu! Are you done yet?!" Yang Tianwang's angry voice rang out. He glanced at Zhao Yu and said coldly, "Within the sect, you bullied the Holy Maiden because of your cultivation and seniority. Everyone saw it, but because of your status, no one wanted to say anything."

"Now that we're outside, you're still doing this! Do you even respect the sect's rules?! Put away your venomous thoughts, and stop acting like a vicious woman, picking on a junior all day!"

Zhao Tianwang's face was so dark it seemed to drip water.

But she dared not contradict Yang Tianwang, and could only harbor even more resentment towards Nangong Hu in her heart.

"Holy Maiden, King Zhao's words have some merit. Your strength is too weak; you're not suited to stand too far forward. If you encounter a powerful opponent, we won't be able to protect you. Although the Sect Leader specifically ordered you to come along, I still think it's best if you return first, or stay here. When the Dao Proving Battle breaks out, absolutely do not approach."

King Yang looked at Nangong Hu with utmost solemnity, speaking earnestly.

Nangong Hu understood the gravity of the situation and nodded in agreement.

The Dao Proving Battle was destined to be incredibly brutal; those below the eighth rank couldn't participate.

Her current realm was only just broken through to the sixth rank of the Yang God Realm.

She couldn't even withstand the aftershocks of a battle of this level.

However, returning was impossible. Having come this far, how could she be content without witnessing the Dao Proving Battle from afar?

Furthermore, she had other thoughts on her mind.

That monk, Kong Wang, seemed to have also come to the Wilderness. She wondered where he was now.

“Contact the Holy Court and tell them we are in position.”

King Yang spoke again. King Gu nodded, took out his communication artifact, and began sending a message to the Holy Court.

Inside the demon camp.

The demon responsible for contacting the Dragon-Slaying Sect received the message and immediately reported it to Qing Yuci.

“Excellent! With four eighth-tier experts, that's enough to deal with the eighth-tier experts of the barbarians!”

Qing Yuci spoke in a deep voice.

He waved for his subordinates to leave.

Then his true spirit left his body and tapped the void.

However, this time his tapping frequency was different, as if he was searching for an anchor point.

After finding this anchor, he tapped again, and a response came from the other side of the void. Only then did he enter the void.

Entering the void, everything here was completely different from the rest of the void.

Most of the void was empty and desolate.

But here stood two enormous palaces, incredibly sacred.

"Qing Yuci requests an audience with the Holy Venerable!" Qing Yuci began to bow from afar.

A response came from one of the azure palaces, inviting him inside.

The palace was resplendent in gold and jade, completely unlike the dilapidated bronze palace of the Witch Goddess.

Qing Yuci walked through the palace for a full quarter of an hour before reaching the main hall.

The main hall was devoid of any living beings, only a dazzling ball of light, blinding and blinding.

Qing Yuci dared not look any longer and immediately knelt, saying, "Greetings, Holy Venerable!"

"Speak," a majestic voice came from within the ball of light.

Qing Yuci, head bowed, said, "Holy Venerable, the Dragon-Slaying Sect is in position. Now everything is ready; we only need to wait for the opportune moment."

"Where is the sect leader?" the Demon Clan Holy Venerable suddenly asked.

"He is hidden in the void, saying he will intervene at the crucial moment," Qing Yuci replied.

"Try to deal with me when the time is right?" the Demon Saint sneered.

Upon hearing this, Qing Yuci broke out in a cold sweat, feeling an immense pressure bearing down on him.

"Your Majesty, please forgive me! I failed to notice anything amiss. I will immediately go and capture that cunning human!" he hurriedly said.

"Ignore him. It's like a mantis trying to stop a chariot; he's overestimating himself."

The Demon Saint said calmly, "How could a half-saint possibly match the might of a saint? Let them cause trouble. If they dare to cause trouble again, we'll annihilate them in one fell swoop."

Qing Yuci was deeply shocked, thinking, "This is what a saint is!

He must possess an aura that looks down upon everything. No matter how much a half-saint schemes, what waves can he stir up in the face of absolute power?"

Chapter 285 Disturbances from All Directions
.

"Your Majesty, I have another matter to report. The barbarians have secretly arranged multiple escape routes, intending to send their younger generation away. Should we intercept and kill them?"

Qing Yuci continued.

"Those escaping ants, you can decide for yourself," the Demon Saint said calmly.

"Yes, I understand," Qing Yuci nodded emphatically, his decision already made.

As the saying goes, if you don't cut the weeds at the root, they'll grow back in the spring breeze.

Rather than leaving future troubles, it's better to put in the extra effort and eradicate the barbarians completely!

Next, Qing Yuci reported some other situations, mostly battle reports from the barbarian battlefield.

The barbarian tribes lost four elders, but the Holy Court suffered even greater losses.

This directly resulted in the Holy Court having fewer and fewer eighth-tier Venerables. Already lacking high-end combat power, the Holy Court's losses were now so severe that it had essentially crippled its very foundation.

"The blood energy generated from annihilating the entire barbarian race is enough to create double-digit numbers of eighth-tier powerhouses. There's no need to worry."

The Demon Saint said calmly.

Qing Yuci nodded, indicating he understood. After a moment's hesitation, he finally spoke, "Your Majesty, I've heard that the Heart Demon is still in seclusion. Is he perhaps attempting to break through to the Saint Realm?"

Aturu's earlier words still lingered in his mind.

Why was he working so hard outside, busy with various affairs, while the Demon Half-Saint was peacefully cultivating in seclusion within the Holy Court?

Previously, he hadn't thought so much about it; he simply assumed that since the Heart Demon was in seclusion, he was handling all the affairs of the Holy Court, thus indirectly wielding most of the court's power.

But now he was thinking more deeply. His inner demon had been in seclusion, perhaps not practicing any special techniques, but secretly attempting to break through to the Saint realm?

This made Qing Yuci uneasy. What if his inner demon broke through to Sainthood before him?

Achieving Sainthood was incredibly difficult, a one in a million chance.

However, their Holy Court had a Saint Venerable, a ready-made success story. With a Saint protecting and guiding them, the chances of becoming a Saint would only increase significantly.

The Demon Saint was incredibly perceptive, seeing through Qing Yuci's thoughts at a glance. He sneered, "Focus on your business and don't let your mind wander. Becoming a Saint isn't that simple. Out of respect for you being my junior, I'll be direct with you."

"As long as the Ancient Saint's Heavenly Connection remains, as long as the shackles of Heaven and Earth remain, no new Saint can be born! Absolutely not!"

Qing Yuci was shocked upon hearing this!

Doesn't what Your Holiness said mean that Aturu's path to enlightenment is doomed to failure?

Since the Dao Proving Conference failed, why did the Holy Court go to the trouble of mobilizing a large army to obstruct it?

Qingyu Ci asked this question.

The Demon Saint scoffed upon hearing this.

"Who said they would stop the barbarian half-saints? The Holy Court's true purpose is to annihilate the entire barbarian race! Obstructing their path to enlightenment is merely a side effect."

Hearing the Holy Venerable's words, Qing Yuci finally understood.

"So...I see! The Holy Venerable is truly wise!" Qing Yuci exclaimed.

"Attaining sainthood is absolutely impossible in this world. So you need not worry. That demon is merely cultivating a secret technique," the Demon Saint said calmly, reassuring Qing Yuci.

After thanking the Holy Venerable, Qing Yuci withdrew from the Void.

Upon returning to the mortal realm, his expression suddenly darkened.

The Holy Venerable's words had indeed dispelled his doubts and suspicions.

But they had also extinguished his hopes.

"In this world, is there no more being capable of attaining sainthood?" Qing Yuci murmured softly.

If that were the case, wouldn't he be forever destined to never reach the Saint realm?

A sense of resentment welled up within Qing Yuci. He recalled the moment when the two Holy Venerables attained sainthood.

A crimson meteorite fell from the sky, shattering the shackles of heaven and earth, and crashing into the heart of the demon realm.

Back then, the Holy Venerables were only at the half-saint level.

They were the first to approach the crimson meteorite, and in the end, no one knew what they discovered or gained there.

All that was known was that after they emerged, they had become unparalleled powerhouses at the Saint realm!

But from that time on, Qing Yuci noticed that the two Holy Venerables' personalities had gradually changed, becoming somewhat cold and ruthless, and the distance between them had grown ever wider.

In terms of clan seniority, the Holy Venerables were still his uncles.

Before becoming a Saint, the Holy Venerables had treated him quite kindly, but after becoming Saints, they were completely different.

Only on certain occasions would they show a little concern.

For example, today, the Holy Venerables had talked about a few things.

Qing Yuci turned his head, looking towards the Holy Court.

The enormous crimson meteorite was still within the Holy Court.

However, it was placed in a forbidden area, and apart from the two Holy Venerables, no one else, including Half-Saints, was allowed to approach it.

"What exactly is this blood-red meteorite?"

A strong sense of curiosity suddenly arose in Qingyu Ci's heart.

In the void.

At the palace of the two saints, two consciousnesses were communicating across the void.

"Those guys outside are urging us again," the Demon Saint said in a deep voice.

"They said that if we delay any longer, they might take drastic action."

"What big moves could they possibly make? They've used up all the energy from breaking the boundary on the two of us; what trouble can they possibly cause?" the Demon Saint scoffed.

"Perhaps they'll go mad," the Demon Saint suddenly said. "The Heavenly Connection won't last much longer; their arrival is only a matter of time."

"Then let them wait a little longer. We've endured ten thousand years; what's a little more time?" the Demon Saint said calmly.

The Demon Saint was silent for a moment, then finally said, "There's a reason. They're in a hurry, saying they've sensed danger. Those ancient saints and gods from a thousand years ago might not be dead yet!"

"How is that possible!?" Upon hearing this, the Demon Saint's consciousness trembled violently.

"The ancient gods fought amongst themselves, eroding even the very rules of heaven and earth. They were certain long ago that the ancient gods had perished, vanished from the world. How could they not be dead?"

The Demon Saint slowly said, "I don't believe it either, but they did sense a crisis, and this crisis is even more intense than before the divine calamity."

"Most importantly, they sensed a new aura within the shackles of heaven and earth. An unknown being is attempting to comprehend the laws of heaven and earth, seeking to understand the entire world, including themselves."

The Demon Saint's tone began to grow solemn.

"They didn't deceive you?"

The Demon Saint shook his head: "No, I can see they're truly anxious."

"They think so too. That's why they keep urging us to execute the plan," the Demon Saint said.

"Execute the plan? With just the two of us at the Saint level?"

Chapter 286 'All Saints' Gather!
.

The Demon Saint spoke in a deep voice, his tone tinged with displeasure.

The strength of a Saint at the first level was merely that of a newly initiated Saint. In the divine calamities of the previous era, such strength would be nothing more than cannon fodder.

To execute the plan, at least the strength of a Saint at the third level was required.

Forcibly executing the plan with the strength of a Saint at the first level would at best result in death and the annihilation of their cultivation, and at least damage to their foundation.

“Once we annihilate the barbarians and completely destroy the fortune of the Great Chu, the two of us will be able to recover to the second level of Sainthood,” the Demon Saint said.

“The strength of the second level of Sainthood is still not enough!”

Although they had helped him enter,

the price had already been agreed upon: the plan would be executed after the two of them recovered to the third level of Sainthood.

How could he accept being asked to advance the timeline?

“They said they would compensate us,” the Demon Saint replied.

The Demon Saint said, "Then what are your thoughts? To execute the plan ahead of schedule?"

"No! How could I possibly do something that harms myself for the benefit of others?"

He was merely relaying their words; it didn't mean he was on their side.

The power of a Saint in this world meant they could roam freely without fear.

However, demons hadn't yet wiped out humanity because of the protection of this fortune.

Luck is a mysterious and unpredictable thing. The demon saints and fiend saints who had just arrived in this world didn't understand the power of human luck and suffered a direct loss.

To this day, they are still plagued by this luck, their strength restricted by a large portion of it.

"It will be soon. Once the barbarians are wiped out, most of this foul luck will be dispelled. We can then wait a while longer and fully recover."

The atmosphere in the capital was different today.

He also knew that the barbarian tribe was about to attain enlightenment, a matter of great importance concerning the entire human race.

Tai Xuan spoke, but her voice was that of a woman.

Jiabi frowned, finding the situation quite troublesome.

Emperor Chu's tone slightly somber. “The strength of the Divine Mountain God is unknown. If He makes any moves, the situation will become very unpredictable.”

Compared to the mountain god, he was more concerned about the two saints, the demon and the monster; they were the real two mountains, suffocating them.

Chapter 287 Escape Route
.

Li Yue's divine sense reached Kong Wang's side and witnessed the barbarian's massive operation.

The news that the Barbarian King would attain enlightenment in seven days had spread, and the entire barbarian tribe was thrilled and excited, shouting loudly.

But at the same time, some secretly felt a pang of sadness, their eyes reddening.

"Ninth Elder, we don't want to leave! We want to stay and live or die with the barbarians!"

"The barbarians have no cowards! What is there to fear in death?!"

"We will fight to the last moment for our King! We will fight the demons to the death!"

Some of the younger barbarians who were ordered to evacuate said through gritted teeth.

The Ninth Elder, his heart heavy, his injuries not yet fully healed, looked at these younger barbarian warriors before him.

These were all selected, talented young barbarians; they were the hope for the future of the barbarians!

"You must leave. There is no point in staying. Only when you leave can the King focus wholeheartedly on attaining enlightenment," the Ninth Elder said solemnly.

“But…”

“No buts!” Elder Nine interrupted directly, “Remember, you are not deserters, but the hope for the continuation of the barbarian bloodline! Escape, survive!”

The atmosphere turned somber. The chosen barbarian youths felt incredibly heavy-hearted; they understood perfectly well.

Perhaps after they left, the barbarian wilderness would cease to exist.

Looking at their somber faces, Elder Nine said, “There’s no need to be so gloomy. Your evacuation is merely a backup plan. As long as the King succeeds in attaining enlightenment, the barbarian race will reverse the situation and rise again!”

“It will definitely happen! The King will succeed!”

The young barbarians said firmly.

“Go, go on your journey!” Elder Nine spoke slowly, looking at the group of young juniors before him with an indescribable complexity in his eyes.

“Elder…then we’re leaving.”

The young barbarians’ voices suddenly choked with emotion.

After saying their farewells, they dared not linger, afraid of being overwhelmed by the emotions of parting.

Shouldering their heavy packs, the several hundred-strong barbarian caravan set off in a grand procession.

On their journey away from their homeland, they frequently looked back, their eyes filled with tears and reluctance.

Among the caravan was a seventh-tier barbarian powerhouse, accompanying them as their protector.

The Ninth Elder watched the caravan depart, then let out a long sigh.

This was just one of many caravans; the barbarians had prepared more than one escape route. Other caravans were likely already on their way.

Some headed for the far north, a land of glaciers, an environment only northerners could adapt to.

The extreme cold was so harsh that even demons avoided it.

Others went to the Great Western Sea, where numerous islands were inhabited by sea creatures.

Stills headed for the Southern Frontier, and so on.

Having seen so many caravans off, this was their final, reluctant decision.

"Your Majesty, you must succeed!" The Ninth Elder looked towards the small hill where the king stood, his eyes filled with hope.

Later, when he arrived at Artur's location, he found that Artur was already gone.

The Ninth Elder frowned slightly, found a guard to inquire, and learned that Artur had gone to the front lines.

"Nonsense!" The Ninth Elder's face darkened slightly, and he said, "Send someone to bring him back to me immediately!"

"Yes, Elder!"

The guard nodded hurriedly and immediately retreated to fetch someone.

The Ninth Elder, however, remained somewhat uneasy, so he went directly to the front lines.

When he arrived, he saw Artur fighting fiercely on the battlefield, his fists whistling through the air, smashing a fourth-tier demon with a single blow.

Artur's face was flushed, clearly he was fighting with great intensity, while Kong Wang was also battling demons nearby.

"You've come too?" an aged voice rang out, startling the Ninth Elder.

"Great...Great Elder? What are you doing here?" the Ninth Elder asked, somewhat surprised.

The Great Elder's gaze was deep as he looked at Artur, who was fighting a bloody battle on the battlefield, and he spoke softly, "He said he wanted to fight one last bloody battle for the barbarians, and I agreed."

"I see." The Ninth Elder understood, and then his gaze towards Artur became complex.

Finally, he sighed, "If it weren't for dire circumstances, why would we have let this brat leave his ancestral lands and become a dependent in the Great Chu?"

"Leaving the ancestral lands or becoming a dependent, as long as the barbarian bloodline continues, that's enough," the First Elder said quietly.

"Yes, everything is for the continuation of the barbarian bloodline,"

the Ninth Elder said.

Atur kept throwing punches; he had lost count of how many demons he had killed.

He only knew how to throw and pull his fists; his mind was completely numb.

Only when the blood of demons splattered on his face could he regain his senses for a moment.

"It's getting dark," Atur said, looking at the horizon. He remembered arriving at dawn.

"The demons have retreated,"

Kong Wang said, coming to Atur's side.

Atur came to his senses and realized that there were no demons left on the battlefield, only corpses scattered everywhere.

The barbarian warriors were currently collecting the bodies of their fallen comrades, the atmosphere incredibly somber.

"It's over," Artur said, bowing his head and joining the other warriors in collecting the corpses.

He remembered his early days on the battlefield, when he was just a common soldier, often doing this kind of work as well.

"By the way, Kongwang, you're a monk, could you perform a ritual for my people, so their souls can find peace?" Artur suddenly asked.

Kongwang hadn't expected Artur to bring this up. He thought for a moment and said, "This humble monk only cultivates this life, not the next. But since you asked, I can perform a ritual for your people."

"Thank you," Artur said, a rare smile appearing on his face.

Kongwang nodded, clasped his hands together, and began chanting Sanskrit scriptures in the air, performing a ritual for the deceased.

As he chanted, golden Sanskrit characters appeared, and Buddhist light enveloped the battlefield, lessening the heavy, murderous atmosphere somewhat.

He continued chanting in this vain manner until he had traversed the entire battlefield, only then stopping.

By this time, the collection of corpses was complete, and Artur had withdrawn from the battlefield.

The two elders had been waiting for a long time. Seeing Artur covered in demon blood, the Ninth Elder said, "You must be tired. Go back and rest."

"No," Artur shook his head, "What is this? If the demons hadn't retreated, I could have fought for another three days and three nights!"

"There isn't enough time for you to fight for another three days and three nights," the Ninth Elder said in a deep voice, then continued, "You have to leave tomorrow, or it will be too late."

"Can't I wait a few more days?" Artur said, pursing his lips.

"No! The demons have already begun a major offensive. If they form an encirclement, we won't be able to escape!"

The Ninth Elder shook his head decisively, his tone leaving no room for doubt.

Artur remained silent. He understood that Artur truly had to leave the Wilderness.

The First Elder looked at Kong Wang beside him and said, "Young friend, we'll leave Artur in your care. We hope you can take them to the Great Chu."


.
Chapter 288 Daring to rival the heavens!
.

"Senior, please rest assured, I will do my utmost to bring them safely to Great Chu," Kong Wang said earnestly.

Atur wasn't traveling alone; he was accompanied by other barbarians.

The group numbered about a hundred, and like other groups, they were all young members of the barbarian tribe.

Moreover, each one was exceptionally talented and promising.

Kong Wang couldn't guarantee that he could bring everyone safely to Great Chu, as his Divine Concealment Talisman couldn't hide so many people.

But he would do his best!

"Atur, you must cooperate and listen to young friend Kong Wang, understand?" the Grand Elder said sternly, looking at Artur.

Artur replied, "I understand."

The Grand Elder then gave several more serious instructions.

Finally, the Grand Elder changed the subject, saying, "By the way, Artur, the King wants to see you."

"The King?" Artur was slightly taken aback.

Although the king was his own brother, the two rarely met, despite their consistently good relationship.

"I understand," Artur nodded, then headed straight for the small hill where Artur was.

The Grand Elder then looked at Kong Wang, "Young friend, we entrust Artur and the others to the sacred mountain!"

"Rest assured, Senior, once they reach the sacred mountain, the Mountain God will surely protect the barbarians!" Kong Wang said firmly.

"I hope so," the Grand Elder murmured.

Then he and the Ninth Elder departed.

Time was of the essence for them, not just for them, but for the entire barbarian tribe.

The entire barbarian tribe was preparing for the king's ascension to the Dao, every warrior prepared to die.

This final battle would mean either annihilation or rebirth!

Artur didn't return to his camp until the very next day.

When Kong Wang saw him again, he noticed Artur's expression was much calmer, lacking the previous heaviness and sorrow.

He wondered what the barbarian demigod had said to Artur.

“Fellow Daoist Kongwang, I'll leave the journey to you in my care,” Artur said solemnly to Kongwang.

“We are both human, what trouble is there?”

Kongwang replied.

At this moment, Artur was ready. Outside, there were over a hundred people, all prepared to accompany him to the Great Chu.

“Artur, we can depart now.”

A burly man walked in, his aura revealing him to be at the peak of the seventh rank.

This was their protector on the journey, accompanying them to the Great Chu.

“Uncle Lu, we are all ready,” Artur said in a deep voice.

“Then let’s go!”

Uncle Lu didn’t say much, immediately giving the order.

Then he looked at Kongwang, his tone much more polite: “Fellow Daoist Kongwang, please lead the way.”

Kongwang nodded, pointed in the direction, and the group officially set off.

Artur glanced back at the desolate wilderness, his gaze complex, but finally turned around and embarked on his journey.

On the hilltop.

A massive figure stood upright, gazing intently in a distant direction, his eyes deep and unfathomable.

Watching Artur depart, gradually disappearing from the wilderness, the barbarian demigod Arturu let out a soft breath.

“Your Majesty, he has been seen off.”

The Grand Elder appeared and reported.

“I saw him,” Arturu said.

"Your Majesty, isn't it inappropriate to give the bone staff to Artur?" The Grand Elder hesitated for a moment before speaking.

"What's inappropriate about it? The bone staff is a sacred artifact of the barbarians; it shouldn't perish here with me. Let Artur take it far away; perhaps it can regain its glory in the future!" Artur said calmly.

"But Your Majesty, without the sacred artifact, facing the Demon Saints and Monster Saints will be even more difficult!" the Grand Elder said with concern.

Artur gently shook his head, saying, "Even with the bone staff's blessing, I can only hold out for a few breaths. Those few breaths are of little help to me."

Attaining sainthood is a great tribulation for cultivators!

It's not like the previous realms, which can be broken through by simply sitting in seclusion and cultivating.

Achieving sainthood is a qualitative change, a transformation of the life form.

Not only does the physical body need to break through, but the true spirit also needs to break through.

In other words, attaining sainthood requires two breakthroughs.

The first step is a physical breakthrough in the mortal realm—this is the most fundamental.

Only after the physical body breaks through to sainthood can it bear the true spirit of a saint.

However, the breakthrough of the true spirit is the most difficult and crucial step in becoming a saint.

Because the breakthrough of the true spirit must occur within the Great Void.

The rules of the Great Void are incredibly complex; even a true spirit will become lost within it after a while, let alone break through.

For Arturu, a physical breakthrough is not difficult; a breakthrough of the true spirit is the real challenge.

“Your Majesty, we will protect the mortal realm, but the Great Void can only be entered by half-saints. Sacred artifacts can also enter the Great Void, but the Bone Staff has already been given to Arturu. The threats and pressures you will face are too great!”

The Grand Elder's face was filled with worry.

The Great Chu might lend a hand, but they can't hold out for long; even ten breaths would be the best Great Chu can do.

Therefore, in the end, it still depends on Your Majesty yourself.

“This step had to be taken eventually,” Aturu said calmly.

He had endured countless hardships to reach this point, the pinnacle of cultivation for all.

For millennia, countless people had attempted to attain sainthood, but none succeeded.

Later, more and more dared not attempt, delaying their final attempt until their lifespan was nearing its end.

But how could one succeed when physically and mentally exhausted?

Even before the Absolute Transmission of Heaven and Earth, cases of cultivators who succeeded despite physical and mental exhaustion were extremely rare.

Let alone now, with the Absolute Transmission of Heaven and Earth.

However, Aturu was now attaining sainthood at his peak.

This was his prime, and instead of retreating, he faced the challenge head-on!

The final result was simply success or failure.

What was there to fear?

The only thing that worried him was the barbarians.

But he chose to attain sainthood for the sake of the barbarians.

"If the situation is irreversible, self-preservation is paramount. Never fight to the death, nor make meaningless sacrifices. Remember this!" Aturu solemnly instructed.

"Also, do not believe the words of the Old Gods. They have long been abandoned by the times, going against the tide of history, and are destined not to last long."

"..."

Aturu spoke as if giving his last words, continuously instructing the Grand Elder on various matters.

The Grand Elder listened silently, a strange sadness appearing on his aged, wrinkled face.

"That's about it. Remember this well. And remember, if I fail, flee immediately. Don't worry about my body; it's pointless!"

Aturu solemnly instructed again.

The Grand Elder nodded heavily.

"Your Majesty, this old minister understands and will certainly do as you say."

"Hmm, then you may leave. I also need to prepare myself. Let's see if the demon's fangs are sharper, or if my fists are more powerful!"

Aturu spoke in a deep voice.

Chapter 289 Taking That Step
.

In two days, Kong Wang led his barbarian group out of the Wilderness and deep into the Demon Realm.

They didn't choose the normal route, but instead took a large detour, avoiding the Wilderness's front lines.

Otherwise, being discovered would be extremely troublesome.

"Fellow Daoist Kong Wang, is Great Chu really that far? Hundreds of thousands of miles?"

Uncle Lu suddenly asked.

"Very far. Hundreds of thousands of miles is just the straight-line distance. We have to avoid the Demon Realm's spies along the way. And we have to take detours, so the actual distance we'll travel is probably much longer."

Kong Wang thought for a moment and replied.

"Hiss! So far!?"

Uncle Lu's face showed surprise, and a sudden, inexplicable sadness welled up inside him.

A hundred thousand miles away, leaving the Wilderness this time, he feared he would never be able to return to his ancestral land.

He pinched the sand in his inner pocket; it was his only memento.

The expressions of Atur and the other barbarian descendants were all incredibly complex.

Two days had passed since they embarked on their journey, yet they still frequently looked back towards the wilderness, their hearts filled with longing.

During their nighttime rest, Kongwang could occasionally hear the sounds of weeping.

"My king, have you begun your path to enlightenment?" a young barbarian asked.

Uncle Lu glanced at the speaker and said calmly, "Don't think about it so much, just keep moving."

Throughout the journey, he had avoided mentioning the wilderness within the group.

Mainly because it would affect everyone's mood, thus lowering their vigilance and speed.

The past two days had not been smooth sailing.

They frequently encountered small groups of demons, and in such situations, they would charge in without hesitation and annihilate them without exception, to prevent their journey from being discovered.

"The demons should know about us by now,"

Artur said gravely.

In two days, even the slow-witted demons would have reacted, with frequent disappearances of demon groups and various unusual behaviors from the barbarians.

Intuitively, it was easy to deduce that the barbarians had already arranged for some of their people to escape.

The demons would then undoubtedly launch a pursuit, not letting any of the barbarians who had been sent away escape.

This would greatly affect their journey to the Great Chu.

“Just now, I discovered a demon scout in the western jungle; I've already killed him. However, this means the demons have discovered our whereabouts, and we must leave this area as soon as possible,”

Kong Wang said solemnly.

Everyone nodded. Uncle Lu spoke up, “Fellow Daoist Kong Wang, you have experience, so you should continue to lead the team.”

He was the strongest in cultivation, but he understood even better that giving orders without understanding the situation would kill everyone!

Therefore, he wisely let Kong Wang, who had traversed the demon territory, lead the team, while he only needed to silently protect them.

“Alright!” Kong Wang nodded solemnly. Feeling the expectations placed on him by a peak seventh-tier expert, he dared not be careless.

The people in the team were the hope for the continuation of the barbarian bloodline; they could not afford to be lost.

Kong Wang used his pupil technique to examine the changing energy in all directions.

He discovered that returning the way they came was the safest option; the chaotic energy in the other directions indicated the presence of unseen beings.

And what other beings existed within the demon realm besides demons?

Having determined the best course of action, Kong Wang immediately stated that they should return the way they came.

Uncle Lu and the others looked puzzled until Kong Wang explained, at which point they understood.

Artur trusted Kong Wang and immediately said, "Listen to Fellow Daoist Kong Wang, return the way we came!"

Without hesitation, everyone quickly prepared and headed back the way they came.

Shortly after they left, a small demon squad arrived.

Leading them was a stork demon at the peak of the seventh rank.

"Hmm? Their aura stopped here, those barbarians have escaped again!" the stork demon said coldly.

"My lord, which direction should we pursue them now?"

A demon asked.

The stork demon looked around. The most likely direction the barbarians would flee was towards the Great Chu.

However, there were demons intercepting them there, so the chances were slim.

But often, the most dangerous place is the safest place!

Thinking this, a glint flashed in the stork demon's eyes, and he sneered, "Pursue them!"

Kong Wang led the barbarians, and after escaping for some distance, finally shook off the demons' pursuit.

Kong Wang then used his pupil technique again, choosing a relatively safe route—a dried-up riverbed—and the group continued on their way.

Meanwhile.

Somewhere on the front lines of the Savage Lands, the Four Heavenly Kings of the Dragon-Slaying Sect had been there for several days.

Nangong Hu, as the Holy Maiden, had come along, but now Heavenly King Yang was demanding that she leave.

“The battle for Dao Proof is extremely brutal! Lord Yantian once guided me, so I don’t want his only bloodline to perish here,” Heavenly King Yang said calmly to Nangong Hu alone.

Nangong Hu lowered her head and said, “I understand all that, but the Sect Leader ordered…”

“You don’t need to concern yourself with the Sect Leader’s orders. He simply wants someone to see you. Whether this is a blessing or a curse for you is still unknown,” Heavenly King Yang interrupted directly.

Nangong Hu frowned slightly, having some guesses in her mind, but she couldn’t be sure yet.

"Go, return to the sect. I will explain to the sect leader myself."

Yang Tianwang said calmly, waving her away.

Finally, Nangong Hu had no choice but to leave. Before departing, she solemnly thanked Yang Tianwang.

Within the Dragon-Slaying Sect, she couldn't distinguish who truly cared for her. She only knew that she became a Holy Maiden because of her father's status and her own special nature.

Even Yang Tianwang, usually an imposing figure, was earnestly advising her to leave today.

Nangong Hu couldn't discern whether he meant her good or bad, but her intuition told her that staying here was dangerous; she had to leave!

So she left.

Following the route she had come by, and given the special nature of her bloodline, the journey wouldn't be dangerous.

"It seems I should take this riverbed?"

Looking at the dried-up riverbed before her, Nangong Hu hesitated for a moment before setting off.

...

A storm was brewing. With the day of the Barbarian King's ascension to the Dao drawing ever closer, the atmosphere throughout the entire Wilderness, including the Holy Court, grew exceptionally heavy.

Even the capital of the Great Chu, hundreds of thousands of miles away, seemed to be affected by this atmosphere, becoming somewhat oppressive.

The Chu Emperor announced he would not govern for a month, with state affairs temporarily managed by the Imperial Preceptor.

The two patriarchs of Jinlong Temple and Xuanqing Temple also announced a month-long retreat.

These unusual signals filled the people of the capital with unease.

Those who knew the inside story were filled with heavy hearts, hoping for a good outcome.

At the Divine Mountain, Li Yue was also paying close attention to the Wilderness.

However, due to Kong Wang's departure, he couldn't directly descend with his divine sense.

But he could still glean information about the situation in the Wilderness through the Void.

"Judging by the days, Aturu will take that step tomorrow, won't he?"

Li Yue thought to himself.

Chapter 290 Difficult, but not impossible!
.

Li Yue activated his divine sense, channeling it entirely into the void to prevent any unforeseen complications.

He also summoned a person—no, a god.

The Goddess of Sorcery, riding her bronze palace, hurried from the depths of the void. Upon seeing Li Yue, her tone was icy: "Fellow Daoist Mountain God, what brings you here?"

She was filled with rage. She hadn't expected that after their last encounter, he had tampered with her bronze palace.

Just now, she had been recovering her strength somewhere in the void when a voice rang out, demanding her immediate presence, or else he would personally "invite" her!

The tone was domineering and brooked no argument. The Goddess of Sorcery immediately recognized it as the unknown deity from the Divine Mountain.

This infuriated her. How could there be such a wicked deity?

She had humbly apologized, offered gifts, and revealed so many secrets, yet this wasn't enough; he wanted to secretly sabotage her?!

If it weren't for her inferior strength, the Goddess of Sorcery would have already erupted.

Li Yue naturally noticed the Goddess of Sorcery's displeasure, but he had no time to dwell on it, cutting straight to the point: "You've come here, there's certainly a good show to see."

"What good show? Are you referring to the barbarian semi-saints attaining enlightenment?" the Goddess of Sorcery asked calmly.

"Yes, attaining sainthood. Wouldn't you like to go and witness it?" Li Yue said.

"Go and witness it? I'm not crazy. When human cultivators attain enlightenment, it inevitably triggers the rules of heaven and earth. For us old gods, abandoned by the times, getting close would be like inviting trouble, likely resulting in our annihilation by heavenly lightning!"

The Goddess of Sorcery raised her voice, then added, "Besides, it's just attaining sainthood, isn't it? Before the divine calamity, even my divine messengers were at least at the Saint realm. What's there to witness?"

"The past is the past, the present is the present. In this world of absolute separation, for cultivators to attain sainthood is worth witnessing!" Li Yue said calmly.

Actually, he hadn't witnessed any of the previous instances of attaining sainthood, so he was definitely going to watch this performance.

"If Fellow Daoist Mountain God wants to see it, then go ahead. As for me, I'll pass," the Goddess of Sorcery replied.

"I'm asking you to go, not inviting you!"

Li Yue said calmly.

The Goddess of Sorcery's expression suddenly darkened, and she said in a deep voice, "What do you mean by this, Fellow Daoist?"

"Nothing much, it's just that while I'm watching the performance, there are people watching me watch it, which makes me very uncomfortable,"

Li Yue said lightly.

The Goddess of Sorcery was both angry and aggrieved. She couldn't refuse his forceful request.

Because she simply couldn't win!

Even a god under the eaves has to bow his head, and in the end, she could only grit her teeth and say, "Fine!"

Li Yue nodded in satisfaction; this was what it meant to be pragmatic.

Actually, he didn't necessarily need to bring the Goddess of Sorcery along, but he felt that these resurrected ancient gods were definitely not so simple.

Who knows what they're secretly plotting? Is the Barbarian King's ascension to the Dao also part of their scheme?

So it's best to bring them along, and we can also ask them some questions.

The Goddess of Sorcery, however, wore a cold expression, her heart filled with rage.

How could she have imagined she would end up being forced into this situation?

Absolutely impossible!

The gods are so high and mighty; even the supreme deity receives the respect due to a fellow god, unlike the strict hierarchical order of the mortal realm.

Li Yue asked, "How strong are the two demon saints? Give me a rough estimate."

"Their origins are unknown, and their strength cannot be judged by ordinary standards, but at most they are at the second level of Sainthood," the Goddess of Sorcery replied after a moment's thought.

When she wandered the void, she had glimpsed the palaces of the Demon Saint and the Demon God, sensing their auras, which weren't particularly strong.

"Second level of Sainthood? How does that compare to the gods?" Li Yue asked again.

The Goddess of Sorcery glanced at Li Yue, whose figure remained blurred, his features indistinct.

"The strength of a second level Sainthood should be slightly stronger than yours, but the difference shouldn't be significant," she replied slowly.

"Oh, stronger than me?"

Li Yue was somewhat surprised; he was currently at the fifth-tier Saint Mountain level.

His realm was completely different from that of a cultivator, but there was a rough conversion: his first level was equivalent to a second-level human cultivator.

Therefore, fifth level was equivalent to tenth level of a cultivator.

A Saint's first realm was ninth level, and second realm was tenth level!

From this perspective, his previous conversion method wasn't wrong; it was basically accurate.

However, there was still a problem: the Goddess of Sorcery might have misjudged his strength.

Last time he fought the Goddess of Sorcery, he only used a portion of his divine sense, but this time he entered the void with all his divine sense, so his strength was definitely much greater than before.

Therefore, his current strength should be stronger than a second-level Saint, but how much stronger was unknown.

“And what about you? How do you compare to the two Demon Saints?” Li Yue suddenly asked.

The Goddess of Sorcery fell silent instantly, and after a long while, she reluctantly replied, "My divine source is damaged, and my strength is only twenty percent of its peak. Compared to a Saint of the Second Realm, I should be inferior."

"Should be? Then you're inferior!"

Li Yue suddenly understood. No wonder the Goddess of Sorcery had been lying low; ultimately, it was because her strength was insufficient.

The Goddess of Sorcery looked somewhat embarrassed and quickly retorted, "Once my divine source recovers to more than forty percent, a Saint of the Second Realm will be like an ant in my eyes! If I can return to my peak, even a Saint of the Fifth Realm will have to kneel before me!"

Li Yue understood. The Goddess of Sorcery's peak strength was roughly at the level of a Saint of the Fifth Realm.

But now, she was only at the level of a Saint of the First Realm, perhaps even weaker than a Second Saint among demons.

He had previously heard the Goddess of Sorcery mention that among the resurrected ancient gods, she was among the small group whose strength recovered the fastest.

In other words, the current ancient gods are at most at the Saint level, and even then, they face various limitations, confining them to the Great Void.

"It seems the ancient gods aren't a significant threat," Li Yue thought to himself.

After asking a few more questions, which the Goddess of Sorcery answered, Li Yue led her to a certain location in the Great Void.

This place corresponds to the present-day Wilderness; the Barbarian King's true spirit entered the Great Void to attain enlightenment here.

"We'll wait here."

Li Yue chose a good spot and then concealed himself.

The Goddess of Sorcery also concealed herself here, though she appeared slightly nervous, seemingly afraid that the laws of heaven and earth would manifest during her enlightenment, harming her.

At this moment.

In the mortal realm, the Barbarian King stood atop a mountain peak, gazing at the horizon.

The weather today was highly unusual; there was no sun, no blue sky, only thick, dense clouds covering the entire sky.

“Enlightenment is difficult, but difficult doesn't mean impossible!”

Aturu's face was resolute, her eyes determined, as she exhaled heavily!

Chapter 291 Dark Clouds Loom Over the City, Threatening to Destroy It
.

Thunder rumbled and churned within the sea of ​​clouds, roaring like furious dragons.

The entire world darkened, as if the heavens had deliberately lowered their gaze.

Below the mountain, the most capable members of the barbarian tribe emerged.

They looked up at the sky, their eyes filled with excitement and tension.

Five elders stood at the forefront, leading a group of powerful barbarian warriors, suspended in the air.

The barbarian tribe now possessed no more than a million soldiers capable of fighting.

The remaining elderly, women, and children remained at home, anxiously awaiting the outcome.

The Grand Elder gazed at the mountain where the king resided, his eyes resolute, and roared in a deep voice: "Fellow tribesmen, today is a day of life and death for the barbarian tribe! Survival or extinction hinges on this day! Let us shed our blood to help the king attain sainthood!"

"Help the king attain sainthood!"

"Help the king attain sainthood!"

The million barbarian warriors roared, their voices shaking the heavens, directly overpowering the thunderous explosions.

Everyone's faces were incredibly resolute, filled with a death wish, a chilling aura emanating from them.

Across from them, on the other side of the battlefield, lay the boundless 'Black Sea'.

In this battle for enlightenment, the demons had mobilized several times the strength of the barbarians, millions of demons rushing to the battlefield.

The demonic energy soared into the sky, overwhelming the barbarian's bloodlust.

Leading the Holy Court was a dragon demon approaching half-saint strength.

Six eighth-tier demons stood to one side.

As for the half-saint Qingyu Ci, he was in the Void, where the most crucial battlefield was.

"Gentlemen, the day of annihilation begins!"

The dragon demon roared coldly, its gaze sweeping over the millions of demons below.

The demon warriors' eyes turned bloodshot, and they roared with excitement.

In their eyes, this was a gluttonous feast.

The great battle was imminent, awaiting only the Barbarian King's ascension to the Dao.

Meanwhile, a hundred miles away, the Four Heavenly Kings of the Dragon-Slaying Sect were also lying in wait.

They had come to assist the Holy Court in fighting the barbarians.

However, their feelings were somewhat complicated.

The Ancient Heavenly King remained silent for a moment, then suddenly spoke: "Is what we are doing right or wrong? Will we become sinners for all time? Traitors?"

These words immediately made the atmosphere strange.

After a long silence, Zhao Tianwang coldly sneered, "Those who achieve great things, why would they care about reputation? Our initial intention is to save humanity indirectly. As long as we can ensure the continuation of the human bloodline, any means necessary are worthwhile!"

"But can the reputation of demons be trusted?" Gu Shouyu asked slowly.

"Shut up! What's the point of discussing this now? We just need to follow the leader's orders; nothing else matters!"

Yang Tianwang said in a deep voice.

"The seven eighth-tier experts of the Holy Court, plus the four of us, are more than enough to deal with the five eighth-tier experts of the barbarians," Zhang Tianwang analyzed, continuing, "However, the barbarians possess the innate divine ability Blood Rage. If they fight desperately, we absolutely cannot withstand the burst of power."

"Don't worry about it. We're just helping out; we don't need to fight to the death. If the barbarians unleash Blood Rage, retreat immediately and let the demons handle it."

Yang Tianwang said calmly.

"That's good." Zhang Tianwang breathed a sigh of relief.

The main problem is that I'm getting old and I don't have that same drive anymore.

Of course, there was another reason: the barbarians were also human. The thought of risking his life against fellow human beings like the barbarians for the sake of demons was something he simply couldn't overcome in his heart.

While the mortal world was in turmoil, the void was fraught with peril.

On the day the Barbarian King attained enlightenment, all the 'saints' of the world gathered, among whom the two True Saints shone most brightly!

The sun shone brilliantly, like the rising sun, and the moon shone its light, illuminating the long, winding road.

The Demon Saint, Qing Tian, ​​descended, stepping on the sun and moon. His body was enormous, hundreds of feet tall, and the illusory shadows of the sun and moon beneath his feet were dazzling, brightening the otherwise empty void.

Qing Tian's expression was cold and stern, his icy eyes sweeping over his surroundings before a cold smile appeared.

"A few ants, do they really dare to come?"

"Since they've come, let's crush them!"

With a disdainful snort, flowers bloomed in the void. These flowers were dark blue, extremely alluring, and emitted a strangely fragrant aroma.

A massive tree trunk shattered the void and suddenly descended.

Its roots plunged directly into the laws of the void, standing firm and blocking out the sky!

The Demon Saint Realm Tree arrived, surveying its surroundings with disdain. The enormous phantom of the demon tree exuded an extremely dangerous aura, inspiring awe.

"What a grand display!"

In a corner, Li Yue witnessed this scene, clicking his tongue in amazement.

He thought to himself, "As expected of a Saint Realm cultivator, even their entrance is accompanied by an extraordinary phenomenon. But this is enough to prove their strength."

"Hmm? It seems there aren't any Saint Realm Two cultivators yet." The Goddess of Witchcraft, hiding in the back, noticed something amiss and said with some surprise.

"Not even Saint Realm Two?"

Li Yue was also surprised. No wonder he felt these two Saint Realm experts weren't very strong; it turned out they weren't even at the Saint Second Realm.

"Yes, but they don't seem to be that far off. They should be at the peak of the Saint First Realm, about to break through."

"The Goddess of Sorcery guessed," she continued.

"Since you're not even at the Saint Realm Level Two, doesn't that mean you could defeat them?" Li Yue suddenly asked.

"No." The witch goddess knew immediately what the mountain god was up to, and quickly said, "My own strength is still some distance from the peak of the Saint Realm. I can barely handle one, let alone two."

"That's a pity."

Li Yue shook his head slightly.

Then he thought that with the two demon saints so powerful, it would be virtually impossible for the barbarian Aturu to attain enlightenment.

Without anyone protecting him, his death was almost certain.

Li Yue voiced his concerns, but the Witch Goddess shook her head slightly, saying, "It's not that simple. Even a saint would pay a price for interfering with the path to enlightenment, and this barbarian brat might have other methods prepared."

"What methods can be used against a saint?" Li Yue asked curiously.

The Witch Goddess replied, "Sacred artifacts! Sacred artifacts can be brought into the Void and controlled by a half-saint, unleashing the power of a saint. While not as powerful as a true saint, they're not far off."

"If Aturu has prepared sacred artifacts, and with the assistance of several half-saints from the Great Chu Dynasty, there's still a very slim chance."

"I see," Li Yue said, understanding dawning on him.

However, given the demons' current lineup, even with preparation, the hope was slim.

It seemed the Barbarian King Aturu still had to take a gamble.

Meanwhile, the Chu Emperor and his two companions had also arrived.

However, they dared not approach too closely, lest they be discovered and captured by the two demon saints.

"It's about time. Once Aturu's physical body attains enlightenment, the true spirit's enlightenment will begin."

Emperor Chu spoke gravely.

Tai Xuan's expression was equally somber as he gazed at the two dazzling figures, a chasm he saw as insurmountable.

Saint Realm!

Just one realm apart, yet it was like the difference between heaven and earth, an insurmountable gap.

Chapter 292 Bloodbath
.

"This old Taoist has brought out all his most treasured ancestral artifacts, hoping to hold out for a little longer,"

Tai Xuan sighed.

To help Aturu attain enlightenment, he had even stopped refining the Nine-Turn Human Pill and restored his own appearance to ensure his cultivation returned to its peak.

"With all three artifacts activated simultaneously, we can at least hold out for ten breaths. Whether we can hold out longer depends on fate,"

Jia Pi frowned, some concerns lingering in his mind.

His main fear was that the half-saint from the Dragon-Slaying Sect might launch a sneak attack; this was an unstable factor.

"Don't worry, I brought two artifacts," Emperor Chu said calmly, seemingly sensing Jia Pi's concerns.

"Two artifacts!?"

These words stunned both Jiabi and Taixuan.

"Your Majesty, where did you get this second sacred artifact?" Taixuan couldn't help but ask.

He remembered clearly that the imperial family's only sacred artifact seemed to be the portrait of the founding emperor. He had never heard of the founding emperor leaving behind anything else.

Jiabi was equally puzzled.

The relationship between the Great Chu imperial family and the Xuanqing Temple of the Golden Dragon Temple was extremely close.

Over the centuries, they knew each other's secrets inside and out. The sudden appearance of another sacred artifact was bound to shock them.

Faced with their questions, the Chu Emperor seemed somewhat hesitant to speak, finally replying with an unnatural tone, "I... dug up the portrait of the founding emperor."

"Dug... dug it up! Hiss!?"

Taixuan and the other man were speechless with shock, looking at the Chu Emperor in disbelief.

"This...is truly filial piety!"

The two remained silent for a long time, finally understanding how the second sacred artifact came to be.

The Great Ancestor was a powerful figure at the third level of the Saint realm, a survivor of the divine calamity. Before his death, he left behind his remains, which were later buried in the imperial mausoleum after the Chu family established a dynasty.

The remains of a Saint are comparable to a sacred artifact, let alone the remains of a Saint at the third level.

"Your Majesty, you truly went to great lengths for the continuation of humanity!" Tai Xuan said with admiration.

The Chu Emperor looked somewhat embarrassed, but still said, "It's just remains. If the Great Ancestor knew in the afterlife, he would surely be gratified. The remains he left behind can serve the human race; it's a good use of them."

"Your Majesty, why didn't you say so sooner? If I had known, I would have dug up the Ancestral Master's remains too,"

Jiabi sighed.

The Great Chu Dynasty had its founding emperor, and their Golden Dragon Temple naturally had its own patriarch. Although his strength couldn't compare to the founding emperor, he was still a Saint of the Second Realm.

"This old Daoist didn't expect this either."

Tai Xuan also shook his head and sighed, mainly because neither of them had considered this point.

"Four sacred artifacts are enough. The difficulty of attaining the Dao lies not in the interference and obstruction of enemies, but mainly in the attainment itself."

The Chu Emperor spoke slowly.

Tai Xuan and the other man's expressions became solemn, and they nodded slowly.

Indeed, even if they blocked the interference of the demons, Atulu might not be able to successfully attain the Dao.

In the era of the severing of heaven and earth, no more saints were born; this was no joke.

Many ancient saints in the past protected their descendants and helped them attain the Dao, but none of them succeeded.

Those ancient saints who survived the divine calamity never gave up helping their descendants attain the Dao until their lifespans were exhausted and they died.

Unfortunately, the Heavenly Gate of Separation had sealed off everything, including the opportunity for these ancient saints to break through their cultivation levels, preventing them from making any progress.

Without reaching the sixth realm of sainthood, the Heavenly Gate of Separation could not be broken.

But the sixth realm of sainthood had all vanished without a trace.

Until the last ancient saint passed away approximately a thousand years ago, there were no more saints in the world.

Therefore, how difficult was it for Aturu to attain enlightenment?

He could only seize the opportunity presented by the opening of the Heavenly Cave to see if he could succeed in attaining enlightenment.

“The attainment of enlightenment begins.”

Emperor Chu suddenly spoke.

Tai Xuan and Jia Pi immediately used their divine senses to look through the void at the present world.

At the same time, Li Yue also sensed the changes in the present world and looked towards the outside.

In the present world.

The heavenly lightning within the dark clouds had been brewing for several hours, its aura growing increasingly terrifying, its power more than ten times stronger than at the beginning.

Aturu stood atop the mountain peak, his veins taut, muscles bulging.

His face was incredibly resolute as he gazed at the heavens.

"Aturu, King of the Barbarians, today, proves his power!"

Aturu's voice boomed like thunder, echoing throughout the heavens and earth.

The heavenly lightning seemed to unleash its power in response to his words, countless bolts of lightning cascading down like a waterfall, exploding with deafening roars.

Aturu roared, his body erupting with blood energy. The lightning struck his flesh, instantly sending white smoke billowing and charring his skin.

The mountain he stood on could not withstand the onslaught of the lightning; sand and rocks melted instantly, flowing downhill like lava.

After the first bolt of lightning, the entire mountain shrank.

But Aturu remained standing, perfectly upright.

His body was charred black, but his life force was incredibly vigorous; he had easily withstood the first wave of lightning.

"As expected of the Barbarian King, the pinnacle of physical cultivation. It seems his path to enlightenment through physical means is without a doubt."

In the void, Li Yue only glanced at Aturu and immediately recognized that, barring unforeseen circumstances, success in Aturu's physical enlightenment was a certainty.

The three Chu Emperors also showed expressions of admiration.

If he were to undergo this level of heavenly lightning baptism, the first wave would likely have left him sweating profusely.

Physical enlightenment is extremely difficult for cultivators of the Great Chu.

The Demon Saint Qingtian and the Demon Saint Jieshu were also watching. Qingtian calmly remarked, "This barbarian has something; his physical body is comparable to a dragon's. He's indeed very powerful."

Jieshu sneered, "Physical enlightenment is merely the first step. The most crucial step is true spiritual enlightenment—this is the key to the transformation of one's life level. Only when the true spiritual enlightenment is successful can one be called a saint!"

"Let's continue watching," Qingtian said calmly.

As the Barbarian King began his enlightenment process, the great war between the barbarians and the demons finally erupted.

Led by the Flood Dragon Demon, the demon army, their eyes bloodshot, charged towards the mountain where the Barbarian King was proving his enlightenment, attempting to obstruct him.

The barbarian warriors, however, stood before the Barbarian King, fighting desperately!

"For the King! For the Barbarians!"

The barbarian warriors roared with all their might, this time willing to give their lives to fight to the death!

Even with millions of demons ahead, what did it matter?

These five giants were incredibly conspicuous on the battlefield, possessing the strength of ten thousand men!

The First Elder, unlike his usual composed self, was now enveloped in a blood-red aura, his eyes filled with murderous intent. With a single strike of his Great Wilderness Hand Seal, thousands of demons perished.

"Who dares to take a step forward!?" The First Elder's voice shook the heavens and earth!

"Old thing, prepare to die!"

The Flood Dragon Demon attacked, its massive body emitting a faint dragon's roar as it charged forward.

Chapter 293 The Battle for Proving the Dao
.

Because supernatural powers can only be cultivated by saints.

Half-saints lack a thorough understanding of their innate path, so there are almost no cases of them successfully cultivating them; even if there are, they are extremely rare geniuses.

Chapter 294 Everything is for the sake of humanity

What was the reason?

No matter how much Ren Aturu guessed, he couldn't figure it out.

The witch goddess, still hiding in the shadows, also had doubts.

She didn't understand why Li Yue would help the barbarians. What was his purpose?

Was he trying to subdue the barbarians and collect their merits?

That wasn't so simple. This era could no longer tolerate ancient gods. To personally intervene and blatantly collect merits and faith was against the will of Heaven and would incur divine punishment.

Even an ancient god like herself with a base of followers only dared to secretly cultivate divine messengers and collect incense offerings on a small scale.

But doing so could only restore her divine source; she couldn't advance any further.

Because the ancient gods no longer belonged to this era.

An existence that should have perished in the previous era has appeared in this one; its very existence is an anomaly.

You want to go any further? That's impossible!

Unless you gain the approval of Heaven and Earth!

The Goddess of Sorcery couldn't understand this, so she suppressed her questions and looked at the three facing off.

She suddenly felt a surge of anticipation, wondering about the true strength of this mountain god.

During their last encounter, she judged his strength to be around the second level of the Saint realm.

Now, facing two unknown beings at the first level of the Saint realm, and possessing sacred artifacts, could she easily defeat him?

"But how come he's unaffected by the tribulation vortex?"

This was something the Goddess of Sorcery couldn't understand at all.

"It seems you've truly had enough of living," the Demon Saint World Tree said coldly.

He hadn't expected such a turn of events.

A deity appearing to aid humanity—how bizarre!

A shrine appeared out of thin air, supported by a pair of enormous hands manifested from the Demon Saint Tree.

He drew his sacred artifact.

The Demon Saint Qingtian, wielding a massive azure sword, his eyes sharp, spoke coldly: "Originally, I only intended to crush a few ants today, but now it seems I must slay a god!"

A strange red light flashed between their brows, extremely eerie.

And immediately after the red light appeared, the auras of the Demon Saint and the Demon Saint began to change in a bizarre way, surging dramatically!

In an instant, he seemed like a completely different person, even his eyes turning empty and violent.

"Hmm?" Li Yue noticed this sudden change and was greatly surprised.

He frowned and looked down, only to be able to glimpse layers of dark red mist!

But through his mental mirror, Li Yue noticed something amiss.

"This is a login from another location, a change of person?"
